### [1] [Symmetry Promotion in Electromagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05549)
*Hunter Swan,Jason M. Hogan*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了电荷或电流分布的离散对称性如何在某些受限区域内"提升"为电磁场更大连续对称性的现象，条件包括场为仿射线性且源分布的对称性在环境空间上不可约作用，并通过多个实例加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常，源分布的对称性决定场的对称性，此直观认知存在反例——源分布的离散对称性在某些条件下可导致场具有更大的连续对称性。该现象在电动力学教学和群表示论应用角度均具有教学价值。

Method: 通过理论推导，给出对称性从离散提升为连续的条件：场须为仿射线性，且源分布的对称性需在环境空间上不可约地作用。随后用多个具体实例对这一效应进行系统的展示与说明。

Result: 确认了特殊条件下源分布的离散对称性可化为所产生电磁场在受限区域内的连续对称性，并给出严格的数学条件（仿射线性场与不可约作用）。多个实例验证了该现象的普遍存在。

Conclusion: 该现象既为静电学与静磁学教学提供灵感来源，也是一个易于理解且可及的群表示论应用示例，具有双重教学意义。

Abstract: A symmetric distribution of charges or currents typically produces an electromagnetic field having the same symmetry group. However, under special circumstances we show that a discrete symmetry of the source distribution leads to a larger continuous symmetry for the resulting field in restricted regions. The conditions for this promotion from discrete to continuous symmetry are that the fields be affine linear and the symmetry of the source distribution act irreducibly on the ambient space. We illustrate this effect with many examples. This phenomenon is pedagogically interesting both as a source of inspiration in electro- and magnetostatics and as an accessible example of group representation theory.

</details>


### [2] [Repeated Binary Direct Collinear Impacts Under Incremental Contact Laws With Permanent Indentation: A Hybrid Systems Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06138)
*Mihails Milehins,Dan B. Marghitu*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将增量接触定律（如基于Bouc-Wen滞回模型的接触定律）嵌入刚性体碰撞动力学的混合动力系统框架。该框架将接触界面建模为携带接触定律及其内部状态的零质量元件，通过相对速度与相互作用力与两刚体耦合，从而将所有切换与重置行为限制在界面模型中，而不改变刚体的几何与运动方程。文章建立了该框架的若干解析性质（被动性、完备性、解的非唯一性），并通过两种Bouc-Wen型接触定律的双体直接共线碰撞仿真加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增量接触定律通过含内部状态的微分方程描述法向接触力，其中一些定律在非零压痕处力即消失（如塑性变形或弹性后效），导致分离时存在残余变形。这类定律与不允许变形的刚体动力学存在根本张力。当压痕相对物体较小时可通过本构方式承载，但仅靠接触定律本身无法确定两体相互作用——因为力与压痕不再同时消失，必须额外规定接触的起始与终止条件，以及分离时变形和内部状态的去向，而这些均不包含在运动方程中。为此需要一个系统化的框架来整合这些问题。

Method: 本文将重复直接共线碰撞的两凸体系统形式化为一个混合动力系统。接触界面被建模为携带接触定律及其内部状态的零质量元件，通过相对速度与取决于接触状态的相互作用力与两个刚体耦合。所有切换与重置逻辑均被限制在界面模型中，刚体的几何与固有运动方程保持不变。文中建立了该框架的主要解析性质（被动性、完备性、解的非唯一性），并使用基于Bouc-Wen滞回模型的两种接触定律对完整两体系统进行仿真演示。

Result: 文章确立了所提出混合动力系统框架的主要解析性质，包括被动性（系统能量的耗散有界性）、完备性（框架能描述所有相关接触行为）以及解的非唯一性。通过包含两种基于Bouc-Wen滞回模型接触定律的完整两体系统仿真，验证了该框架能够有效地整合接触定律与刚体动力学，并正确处理接触启停条件及分离时内部状态的去向问题。

Conclusion: 该混合动力系统框架为将带内部状态的增量接触定律嵌入刚体碰撞动力学提供了一套自洽的建模方式。通过将接触界面的状态与逻辑隔离在零质量界面元件中，既保持了刚体运动方程的简洁性，又完整地刻画了接触启停、残余变形和内部状态重置等行为。该框架的解析性质为工程应用提供了理论保证，Bouc-Wen模型仿真证明了其可行性。

Abstract: Incremental contact laws specify the normal contact force through a differential equation carrying an internal state, driven by the indentation and its rate. In some, the force is extinguished at a nonzero indentation, whether by plastic deformation or by an elastic aftereffect, so that a residual deformation remains at the separation. Such laws sit uneasily within rigid body dynamics, which admits no deformation. The tension is tolerable when the indentation is small relative to the bodies, so that it may be carried constitutively rather than geometrically. Even then, the contact law alone does not determine the interaction of the bodies. Because force and indentation no longer vanish together, conditions for the commencement and termination of contact must be supplied separately. So must the fate of the deformation and internal state at separation, neither of which the equations of motion contain. This article formulates the repeated direct collinear impact of two convex bodies under external forces as a hybrid dynamical system. The contact interface is modeled as a massless element carrying the contact law and its state, coupled to the bodies through relative velocity and an interaction force dictated by the contact state. Consequently, all switching and resets are confined to the interface model, leaving the geometry and the inherent equations of motion of the bodies unaltered. The principal analytical properties of the resulting formulations are established, among them passivity, completeness, and non-uniqueness of the solutions. The framework is demonstrated through simulations of the complete two-body system incorporating two contact laws based on the Bouc-Wen model of hysteresis.

</details>


### [3] [Induced electromotive force of a thin metal rod in the alternating electromagnetic field of Helmholtz coil: experimental results and theoretical analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06548)
*Yilin Shao,Minghan Gao,Baiqing Li,Xiaoguang Li,Chengfu Mu*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用细铜棒作为探针，在亥姆霍兹线圈产生的交变磁场中测量不同径向位置上铜棒感应电动势的变化。实验发现感应电动势在铜棒中心通过磁场中心时为零，随径向位移先增大后减小，存在极值点。作者构建了四种简化磁场分布模型，理论结果均能定性解释实验现象，其中分段函数拟合模型与实验数据定量吻合最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种测量亥姆霍兹线圈内部磁场分布的新方法，利用细金属棒感应电动势的径向变化规律来表征磁场分布特征，同时为本科生物理实验教学提供有价值的教学案例。

Method: 实验中，以细铜棒为探针置于亥姆霍兹线圈产生的交变磁场中，测量不同径向位置处诱导的感应电动势。理论上，基于亥姆霍兹线圈的实际磁场分布构建了四种简化的磁场分布模型，定量研究感应电动势沿铜棒的径向变化规律。

Result: 实验结果显示：铜棒中心通过磁场中心时感应电动势为零；偏离中心后电动势先增大至最大值，随后随径向距离增大而单调减小。四种理论模型结果相近，均能定性解释实验曲线，尤其能很好地复现电动势的变化趋势和极值点位置；其中分段函数拟合模型与实验数据定量吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解亥姆霍兹线圈内部磁场分布及其感应现象提供了新的实验与理论框架，四种简化模型验证了方法的可靠性，且工作对本科生物理实验教学具有显著的帮助和指导意义。

Abstract: We apply a thin metal rod (copper rod) as a probe in the alternating magnetic field generated by a Helmholtz coil, and measure the variation of the induced electromotive force (EMF) on the metal rod at different radial positions of Helmholtz coil. Experimental results show that the induced EMF is zero when the center of the metal rod passes through the center of the cylindrical magnetic field inside the Helmholtz coil. When the metal rod is displaced from the center of field to different radial positions, the induced EMF gradually increases from zero, reaches a maximum at a certain position, and then decreases monotonically as the radial distance continues to increase. At the position where the induced EMF reaches its maximum, the metal rod intersects the radial cross-section of the internal magnetic field of the Helmholtz coil at two points, with a small central portion of the rod located inside the Helmholtz coil and the two end portions outside the coil. To explain the experimental phenomena, we construct four simplified models of the magnetic field distribution based on the actual field distribution of the Helmholtz coil to quantitatively investigate the radial variation of the induced EMF along the metal rod. Our theoretical results show that the four models yield similar results and can all qualitatively explain the experimental data curves, particularly reproducing well the variation trend of the induced EMF and the position of the extremum point. The result of piecewise function fitting model is quantitatively in good agreement with the experimental data. This work is also very much helpful and instructive for undergraduate-level students.

</details>


### [4] [A purely mechanical system realizing a Coulomb-like interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06917)
*June-Haak Ee,U-Rae Kim,Jungil Lee*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文闭式求解一维相对论系统：两个质量通过无质量中介体之间的弹性碰撞相互作用。动量、时间和位置均为碰撞指数的双曲函数。中介体能量（视为系统有效势）满足精确的离散库仑定律 V∝1/r，并以洛伦兹不变作用量作为耦合。有质量牛顿中介体则传递1/r³力；一个绝热不变量将两种定律追溯到中介体的色散关系。当中介体能量延拓为负时，闭式解变为三角函数形式，形成了库仑原子的二维力学类比——一维机械"库仑原子"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一维相对论动力学系统中由无质量/有质量中介体通过弹性碰撞传递相互作用的基本模型，揭示其与经典库仑势及量子（Coulomb原子）物理之间的深层数学联系。

Method: 采用闭式解析方法对双质量间弹性碰撞序列进行精确求解，将质点动量、时间和坐标表示为碰撞指数的双曲（或三角函数）形式；通过物理量的分析识别中介体的有效势与色散关系，并借助绝热不变量建立统一理论框架。

Result: 得到了无质量中介体情形下精确的离散库仑定律；有质量牛顿中介体产生1/r³力；通过负能量延拓闭式解从双曲过渡为三角函数，实现一维机械库仑原子的束缚态描述。

Conclusion: 该可解相对论系统揭示了力学碰撞系统与电磁相互作用（库仑势）、特殊相对论及量子束缚态之间由色散关系和绝热不变量统一起来的深层联系，为一维相对论/量子模拟提供了理想的理论范式。

Abstract: We solve in closed form a one-dimensional relativistic system: two masses interacting only through elastic collisions with a massless mediator bouncing between them. Momenta, times, and positions are hyperbolic functions of the collision index. The mediator energy, interpreted as the pair's effective potential, obeys an exact discrete Coulomb law, $V\propto 1/r$, with a Lorentz-invariant action as coupling. A massive Newtonian mediator instead transmits a $1/r^{3}$ force; one adiabatic invariant traces both laws to the mediator's dispersion relation. Continued to negative mediator energy, the closed forms turn trigonometric, binding a one-dimensional mechanical analog of the Coulomb atom.

</details>


### [5] [Formation of grain boundaries in ductile single crystals under plane-strain simple shear: a block-coordinate finite element method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07016)
*Khanh Chau Le,Thanh Danh Nguyen*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究延性晶体在大塑性简单剪切下因弹性能失去凸性而自发细分为具有位错墙分隔的错向晶粒这一图案形成不稳定性，在连续位错理论框架下采用多凸(Ciarlet–Geymonat)弹性能，通过凝聚塑性滑移得到双阱形式的非拟凸能量，并由几何必需位错密度梯度实现正则化，最终用块坐标有限元法（交替凸非光滑滑移求解与Levenberg正则化Newton变形求解）数值复现了层状晶粒微结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解延性晶体在大塑性变形下如何自发从均匀单晶细分为错向晶粒与位错墙这一图案形成不稳定性，并建立兼具理论正确性（确保最小元存在）与数值可解性的连续介质模型。

Method: 采用连续位错理论描述平面应变简单剪切下的延性晶体变形；使用Ciarlet–Geymonat多凸弹性能以保证变形-滑移耦合问题的最小元存在性；对塑性滑移求极小化以获得双阱形式的凝聚能量，其非拟凸性支持层状微结构；以几何必需位错密度梯度作为正则化项使晶界具备有限厚度与能量；采用块坐标有限元法，交替进行凸非光滑滑移求解与Levenberg正则化的Newton变形求解。

Result: 数值方法能够分辨该微结构的自发涌现，数值结果与闭式分析一致，再现了层状晶粒结构；晶界厚度与能量可作为错向角的函数给出。

Conclusion: 该多凸连续位错理论框架结合正则化与块坐标数值算法，能有效捕获大变形单晶自发细分为层状晶粒/位错墙图案的物理机制，并在理论上保证了耦合问题适定性、数值上验证了理论预言。

Abstract: Large plastic deformation can drive an initially uniform single crystal to spontaneously subdivide into misoriented grains separated by thin dislocation walls -- a pattern-forming instability rooted in the loss of convexity of the crystal's elastic energy at large strain. We study this phenomenon for a ductile crystal in plane-strain simple shear within continuum dislocation theory, using a polyconvex (Ciarlet--Geymonat) elastic energy that guarantees existence of minimizers for the coupled deformation--slip problem. Minimizing over the plastic slip yields a condensed energy of double-well form whose non-quasiconvexity favours a lamellar microstructure; the gradient of the geometrically necessary dislocation density regularizes it, giving the grain boundaries a finite thickness and energy as functions of the misorientation angle. A block-coordinate finite element scheme -- alternating a convex non-smooth solve for the slip with a Levenberg-regularized Newton solve for the deformation -- resolves this microstructure numerically and detects its spontaneous onset, reproducing the lamellar grain structure in agreement with the closed-form analysis.

</details>


### [6] [Conformal Symmetry and Planck's Thermal Radiation in Classical Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07904)
*Timothy H. Boyer*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文从经典标量场的视角讨论了热辐射问题，在共形对称框架下处理膨胀与加速度与零点辐射及平衡热辐射的关系。经典零点辐射在惯性参考系中完全不变，但在Rindler参考系中呈现不同形式。传统观点认为其呈现Planck热谱（Unruh温度），但经典视角表明Rindler系中的零点辐射具有发散频率谱，并非真正的热辐射；Unruh形式实际上意味着存在一个额外的满足Stefan-Boltzmann关系的经典热谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清经典场论框架下零点辐射在Rindler参考系中的物理本质，重新审视Unruh热效应在经典层面的诠释，并探讨共形对称性（膨胀与加速度）在零点辐射与热辐射中的联系。

Method: 采用经典标量场的解析方法，利用共形对称性将惯性系中完全膨胀不变的零点辐射变换到Rindler（加速）参考系中，比较所得频谱与Planck热谱及Unruh温度公式的关系。

Result: Rindler参考系中的零点辐射具有发散频率谱，不同于真正的热辐射谱；Unruh形式的出现暗示存在额外的经典热谱分量，该热谱满足Stefan-Boltzmann关系。

Conclusion: 经典的Unruh效应不应被简单诠释为零点辐射本身的热化，而是需要在经典框架下将零点辐射与额外热谱区分开来；Rindler系中零点辐射并非热辐射，Unruh热形式对应的是独立的经典热辐射分量。

Abstract: Thermal radiation is discussed for a classical scalar field. Dilation and acceleration appear in conformal symmetry, and so are treated in connection with zero-point radiation and equilibrium thermal radiation. Classical zero-point radiation is completely invariant under dilation in a inertial frame. However, in a Rindler frame, zero-point radiation takes a different form which is related to Planck's thermal spectrum with temperature T related to acceleration. Traditionally, zero-point radiation is said to take a thermal (Unruh) form in a Rindler frame. However, the classical viewpoint indicates that zero-point radiation in a Rindler frame requires a divergent frequency spectrum and so is not thermal radiation. The Unruh form suggests an additional classical thermal spectrum satisfying the Stefan-Boltzmann relation.

</details>


### [7] [Projection Angles of Projectiles in Sports: Qualitative Assessment of the Effects of Aerodynamic Forces or Run-Up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08249)
*Kazuhiro Tsuboi*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究影响运动员最佳投掷/起跳角度的两大因素——空气动力学力和助跑效应。通过线性化包含阻力和自旋升力的运动方程，推导出扰动解，发现阻力和升力都会降低最佳投掷角度，其中升力影响更强。通过扩展投影模型分析助跑效应，发现更强的助跑会增大相对投射角但减小地面观测的起跳角。该理论框架为铅球出手角和跳远起跳角提供了力学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在体育运动中，最佳投射角度（如铅球出手角、跳远起跳角）的确定受多种因素影响，但空气动力学（阻力、自旋产生的升力）与助跑效应对最佳角度的影响尚未被清晰分离与阐明。作者旨在建立一个简单而统一的理论框架，系统地解释和区分这些因素各自的作用。

Method: (1) 将运动方程线性化，考虑阻力和自旋升力，推导出相对于阻力系数和升力系数的扰动解，分析其对最佳投射角的定性影响。(2) 构建扩展投影模型，使初始速度依赖于初始角度，以模拟助跑效应，并比较相对投射角与地面坐标系下起跳角的变化。

Result: 结果表明，阻力和升力均使最佳投射角减小，且升力的影响强于阻力。助跑效应的分析显示，更强的助跑使相对投射角增大，但从地面观测到的起跳角则减小。这些结果分别与铅球出手角和跳远起跳角的实际数据相符。

Conclusion: 建立了一个简单的理论框架，可明确区分空气动力学效应与助跑效应各自对最佳投射角的作用。该框架能为铅球和跳远等运动提供力学原理性解释，有助于理解最佳投射角的确定机制。

Abstract: We examine two major factors that influence the optimum projection angle: aerodynamic forces and the effect of run-up. With respect to aerodynamics, we consider not only the drag but also the lift generated by spin during flight. By linearizing the equations of motion that include these forces, we derive perturbation solutions with respect to drag and lift coefficients and clarify their qualitative effects. The results show that both drag and lift reduce the optimum projection angle, with the latter exerting a stronger influence. To investigate the effect of run-up, we use an extended projection model in which the initial speed depends on the initial angle. Analysis of this model reveals that a stronger run-up increases the relative projection angle but decreases the launch angle observed from the ground. These findings provide a mechanical explanation for the release angle in shot put and the takeoff angle in long jump. The present study establishes a simple theoretical framework for clarifying the respective roles of aerodynamic and run-up effects in determining the optimum projection angles in sports.

</details>

### [8] [Comment on "Space-time shifted solitons and solution interactions for a generalized nonlocal nonlinear Schrödinger equation"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05483)
*Aslı Pekcan*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文审查了Zhou和Yu关于space-time shifted非局部非线性薛定谔(NLS)方程可积性的论断。通过将其与标准可积NLS系统的约化进行比较，发现仅当λ=1且t₀=0时方程与NLS系统相容；λ=-1的情况虽构成有效非局部方程，但不能由标准NLS系统通过对应的复共轭移位非局部约化导出，因此其可积性不成立。此外还讨论了带实移位的非局部约化与无移位约化之间的等价性，并给出了修正的Hirota双线性方程形式。

Abstract: Zhou and Yu [Appl. Math. Lett. 178 (2026) 109937] studied the space-time shifted nonlocal nonlinear Schrödinger (NLS) equation $iq_t(x,t)=q_{xx}(x,t)-2σq^2(x,t)
  \bar{q}(x_0-x,t_0+λt),\,\, λ=\pm1$, and claimed that for both choices of $λ$ the equation is integrable. Here we examine this claim from the perspective of reductions of the standard integrable NLS system. We find that the compatibility of the equation with the NLS system is true only when there is no time reversal in the argument. In other words, for this equation it requires $λ=1$ and $t_0=0$. The case with $λ=-1$ is a valid nonlocal equation, but cannot be derived from the standard NLS system by the corresponding complex conjugate shifted nonlocal reduction, and its integrability therefore does not follow from that integrable NLS system. Abstract: This paper deals with two kinds of Bäcklund transformations for scalar hyperbolic partial differential equations. We prove that both these types of transformations map solutions of a Darboux integrable equation into solutions of, generally speaking, another but also Darboux integrable equation. The latter fact can be used to roughly check the completeness of a list of Darboux integrable equations. To illustrate this, we apply the above transformations to several equations from a well-known list of Darboux integrable equations and, as a result, obtain a Darboux integrable equation which is absent in this list, but is already known at present. As a generalization of the last equation, we construct a family of Darboux integrable equations that is parametrized by three arbitrary functions, each of which depends on two arguments. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两种相隔二十年的独立尝试——固定阶数的加性Chen模型与自适应算子AFNS——都采用分数阶拉普拉斯编码湍流惯性区的耗散，且收敛到相同的算子阶数。作者的核心动机是厘清：尽管共享相同的不动点/签名，这两种构造究竟是否本质等价？它们是否属于同一普适类？这一区分对于理解湍流惯性区耗散机制和分数阶算子在流体力学中的理论角色至关重要。

Result: 解析结果显示：在二维极限中关闭涡旋拉伸后，稳定过程指数与经验对分散度律的匹配机制不存在解。这意味着AFNS所依赖的局域Reynolds数驱动的算子阶数流动机制，在无涡旋拉伸的约束下无法自洽生成。这给出了一个纯粹解析的判据来区分两种模型：Chen算子的叠加结构在二维极限下仍可存活，而AFNS的动态阶数机制在此框架下失效。

Conclusion: 尽管Chen算子和AFNS在完全发展的惯性区共享同一个算子阶数签名（不动点），但它们在普适类意义上有本质区别。该区别在二维极限下可用纯解析方法判定。这一二分法是否在真实准二维流动中留下可观测的残差特征，被作者留作开放的实验挑战。

Abstract: Two independent attempts, separated by two decades, to encode turbulent dissipation with a fractional Laplacian both converge on the operator order as the signature of the fully developedinertial range: the additive, fixed order model, and the dynamically deforming operator of the Adaptive Fractional Navier Stokes. We show that, despite sharing this fixed point, the two constructions belongto distinct universality classes: Chen operator is a superposition of two fixed order kernels whose relative weight drifts with scale, while AFNS posits a single operator whose order itself flows continuously with the local Reynolds number. We derive a scale resolved observable, the effective spectral order, whose logarithmic anomaly term discriminates between these two mechanisms in principle, and then obtain a sharper, purely analytic verdict from the two dimensional, enstrophy conserving limit: the generative mechanism matching a stable process index to the empirical pair dispersion law has no solution once vortex stretching is kinematically switched off. Whether this dichotomy leaves a residual signature in real quasi two dimensional flows is left as an open experimental challenge.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 该方法包括：1) 将∇·v=0诠释为Coulomb规范固定，建立流体力学的规范对称结构；2) 构造Martin-Siggia-Rose随机作用量并识别其完整BRST对称性；3) 引入Gribov参数γ和monodromy相位θ的BRST双重态以处理规范冗余；4) 通过Higgs机制使γ获得真空期望值γ₀，生成涡量和鬼场的质量尺度；5) 计算单圈有效势分析真空稳定性；6) 使用Gribov启发的log-Poisson层级结构描述间歇性涨落，满足ζ₃=1；7) 通过与DNS数据拟合确定自由参数（分形维数D_f等）；8) 在d=3中计算单圈反常维度Δ_σ以支持所提出的形式主义。

Result: 主要结果为：γ通过Higgs机制获得真空期望值γ₀，产生质量尺度；单圈有效势已计算并分析真空稳定性；间歇性指数由Higgs场σ的涨落通过log-Poisson层级精确描述，严格满足Kolmogorov四分之五定律ζ₃=1；DNS数据拟合得出丝状涡量结构，单参数拟合给出D_f≈1，两参数拟合给出D_f≈2.1和Δ≈0.44；d=3中计算得到单圈反常维度Δ_σ≈0.50，支持D_f=2Δ_σ的识别。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Navier-Stokes方程与规范理论及自发对称破缺之间的形式化统一，展示了将量子场论方法系统应用于湍流和间歇性研究的可行性。所提出框架能精确重现Kolmogorov定律，并与DNS数据合理拟合，表明该理论路径具有充分的发展潜力，为湍流的非微扰分析和普适性研究开辟了新的理论平台。

Abstract: We formalize the gauge structure of the Navier--Stokes equation for incompressible fluids, interpreting $\nabla\cdot\mathbf{v}=0$ as a gauge fixing analogous to the Coulomb gauge in electromagnetism. We construct the Martin--Siggia--Rose action and its full BRST symmetry, introducing BRST doublets for the Gribov parameter $γ$ and the monodromy phase $θ$. Through a Higgs mechanism, $γ$ acquires a vacuum expectation value $γ_{0}$, generating a mass scale for the vorticity and the ghosts; we compute the one-loop effective potential and analyze vacuum stability. Intermittency --- measured by the exponents $ζ_{n}$ --- is described by fluctuations of the Higgs field $σ$ around the condensate, via a Gribov-inspired log-Poisson hierarchy that satisfies exactly $ζ_{3}=1$ (Kolmogorov's four-fifths law). Fits to DNS data favor filamentary vorticity structures, with $D_{f}\approx1$ (one parameter) or $D_{f}\approx2.1$, $Δ\approx0.44$ (two parameters). The one-loop anomalous dimension in $d=3$, $Δ_σ\approx0.50$, supports the identification $D_{f}=2Δ_σ$. The formalism unifies classical hydrodynamics with gauge theory and spontaneous symmetry breaking, opening the study of turbulence and intermittency to quantum field theory methods.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 所提出的ADCBC和APILR方法能够以显著降低的计算开销实现燃烧不稳定性的数值模拟，为该领域的高效研究提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Thermoacoustic instability in premixed flames results from the coupling between the flame's heat release, as determined by combustion parameters, and surrounding acoustics, as determined by combustor geometry. A primary instability results in flame flattening as intrinsic flame instability modes are stabilised. Secondary thermoacoustic instability results in a parametric flame instability and drastic growth of acoustic amplitudes. Due to their relative expense, numerical simulations of these phenomena remain scarce. In this work, Direct Numerical Simulations (DNS) of thermoacoustically unstable idealised premixed flames in a tube with acoustically closed upstream and open downstream ends are presented. Results herein demonstrate nonlinear saturation of the primary instability as the flame flattens as well as an oscillating flame fingering characteristic of the unsteady Rayleigh-Taylor effect. To reduce computational cost, we perform DNS only on the region surrounding the flame. Acoustics at in- and outflows are described using the Navier-Stokes Characteristic Boundary Condition (NSCBC) method to model their delayed reentry into the domain in a formulation referred to as the Acoustic Delay Characteristic Boundary Condition (ADCBC) method. A new Averaged Proportional and Integral Linear Relaxation (APILR) method is also introduced, which modifies the Classic Linear Relaxation (CLR) method to maintain time-averaged values of inflow velocity and outflow pressure. Here, an integral control term is used to remove non-zero equilibrium time-averaged inflow velocities which impinge control over flame position. Both new methods demonstrate their capability in inert and counterflow flames test cases. These methods enable the numerical simulation of combustion instabilities at significantly reduced computational expense.

</details>

Abstract: An approximate analytical solution for the unsteady lift of a thin airfoil with a general unsteady motion is derived from a viscous-flow perspective, where the wake vortex-sheet strength is given in an explicit convolution-type expression as an approximate solution of the Wagner integral equation. For validation, this analytical solution is applied to the Wagner and Theodorsen problems, giving the explicit integral forms of the Wagner and Theodorsen functions as the reduced cases. Further, this analytical solution is applied to the starting flow with a finite timescale in the generalized Wagner problem, revealing the self-similarity of the re-normalized circulatory lift coefficient and its equivalence to the re-normalized Wagner function in a finite time domain. More importantly, the self-similar Wagner effect is found in numerical simulation of the flow over a starting flat-plate airfoil at low Reynolds numbers even when the flow is moderately separated. This self-similarity represents the Reynolds-number-invariance.

Higher-order polynomials are used along each subdomain edge to represent the unknown field, i.e., velocity potential. Assessing the overall effectiveness, top-mounted convex baffle configuration emerges as the most efficient configuration for sloshing suppression, achieving a well-balanced reduction across all modes.

Abstract: Low-frequency cavity breathing oscillations can strongly affect flame stabilization and operability in cavity-stabilized supersonic combustors, yet their hydrodynamic origin and suppression remain insufficiently understood in opposed twin-cavity configurations. Measurements and simulations show that periodic mass exchange between the cavity and freestream drives cyclic cavity depressurization and repressurization, establishing a pressure-shear-layer feedback loop that sustains the oscillation. A two-pressure-state reduced-order model, coupled with a shear-layer displacement coordinate, reproduces the measured frequency, pressure phase relationship, and stability characteristics, demonstrating that both bulk-cavity and reattachment/shock-foot pressure states are required to capture the dynamics. In contrast, ethylene injection followed by ignition permanently suppresses the oscillation, with the nonlinear attractor collapsing to a stable equilibrium. The proposed mappings remain applicable across a broad range of density ratios, from light to heavy particles, and particle sizes spanning two orders of magnitude in Stokes number.

Abstract: Motile microorganisms inhabit oscillatory flows encountered in physiological and engineering systems, yet the influence of flow unsteadiness on their shear-induced preferential concentration remains less understood. Our asymptotic solution recovers the steady-flow weak-shear scaling of $I_D$ with flow Peclet number, $Pe_f$, of $I_D \propto Pe_f^2$, and reveals that oscillatory forcing attenuates this response by a factor $16β^2/(1+16β^2)$. Abstract: Flapping insect wings generate leading-edge vortices (LEVs) that produce much of the lift required for hovering, yet the frequency governing their formation has not been connected to classical vortex shedding. We first develop an approximate scaling for stationary flat-plate wings in which discrete LEVs are generated by periodic leading-edge perturbations, and show that maximum lift consistently occurs within the universal Strouhal range. Despite large differences in morphology, kinematics and Reynolds number, the insect data recover the same Strouhal range. These results reveal a common vortex-formation timescale linking classical separated flows, artificially generated LEVs and the natural LEVs that sustain insect hovering.

Shortly after the jump-off, the velocity of the bubble is considerably damped and rapidly approaches its free-rise terminal velocity. Abstract: We employ weakly nonlinear theory to investigate how an externally imposed simple shear flow affects the onset of spontaneous dynamics of an inertialess active filament deforming in Stokes flow. The filament, clamped to a wall at one end, is subjected to a compressive "follower force" applied tangentially at its free end. By extending my earlier weakly nonlinear analysis of the shear-free case (J. Fluid Mech., 1007 A65, 2025), we derive a generalized amplitude equation governing the near-onset dynamics under weak shear. Analysis of the amplitude equation shows that, besides inducing a steady deflection, the shear damps the filament's intrinsic oscillations. This damping arises from a subtle nonlinear resonance between the shear and the intrinsic oscillations, scales quadratically with the shear rate, and is anisotropic---stronger in the flow direction than normal to it. Without shear, stable whirling states, where the filament tip traces a circular orbit in a plane parallel to the wall, and unstable planar-beating states are known to simultaneously emerge at a critical follower-force value, with circular whirling typically observed beyond this threshold. Shear breaks this degeneracy, driving a sequence of dynamical transitions: from circular to elliptical whirling, then to transverse beating (normal to the shear), and ultimately to steady deflection.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This work presents a comparative analysis of Reduced-Order Models (ROMs) applied to incompressible fluid dynamics within geometrically parametrized domains. Two distinct reduced-order solution strategies are investigated and compared: a monolithic coupled solver and a segregated SIMPLE-based algorithm. Their performance is assessed on two steady, two-dimensional benchmark cases: a lid-driven cavity flow and a flow past a cylindrical obstacle. The two algorithms are compared in terms of fields evaluation and aerodynamic coefficients prediction. The computational results highlight a fundamental trade-off between accuracy and numerical efficiency. On the one hand, after an opportune supremizers enrichment, the coupled approach guarantees a faster convergence, despite the need of a larger number of degrees of freedom. On the other hand, the segregated SIMPLE algorithm yields superior reconstruction accuracy, particularly for lower-dimensional reduced spaces, at the cost of a slower convergence rate.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用风洞测试方法，通过将电机直立或倒置两种方式引入轴向变化，并设置两个相对机翼的纵向位置，构建多种升力+巡航机翼构型。实验在雷诺数52,000到377,000范围内开展，结合流场显示（tufts）、压力传感器（pressure taps）和载荷传感器（load cells）等手段综合获取气动特性数据。

Decreasing the longitudinal separation amplified both the beneficial lift contribution of the rear propeller and the generally adverse effects of the front propeller. Method: 1) 对构象张量施加Cholesky分解，通过数学约束保证应力场正定性，从而稳定梯度；2) 采用稀疏数据同化，将物理损失与针对性的CFD数据点锚定，以克服深层模型的频谱偏置问题；3) 利用迁移学习加速训练；4) 采用构造实体几何（CSG）方法处理复杂几何体，绕过传统网格重划分步骤。

Abstract: Grid-based fluid dynamics solvers routinely struggle with exhaustive meshing demands and ill-posed inverse problems. Finally, we scale the framework to a complex 3-cylinder array which proved our constructive solid geometry approach completely bypasses the tedious re-meshing steps of traditional CFD. The combined framework accurately captured sharp viscoelastic wakes, providing a stable computational tool for complex rheological modeling.

Conclusion: 该理论连接了局部流体动力学与标准血流动力学指标，提供了时间平均壁面剪切应力和振荡剪切指数（OSI）的闭式表达式，为波纹管中脉动流的定量预测和血流动力学评估建立了解析框架。

The analysis shows that the local velocity profile varies strongly along the tube, ranging from plug-like in wide sections to more parabolic near bottlenecks. The cycle-maximum flow rate decreases with increasing corrugation, but this reduction weakens as pulsatility increases, reflecting a crossover from the quasi-steady scaling $\langle R^{-4}\rangle^{-1}$ to the high-frequency scaling $\langle R^{-2}\rangle^{-1}$. The wall shear stress is maximal at the bottleneck and decreases with Womersley number. Abstract: Graph-based surrogate models offer a promising route to accelerate computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations on unstructured meshes. However, their development is limited by the scarcity of benchmark datasets spanning multiple flow regimes and standardized protocols for long-horizon autoregressive prediction. We introduce ONECYL (ONE CYLinder), a new benchmark for unsteady flow past a circular cylinder across laminar, transitional, and high-Reynolds-number regimes. The benchmark comprises 450 high-fidelity Variational Multiscale finite-element simulations (270,000 flow snapshots) with randomized cylinder geometries, providing time-resolved velocity and pressure fields together with mesh connectivity, geometric descriptors, Reynolds numbers, and integrated aerodynamic quantities. Beyond the dataset, ONECYL establishes a unified evaluation framework combining full-field rollout errors, virtual probes, and drag and lift predictions to assess numerical accuracy and physical fidelity. To accompany the benchmark, we develop a Graph Transformer as a reference baseline predicting velocity and pressure fields autoregressively on unstructured meshes. Using ONECYL, we investigate geometric representations and physics-based regularization across the three Reynolds-number regimes. The results show that explicitly encoding the cylinder geometry through a level-set representation consistently improves long-horizon prediction accuracy and generalization to unseen geometries, while divergence-based regularization becomes increasingly beneficial as flow complexity increases. Abstract: In aquatic environments, gradients in flow velocity and turbulence define a constantly shifting landscape that sets the physical constraints on propulsion and energy expenditure in fish. Successfully navigating these complex flows depends on the ability to sense and respond to subtle hydrodynamic cues in an energy-efficient manner. Revealing these mechanisms is central to understanding how fish identify and exploit favorable flow conditions. To this end, we designed a hydrodynamic maze with spatially varying mean flow and turbulence, creating a controlled heterogeneous flow landscape for understanding fish movement strategies. We found that fish escape energetically unfavorable regions by yawing their bodies relative to the flow. These yawed orientations appear to facilitate lateral migration through lift forces and may improve sensitivity to local flow variations. Notably, fish maintain body orientations near, but below, predicted stall conditions to exploit lift without a significant increase in drag. These findings provide insight into how fish navigate heterogeneous hydrodynamic environments, with potential relevance for both natural and engineered flow systems.

For intact specimens, both the recovery rate, $m_v$, and velocity drop magnitude, $|c|$, generally increased with compressive strength. When the same stress was applied, ASR-damaged specimens had increased softening and faster recovery rates, but longer recovery times (up to 458 h). These findings demonstrate the potential of slow dynamics in concrete characterization, through its ability to evaluate the strength of intact samples and its sensitivity to microcracking in damaged samples.

### [35] [Physics-Constrained Inverse Estimation of Irradiation-Induced Strain in He-H Ion-Implanted 4H-SiC Using Nanoindentation and Finite Element Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07157)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Quasi-static sensing, i.e., measurement of a sensor signal change induced by analyte presence with respect to a baseline signal observed in a reference gas beforehand, is the state of the art for qualitative and quantitative analyte identification in chemiresistive sensing. However, this approach is prone to baseline drift and, especially for inexpensive miniaturized point-of-care sensors, repeated calibration with reference gas is not feasible. To access reliable baseline information, active switching between analyte and reference gas would be required, causing a limitation for miniaturized implementation. Dynamic excitation of sensors offers an alternative: Via external stimuli the sensor is reversibly driven out of equilibrium in a controlled way, and the resulting transient sensor response, which is dependent on analytes present, is recorded and interpreted. Correlation of excitation and response signals can reduce effects of baseline drift and furthermore, dynamic features in sensor responses may contain valuable information. Especially light activation is a powerful and potentially miniaturizable approach to agitate sorption processes and therefore induce dynamics. In this work we report photothermal heating of hybrid, chemiresistive gold nanoparticle composites via LED excitation matching the plasmon resonance, to dynamically shift the analyte sorption equilibrium and induce dynamic sensor responses in a highly controlled way. We demonstrate that these sensor responses enable the detection of volatile organic compounds (VOC) via interpretation of differential signal components and that this approach improves the chemiresistors' baseline stability.

Abstract: We introduce "matrix product belief propagation" (MP-BP) as a controlled method for the approximate contraction of two-dimensional tensor networks and graphical models, which generically leads to a quadratic reduction of errors relative to existing methods: at similar computational effort, the number of accurate digits is asymptotically doubled. In the limit of unit matrix-product rank, MP-BP is equivalent to belief propagation, and more generally, can be understood as a generalization of BP in which the "messages" live on surfaces and are assumed to have a matrix-product factorization. Conclusion: Walking Cat架构展现出对具体密码学应用的强适应性和可扩展性，通过对电路布局、测量并行性、辅助态制备和资源回收等多层面的综合优化，能够以合理的物理资源规模大幅提升破解secp256k1 ECDLP的效率，为该架构在量子密码分析中的实际应用提供了可行性证据。

Abstract: One of the strengths of our recently proposed Walking Cat Architecture for a trapped-ion quantum computer is that it is straightforward to extend and optimize for a specific application. As a proof-of-concept, here we present such optimizations for solving the $256$-bit elliptic curve discrete logarithm problem (ECDLP) on $\mathtt{secp256k1}$, which is the elliptic curve used by blockchain technologies such as Bitcoin, using Shor's algorithm. We optimize the circuits from Schrottenloher's recent work and arrive at a logical quantum circuit for solving the ECDLP using about $1450$ qubits and $40\cdot 10^6$ Toffoli gates, with a rigorous lower bound on the logical-level success probability that holds with confidence at least $1-2^{-128}$. Using our compilation toolchain with manual optimization of the logical layout and integrated routing, we produce estimates for the logical measurement depth and the required number of physical qubits by compiling all components to measurement schedules that obey the architectural constraints. A key ingredient is a fast CCZ magic-state factory and a depth-one CCZ state injection, reducing the execution time of CCZ gates by a factor of $31$. We increase the logical-measurement parallelism using non-overlapping cat-based measurements in parallel, and we leverage the recently proposed logical CliNR protocol to speed up Clifford operations. To reduce the qubit overhead, we introduce a more efficient loss correction protocol, design a layout that allows us to recycle the CliNR ancilla qubits, and provision reusable cat-state resources according to the circuit's peak measurement parallelism. All results and optimizations combined, we conclude that a trapped-ion quantum computer based on our architecture can solve the ECDLP on $\mathtt{secp256k1}$ in approximately 25.7 days using 19,397 physical qubits with an estimated success probability of $63\%$.

We also uncover the missing non-affine parameters of the $6$-dimensional DFT, and show that the scaling law for implementing the $N$-dimensional DFT with our building blocks is $\mathcal{O}(N\log\log{N})$. This allows assembling the $2520$-dimensional DFT with only $2625$ interferometers, in contrast to the $\approx 3\times 10^6$ of Reck and Clements architectures.

Abstract: We introduce ordered-angle coding for binary-unanimity testing among $n$ transformation-only users in a serial quantum architecture inherited from Loop-Back communication. User $B_i$ fixes one private sign across a logical word and applies $R(s_iα_j)$ in trial $j$. If $w$ users choose the negative sign, serial composition gives $R[(n-2w)α_j]$. We show that every fixed same-axis pulse that is deterministic for both unanimous inputs under the binary Bell readout has $α_j=q_jπ/(2n)$ with integer $q_j$. For a nonadaptive word $\mathbf q=(q_1,\ldots,q_m)$, a mixed Hamming weight imitates the unanimous signature with probability \[ M_{\mathbf q}(w)=\prod_{j=1}^{m}\cos^2\!\left(\frac{πq_jw}{n}\right). \] Within this complete deterministic-unanimity pulse family, a finite perfect word exists if and only if $n$ is a power of two. For $n=2^r$, the dyadic word $(1,2,4,\ldots,2^{r-1})$ is exact and pulse-minimal with $m_{\min}=r=\log_2n$ trials. It replaces the $O(n^2\log(1/\varepsilon))$ worst-case burden of repeated smallest-angle tests by exact $O(\log n)$ verification. Odd primes instead admit balanced statistical words with uniform mixed-weight imitation $2^{-(p-1)}$.

This yields explicit robustness bounds with angular and binary-readout errors. Bell entanglement is not required for the additive algebra, but it keeps the traveler locally maximally mixed in every honest trial. We therefore present the result as a coded multiuser relation primitive with optional raw conference-key-candidate use, not as a composably secure conference-key protocol.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 证明了稳定化定理：对于每个p≥3的非全qubit系统，只要正偶数的局部维数个数为正且为偶数，并且至少一个局部维数大于2，则f_m(d_1,...,d_p)=f_LB(d_1,...,d_p)+1。结合此前工作，完全解决了所有有限量子系统中UPB的最小基数问题。

Conclusion: 通过统一图论框架证明了稳定化定理，彻底解答了UPB最小基数问题。这一结果与先前工作共同完成了对所有有限量子系统中UPB最小值问题的完整刻画，为量子信息论中UPB相关研究提供了确定性的理论依据。

Abstract: In quantum information theory, the state space of a multipartite quantum system is modeled by a tensor product. In the tensor-product space $\mathbb C^{d_1}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbb C^{d_p}$, a nonzero vector is a \emph{product state} if it can be written as $\lvert \varphi_1\rangle\otimes\cdots\otimes\lvert \varphi_p\rangle$ with $\lvert \varphi_j\rangle\in\mathbb C^{d_j}\setminus\{0\}$. An \emph{unextendible product basis} (UPB) is a finite family of pairwise orthogonal product states such that no nonzero product state is orthogonal to all of them. UPBs play a key role in investigating quantum entanglement and nonlocal phenomena. Finding a smallest UPB is a natural extremal problem: it asks how few pairwise orthogonal product states suffice to prevent any further product state from being added. The general minimum-size problem for UPBs has been studied for over two decades since the seminal work of Alon and Lovász. For local dimensions $d_1,\ldots,d_p\ge2$, let $f_m(d_1,\ldots,d_p)$ be the minimum cardinality of a UPB and let $f_{LB}(d_1,\ldots,d_p)=1+\sum_{j=1}^{p}(d_j-1)$ be the natural lower bound. Alon and Lovász determined exactly when $f_m$ attains the lower bound $f_{LB}$, but the obstructed multipartite cases remained open in general.
  We prove a stabilization theorem: for every non-all-qubit system with $p\ge3$, whenever parity prevents the natural lower bound $f_{LB}$ from being attained, the true minimum is exactly $f_{LB}+1$. Equivalently, if the number of even local dimensions is positive and even, and at least one local dimension is greater than two, then $f_m(d_1,\ldots,d_p)=f_{LB}(d_1,\ldots,d_p)+1$. The proof is built on a unified graph-theoretic framework. Our result, together with earlier work, settles the minimum-cardinality problem for UPBs in all finite quantum systems.

</details>

Abstract: The robustness of quantum synchronization induced by the dynamical Casimir effect (DCE) is analyzed within a circuit quantum electrodynamics (cQED) architecture consisting of two superconducting qubits coupled to a shared, parametrically driven cavity. Using a Lindblad master equation approach, this study evaluates the impact of various decoherence channels (including photon loss, relaxation, thermal excitation, and pure dephasing) on the resulting synchronization dynamics. The results demonstrate that DCE-induced synchronization persists under dissipation rates achievable in state-of-the-art superconducting platforms, confirming its experimental feasibility. Among the studied mechanisms, pure dephasing is identified as the dominant factor limiting the fidelity of the synchronized state. Furthermore, it is shown that in strongly dissipative regimes, the Pearson correlation coefficient may overestimate the degree of quantum synchronization, as environmental relaxation can generate classical correlations that mimic synchronized behavior. These findings establish the parameter regimes necessary for observing genuine DCE-induced quantum synchronization and provide practical guidelines for future cQED experiments.

Abstract: High-fidelity single-shot readout of superconducting qubits is essential for fault-tolerant quantum computation. Without a quantum-limited amplifier such as a Josephson parametric amplifier, the cryogenic high-electron-mobility-transistor (HEMT) low-noise amplifier (LNA) at the \SI{4}{K} stage is the dominant noise source in the readout chain. HEMT LNAs are conventionally characterized by Y-factor measurements of the noise temperature $\Tn$, independently of the quantum measurement they ultimately serve. We apply it to three state-of-the-art cryogenic InP HEMT LNAs with 53\%, 60\%, and 70\% channel indium content, together with a commercial reference amplifier. The 60\% and 70\% devices have similar $\Tn$ in Y-factor measurements, both lower than the 53\% device. The 70\% device has the highest gain, but qubit readout SNR and assignment fidelity instead peak at 60\%. Using this method, we map assignment fidelity against HEMT LNA dc power consumption for each device. Fidelity does not simply saturate with increasing power. For several devices it declines above a device-dependent power well below typical operating points. For the 60\% device, which has the highest overall fidelity, about \SI{1}{mW} is needed to maintain $\fid > 85\%$. Across all four devices, $\fid > 80\%$ is reached with as little as about \SI{0.3}{mW}, roughly an order of magnitude below typical operating points. These results establish a qubit-referenced benchmarking methodology and provide practical guidance for amplifier selection and bias in power-constrained multi-qubit readout systems.

Abstract: We investigate how Kerr nonlinearity modifies quantum synchronization in a squeezed quantum van der Pol oscillator. We show that the Kerr interaction produces an amplitude-dependent frequency shift that drives a saddle-node bifurcation, transforming the classical phase-space structure from bistable to monostable dynamics. In the quantum regime, this transition manifests as systematic frequency pulling and spectral broadening, while the steady-state Wigner function reveals a continuous correspondence between the quantum state and the semiclassical attractor despite finite quantum fluctuations. By constructing global synchronization phase diagrams in the squeezing--Kerr parameter space, we uncover a remarkably linear dependence of the critical squeezing strength required to maintain phase locking on the Kerr nonlinearity. We further demonstrate that the synchronization boundary does not coincide with the crossover between super- and sub-Poissonian photon statistics, showing that synchronization and photon-number statistics characterize distinct aspects of the quantum steady state. These results provide quantitative design principles for controlling quantum synchronization through Kerr nonlinearity, with potential relevance to trapped-ion, superconducting-circuit, and optomechanical platforms.

Abstract: Parametric two-qubit gates using tunable couplers are a promising approach to scalable superconducting quantum processors. However, conventional theories formulated in the idle eigenbasis break down in the large-amplitude regime required for fast gate operations. We show that this theory achieves quantitative agreement with exact numerical simulations across representative tunable-coupler architectures, including double-transmon, capacitively shunted double-transmon, and single-transmon couplers. The description remains accurate even in regimes where idle-frame descriptions fail, while substantially reducing the reliance on computationally demanding exact simulations. These results establish an instantaneous-frame theory of parametric gates beyond conventional idle-frame models and provide a new perspective on driven quantum dynamics.

Abstract: Quantum Machine Learning (QML) has shown rapid advances by utilizing quantum computing for machine learning tasks. Meanwhile, the privacy risks accompanying QML is also starting to be studied, which inherit privacy leakage channels from "classical" ML and also quantum-unique risks. Existing work on privacy-preserving QML largely focuses on a QML-as-a-service scenario, which generally assumes that the QML model owner provides only classical bit outputs to queries, while users (and adversaries) have only classical computing abilities. However, this view is increasingly challenged in a quantum-native world of quantum-capable users/adversaries, which may have access to both quantum computing abilities and access to quantum information output from service providers.

These results show that existing research on privacy leakage on QML models underestimate privacy leakage in an emergent quantum-native access regimes, which we hope to establish a first step into examining potential privacy leakages for QML in the quantum-native world.These results show that existing research on privacy leakage in QML models underestimates privacy leakage in emergent quantum-native access regimes, and we hope to establish a first step in examining potential privacy leakages for QML in the quantum-native world.

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) is a promising variational approach for solving Boolean satisfiability (SAT) problems. Existing SAT-to-QAOA workflows first translate Boolean formulas into penalty Hamiltonians before applying generic quantum compilation, thereby discarding SAT-specific information such as clause structure, logical operators, and variable dependencies. Consequently, subsequent compiler optimizations cannot exploit the underlying Boolean formulation. This paper presents SAQC, a SAT-aware compilation framework that introduces a SAT Intermediate Representation (SIR) to preserve Boolean structure during compilation. The proposed framework supports both CNF and XOR-extended CNF (eCNF) formulations and performs SAT-aware optimizations including clause rewriting, dependency analysis, and clause scheduling prior to quantum lowering. Building on the optimized SIR, SAQC provides a unified framework for both penalty Hamiltonian generation and direct clause-to-ansatz synthesis, together with ansatz optimizations based on relative-phase decomposition, ancilla-assisted synthesis, and dynamic uncomputation. Experimental results demonstrate significant reductions in circuit depth, two-qubit gate count, and compilation time compared with conventional Hamiltonian-based workflows while remaining compatible with existing quantum compilation frameworks.

The number of training questions supported by fixed-size policies trained at each amplification budget also grows faster with the budget than with matched classical repetition. Our results justify the use of BPQM in the decoding step of DQI and of coding-theoretic algorithms based on Regev's reduction whenever the code is drawn from one of the random LDPC ensembles analyzed here and the induced memoryless symmetric pure-state channel lies in the BPQM success region.

In contrast, stochastic jump trajectories can preserve retrieval over extended times when the post-measurement update remains compatible with the coherent memory structure. Abstract: Nonstabilizerness (magic) is the quantum resource that, together with stabilizer operations, makes universal quantum computation possible. It appears in fault-tolerant codes and topological order. However, quantifying nonstabilizerness is difficult. The standard measure sums over exponentially many Pauli operators, and for quantum codes no quantitative theory has been available. We resolve this by a structural observation: the Clifford sector carries no magic and can be removed by a Clifford transformation, leaving an order-three object whose magic is an exactly evaluable fourth moment. Removing this inert background yields closed forms for several code families: a formula for all Dicke states, reducing a 100-qubit case from $4^{100}$ terms to a short binomial sum; a bound for cubic-phase codes (twisted quantum doubles and non-Abelian topological order), saturated only by the $D_4$ code; and a cyclic/zero criterion for group multiplication states. Together these results reduce the computation of nonstabilizerness for a broad class of codes to finite classical counting problems, and identify the maximally magical codes among them.

Abstract: The quantum lattice Boltzmann method (QLBM) holds promise for efficient fluid simulations, yet the treatment of realistic boundary conditions, particularly solid wall boundary with arbitrary geometry, remains a critical open challenge. For boundary enforcement, we design a quantum implementation of the half-way bounce-back scheme and introduce a component exchange step prior to the streaming step, which naturally enforces the no-slip condition without modifying the transport operation. Inlet and outlet conditions are imposed via controlled SWAP operations combined with ancilla registers prepared in the prescribed velocity states. Our QLBM is quantitatively validated through simulations of two-dimensional flows past a backward-facing step, a cylinder, and an obstacle with complex geometry, demonstrating both accuracy and extensibility of our boundary algorithm.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的原子、分子与光学物理实时控制系统在全局时钟上以程序方式描述实验，每个阶段边界都是绝对时间，导致描述与前面所有内容强耦合——修改一个阶段会影响所有后续阶段，难以复用、检查和迁移。这种紧耦合的表述方式限制了实验的灵活性和系统重构能力。

Method: Wigner Time将实验过程表示为数据而非程序：一张定时更新表（table of timed updates），通过组合函数组装生成，并引用实验中命名的时间点而不依赖绝对时刻。所有与设备相关的具体转换被推迟到最终的转换步骤，从而保持实验描述的通用性、可读性与后端无关性。

Result: Wigner Time已在两个冷原子实验装置上运行超过两年。在与手写实时程序对比评估中，其改进后的系统将可实现的时间分辨率提升了五倍。

Conclusion: 将实验时序描述从过程式程序重构为数据驱动表示，可有效解决多设备精确时序控制中的耦合性与可移植性问题。该设计不仅适用于原子、分子与光学物理学，也适用于任何需要精确多设备定时控制的领域。

Abstract: Precisely timed, multi-device control in atomic, molecular and optical physics is provided by specialized real-time systems, which impose their own terms on the description of the experiment: a program over a global clock, in which every stage boundary is an absolute time computed from everything preceding it. Such descriptions are tightly coupled -- changing one stage affects all later ones -- and are correspondingly hard to reuse, inspect, or move elsewhere. We introduce Wigner Time, a Python package in which the experimental procedure is instead represented as data: a table of timed updates, assembled by composing functions and referred to named points in the experiment rather than to absolute instants. Hardware enters only at a final conversion step, leaving the description readable and back-end agnostic. Wigner Time has run two cold-atom setups for more than two years, where replacing a hand-written real-time program improved the achievable temporal resolution five-fold; the design applies to any domain requiring precise multi-device timing.

</details>


### [84] [Effects of Trotter Error, Digitization Error, and Initial State Overlap on Tapered Quantum Phase Estimation for Minimum Eigenvalue Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06249)

TL;DR: 本文通过数值方式实现了基于最优锥形量子相位估计（tQPE）的完整量子电路，用于最小特征值计算。在标准Trotter化实现哈密顿量时间演化酉算子、以及使用优化带宽限制锥形(D P S S)构造锥形态的基础上，作者在代表性量子哈密顿量上进行了精确的经典数值电路模拟，系统研究了tQPE算法参数（Trotter误差、有限采样、非理想初态、总演化时间和数字误差）对最优化相位采样率、绝对误差和最优相位分布香农熵的影响。数值实验涵盖最多10个相位量子比特精度、3个额外相位寄存器误差抑制量子比特，作用于4量子比特海森堡量子磁体模型哈密顿量，并使用最高达12阶Trotter化。研究发现：i) 稳态最优相位采样率由初始态与基态的重叠决定；ii) 关于相位采样概率或特征值采样误差率，tQPE没有强烈的演化时间依赖性，除非Trotter误差较高；iii) tQPE中近似的含时演化酉算子（如同标准QPE中一样）会产生显著的振铃效应（ringing），导致非物理的特征值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计算中，量子相位估计（QPE）是诸多算法（如量子化学、材料科学中的特征值问题）的核心子程序。然而标准QPE具有高概率失败的特征，且在实际硬件中受限于有限的退相干时间和噪声。锥形量子相位估计（tapered QPE, tQPE）通过修改相位测量窗口以在最低特征值处获得最优采样概率，从而优于标准QPE。然而，此前缺乏相对于标准QPE的实验分析，也缺乏在实际约束（如Trotter化误差、有限采样、有限初始态保真度、有限总演化时间和相位数字化误差）下的系统数值验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过全量子电路的精确数值模拟，评估tQPE在非理想条件下针对最小特征值计算的实用性能。

Method: 本文采用如下方法：(1) 数值实现最优锥形QPE的完整量子电路，采用可实现的近似带限DPSS（离散长球面序列）锥形；(2) 使用标准Trotter化（最高12阶）实现哈密顿量时间演化酉算子U=e^{-iHt}；(3) 使用优化带宽限制锥形构造锥形态；(4) 在4量子比特的海森堡量子磁体模型哈密顿量上，通过精确的经典数值量子电路模拟，系统扫描更改tQPE算法参数——包括Trotter误差、有限采样次数、非理想初始态、总演化时间以及相位量子比特数（数字误差）——来观察其对最优相位采样率、绝对误差和最优相位分布香农熵的影响。实验规模包含多达10个相位精度量子比特（ℓ=10）和3个额外相位寄存器误差抑制量子比特（m=3）。

Result: 数值实验结果表明：i) tQPE的稳态最优相位采样率主要由初始态与目标基态的重叠（overlap）决定，而非其他参数；ii) 当Trotter误差不太大时，tQPE在相位采样概率和整体特征值采样误差率方面几乎不随总演化时间变化，即无显著演化时间依赖性；iii) Trotter化近似的时间演化酉算子会在tQPE中（如同在标准QPE中一样）引入显著的振铃效应（ringing），从而可能导致非物理的特征值估计。这些发现对tQPE在实际量子计算中的参数选择和误差控制具有重要指导意义。

Conclusion: 通过全面的数值验证，本文确认了tQPE（锥形QPE）在最小特征值计算中的可行性及其性能特征。核心结论是：(1) tQPE的最优相位采样率本质上由初始态与基态的重叠决定；(2) tQPE在大多数参数设置下对于总演化时间具有鲁棒性，但高Trotter误差会破坏这一特性；(3) Trotter化固有的近似误差会导致振铃伪影，在tQPE和标准QPE中均可能产生非物理特征值。这些结果强调了在实际应用中需要谨慎选择Trotter阶数和初始态制备，同时也为tQPE算法的实用化设计提供了重要的数值依据。

Abstract: We numerically implement full quantum circuits of optimal tapered Quantum Phase Estimation (in the form of an implementable approximate bandwidth-limited DPSS taper) for the purpose of minimum eigenvalue computation, where the Hamiltonian time-evolution unitary is implemented using standard Trotterization, and the taper state is constructed using an optimized bandwidth-limited taper. We illustrate, on representative quantum Hamiltonians, with tractable exact classical numerical quantum circuit simulations, how tapered QPE performs with respect to optimal phase sampling rate, absolute error, Shannon entropy of the optimal phase distribution, when the tQPE algorithm parameters are changed. Those algorithm parameters are Trotter error, finite sampling, imperfect initial states, total evolution time, and digitization error (number of phase qubits). The reported numerical experiments include up to $\ell=10$ phase qubits of precision with $m=3$ additional phase-register error suppression qubits, on a $4$-qubit Heisenberg quantum magnet model Hamiltonian, using up to 12th-order Trotterization. We numerically show i) the steady-state optimal phase sampling rate is determined by the initial state overlap with the ground-state, ii) with respect to phase sampling probability, or overall eigenvalue sampling error rate, there is no strong evolution time dependence for tQPE unless there is high Trotter error, iii) the approximated time-evolution unitaries in tQPE, like in standard QPE, can result in substantial ringing, which leads to non-physical eigenvalue estimates.

</details>


### [85] [Algorithmic Randomness and Physical Typicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06268)

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何利用算法随机性理论为物理学中的"典型性"(typicality)概念提供精确的数学内容。以玻姆力学为例，作者表明将物理状态定义为关于可计算测度的Martin-Löf随机，可以将玻姆力学中的分布公设表述为一种统计约束定律，并通过玩具模型证明该约束能保证可计算实验协议下标准的玻恩统计结果，从而提出算法玻姆力学(aBM)。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者借用算法信息论/算法随机性领域的Martin-Löf随机性概念，将物理状态在可计算测度下为Martin-Löf随机定义为"典型"。将此应用于玻姆力学，将分布公设重新表述为统计约束定律，并通过一个玩具模型论证该约束可推导标准玻恩统计。

Result: 提出了算法玻姆力学(aBM)框架，展示了如何将分布公设精确化为"物理状态关于可计算测度是Martin-Löf随机的"这一要求，并在玩具模型中证明该表述可保证可计算实验协议下的玻恩统计规则成立。

Conclusion: 算法随机性为物理典型性提供了一种精确的数学刻画途径，aBM作为范例表明算法随机性可用于为统计定律提供严格的内容，避免了典型性概念的模糊性。

Abstract: Appeals to typicality are common in physics, but it is often unclear what it means for a physical state to be typical relative to a probability measure, and correspondingly unclear what a law that appeals to typicality asserts. Here we consider how one might characterize physical typicality using ideas from the theory of algorithmic randomness. As a concrete example, we show how taking a physical state to be typical relative to a computable measure when it is Martin-Löf random allows one to formulate the distribution postulate in Bohmian mechanics as a statistical constraining law of the theory. Using a toy model, we show how this constraint guarantees the standard Born statistics for computable experimental protocols. Algorithmic Bohmian mechanics (aBM) thus illustrates how algorithmic randomness may be used to provide precise content to a statistical law.

</details>


### [86] [Efficient Quantum Circuits for Electronic Hamiltonian Simulation without Pauli Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06285)

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Lasp（ladder-string-pair）对角化的电子哈密顿量时间演化电路构造方法，不经过Pauli展开，直接从二阶量子化电子哈密顿量构造电路。通过在单算子、三算子三元组到O(n)个三元组序列三个层次上优化，将CX门数量从O(n²)降至O(n)，且在这些阶段无需引入Trotter误差，同时天然支持受控时间演化和实值哈密顿量的进一步优化。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的电子哈密顿量模拟通常先将费米子算子映射为量子比特算子，再将梯形算子乘积展开为Pauli字符串。这种方法虽然通用，但掩盖了更高层的费米子结构，可能错失电路优化的机会。

Method: 基于Lasp对角化技术，直接为二阶量子化电子哈密顿量构造时间演化电路（不进行Pauli展开）。在三个层次上扩大优化范围：(1) 单个两体费米子Lasp算子（对应16个Pauli字符串），此阶段无Trotter误差；(2) 三个共享相同四个自旋轨道指标的Lasp算子构成三元组，系统性地消除CX门（示例中从36降至12）；(3) O(n)个适当排序的三元组序列在跨界处实现CX门级联削减，使总CX数从O(n²)降至O(n)。方法还天然支持受控时间演化，并对实值哈密顿量提供额外优化。

Result: 两体费米子Lasp算子的时间演化电路对应16个Pauli字符串但无需此阶段Trotter误差；三元组优化将CX门从36降至12；将优化范围扩展到O(n)个有序三元组序列后，CX门数量从O(n²)降至O(n)；实值哈密顿量可获得进一步优化。

Conclusion: Lasp方法通过保留高层电路结构并扩大优化范围，为电子哈密顿量模拟提供了更高效的时间演化电路，为更高效的量子化学模拟开辟了系统性路径。

Abstract: Electronic Hamiltonian simulation is commonly formulated by mapping fermionic operators to qubit operators and subsequently expanding the resulting ladder-operator products into Pauli strings. While general, this procedure obscures the higher-level fermionic structure and can hide opportunities for circuit optimization. Building on ladder-string-pair (Lasp) diagonalization originally developed for Hamiltonian simulation of partial differential equations, we construct time-evolution circuits for the second-quantized electronic Hamiltonian without performing a Pauli expansion. For the most general case of complex-valued coefficients, we present a time-evolution circuit for a two-body fermionic Lasp operator that corresponds to 16 Pauli strings in the Pauli-expansion approach but does not suffer from Trotter error at this stage. Expanding the optimization scope from a single operator to a triad of three fermionic Lasp operators sharing the same four spin-orbital indices enables systematic cancellation of CX gates, reducing the CX-gate count from 36 to 12 in the example considered, still without introducing Trotter error at this stage. For $n$ spin orbitals, further expanding the optimization scope to a sequence of $O(n)$ suitably ordered triads enables a cascade of CX-gate reductions across triad boundaries, reducing the CX-gate count from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n)$. The Lasp-based approach also naturally accommodates controlled time evolution and yields further optimizations for real-valued Hamiltonians. These results demonstrate that the Lasp-based approach enables more efficient time-evolution circuits by preserving high-level circuit structures and thereby expanding the scope of optimization, providing a systematic route toward more efficient electronic Hamiltonian simulation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出基于金刚石氮-空位（NV）色心集合的传感器内量子储备池计算（NV-QRC），用于感知不可重复事件。与固定脉冲序列的动力学退耦（DD）磁力测定相比，NV-QRC利用场驱动的多体动力学和多个空间区域的同步荧光读出，可在固定DD协议失效的情况下保留类相关信息，实现二元相位分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子传感协议依赖可重复的目标场以在不同传感条件下重复测量。然而对于不可重复事件，同一场波形无法在不同设置下再现，传统DD磁力测定方法（依赖扫掠π脉冲间隔）无法适用，因此需要一种能从单次事件中提取多个特征的传感方案。

Method: 提出在NV色心集合内部实现量子储备池计算（NV-QRC）：利用场驱动的多体动力学产生复杂量子响应，并通过多个空间区域的同步荧光读出，为经典分类器提供来自单次事件的多特征输入。在二元相位分类任务中，与使用预先固定的单脉冲序列的DD磁力测定进行基准对比。

Result: 研究表明，在固定DD协议无法捕获类依赖信息的参数区间内，NV-QRC仍然能够保留类相关信息，表现出优于固定DD方案的分辨能力。

Conclusion: 这些结果将不可重复事件感知确定为量子储备池计算的一个有前景的应用领域，拓展了NV量子传感的适用范围。

Abstract: High-density nitrogen-vacancy (NV) ensembles in diamond enable sensitive magnetometry. Many quantum-sensing protocols rely on reproducible target fields, allowing repeated measurements under different sensing conditions. In dynamical decoupling (DD) magnetometry, sweeping the interval between $π$ pulses across repeated measurements enables estimation of the frequency and amplitude of an unknown alternating field. For non-repeatable events, however, the same field waveform cannot be reproduced for measurements under different settings. Here we propose NV-based in-sensor quantum reservoir computing (NV-QRC) for sensing such events. Field-driven many-body dynamics and simultaneous fluorescence readout from multiple spatial regions provide a classical classifier with multiple features from a single event. For binary phase classification, we benchmark NV-QRC against DD magnetometry using a single pulse sequence fixed in advance. We show that NV-QRC can retain class-dependent information in regimes where the fixed-DD protocol fails to capture it. These results identify non-repeatable-event sensing as a promising application of quantum reservoir computing.

</details>


### [88] [Hierarchical Fourier Approximation for Variational Quantum Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06307)
*Taha Hoseinpour Asli,Sajjad Hashemian,Ebrahim Ardeshir-Larijani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Walsh-Fourier谱截断层次的变分量子分布学习框架。通过从低阶到高阶逐级学习目标傅里叶系数，利用热启动映射连接各层参数，并给出了端到端的期望学习保证（包括近似误差、统计估计误差与优化误差的三项分解）。该框架在总变差距离下存在显式的2^(n-1)因子，因此仅对傅里叶尾部足够集中的分布具有信息量，且不声称全局可训练性或消除贫瘠高原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子分布学习（如量子电路玻恩机）虽在理论上具有通用性，但缺乏系统性的可学习性理论分析。作者试图通过谱视角（Walsh-Fourier近似）建立从低阶到高阶的分层学习范式，以提供可解析的近似-估计-优化（A-E-O）端到端保证，弥补该领域缺乏明确学习误差分解的空白。

Method: (1) 在布尔立方体上构建Walsh-Fourier谱截断层次，每层选取特定目标傅里叶系数集合构成谱截断投影到概率单纯形；(2) 将截断后的目标分布作为量子电路玻恩机的学习目标；(3) 通过热启动映射将前一层学到的参数作为下一层初始值；(4) 证明端到端期望学习保证：近似项由省略的傅里叶质量决定，归一化无偏估计器给出经验截断的显式统计界；(5) 将抽象差异条件具体化为全变差距离，并将分布误差与量子态保真度关联，推导出显式因子2^(n-1)。

Result: 获得了包含近似项（省略的傅里叶质量）、估计项（无偏估计器统计界）和优化项的端到端学习保证；在全变差距离具体化下，建立了分布误差与量子态保真度之间的关系，但导数显式的2^(n-1)维度因子，说明该理论仅在傅里叶尾部足够集中时具有实用性。论文同时明确其框架不证明全局可训练性，也不消除贫瘠高原问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为低到高阶谱训练量子分布学习提供了理论化的A-E-O分析框架，揭示了此类方法的适用条件（傅里叶谱集中性）与固有局限（无全局可训练性保证、存在维度代价）。结论强调框架的价值在于识别可行条件而非声称普适性。

Abstract: We study variational quantum distribution learning through a hierarchy of Walsh--Fourier approximations on the Boolean cube. At each level, a selected set of target Fourier coefficients defines a spectral truncation, which is projected onto the probability simplex and used as the target of a quantum circuit Born machine. Parameters learned at one level initialize the next through a warm-start map. We prove an end-to-end expected learning guarantee where the approximation term is determined by the omitted Fourier mass, while a normalized unbiased estimator yields an explicit statistical bound for empirical truncations. We then instantiate the abstract discrepancy conditions for total variation distance and relate the resulting distributional error to quantum-state fidelity. The total-variation specialization incurs the explicit factor $2^{n-1}$ under our normalized $\ell_2$ convention and is therefore informative only for sufficiently concentrated Fourier tails. The framework does not establish global trainability or eliminate barren plateaus; rather, it identifies the conditions under which low-to-high spectral training admits a approximation--estimation--optimization analysis.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性偏微分方程在科学与工程中非常重要，量子计算被视为一种有前景的替代计算范式。然而，现有方法在求解非线性PDE时面临可扩展性挑战，包括线性化后希尔伯特空间维度膨胀以及量子资源消耗过大等问题，亟需一种更高效的量子求解方法。

Method: 作者提出了QHPM方法，其核心包括：（1）利用同伦扰动技术将非线性PDE线性化，同时保持希尔伯特空间维度不变；（2）采用变分量子模拟框架求解，通过功能编码减少所需量子比特数，并通过参数化电路的深度缩减降低计算成本；（3）提出了新的准则用于在同伦级数截断阶数与电路深度之间进行成本效益选择。

Result: 该方法通过多个示例进行了验证，包括涡量输运方程和约化磁流体动力学方程，表明QHPM能够在这些非线性PDE问题上有效运行。

Conclusion: QHPM在可扩展性和量子资源利用方面显著改善了非线性PDE的量子求解方案，为量子计算在非线性科学计算领域的应用提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Solving nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs) is important in various scientific and engineering applications. Recently, quantum computing was introduced as an alternative computational paradigm for solving nonlinear PDEs. In this paper, a new method called the quantum homotopy perturbation method (QHPM) is proposed to improve the scalability of solving nonlinear PDEs through two aspects. First, the dimension of the Hilbert space remains the same after the nonlinear PDE is linearized through the homotopy perturbation. Second, the solutions are obtained with a variational quantum simulation framework, where the number of qubits is decreased with functional encoding and the depth of parametrized circuits is reduced. The additional contribution of this paper is the introduction of new criteria for selecting the homotopy series truncation order and circuit depth for cost-effective QHPM. The proposed approach is demonstrated with several examples, including the vorticity transport equation and the reduced magnetohydrodynamics equations.

</details>


### [90] [Trapped Ion Quantum Networking and Telecommunications Coexisting on One Fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06387)
*Denton Wu,Mingzhe Han,Zehao Wang,Ana Luiza Ferrari,Mika Zalewski,Yuanheng Xie,Tingjun Chen,Norbert Linke*

TL;DR: 该论文首次实验演示了量子记忆网络与电信流量在同一条光纤上共存。研究人员通过部署的2.8公里光纤环路，该光纤同时承载以太网和5G流量，成功实现了使用锶-88离子发射的1092 nm光子产生的离子-光子纠缠分布。所有协调量子发射器和接收器系统的经典控制信号（包括用于偏振稳定的光纤传感）都在同一光纤上共传播。这一成果证明，基于量子记忆的量子网络可以在活跃的经典网络基础设施上实现，从而将量子网络的应用范围从暗光纤扩展到了全部已部署的光纤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前基于量子记忆的长距离量子网络研究完全依赖暗光纤（dark fiber），以避免电信（telecom）流量产生的探测器背景噪声。但这排除了大量已在现场部署的光纤。如果记忆-光子纠缠和电信信号能在同一根光纤上共存，那么整个经典光纤基础设施都可以用于量子链路，极大扩展量子网络的可部署范围。

Result: 首次在实验上证明了量子记忆网络中可以与活跃的经典电信流量共存。离子-光子纠缠成功通过承载以太网和5G流量的已部署2.8公里光纤分发，且所有经典控制信号在同一光纤上稳定运行。

Conclusion: 该研究证明基于量子记忆的量子网络能够直接在活跃的经典网络基础设施上实现，无需依赖暗光纤。这一突破使所有已部署的光纤资源都可被用于构建量子网络，大幅提高了量子网络的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Research into long-distance quantum memory-based networking to date has exclusively used dark fibers. This avoids the detector background from telecommunications (telecom) traffic, but as a result excludes many fibers deployed in the field. If memory-photon entanglement and telecom signals coexist on one fiber, the entire classical fiber infrastructure becomes available for quantum links. We present the first experimental demonstration of such coexistence. Ion-photon entanglement using 1092 nm photons emitted by a Strontium-88 ion is distributed over a deployed 2.8 km fiber loop which carries Ethernet and 5G traffic. All classical control signals required to coordinate the quantum transmitter and receiver systems co-propagate on the same fiber. These include fiber sensing for polarization stabilization. Our results demonstrate that memory-based quantum networks can be realized on active classical network infrastructure.

</details>

Abstract: Scalable quantum networks require quantum memories that are chip-integrated, telecom-band compatible, and capable of flexible retrieval. Nanofabricated mechanical resonators meet these criteria. They offer independent tunability of optical and mechanical modes, long-lived phonon states, and design flexibility beyond atomic systems, making them strong candidates for practical integrated quantum memory. Here, we demonstrate an on-chip, absorptive optomechanical memory for telecom-band photons, based on optomechanically induced transparency (OMIT) and operating near the mechanical ground state. The device stores telecom-band photons, demonstrating compatibility with external photon sources at the few-photon level, while enabling on-demand retrieval. By placing the device in a dilution refrigerator at 20 mK and tailoring the control field to suppress optical heating, we achieve a remarkably low phonon occupancy of just 0.32 during the storage process. Our results lay the groundwork for scalable, phonon-based quantum memory devices and open new avenues for integrating mechanical systems into practical quantum network architectures.

</details>


### [92] [After Theft: From Revocation to Neutralization in the Custody of Quantum Clones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06415)

TL;DR: 本文论证了加密量子克隆（encrypted quantum cloning）可用于实现一种名为"中和"（neutralization）的后失窃/后妥协安全原语：窃取加密量子克隆的攻击者因无法获得有效的解密资源，而合法的托管者可通过解开另一可信克隆耗尽唯一的解密机会，使窃取的克隆永久失效。这与传统的"撤销"（revocation）不同，具有更强的后窃取响应能力。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者先提出威胁模型（攻击者窃取加密克隆但未持有解密密钥），再论证合法托管者可通过解密另一受信克隆耗尽唯一可用解密机会，从而使被窃克隆永久不可用；将该机制形式化，与"撤销"区分，并将其定位于更广泛的后窃取响应空间中；引入时间威胁模型，并与最接近的经典类比机制进行比较（经典方案需通过外部程序化组合才能复制相同策略结果）。

Result: 提出了一个超越传统撤销的"中和"（neutralization）后妥协响应概念，表明加密量子克隆内在具备单次解密资源消耗带来的永久性失窃失效能力；与经典类比对比显示经典机制无法天然实现该策略输出，需依赖外部程序化组合。

Conclusion: 加密量子克隆可被解释为面向分布式保存场景的后妥协量子托管原语，在Cyber-Humanities导向的保存架构中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Encrypted quantum cloning enables the creation of multiple encrypted clones of an unknown quantum state while allowing only one effective decryption, with the decryption resource being intrinsically consumed in the process. In this paper, we argue that this property supports a distinctive custodial security primitive for post-compromise response. We consider a threat model in which an adversary steals an encrypted quantum clone but does not yet possess the corresponding decryption key. In such a scenario, a legitimate custodian may unseal a different trusted clone, thereby exhausting the sole available unsealing opportunity and rendering the stolen clone permanently useless. We argue that this mechanism is not adequately described as mere revocation. Rather, it realizes a stronger form of post-theft response, which we call neutralization. We formalize this distinction, locate it within a broader post-theft response space, and introduce a temporal threat model. We compare the mechanism with its closest classical analogue, showing that the classical case can reproduce the policy outcome only through external procedural composition. We finally interpret encrypted quantum cloning as a primitive for post-compromise quantum custody in distributed preservation settings, with prospective relevance for Cyber-Humanities-oriented preservation architectures.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum processors scale with the modes they can transform, and each transformation adds loss. We demonstrate optoelectronic quantum information processing, moving the mixing into radio-frequency electronics after homodyne detection. Squeezed vacuum falls on a large-scale integrated photonic-electronic circuit, a 32-channel coherent receiver array whose radio-frequency network mixes the analog photocurrents. A local-oscillator ramp reconstructs the two-mode covariance at the aperture, which is physical, and its partial transpose has smallest symplectic eigenvalue 0.99270, three standard deviations below the separability bound. Mode transformation becomes a circuit function whose scale follows integrated electronics. With electro-optic transducers in place of the photodiodes, the same receiver would transfer optical modes into superconducting circuits and back, toward a photonic-electronic quantum computer spanning the optical and microwave bands.

Our treatment assumes no approximations, apart from the typical Jaynes-Cummings model assumption of only a few discrete quantum modes of light. The paper is dedicated to Joseph Henry Eberly, whose remarkable research has left indelible mark on both strong field physics and quantum optics.

Abstract: Random unitaries are fundamental to quantum information and many-body physics, with widespread applications ranging from quantum learning and metrology to device benchmarking. A central pursuit is to minimize the space and circuit depth required to generate them. However, existing methods for generating low-depth random unitaries rely heavily on an extensive number of ancillary qubits, imposing severe spatial overhead. In this work, we prove that random unitaries can be generated in optimal depth without ancillae. For multiplicative-error approximate $k$-designs on $n$ qubits, our circuits achieve a depth of $\widetilde{O} (k) (\log n)^{1/δ}$ on $δ$-dimensional architectures and $\widetilde{O}(k) \log \log n$ with all-to-all connectivity. Furthermore, by introducing a general exactification lemma, we lift our construction to optimal-depth exact $k$-designs, yielding an exponential resource reduction over state-of-the-art exact constructions. Our results minimize the space-time costs for a wide range of quantum protocols.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bravyi等人的双变量自行车（BB）码是权重6校验子的量子LDPC码（如[[144,12,12]]，kd²/n=12），但更高权重校验子码类（如权重8）的结构理论尚未被系统开发。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过建立BB类权重8码的代数结构理论，并配合精确验证的搜索管线，构造和严格认证超越BB基准的新码，同时量化更重校验子带来的阈值代价。

Method: (1) 发展BB类权重8校验子（即权重4生成多项式）的代数结构理论，包括证明精确维度公式k=2dimR/(A,B)、生成对上的4ℓm元素对称群、X/Z距离相等性以及给出距离上界的子群-陪集核向量族；(2) 构建并交叉验证的搜索管线和逐位掩码（bit-mask）穷举距离验证器；(3) 在n=144和n=72参数下进行系统搜索，形成码的普查（census）；(4) 通过电路级存续实验对比权重8码与BB参考码的伪阈值。

Result: 在n=144得到53个码的普查：[[144,6,d≥15]]超越BB码距离12（认证d≥15），[[144,10,12]]达到距离12，[[144,16,10]]以kd²/n=11.11（仅低于基准7.4%）编码多三分之一的逻辑量子比特且解码性能不差。在n=72，[[72,14,8]]达到kd²/n=12.44，是同长度BB码的两倍以上且解码更优。电路级存续实验显示权重8码伪阈值为≈0.1%，BB参考为≈0.4%。所有结构命题均在全部普查码上进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了权重8（权重4生成多项式）BB类码的完整代数结构理论，证明了若干关键的结构性定理，并通过严格验证的搜索方法构造了多个在有限码长下超越BB基准的新码，尤其在编码率方面具有显著优势。然而，较重校验子带来约4倍左右的伪阈值代价（≈0.1% vs ≈0.4%），这是实际部署中需要权衡的因素。研究同时提供了高距离构造的设计规则和严格认证方法，为后续在更高码长和更高校验子权重下的量子LDPC码设计奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Bivariate bicycle (BB) codes of Bravyi \emph{et al.}~\cite{Bravyi2024} are quantum low-density parity-check codes with weight-$6$ checks, exemplified by $[[144,12,12]]$ with $kd^2/n=12$. We develop the algebraic structure theory of BB-type codes with weight-$8$ checks (weight-$4$ generator polynomials) and use it, together with an exactly validated search pipeline, to construct and certify new codes. We prove an exact dimension formula $k=2\dim R/(A,B)$ (forcing even $k$), a $4\ell m$-element symmetry group on generator pairs, an $X/Z$ distance equality $d_X=d_Z$, and a family of subgroup-coset kernel vectors giving rigorous distance upper bounds and a design rule for high-distance constructions; all distances are computed exhaustively by a cross-validated bit-mask verifier. At $n=144$ the pipeline returns a census of $53$ codes whose strongest members surpass the BB benchmark: $[[144,6,d\ge 15]]$ exceeds the benchmark distance $12$ (certified $d\ge 15$), $[[144,10,12]]$ reaches it with weight-$8$ checks, and $[[144,16,10]]$ encodes a third more logical qubits at $kd^2/n=11.11$ ($7.4\%$ below benchmark) while decoding no worse. At $n=72$, $[[72,14,8]]$ attains $kd^2/n=12.44$---more than twice the same-length BB code---and decodes better; a circuit-level memory experiment places our weight-$8$ codes at $\approx 0.1\%$ pseudo-threshold versus $\approx 0.4\%$ for the BB reference under an identical model, quantifying the threshold cost of the heavier checks. All structural statements are verified numerically on the whole census.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光纳米金刚石是量子技术的重要平台，其中NV⁻中心可在室温下作为量子比特，但其稳定性受表面官能团调控。作者希望探索新的表面氟化途径以稳定NV⁻负电荷态，同时评估其对自旋相干性的影响，从而厘清电荷态稳定性与自旋寿命之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出两条溶液相氟化路线：(1) 利用二氟化氙（XeF₂）通过自由基脱羧机制形成直接C-F键；(2) Balz-Schiemann反应将表面氨基置换为氟原子。通过红外光谱、X射线光电子能谱、能量色散X射线、拉曼光谱和光致发光光谱对两种路线进行系统对比表征，并使用频域弛豫测量评估自旋-晶格弛豫时间。

Result: 两种氟化路线均获得高NV⁻占比（平均约90%，氟富集区域接近100%），为同类尺寸表面终端化纳米金刚石（尤其氟终端）中报道的最高值之一。氟化颗粒保留较长自旋-晶格弛豫时间（XeF₂路线733±56 μs，Balz-Schiemann路线712±20 μs），远高于商用HPHT纳米金刚石，但短于原始材料的1173±123 μs。

Conclusion: 氟化处理激活了近表面NV⁻中心，这些色心最易受表面噪声影响，但也是弛豫传感中的主导贡献者。因此电荷态稳定性与自旋寿命之间存在权衡：氟化提升了NV⁻比例却牺牲了部分自旋寿命，需要在实际应用中根据传感需求进行取舍。

Abstract: Nanodiamonds hosting luminescent point defects, known as fluorescent nanodiamonds (FND), are a leading platform for quantum technology. The nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centre is the most intensively studied of these; its negatively charged state (NV$^-$) can serve as a qubit at room temperature, and its stability is governed by surface functional groups. We present two solution-phase fluorination routes for stabilising NV$^-$: direct C-F bond formation by decarboxylation with xenon difluoride via a radical mechanism, and the Balz-Schiemann reaction, which replaces surface amino groups with fluorine. The two routes were compared by infrared, X-ray photoelectron, energy-dispersive X-ray, Raman and photoluminescence spectroscopy. Both gave a high NV$^-$ fraction, up to $\sim$90% on average and approaching 100% in fluorine-rich regions, which to our knowledge is among the highest reported for surface-terminated nanodiamonds of this size and, in particular, for fluorine termination. Frequency-domain relaxometry shows that the fluorinated particles retain a long spin-lattice relaxation time, $733\pm56$ and $712\pm20$ $μ$s for the XeF$_2$ and Balz-Schiemann routes, several times the values reported for commercial HPHT nanodiamonds, although shorter than the $1173\pm123$ $μ$s of the as-received material. Charge-state stability and spin lifetime therefore do not improve together: fluorination activates near-surface NV$^-$ centres, which are the most exposed to surface noise but also the ones that dominate relaxometric sensing.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负性作为一种纠缠度量，物理意义清晰且可直接从部分转置的谱计算，但它只捕获了负特征值的总权重，忽略了负谱的精细结构。作者旨在填补这一空白，引入能反映更精细谱结构的分层纠缠度量。

Method: 1) 引入Ky Fan k-负性作为负性的分层推广；2) 建立统一分析框架推导部分转置谱界；3) 将绝对界的最大化问题约化为谱图优化问题，其解通过单个三次方程给出每个k的精确界；4) 在固定纯度约束下进行解析与数值研究；5) 完全求解k=1情形并揭示底层简单图结构。

Result: 1) 通过谱图优化获得了每个k的精确谱界；2) 在固定纯度约束下完整求解了k=1的情形，并发现简单的底层图结构；3) Ky Fan k-负性能稳健地认证真多能级纠缠；4) 将p₃-PPT条件转化为负性的定量下界。

Conclusion: Ky Fan k-负性为量子纠缠研究提供了一族新的分层度量工具，既深化了对部分转置负谱结构的理解，又具有可计算性和实际应用价值，包括真多能级纠缠认证和负性下界的定量推导。

Abstract: Among the various entanglement measures, the negativity stands out not only for its clear physical meaning but also for being directly computable from the spectrum of the partial transpose.
