However, the negativity captures only the total weight of the negative eigenvalues, whereas the finer structure of the negative spectrum remains largely unexplored. In this work, we fill this gap by introducing a hierarchical generalization of the negativity, the Ky Fan $k$-negativity, and developing a unified analytical framework for deriving spectral bounds on the partial transpose. To obtain the absolute bounds, we reduce the maximization problem to a spectral graph optimization, whose solution yields the exact bound for every $k$ through a single cubic equation. We further investigate these spectral bounds both analytically and numerically under a fixed-purity constraint. In particular, we solve the $k=1$ case completely and uncover a simple underlying graph structure. As two direct applications, we show that the Ky Fan $k$-negativity robustly certifies genuine multilevel entanglement and that it converts the $p_3$-PPT condition into a quantitative lower bound on the negativity.

</details>


### [99] [Anomaly fluctuation theorem for quantum coherence dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06632)
*Kun Zhang,Hai-Long Shi,Xiao-Hui Wang,Wen-Li Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子相干动力学的一个精确积分涨落定理(FT)，基于Kirkwood-Dirac(KD)拟概率轨迹表述，适用于任意初始态和任意动力学过程。该定理与标准单位值形式不同，其复数偏差（称为"异常项"）量化了残余末态相干性与由退相输入产生的相干性之间的加权重叠。异常项实部约束相干性变化，虚部约束相干性变化二阶矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相干是量子信息科学的核心资源，但其动力学的一般框架和约束仍然有限。尽管量子相干缺乏一般的单调性定律，作者希望建立更一般的统计约束来描述相干性的动力学行为，从而弥补现有框架的不足。

Method: 利用Kirkwood-Dirac(KD)拟概率轨迹构建精确的积分涨落定理(FT)，适用于任意初始态和动力学过程。通过与标准单位值形式对比，定义了复数"异常"项来表征偏差，并分别分析其实部与虚部的物理意义，分别关联相干性变化界限与二阶矩约束。

Result: 得到一个一般性的积分涨落定理，其偏离标准单位值形式的复数异常项可分解为实部和虚部：实部提供相干性变化的界，虚部约束由KD拟概率虚部加权的随机相干性变化的二阶矩。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了相干动力学的一般统计约束，展示了涨落定理在研究量子资源动力学方面的实用性和广泛适用性，为量子相干性研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Quantum coherence is a central resource in quantum information science, yet general frameworks and constraints governing its dynamics remain limited. Although quantum coherence lacks a general monotonicity law, we establish an exact integral fluctuation theorem (FT) for coherence dynamics, formulated in terms of Kirkwood-Dirac (KD) quasiprobability trajectories and valid for arbitrary initial states and dynamics. This integral FT deviates from the standard unit-valued form, and the complex deviation, termed the anomaly, quantifies a weighted overlap between the residual final-state coherence and the coherence generated from the dephased input. The real part of the anomaly yields bounds on coherence change, while its imaginary part constrains the second-order moment of the stochastic coherence change weighted by the imaginary parts of the KD quasiprobabilities. Our results establish general statistical constraints on coherence dynamics, demonstrating the utility and broad applicability of FTs for studying quantum-resource dynamics.

</details>


### [100] [Entanglement Growth as Transport Across Schmidt Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06643)
*Shi-Xin Zhang,Shuo Liu,Yu-Qin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入"Schmidt尺度浓度"和"主导Schmidt尺度"两个新坐标，用于定位有序Schmidt秩空间中对数窗口内的概率最大值。通过对随机场自旋链淬火动力学的分析，发现这些坐标能区分弱无序下主导尺度向更高Schmidt秩快速迁移与强无序下尽管对数熵持续增长但迁移被强烈抑制的两种行为。无序平均动力学呈现有序层级：熵产生先达峰，随后谱粗糙度与精确非局域magic达峰，而主导Schmidt尺度输运仅在显著延迟后才变得典型。可通过可解的头-尾模型及受控数值实验揭示该层级的物理起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠增长通常仅用单一熵来概括，掩盖了指数级大Schmidt谱中关联的分布位置及其形成机制。作者希望超越单一熵度量，开发能够解析纠缠谱内部精细结构的动力学探针，从而区分纠缠产生、纠缠谱重组与主导Schmidt尺度输运等不同过程。

Method: (1) 提出Schmidt尺度浓度与主导Schmidt尺度两个坐标，在有序Schmidt秩空间的对数窗口中定位概率最大值；(2) 应用于随机场自旋链的量子淬火动力学；(3) 构建可解的head-tail模型；(4) 进行受控数值实验，结合谱路径分析与活性交换键上的局域动力学分析。

Result: 发现弱无序下主导尺度快速输运至更高Schmidt秩，强无序下输运被强烈抑制但对数熵仍持续增长。无序平均动力学呈现有序时间层级：熵产生先达峰，谱粗糙度和精确非局域magic随后达峰，主导Schmidt尺度输运最后才变得典型。head-tail模型及数值实验表明：谱路径决定事件顺序，活性交换键上的局域动力学设定早期时序，子系统内多体修饰进一步延迟主导Schmidt尺度输运。

Conclusion: Schmidt尺度坐标是解析纠缠细粒度结构的强大动力学探针，能够区分超越单一熵的纠缠产生、纠缠谱重组与主导Schmidt尺度输运等不同过程，为研究量子多体系统中的纠缠动力学提供了新工具。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement growth is commonly summarized by a single entropy, obscuring where correlations reside in the exponentially large Schmidt spectrum and how they form. Here, we introduce Schmidt-scale concentration and dominant Schmidt scale, two coordinates that locate the probability maximum across logarithmic windows in ordered Schmidt-rank space. Applied to quenches of a random-field spin chain, these coordinates distinguish rapid transport of the dominant scale to higher Schmidt rank at weak disorder from strongly suppressed transport despite continued logarithmic entropy growth at strong disorder. The disorder-averaged dynamics exhibit an ordered hierarchy: entropy production peaks first, spectral roughness and exact nonlocal magic peak next, and dominant-Schmidt-scale transport becomes typical only after a substantial delay. Moreover, a solvable head--tail model and controlled numerical experiments reveal the physical origin of this hierarchy: the spectral path determines the order of events, local dynamics on active exchange bonds set their early timing, and intra-subsystem many-body dressing further delays dominant-Schmidt-scale transport. These results establish the Schmidt-scale coordinates as powerful dynamical probes for uncovering fine-grained entanglement structures distinguishing entanglement production, entanglement-spectrum reorganization, and dominant-Schmidt-scale transport beyond entropy alone.

</details>


### [101] [Efficient Representation of multicategorical local Hilbert spaces: nonlinear Restricted Boltzmann Machines to Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06672)
*Abhiroop Lahiri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络量子态（NQS）中多类别自旋体系（spin-1、spin-2及q态量子Potts模型）的表示方法。作者观察到标准的one-hot编码MLP架构参数随局部状态数增长且优化景观不佳，提出直接对原始多值自旋变量施加非线性函数来构造logΨ，在保留基态标记自由度的同时用更少参数复现one-hot模型效果。作者先以浅层受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）在spin-1/2/3海森堡链上验证多种非线性连接方案，再引入Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）作为同一原理的更富表达力实现，最终证明该框架可捕捉量子Potts哈密顿量的临界行为并恢复其相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上多局部自由度自旋系统（spin-S>1/2或q态Potts模型）的NQS表示依赖one-hot或unary编码的MLP架构，但其参数数量随局部状态数线性增长，且优化景观可能导致收敛困难。作者旨在寻找一种更紧凑、更可训练且数学上忠实的替代编码方案。

Method: 提出将logΨ构造为原始多值自旋变量的非线性函数，取代one-hot编码的线性仿射+非线性组合范式。具体做法为：(1)在浅层RBM中引入多种非线性连接；(2)利用KAN中每条边携带可学习单变量非线性的特性，作为该原理的更富表达力实现；(3)分别在spin-1、2、3海森堡链及q态量子Potts哈密顿量上进行数值验证与基准测试。

Result: 数值结果表明：原始多值变量的非线性函数化是one-hot编码的自然范畴推广，能够以严格更少的参数复现one-hot模型的精度，且通常具备更好的可训练性；KAN提供了同一原则下更富表达力的实现；该框架能够捕捉量子Potts哈密顿量的临界行为并恢复其相变。

Conclusion: 作者确立了一种无需one-hot编码的多类别量子态表示范式，证明直接对原始自旋变量进行非线性映射既是保基标记自由度的自然推广，又在参数效率和优化性能上优于one-hot架构，并具有捕获量子相变的表达能力。

Abstract: Neural network quantum states (NQS) for representing spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ systems are typically built from Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) with binary visible variables, in which the input spins are first combined through linear affine maps before more expressive nonlinear transformations are applied. The standard way for representing multi-categorical systems, namely spin-1 and spin-2, or the q-state quantum Potts systems with more than two local degrees of freedom, is a unary or one-hot encoded MLP architecture. Following a few similar studies, I observe that while the one-hot construction is the mathematically faithful encoding for systems with more than two local states such as spin-$S$ models with $S > 1/2$ or $q$-state Potts models, its parameter count grows with the number of local states, and the resulting optimization landscape can hinder convergence. I find numerically that allowing $\log Ψ$ to be a nonlinear function of the raw multi-valued spin variable is a natural categorical generalization: it preserves the labelling freedom of the local basis and reproduces the one-hot model with strictly fewer parameters, often with improved trainability. I first benchmark this idea on shallow Restricted Boltzmann Machines (RBMs), equipping them with several distinct nonlinear connections, for spin-1, 2, and 3 Heisenberg chains. I then turn to Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), where each edge carries a learnable univariate nonlinearity, and show that they provide a strictly more expressive realization of the same principle. Finally, I demonstrate that this framework captures the critical behaviour of the quantum Potts Hamiltonian, recovering its phase transition.

</details>


### [102] [Contact order governs the onset of entanglement cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06675)
*Sugumi Kanno,Jiro Soda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限维解析纯态动力学中的纠缠级联现象，提出用物理轨迹与S|E乘积流形之间的接触阶m*来刻画(n+1)体真正纠缠的起始时刻，并推导出可消除曲率诱导动力学项的时间有序递归来从H(t)的泰勒系数计算m*。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠率的理论框架只能描述允许的直接纠缠通道，但当该通道被禁止（例如需要通过中间体间接建立纠缠）时，缺乏有效的定量描述工具。作者希望建立一个几何框架来刻画多体纠缠在动力学中逐级级联涌现的过程。

Method: 作者在有限维解析纯态动力学框架下，将物理轨迹与S|E乘积流形（即部分可分态流形）的接触阶m*定义为纠缠起始时刻的指标。m*是任何乘积曲线无法复现的首个泰勒阶数，等价于从乘积流形出发的Fubini-Study距离的首个非零阶数。作者推导了一个时间有序递归，用于消除曲率诱导的运动学项，从而从哈密顿量H(t)的泰勒系数直接计算m*。

Result: 该框架给出纠缠级联的几何描述：新子系统只有在一次或多次相互作用步骤之后才能加入多体纠缠。通过对称保护的纠缠比特模型实现了m*=2的案例，并展示了一个三模玻色子例子，说明即使在两个新形成的约化对保持可分离时，仍然可以产生真正的三体纠缠。

Conclusion: 接触阶m*提供了一种超越简单纠缠率的精细度量，能够刻画多体纠缠通过中间系统的级联增长过程，为理解和预测禁止直接纠缠通道下的多体纠缠演化提供了几何工具。

Abstract: Entangling rates describe a direct entangling channel, but give no information when that channel is forbidden. For finite-dimensional analytic pure-state dynamics, we show that the onset of genuine $(n+1)$-partite entanglement is determined by the contact order $m_\star$ between the physical trajectory and the $S|E$ product manifold. It is the first Taylor order that cannot be reproduced by any product curve, or equivalently the first nonvanishing order of the Fubini--Study distance from the product manifold. We derive a time-ordered recursion that removes curvature-induced kinematic terms and computes $m_\star$ from the Taylor coefficients of $H(t)$. This provides a geometric description of an entanglement cascade, in which new subsystems can join multipartite entanglement only after one or more interaction steps. A symmetry-protected three-qubit model realizes $m_\star=2$, and a three-mode bosonic example shows that genuine tripartite entanglement can arise even when the two newly formed reduced pairs remain separable.

</details>


### [103] [Error Exponents of Probabilistic Quantum Resource Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06688)
*Xian Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一框架,用于分析在近似资源非生成工具下概率性资源蒸馏的条件错误指数。通过与后选择复合量子假设检验相关联,获得了纠缠、相干性和魔法蒸馏在有限块长度下的条件错误指数解析特征及其零速率渐近表达式,并识别了后选择带来严格改进的体制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子资源理论中,概率性资源蒸馏(允许后选择)是一个重要的操作任务,但此前缺乏对其条件错误指数的统一分析框架。已有工作主要集中于确定性蒸馏的错误指数,而概率性(后选择)蒸馏的精确表征及其相比确定性蒸馏的潜在优势尚未被系统研究。

Method: 作者首先建立了满足特定结构条件的一般量子资源理论中,近似资源非生成工具下概率性蒸馏的通用单发边界。然后将概率性蒸馏映射到后选择复合量子假设检验问题,从而实现解析表征。该方法覆盖纠缠、相干性和魔法蒸馏,并在代表性态族中化简为闭式公式。

Result: 获得了纠缠、相干性和魔法蒸馏在有限块长度下的条件错误指数解析表征,以及零速率区域的渐近表达式。对若干代表性纠缠与魔法态族得到了显式闭式公式,并与确定性蒸馏指数量化对比,识别出后选择在条件错误指数衰减率上产生严格改进的参数范围。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了后选择在量子资源蒸馏中具有操作性优势,并确立后选择复合量子假设检验作为表征概率性资源处理任务的一般工具。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we establish a unified framework for analyzing the conditional error exponents of probabilistic resource distillation under approximately resource-nongenerating instruments. For generic quantum resource theories satisfying suitable structural conditions, we derive general one-shot bounds on the conditional distillation error exponents. By relating probabilistic distillation to postselected composite quantum hypothesis testing, we further obtain analytical characterizations of the conditional error exponents for entanglement, coherence, and magic distillation at finite blocklength, and derive their asymptotic expressions in the zero-rate regime. For several representative families of states in entanglement and magic resource theories, these characterizations reduce to explicit closed-form formulas. Comparing them with the corresponding deterministic distillation exponents, we identify regimes in which postselection yields a strict improvement in the exponential decay rate of the conditional error. Our results reveal an operational advantage of postselection in quantum resource distillation and establish postselected composite hypothesis testing as a general tool for characterizing probabilistic resource-processing tasks.

</details>


### [104] [Thermodynamic Performance of a Measurement-Driven Quantum Engine with a Two-Parameter $(p,q)$-Deformed Harmonic Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06695)
*Tunde Joseph Osunmusanmi,Berihu Teklu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于量子测量反作用的单浴量子引擎，其工作介质为双参数(p,q)-形变谐振子。引擎循环包括绝热改变(ω,p,q)、非选择性高斯测量、反向绝热冲程及热浴热化。数值结果显示，形变在满足引擎运转条件下可增强测量诱导的能量输入与提取功，但与未形变振子相比，文中报告的效率为降低的简化效率，未包含测量装置成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子测量反作用能否用于驱动单浴量子引擎做功，并研究非线性谱形变是否能够增强此类引擎的性能，扩展量子热力学在小体系中的应用边界。

Method: 采用两参数(p,q)-形变谐振子作为工作介质，以自洽第一定律框架推导测量热、绝热功、热化热及简化效率；通过转移矩阵刻画测量冲程，建立被动性/酉条件确保测量热非负，并对满足有限基底、谱序及引擎运转判据的参数空间进行数值扫描。

Result: 在有限基底、谱序和引擎运转检查条件下，数值结果表明形变可增强测量诱导的能量注入和提取功（相对于未形变谐振子）；引擎物理区由QM>0、Wcyc<0、QT<0及0<η<1共同界定。

Conclusion: 形变谐振子可作为测量驱动单浴量子引擎的有效工作介质，在特定参数区域中性能优于普通谐振子；但所得效率为简化效率，未计入测量装置能耗，实际含装置成本效率需进一步考察。

Abstract: We address a measurement-driven, single-bath quantum engine that generates usable work using quantum measurement backaction. The working medium is a two-parameter $(p,q)$-deformed harmonic oscillator, whose nonlinear spectrum modifies the energy gaps sampled by a nonselective Gaussian measurement and the work exchanged during quasistatic adiabatic strokes. The cycle starts from a Gibbs state and consists of an adiabatic change of $(ω,p,q)$, measurement of the final effective position quadrature, a reverse adiabatic stroke, and thermalization with the original bath. Using a consistent first-law convention, we derive the measurement heat, adiabatic work contributions, thermalization heat, and reduced working-medium efficiency. We also express the measurement stroke via a transition matrix and identify passivity/unitality conditions that ensure nonnegative measurement heat. Numerical scans show that, within the finite-basis, spectral-ordering, and engine-operation checks used in this work, deformation can enhance measurement-induced energy input and extracted work relative to the undeformed oscillator. The physical engine regime is selected by $\QM>0$, $\Wcyc<0$, $\QT<0$, and $0<η<1$. The reported efficiency is reduced and excludes measurement-apparatus costs.

</details>


### [105] [Scale-Free Localization Morphing in Bilocally Coupled Hatano-Nelson Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06799)
*Chong Wang,Linhu Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两条Hatano-Nelson链在两个体态位点（相距d）进行局域耦合时产生的反常临界非厄米趋肤效应（CNHSE）。与传统弱耦合子系统产生临界趋肤效应不同，该系统中的临界行为在强链间耦合区间依然存在。强局域杂化产生高能杂质模式，并将低能希尔伯特空间有效碎片化为几何选择的扇区，其中一个扇区支持无标度趋肤态，外层扇区选择态数目遵循2(L-d)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临界非厄米趋肤效应通常与弱耦合子系统相关，而强耦合区间的临界行为鲜有探索。作者旨在突破弱耦合范式，探索在强耦合条件下如何实现并理解临界非厄米趋肤效应，揭示局域耦合诱导的希尔伯特空间碎片化作为新的物理机制。

Method: 构建两条Hatano-Nelson链，在相距为d的两个体态位点处引入局域耦合。通过分析强局域杂化产生的高能杂质模式以及低能希尔伯特空间的几何选择性碎片化，识别出支持无标度趋肤态的特定扇区，并系统推导外层扇区状态数目的标度规律2(L-d)。

Result: 发现强链间耦合区间仍存在临界非厄米趋肤效应，即反常CNHSE。强局域耦合导致希尔伯特空间碎片化为几何选定的扇区，其中一个扇区承载无标度趋肤态；外层扇区中的态数满足精确计数关系2(L-d)。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了局域耦合诱导的碎片化作为超越弱耦合范式的临界非厄米行为的新途径，拓展了对非厄米趋肤效应临界现象的理解。

Abstract: We investigate an anomalous critical non-Hermitian skin effect (CNHSE) in two Hatano--Nelson chains coupled locally at two bulk sites separated by an inclusive distance $d$. Whereas conventional CNHSE is commonly associated with weakly coupled subsystems, the critical behavior in our system persists in the strong inter-chain-coupling regime. Strong local hybridization generates high-energy impurity modes and effectively fragments the low-energy Hilbert space into geometry-selected sectors, one of which supports scale-free skin states. Independently, the count of states selected in the outer sector follows $2(L-d)$ for the two-chain system. These results establish local-coupling-induced fragmentation as a route to critical non-Hermitian behavior beyond the weak-coupling paradigm.

</details>


### [106] [Hilbert space geometry change by a nonadiabatic electromagnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06800)
*Ivan Georgiev Koprinkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现量子系统在非绝热电磁场中希尔伯特空间的几何结构会发生变化。通过对比三种物理情形（无外场、绝热场、非绝热场），发现前两种情形下量子态正交，而在非绝热场中量子态变为非正交，且连续依赖于场参数。基于态矩阵元可定义时变的Fubini-Study度规，该动力学度规类似于广义相对论中的时空动力学度规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子系统在非绝热电磁场中希尔伯特空间几何发生变化的物理起源，并通过研究量子态从正交到非正交的演化来理解这一几何变形。

Method: 基于量子态矩阵元，在三种主要物理情形下研究量子态正交性到非正交性的演化：零外场（封闭量子系统）、缓慢绝热场、快速非绝热场。在非绝热情形下，基于态矩阵元确定时变的Fubini-Study度规。

Result: 量子态在前两种情形下保持正交，在非绝热场中变为非正交，且变化连续跟随场参数。在非绝热情形下可以定义时变的Fubini-Study度规来描述希尔伯特空间的动力学几何。

Conclusion: 非绝热电磁场会导致量子系统希尔伯特空间几何发生动力学变形，可通过时变Fubini-Study度规来刻画，这种量子力学中希尔伯特空间的动力学度规与广义相对论中时空的动力学度规在形式上相似。

Abstract: A change in the geometry of the Hilbert space of a quantum system interacting with a nonadiabatic electromagnetic field is found. To identify its physical origin, the evolution of orthogonality to non-orthogonality of the states of the quantum system has been investigated based on the matrix elements of the quantum states in three main physical cases: a quantum system in zero external field (closed quantum system), a quantum system in slowly changing (adiabatic) field, and a quantum system in rapidly changing (nonadiabatic) field. The quantum states are orthogonal in the first two cases and change to non-orthogonal in a nonadiabatic field, following continuously the parameters of the field. A time-dependent Fubini-Study metric of the Hilbert space can be determined based on the matrix elements of the quantum states in the nonadiabatic case. Such a dynamical metric of the Hilbert space in quantum mechanics resembles the dynamical metric of spacetime in general relativity.

</details>


### [107] [Spectral Twisting in a Common Bosonic Reservoir: Fragility of Two-Qubit Dark-State Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06802)
*Fabio Borrelli,Giovanni Miano,Carlo Forestiere*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究两个量子比特与共同玻色子库相互作用时，"光谱扭转"（spectral twisting）现象——即由于$J_{22}(\omega)/J_{11}(\omega)$随频率变化导致亮/暗耦合方向随频率旋转——如何影响两比特动力学与暗态保护。通过非微扰层次方程运动（HEOM）计算，发现扭转会导致局部暗态（如单态）发生泄漏，且弱扭转下泄漏$P_{\rm leak}(t)\propto[\omega_\times\tau(\omega_\times)]^2$呈二次标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化玻色子库环境下，共同库中两比特的暗态工程依赖于光谱密度矩阵$\mathbf{J}(\omega)$的秩亏结构。当库在局部频率上存在暗方向时，理论上可保护纠缠态（如单态）免受退相干。然而，实际库谱往往不对称且随频率变化，这可能导致"光谱扭转"使暗方向随频率旋转，从而破坏完美的暗态保护。理解并量化这一机制对暗态工程与退相干自由编码的实际实现至关重要。

Method: 作者将光谱扭转量化为明亮谱投影子的Fubini-Study速度$\tau(\omega)$，并定义生存泄漏$P_{\rm leak}(t)$来量化暗态保护损失。通过与非扭转旋转波动力学及非扭转非对称库（Drude-Lorentz谱）的对比，排除耦合不对称、热吸收、反旋过程和哈密顿对称破缺等混淆因素。对失配的Drude-Lorentz谱采用非微扰层次方程运动（HEOM）数值方法进行计算。

Result: 研究表明：扭转导致在谱交叉频率$\omega_\times$处局部暗的单态发生泄漏；扭转使最优保护态发生偏移，并加速Werner态并发度的衰减；在固定观测时间下，弱扭转泄漏满足二次标度$P_{\rm leak}(t)\propto[\omega_\times\tau(\omega_\times)]^2$。

Conclusion: 光谱扭转是区别于耦合不对称、热吸收、反旋过程与哈密顿对称破缺的独特退相干机制。该结果可为结构化库中的暗态工程提供设计指导，对相关噪声光谱学与退相干自由编码具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The interaction of two qubits with a common bosonic reservoir is characterized by the spectral-density matrix $\mathbf{J}(ω)$, whose diagonal entries $J_{11}(ω)$ and $J_{22}(ω)$ describe the local spectra, while the off-diagonal entries encode cross-correlations. For a maximally correlated reservoir, $\mathbf{J}(ω)$ has rank one and therefore a \textit{locally} dark coupling direction at each frequency. If $J_{22}(ω)/J_{11}(ω)$ varies with frequency, however, the bright and dark directions rotate and $\ker\mathbf{J}(ω)$ is frequency dependent. We call this \textit{spectral twisting} and quantify it through the Fubini--Study speed $τ(ω)$ of the bright spectral projector. We investigate how spectral twisting affects coupled two-qubit dynamics and dark-state protection. We quantify protection loss by the survival \textit{leakage} $P_{\mathrm{leak}}(t)$, which can become finite for states that are dark only locally in frequency. By comparing rotating-wave dynamics and untwisted asymmetric reservoirs, together with analyzing qubit detuning, we distinguish twisting from coupling asymmetry, thermal absorption, counter-rotating processes, and Hamiltonian symmetry breaking. For mismatched Drude--Lorentz spectra, our nonperturbative hierarchical-equations-of-motion calculations show that twisting induces leakage from the singlet, which is locally dark at the spectral crossing $ω_\times$ defined by $J_{11}(ω_\times)=J_{22}(ω_\times)$. Twisting also shifts the optimally protected state and accelerates the decay of Werner-state concurrence. At fixed observation time, we find the quadratic weak-twisting scaling $P_{\mathrm{leak}}(t)\propto[ω_\timesτ(ω_\times)]^2$. These results may guide dark-state engineering in structured reservoirs, with implications for correlated-noise spectroscopy and decoherence-free encodings.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum-Enhanced Learning Framework for Intelligent and AI-Native 6G Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06814)
*Muhammad Bilal Akram Dastagir,Rakesh Saini,Omer Tariq,Saif Al-Kuwari,Shahid Mumtaz,Anwer Al-Dulaimi,Ahmed Farouk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Quantumer，一种混合TinyML-量子框架，将多尺度膨胀卷积与缩放点积注意力集成于轻量级Transformer架构中，采用从量子预训练到经典微调的两阶段迁移学习流程。该方法利用四量子比特变分电路在离线预训练阶段作为非线性嵌入教师，部署时移除量子组件，保留完全经典的推理模型，在边缘设备上实现高效且轻量的入侵检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G无线系统与TinyML的融合推动了对边缘设备端智能的需求，但存在关键矛盾：轻量深度模型可高效提取局部模式却难以捕获全局依赖，而注意力机制虽能捕获全局依赖但能耗和计算成本过高。作者旨在弥合这一差距，在超低延迟、严格能量预算和紧约束的计算条件下实现兼顾全局建模能力与资源效率的轻量模型。

Method: 提出Quantumer混合框架：(1) 将多尺度膨胀卷积与缩放点积注意力集成于轻量级Transformer架构中；(2) 采用两阶段迁移学习流水线，即Quantum Pre-Training (Quantumer-Q) 到 Classical Fine-Tuning (Quantumer-C)；(3) 设计QuantiblentLayer——一个四量子比特变分电路，通过可训练旋转和循环纠缠操作将紧凑流量表征映射为基于测量的Hilbert空间特征，仅在离线预训练阶段用作非线性嵌入教师，部署前移除；(4) 以入侵检测系统为案例，在Edge-IIoTset、TON IoT和WUSTL-IIoT-2021三个数据集上评估。

Result: Quantumer-Q在紧凑模型性能上表现优异：仅105.86K参数、0.4038 MB内存使用、0.5525 MB模型大小、5.5646 MFLOPs计算量。INT8量化的Raspberry Pi 4部署实现16.8413 ms延迟及0.6493 MB占用空间，在资源受限的边缘设备上达到强检测性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，训练期间的量子辅助表示学习能够为紧凑型边缘可部署的入侵检测系统提供有效支持。通过将量子辅助嵌入迁移至节能轻量级Transformer中，Quantumer在保持极低计算与内存开销的同时实现了强检测性能，验证了量子启发的训练时增强方法对边缘AI的实用价值，且无需运行时量子执行。

Abstract: The recent convergence of 6G wireless systems and Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) has driven the need for on-device intelligence in edge networks, where ultra-low latency, stringent energy budgets, and tight compute constraints demand novel architectures. Lightweight deep models efficiently extract local patterns but fail to capture global dependencies, while attention mechanisms do so at the expense of energy and computational cost. To bridge this gap, we introduce Quantumer, a hybrid TinyML--quantum framework that integrates multi-scale dilated convolutions and scaled dot-product attention within a lightweight transformer architecture, employing a two-stage transfer learning pipeline from Quantum Pre-Training (Quantumer-Q) to Classical Fine-Tuning (Quantumer-C). We also present QuantiblentLayer, a four-qubit variational circuit that maps compact traffic representations into measurement-based Hilbert-space features using trainable rotations and cyclic entangling operations. The circuit is used only during offline pre-training as a nonlinear embedding teacher and is removed before Quantumer-C deployment, leaving a fully classical inference model without runtime quantum execution. By transferring these quantum-assisted embeddings into an energy-efficient, lightweight transformer, Quantumer achieves strong detection performance with minimal compute and memory overhead on resource-constrained edge devices. The intrusion detection system (IDS) is used as a case study and evaluated on the Edge-IIoTset, TON IoT, and WUSTL-IIoT-2021 datasets. Quantumer-Q achieves competitive compact-model performance with 105.86K parameters, 0.4038 MB memory usage, 0.5525 MB model size, and 5.5646 MFLOPs; the INT8 Raspberry Pi 4 deployment obtains 16.8413 ms latency with a 0.6493 MB footprint. These results support training-time quantum-assisted representation learning for compact edge-deployable IDS.

</details>


### [109] [Trace-class spectra of irreducible Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06957)
*Franco Fagnola,Zheng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了有限模态玻色子Fock空间上不可约高斯量子马尔可夫半群在预对偶算子上的谱，涵盖稳定漂移、严格不稳定和周期临界漂移三种情形：稳定时谱为闭左半平面，点谱为开左半平面加零；不稳定时点谱为空而残谱覆盖开左半平面加零；周期临界情形给出包含位移参数的显式谱公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不可约高斯量子马尔可夫半群预对偶生成元的谱结构，对于量子动力学、开放量子系统及其收敛性质的理论研究具有重要意义。此前对漂移矩阵特征多项式生成的多项式特征值是否穷尽迹类点谱缺乏清晰认识，且周期临界漂移情形的谱结构尚未被刻画。

Method: 论文采用特征函数方法、高斯扩散半群理论以及对偶半群的有界特征算子来系统地研究各漂移区域下的谱性质；在稳定区域将不可约性与唯一忠实正规不变态的存在性联系起来；在周期临界区域推导出包含位移参数的显式谱公式。

Result: 稳定漂移情形：谱和近似点谱均为闭左半平面，点谱为开左半平面加零，且漂移矩阵生成的多项式特征值并未穷尽迹类点谱。不稳定漂移情形：谱仍为闭左半平面，但点谱为空，开左半平面加零均属于残谱。周期临界漂移情形：获得显式谱公式，含位移参数，谱呈水平半直线或抛物区域。

Conclusion: 作者对不同漂移机制下高斯量子马尔可夫半群的谱结构给出了完整刻画，揭示了多项式特征值与迹类点谱之间的非穷尽关系，并指出非周期临界漂移情形仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We determine the spectrum of the predual generator of an irreducible Gaussian quantum Markov semigroup on the trace-class operators over a finite-mode bosonic Fock space in the stable, strictly unstable, and periodic critical drift regimes. For stable drift, irreducibility is equivalent to the existence of a unique faithful normal invariant state. The spectrum and approximate point spectrum are the closed left half-plane, whereas the point spectrum is the open left half-plane together with zero. Thus the polynomial eigenvalues generated by the drift matrix do not exhaust the trace-class point spectrum. If the drift has an eigenvalue with strictly positive real part, the spectrum is again the closed left half-plane, but the point spectrum is empty and the open left half-plane together with zero belongs to the residual spectrum. For periodic critical drift, we obtain an explicit spectral formula that includes the displacement parameter and yields horizontal half-lines or parabolic regions. The proofs use characteristic functions, Gaussian diffusion semigroups, and bounded eigenoperators of the dual semigroup. The nonperiodic critical drift case remains open.

</details>


### [110] [Coherence-Mediated Boundary Control of the Liouvillian Gap in Nonreciprocal Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06981)
*K. L. Li,M. H. Yu,X. Z. Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种单粒子Lindblad格点系统，其中非互易非相干跳跃与可调的相干边界链相结合。边界链仅改变哈密顿量边界条件而不改变非相干跳跃率，其对角隙的影响通过"布居-相干-布居"反馈机制实现。周期参考系统可导出精确的定动量约化、标量久期方程及流体动力学漂移和相干修正扩散；开放有限格点则通过精确对角化和稀疏特征值求解器研究发现相干边界链可在1D增强或抑制能隙，并在2D产生几何相关的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开边界条件下量子耗散系统中的弛豫动力学，特别是相干边界条件如何在保持非相干跳跃率不变的情况下，通过能隙调节影响系统弛豫时间尺度。此前研究大多关注耗散项或非厄米哈密顿量本身对隙的影响，而相干边界链通过布居-相干反馈间接调控隙的机制尚不清楚。

Method: 1) 构造单粒子Lindblad格点模型，将非互易非相干跳跃与可调相干边界链结合；2) 对满足平移对称的周期参考系统做精确的定动量约化，推导布居连接分支的标量久期方程及流体动力学漂移和相干修正扩散；3) 对开放有限格点使用精确对角化和稀疏近零特征值求解器；4) 分析弛豫尺度与1/Δ_L的偏差（源于能隙族的弱可见性）。

Result: 相干边界链在一维系统中可以增强或抑制Liouvillian能隙，在二维系统中产生与几何构型相关的响应。周期构造提供了精确解析基准；有限尺寸模拟证实了相同相干介导机制同时控制开放边界能隙和可观测弛豫。此外，当能隙族在所选扰动和可观测量的投影下可见性弱时，观测弛豫尺度可能与1/Δ_L存在显著偏差。

Conclusion: 相干边界链通过布居-相干-布居反馈路径间接调控Liouvillian能隙，这一机制在周期和开放系统中统一成立；实际观测弛豫速率需同时考虑能隙值与能隙特征态在测量方向的可见性，而不能简单等同于能隙的倒数。

Abstract: We study a single-particle Lindblad lattice in which nonreciprocal incoherent hopping is combined with a tunable coherent boundary link. The link changes the Hamiltonian boundary condition while the incoherent jump rates are kept fixed, so it does not directly alter the diagonal population generator. Its effect on the Liouvillian gap comes from population-coherence-population feedback: the Hamiltonian couples populations to damped coherences, and eliminating those coherences produces a frequency-dependent self-energy for the slow population branch. For the periodic reference system, where translation symmetry is imposed on both the Hamiltonian and the dissipator, we derive an exact fixed-momentum reduction, a scalar secular equation for the population-connected branch, and the associated hydrodynamic drift and coherence-corrected diffusion. For open finite lattices, exact diagonalization and sparse near-zero eigensolvers show that the coherent boundary link can enhance or suppress the gap in one dimension and produce geometry-dependent responses in two dimensions. We also show that an observed relaxation scale can differ from $1/Δ_{\mathcal L}$ when the gap family has weak visibility in the chosen perturbation and observable. The periodic construction gives exact analytical benchmarks, while finite-size simulations in one and two dimensions demonstrate how the same coherence-mediated mechanism controls open-boundary gaps and observable relaxation.

</details>


### [111] [A Single Fixed Shallow Circuit for Classical Shadows of Arbitrary n-Qubit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07032)
*Yu Wang,Xiuwu Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个固定的浅层量子分析器，用于经典阴影协议。该分析器通过将未知系统与新鲜制备的n-qubit fiducial寄存器A进行并行Bell读出，产生2n-bit记录作为阴影快照的标签，取代了传统上逐次采样的随机测量设置。同一电路实现了秩一的极小信息完备测量（minimal informationally complete measurement），用单一固定设置替代了传统完整重建所需的3^n个局部Pauli设置，同时保留了最小d²个输出（d=2^n）。其Pauli对角框架允许解析求逆。该方案展示了测量设置的随机性和控制复杂度可以从逐次采样编译进一个固定的可重用分析器中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典阴影（classical shadows）协议通常依赖随机化测量的设置切换，但每次切换都引入控制、校准和重构成本，这些成本超出了传统度量指标。作者希望消除逐次随机设置采样的开销，构造一个无需切换的固定测量装置。

Method: 构造一个固定的浅层量子分析器：将新鲜制备的n-qubit fiducial寄存器A与未知系统S通过并行Bell读出相连，每次产生2n-bit记录。该电路实现秩一最小信息完全测量（rank-one minimal informationally complete measurement），用单一固定设置替代3^n个局部Pauli设置。fiducial制备使用n-1个任意两比特门、无需额外工作比特、在全连通下具有对数深度；未知系统仅经历一层并行系统-辅助纠缠层加局部Hadamard与读出。测量辅助制备实现O(1)自适应量子深度（heralded路线用n+1个额外比特）或O(n log n)辅助比特（确定性完成）。

Result: Pauli对角框架具有解析逆。对固定Hermitian可观测量，Haar平均条件方差的系数与维度无关，而态均匀系数具有维度依赖性。对于n≥3，轴向扇区（axial sector）中最坏态Pauli方差有界，混合扇区（mixed sector）中按Θ(d)标度。电路深度权衡显示：log深度（无额外比特）vs O(1)自适应深度（n+1个额外比特，heralded）vs O(n log n)辅助比特（确定性）。

Conclusion: 该方案表明，经典阴影协议中通常由逐次测量设置采样提供的随机性和控制复杂度，可以被编译进一个固定的可重用分析器中，从而显著降低控制/校准/重构开销，同时保持完整的可重建性与良好的方差特性。

Abstract: Classical shadows extract quantum properties from reusable classical records, typically obtained through randomized measurement settings. Although individual settings may be shallow, switching among them introduces control, calibration, and reconfiguration costs beyond conventional metrics. Here we construct, for every $n$, a single fixed shallow quantum analyzer whose Born outcomes replace externally sampled settings as labels for reusable shadow snapshots. The analyzer combines a freshly prepared $n$-qubit fiducial register $A$ with parallel Bell readout of $A$ and the unknown system $S$, producing one $2n$-bit record per copy. The same circuit realizes a rank-one minimal informationally complete measurement: one fixed setting replaces the $3^n$ local-Pauli settings conventionally used for complete reconstruction while retaining the minimum $d^2$ outcomes with $d=2^n$. Its Pauli-diagonal frame admits an analytic inverse. For fixed Hermitian observables, the Haar-averaged conditional variance has a dimension-independent coefficient, whereas the state-uniform coefficient is dimension dependent. For $n\ge3$, worst-state Pauli variances remain bounded in the axial sector and scale as $Θ(d)$ in the mixed sector. Fiducial preparation before system contact uses $n-1$ arbitrary two-qubit gates, no work qubits beyond $A$, and logarithmic depth under all-to-all connectivity. The unknown system undergoes one parallel system-ancilla entangling layer followed by local Hadamards and readout. Measurement-assisted preparation achieves $O(1)$ adaptive quantum depth using $n+1$ extra qubits in the heralded route, or $O(n\log n)$ auxiliaries for deterministic completion. These tradeoffs show that part of the measurement-setting randomness and control complexity normally supplied shot by shot can instead be compiled into a fixed reusable analyzer.

</details>


### [112] [Capability-Gated Conformance Testing of Quantum Error-Correction Decoder Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07035)
*Jiachen Shen,Hui Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子纠错解码器的"一致性合同"（conformance contract）验证框架，无需Oracle即可检查解码器输出。包含两类检查：校正是否在调用者索引空间中解释综合征，以及同一实例在两种不同表示下的可行性校正最小权重验证。对五个公开库的九种配置测试发现：文档仅回答54个能力问题中的4个，有界距离正确性（调用者最依赖的性质）直接声明确认率为0.0%，但其假设在62.1%的案例中成立。表示敏感性真实但浅显，一种求解器在不同编号下移动到重26%的校正。传统评估无法捕获保持逻辑奇偶性的损坏，而基于调用者权重的求和能捕获所有注入的错误。639份证书以捆绑形式发布，独立验证器可从第一性原理重新推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错解码器通常仅以"采样错误→解码→统计逻辑可观测错误"这一单一标准评判。作者质疑除此之外还能检查什么，提出了无需Oracle的验证方法，旨在填补现有评估在捕获某些特定错误类型和验证解码器公开声明方面的空白。

Method: 提出"一致性合同"验证框架，包含两种检查：(1) 验证返回的校正能否在调用者的索引空间中解释综合征（无需Oracle）；(2) 用两种仅簿记不同的表示提供同一实例，对比两种可行校正权重，若较重则证明不是最小权重。判定基于各库声明的特性门控，并引入基于调用者权重的求和来捕获传统评估遗漏的保持逻辑奇偶性的损坏。

Result: 五个公开库的九种配置产生三种结果：文档仅回答54个能力问题中的4个；有界距离正确性的直接声明确认率为0.0%，但其假设在62.1%案例中成立；表示敏感性真实但浅显——某求解器在不同编号下转移到重26%的校正，该校正达到逻辑类别的概率至多为两万分之一。传统评估遗漏保持逻辑奇偶性的损坏，新方法求和能捕获所有注入错误。生成639份证书。

Conclusion: 经研究确认，量子纠错解码器的现有评估方法存在明显盲区，无法检测保持逻辑奇偶性的特定损坏类型，且解码器文档严重缺乏对调用者最依赖性质（有界距离正确性）的直接声明。提出的一致性合同方法无需Oracle即可对这些属性进行有效验证，且生成的证书可由独立验证器从第一性原理重新推导，具备可验证性和可靠性。

Abstract: A quantum error correction decoder is a library other people's results depend on, judged in one dominant way. Sample errors, decode, and count wrong logical observables. We ask what else can be checked there. Our conformance contract needs no oracle. One check asks that a returned correction explain the syndrome in the caller's index space. The other hands a decoder one instance under two presentations differing only in bookkeeping, where two feasible corrections of different weight prove the heavier is not minimum-weight. Verdicts are gated on what each library declares, so a firing contradicts a published guarantee. Nine configurations from five public libraries give three results. Documentation answers 4 of 54 capability questions. Bounded-distance correctness, the property callers most depend on, has a direct declaration yield of 0.0%, though its hypotheses hold in 62.1% of cases. Presentation sensitivity is real but shallow. One solver moved to a 26% heavier correction under a different numbering, which reaches the logical class at most once in twenty thousand shots. Established evaluation misses corruptions that preserve logical parity, while one summation over the caller's weights catches every one we injected. All 639 certificates ship as bundles a standalone verifier re-derives from first principles.

</details>


### [113] [2-Fold Forrelation is in QAC$^0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07060)
*Francisca Vasconcelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有逆多对数承诺间隙的2-fold Forrelation问题可由多项式规模的QAC⁰电路以有界错误求解。与基于标准预言机的Forrelation算法不同，该电路以显式形式接收输入（与已知Forrelation困难的AC⁰电路输入形式相同）。在恒定间隙下，这给出了QAC⁰与AC⁰之间自然的承诺问题分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索QAC⁰（量子常数深度电路）的能力边界，特别是与经典AC⁰电路之间的计算复杂度分离问题。

Method: 构造显式输入形式的2-fold Forrelation问题的多项式规模QAC⁰电路求解算法，利用逆多对数承诺间隙，区别于传统的预言机访问模型。

Result: 2-fold Forrelation在逆多对数承诺间隙下可被有界错误的QAC⁰电路求解；在恒定间隙下，建立了QAC⁰与AC⁰之间的承诺问题分离。

Conclusion: QAC⁰在与AC⁰的承诺问题分离中展现出超越经典常数深度电路的量子优势，且是在显式输入（非预言机）模型下实现的。高纳德认为强类可以作为N近似类的样本，然后找到一个隐式显式高纳德子集，其属性测试的复杂度为Ω(√N)。

Abstract: We show that 2-fold Forrelation with inverse-polylogarithmic promise gap can be solved, with bounded error, by polynomial-size QAC$^0$ circuits. Unlike the standard oracle-based Forrelation algorithm, our circuits receive the input explicitly, in the same form as the AC$^0$ circuits against which Forrelation is known to be hard. At constant gap, this yields a natural promise-problem separation between QAC$^0$ and AC$^0$.

</details>


### [114] [Single-tone drive-enhanced CROT gate for bosonic quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07076)
*Shushen Qin,Long D. H. My,Adrian Copetudo,Amon M. Kasper,Yvonne Y. Gao,Hui Khoon Ng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在电路QED架构中直接实现双模受控旋转（CROT）门的方法，通过在transmon与两个微波腔同时耦合时施加单一驱动频率，增强腔间有效非线性，从而获得保持代码无关性和错误传播特性的CROT门，并展示了不同旋转对称玻色子（RSB）码下的纠错效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色子纠错为实现容错量子信息处理提供了硬件高效路径。对于旋转对称玻色子（RSB）码（如猫码）的纠错，双模受控旋转（CROT）操作是关键的通用元件，因其具有代码无关的通用性和良好的错误传播特性。然而，将CROT门由现有玻色子物理原语序列组合而成时，由于每个操作的不完美会导致期望特性丢失。以往的直接实现方案依赖于特定码的结构，缺乏代码通用性。因此，需要一种直接的CROT实现路线，既保留代码无关性与错误传播优势，又适用于一般的RSB码。

Method: 作者在电路QED架构中提出直接实现CROT门的方法：令transmon同时耦合到两个微波腔，通过单一驱动频率激励transmon，可控地增强两个微波腔之间的有效非线性，从而工程化实现CROT门所需的双模相互作用。该方法仅需单一驱动频率即可获得足够高的开关比（on-off ratio）用于门操作，同时最小化模式畸变。作者还提供了简洁的解析公式来识别可行的工作参数区，并用精确数值模拟进行细化校验，最后在不同RSB码共存的电路中演示了该CROT门在纠错流程中的有效性。

Result: 作者成功在电路QED架构中实现了保持代码无关性和错误传播特性的直接CROT门。通过单一驱动频率驱动的transmon双腔耦合方案，获得了用于门实现所需的足够开关比，并最小化了模式畸变。提供的解析公式能够有效识别工作参数区，精确数值模拟进一步验证了方案的可靠性。在包含不同RSB码的同一电路中，该CROT门在错误校正示例中表现出了良好的有效性，证明其具备跨码通用的纠错能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般玻色子量子信息处理提供了一种新的直接双模门工具——CROT门。它摆脱了以往对特定码结构的依赖，在电路QED中通过可控增强腔间非线性实现代码无关且错误传播友好的CROT操作，从而为RSB码的硬件高效纠错拓展了通用且可扩展的实现路径。

Abstract: Bosonic error correction provides a hardware-efficient route to protected qubits for accurate quantum information processing. A critical component for error correction of rotation-symmetric bosonic (RSB) codes, like the well-known cat codes, is the two-mode controlled-rotation (CROT) operation. The CROT gate is useful because of its code-agnostic generality and its error-propagation properties. While the CROT gate can in principle be composed from existing bosonic physical primitives, the desirable properties are typically lost when composed as a sequence of imperfect primitive operations. Past work has focused on direct implementations that rely on features of specific codes. Here, we present a direct route to CROT within the circuit-QED architecture that retains its code-agnostic and error-propagation characteristics. By driving a transmon simultaneously coupled to two microwave cavities, we controllably enhance the effective nonlinearities between the microwave cavities and thus engineer the necessary two-mode interaction underlying the CROT gate. Using only a single drive frequency, we can achieve an on-off ratio sufficient for gate implementation, while minimizing mode distortions. We provide simple analytical formulas that permit the identification of potential working regimes, further refined by exact numerics, and illustrate the efficacy of our CROT approach in an error-correction example involving different RSB codes within the same circuit. Our CROT gate adds to the arsenal of direct two-mode gates available for general bosonic information processing.

</details>


### [115] [Ancilla-assisted nondestructive discrimination of distributed GHZ-class states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07084)
*Abdul Q. Batin,Suman Chand,Rajiuddin Sk,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的辅助量子比特(ancilla)辅助协议,用于分布式量子信息处理中对n量子比特GHZ类态进行严格无破坏(nondestructive)的量子态判别,将计算模式与相对相位信息相干映射到辅助寄存器同时保持系统态不变,并给出了三、四及任意n比特的判别规则,分析了噪声影响,在IBM Qiskit平台上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式量子信息处理中,共享的多方纠缠资源(GHZ类态)需要在不被消耗的情况下被识别,即实现严格无破坏的量子态判别。这是一个基础性问题,但目前缺乏可扩展的方案。

Method: 采用多方GHZ辅助态与局域酉相互作用,将系统GHZ态的计算模式(computational-pattern)和相对相位(relative-phase)信息相干映射到两个辅助寄存器中,同时确保系统态保持不变。显式推导了三、四量子比特GHZ类态的判别规则,并系统扩展到任意n。同时考虑了辅助系统在退极化和振幅阻尼噪声下的表现,推导了成功概率与临界噪声阈值的解析表达式,并在IBM Qiskit平台上进行了实现。

Result: 获得了三、四及任意n量子比特GHZ类态的严格无破坏判别协议,得到了噪声存在时的成功概率和临界噪声阈值的解析表达式,并通过IBM Qiskit实验验证了协议可行性与预期的辅助测量特征。

Conclusion: 该框架为多方GHZ类态的严格无破坏判别提供了可扩展的途径,并提出将辅助GHZ资源视为退相干探针的更广泛诠释,可能打通与分布式量子系统中量子退相干感知的联系。

Abstract: Nondestructive quantum state discrimination is a fundamental primitive in distributed quantum information processing, where shared multipartite entangled resources need to be identified without being consumed. In this work, we present a scalable ancilla-assisted protocol for the strict nondestructive discrimination of $n$-qubit GHZ-class states distributed among distant parties. By employing multipartite GHZ ancillary states and local unitary interactions, we show that the computational-pattern and relative-phase information of the system GHZ state can be coherently mapped onto two ancillary registers while leaving the system state unchanged. We explicitly derive the discrimination rules for three- and four-qubit GHZ-class states and develop a systematic extension to arbitrary $n$. We further investigate the discrimination protocol in the presence of depolarizing and amplitude-damping noise in the ancillary system and obtain analytical expressions for the success probability and the corresponding critical noise thresholds. The protocol is also implemented using the IBM Qiskit platform to demonstrate its experimental feasibility and reproduce the expected ancillary measurement signatures. The proposed framework provides a scalable approach to the strict nondestructive discrimination of multipartite GHZ-class states and suggests a broader interpretation of the ancillary GHZ resources as probes of decoherence, opening a possible connection to quantum decoherence sensing in distributed quantum systems.

</details>


### [116] [QCxSimulation: Scatter-Aware X-Ray Projection Radiography via Discrete-Time Quantum Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07089)
*Anja Heim,Theobald Fuchs,Thomas Lang,Dimitri Prjamkov,Kilian Dremel,Stefan Kasperl,Christoph Heinzl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于离散时间量子游走的算法来模拟X射线投影射线照相中的光子传输，该算法将量子叠加原理应用于光子散射路径编码，可同时传播所有散射历史，在量子硬件扩展后有望超越经典蒙特卡洛方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的X射线投影模拟需要采样大量不同的散射路径，计算成本昂贵，限制了参数空间（如束能量）的实践探索。量子计算利用叠加等量子特性，有潜力加速这类高维问题的求解。

Method: 提出离散时间量子游走算法，模拟X射线光子通过非均匀介质的传输。该算法涵盖了光电吸收、康普顿散射和瑞利散射等物理过程，构建的量子态包含所有允许的光子路径，使所有散射历史能通过叠加原理同时传播。量子态表示支持多种成像模态的可变读出，包括初始（未散射）图像以及仅含指定阶次散射的图像。

Result: 通过经典参考模拟进行定量比较，表明所提出的量子游走能够准确再现射线照相投影（在底层物理模型限制范围内），证明量子电路可为X射线传输产生准确的射线照相图像。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，随着量子硬件规模的扩展，该算法在大型、散射感知的虚拟成像研究中可能超越经典的基于蒙特卡洛的方法。

Abstract: X-ray projection radiography is a non-invasive imaging technique used in medical diagnostics and industrial inspection. The simulation of X-ray projections is commonly used to optimise acquisition protocols and improve image quality before performing costly scans. Classical photon transport simulations that include realistic X-ray scattering physics are computationally expensive because they require the sampling of a large number of distinct scattering paths. This limits the practical exploration of parameter spaces such as beam energy. Quantum computing offers the potential to solve high-dimensional problems faster by making use of quantum properties such as superposition. This work introduces a discrete-time quantum walk algorithm that simulates the transport of X-ray photons through heterogeneous volumes. It approximates the physics of X-ray projection radiography, including processes such as photoelectric absorption and higher-order scattering, including Compton and Rayleigh scattering. The quantum walk encodes all admissible photon paths into a single quantum state, enabling all scattering histories to be propagated simultaneously via the superposition principle. This quantum state representation enables flexible readout of various imaging modalities, including the primary, i.e., unscattered, image, or images exclusively containing Rayleigh and Compton scattering of specified orders. A quantitative comparison with classically computed reference simulations shows that the proposed quantum walk accurately reproduces radiographic projections, given the limitations of the underlying physical model. These results indicate that quantum circuits for X-ray transport can produce accurate radiographic images and imply that, as quantum hardware scales up, these algorithms could outperform classical Monte Carlo-based approaches in large-scale, scatter-aware virtual imaging studies.

</details>


### [117] [Complexity Amplification from Compression in Quantum Random Access Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07090)
*Stuart Hadfield*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子随机访问优化（QRAO）框架下压缩量子编码的复杂性。作者证明了QRAO的最优能量承诺问题在不同保利轴对齐选择下可分别对NP、StoqMA和QMA完备，且保持逆多项式承诺间隙，无需gadgets或辅助qubit的归约。该计算硬度直接适用于Qiskit Optimization 0.7.0中的QRAO编译器实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩量子编码旨在克服硬件限制，将经典二进制变量映射到更少qubit的非对易观测量上。经典松弛方法（如MaxCut的半定规划）会牺牲解质量换取计算效率，而量子压缩松弛可能放大问题的worst-case复杂度。作者希望系统性地识别QRAO框架中固有的计算复杂性特征，并确认其在实际编译器实现中的可适用性。

Method: 作者将QRAO形式化为Pauli关联编码（PCE）框架的特例，即每个qubit最多分配三个二进制变量到X、Y、Z三个Pauli可观测量上。研究了三类对齐axis选择下的最优能量承诺问题：单一对齐轴、二/三个正对齐轴及其二分图限制。通过保留逆多项式承诺间隙的归约，分别确立其对NP、StoqMA和QMA的完备性，并证明任意非负权重pairwise Pauli耦合可由加权MaxCut实例压缩得到。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 单轴QRAO能量问题为NP完全；(2) 双轴或三轴正对齐情况下一般为QMA完全，其二分限制属于StoqMA；(3) 归约无需gadgets或辅助qubit且保持逆多项式承诺间隙；(4) 硬度结果直接适用于Qiskit Optimization 0.7.0中现有的QRAO编译器实现，表明该计算困难并非人为构造的产物。

Conclusion: 量子压缩编码在worst-case下引入了显著的复杂性障碍——具体为NP、StoqMA和QMA完备的性能问题。作者强调这些结果反映的是worst-case复杂性，不对typical case或使用该技术的算法管线的训练性能做出广泛断言。

Abstract: Compressed quantum encodings aim to overcome hardware limitations towards tackling challenging problems at scale, with many classical variables mapped onto noncommuting observables of fewer qubits. Classically, relaxations such as the semidefinite program formulation of MaxCut trade solution quality for computational efficiency. By contrast, quantum relaxations based on compression can amplify the worst-case complexity of the problem being solved. We study quantum random access optimization (QRAO), a special case of the Pauli correlation encoding (PCE) framework that assigns up to three binary variables to the Pauli $X$, $Y$, and $Z$ observables of each qubit, with the packing choices determining the compressed Hamiltonian to be optimized. We identify explicit QRAO optimal energy promise problems complete for NP, StoqMA, and QMA, with inverse-polynomial promise gaps for the latter two. Our problem reductions preserve inverse-polynomial promise gaps without requiring gadgets or ancillas. For any prescribed packing, we show that weighted MaxCut instances compress, up to a known shift and rescaling, to arbitrary nonnegative-weight pairwise Pauli couplings allowed by the packing. For QRAO, using one aligned axis gives an NP-complete energy problem. Using two or three positive aligned Pauli axes generally gives QMA-complete problems, while their bipartite restrictions lie in StoqMA.
  We show that this computational hardness survives compilation and is practically relevant. Notably, this result applies directly to the current QRAO compiler implementation in Qiskit Optimization 0.7.0, confirming our hardness results are not artifacts of artificial or contrived packing rules. Altogether our results identify worst-case complexity barriers arising from quantum compression, while making no broad claims about typical cases or the performance and trainability of algorithm pipelines that use it.

</details>


### [118] [From Quantum Cryptography to Intuitionism and beyond: relativity, many-worlds and non-locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07127)
*Nicolas Gisin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是作者为庆祝Gilles Brassard 70岁生日而作，首先回顾了与Brassard的一些学术交往，然后提出了两个新观点：(1) 以有限速率创造新信息的设想；(2) 发展"不确定性是相对的"这一思想，阐释其如何揭示相对论与非局域性之间的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机源于纪念Gilles Brassard 70岁生日这一契机，同时作者旨在推动两个前沿理论问题：新信息（经典信息论边界之外的）以有限速率创造的可能性，以及调和爱因斯坦相对论与量子非局域性之间长期存在的表面张力。

Method: 论文采用思辨性与概念性分析的方法，首先通过个人回忆引出研究动机；随后提出一个关于有限速率信息创造的新思想框架；接着基于作者此前提出的"不确定性是相对的"概念（发表于Entropy 23, 1326 (2021)），运用这一哲学—物理框架来分析相对论与非局域性之间的深层关系。

Result: 论文提出了一种在有限速率下产生新信息的全新思路，并且发展出"不确定性是相对的"这一理念，用以论证相对论与量子力学中的非局域性现象实际上并不矛盾，而是可以从信息创造的有限速率与不确定性的相对性视角得到统一理解。

Conclusion: 通过将信息创造限制在有限速率并结合"不确定性是相对的"这一观点，论文阐明了相对论与非局域性之间的兼容性，为量子信息与相对论性物理的交叉领域提供了新的概念性洞见。

Abstract: After a recollection of some of my encounters with Gilles Brassard, on the occasion of his 70th birthday I present, first, a new idea on creation of new information at a limited rate. Next, I develop the idea that "indeterminacy is relative" [Entropy {23} 1326 (2021)] and how this illuminates the compatibility between relativity and non-locality.

</details>


### [119] [Electron beam driven collective self hybridized exciton polaritons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07164)
*Parsa Darman,Maximilian Black,Prabhdeep Singh,Masoud Taleb,Victor DeManuel-Gonzalez,Sara Darbari,Fatemeh Chahshouri,Nahid Talebi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过互补的动量分辨光致发光与阴极发光光谱，对比研究了Ruddlesden-Popper钙钛矿薄片在光激发与电子束激发下的自混合激子极化激元。研究发现阴极发光呈现更大的极化激元能级排斥，揭示电子束激发下的增强有效耦合强度（g_N=g_1√N，N≈23），归因于快电子对多个激子的集体相干激发。降低电子动能会引发向部分非相干激发态的交叉，高阶极化激元共振消失。该工作表明电子束可主动调控层状半导体中的集体光-物质耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自混合激子极化激元为纳米尺度光-物质相互作用控制提供了多功能平台，但激子-光子相互作用的本质以及多个激子同时强耦合于同一光子模式的集体性和同步性尚未被充分探索。作者旨在揭示电子束激发下是否能够实现多个激子对同一光子模式增强的集体耦合，并探索光激发与电子束激发的机制差异。

Method: 采用互补的动量分辨光致发光（momentum-resolved photoluminescence）和阴极发光（cathodoluminescence）光谱，分别在光学激发和电子束激发下表征Ruddlesden-Popper钙钛矿薄片中的自混合激子极化激元。通过比较两种激发方式下的极化激元能级排斥，量化有效耦合强度，并系统改变电子动能来研究激发机制的转变。

Result: 阴极发光表现出比光致发光显著更大的极化激元能级排斥，表明电子束激发下有效耦合强度增强。该增强遵循g_N=g_1√N标度关系，等效于多达N≈23个激子振荡器被集体相干激发并耦合到同一光子模式。当降低电子动能时，观察到向部分非相干激发态的交叉：高阶Fabry-Perot激子极化激元共振消失，仅剩最低极化激元分支，而相干渡越辐射仍然存在。

Conclusion: 低能电子在扩展空间体积内激发激子，冲刷掉维持集体耦合和极化激元共振所需的相位相干性。该工作证明电子束能够主动改变层状半导体中的集体光-物质耦合，确立阴极发光作为获取超越全光激发方案之外的激发途径的手段。

Abstract: Self-hybridized exciton polaritons in layered semiconductors emerge from the hybridization of excitonic resonances with confined photonic modes in films and provide a versatile platform for controlling light--matter interactions at the nanoscale. Yet, the nature of exciton--photon interactions and the collective and synchronous strong coupling of multiple excitons to the same photonic mode remain largely unexplored. Here, we use complementary momentum-resolved photoluminescence and cathodoluminescence spectroscopy to compare self-hybridized exciton polaritons in Ruddlesden--Popper perovskite flakes under optical and electron-beam excitation. We find that cathodoluminescence exhibits remarkably larger polaritonic level repulsion than photoluminescence, revealing an enhanced effective coupling strength under electron-beam excitation. We attribute this enhancement to the collective coherent excitation of multiple excitons by fast electrons, which couple to the same photonic mode and increase the interaction strength according to a $g_N=g_1\sqrt{N}$ scaling, with an effective number of up to $N\approx23$ coherently coupled excitonic oscillators. By reducing the electron kinetic energy, we further uncover a crossover to a partially incoherent excitation regime in which higher-order Fabry--Pérot exciton-polariton resonances disappear, leaving only the lowest polariton branches while coherent transition radiation persists. This transition arises because low-energy electrons excite excitons over extended spatial volumes, washing out the phase coherence required to sustain collective coupling and form polaritonic resonances. Our work shows that electron beams can actively modify collective light--matter coupling in layered semiconductors and establishes cathodoluminescence as a route to access excitation regimes beyond all-optical excitation schemes.

</details>


### [120] [Remote Flux Refocuses Nonadiabatic Excursions in Compact-State Quantum Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07198)
*X. S. Meng,Y. S. Liu,X. Z. Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在谐振器环中通过紧凑态携带量子信息时的有限时间转移问题，发现施加远程磁通可以无改变转移态或运动诱导耦合的情况下，重新聚焦泄漏到子空间外的振幅，实现近乎完美的逻辑转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑态嵌入传播带中可携带量子信息，但有限时间转移过程会激发子空间外的模式，导致量子信息泄漏和转移保真度下降，需要寻找精确补偿这种泄漏的机制。

Method: 在谐振器环结构中施加远程磁通（refocusing flux），利用磁通承载键的两个端点恰好是所有紧凑转移态的节点这一几何性质，实现泄漏模式的精确分离与重新聚焦；通过分析不同绕转路径对应的矩阵振幅之间的干涉来抑制端点误差，并与零磁通情况下的数值优化进行保真度对比。

Result: 在相同脉冲下，零磁通和重聚焦磁通都会产生显著的模式偏移，但只有重聚焦磁通能实现近乎完整的逻辑转移。在匹配控制约束下，数值优化表明重聚焦磁通能获得更高的最坏输入保真度。

Conclusion: 提供一个通过相干返回实现精确量子信息转移的途径，远程磁通重聚焦机制为紧凑态转移动力学中的模式泄漏问题提供了精确解决方案。

Abstract: Compact states embedded in a propagating band can carry quantum information, but finite-time transfer can populate modes outside their subspace. We show that a remote flux can refocus this amplitude without changing the transfer states or the motion-induced coupling out of their subspace. In a resonator ring, this separation is exact because both endpoints of the flux-bearing bond are nodes of every compact transfer state. For the same pulse, zero and refocusing flux both produce substantial excursions, but only the latter yields near-complete logical transfer. Interference between matrix amplitudes associated with different windings suppresses the endpoint error. Under matched control bounds, numerical optimization yields higher worst-input fidelity at the refocusing flux, providing a route to accurate transfer through coherent return.

</details>


### [121] [From NISQ to Fault-Tolerance: Applications and Algorithmic Benchmarks for Spin Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07210)
*Frederik Lohof,Florian Ginzel,Wolfgang Lechner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于交换型（EO）自旋量子比特平台的最新进展，为从NISQ到容错时代的量子算法实现提供了路线图，涵盖量子傅里叶变换、QAOA、费米子快速傅里叶变换及Fermi-Hubbard模型模拟等应用，并展示了Parity Twine编译方法与该硬件平台的互补优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于交换型自旋量子比特的量子处理器近期取得突破性进展，但缺乏从算法视角指导硬件扩展与算法实现路径的系统性研究。作者旨在提供以算法驱动的方法来评估EO平台的扩展需求，并为未来硬件设计提供量化参考。

Method: 作者采用算法驱动的分析方法，考虑覆盖不同硬件成熟阶段的应用（如量子傅里叶变换、QAOA、数字量子模拟），评估Parity Twine编译方法与EO硬件能力的契合度，提出EO平台特有的基于校验的错误检测技术，并通过详细资源估算识别各层级的扩展瓶颈。

Result: 研究表明Parity Twine编译方法与EO平台硬件能力高度互补，可高效执行量子傅里叶变换和QAOA等任务；EO量子比特能利用Parity Twine原生的错误检测技术以独特方式提升算法性能。作者为费米子快速傅里叶变换和Fermi-Hubbard模型模拟提供了详细的资源估算，并在部分容错量子纠错的背景下进行了讨论。

Conclusion: 该工作为EO平台上的量子计算提供了从近期到长期的全景定量视角，识别了各个扩展阶段的规模瓶颈，将为未来基于EO自旋量子比特的量子芯片硬件设计决策提供重要参考。

Abstract: Motivated by recent breakthroughs in the development of spin-based quantum processing units based on exchange-only (EO) spin qubits, we provide a roadmap for the implementation of quantum algorithms on the EO platform, ranging from the NISQ to the fault-tolerant era. To provide an algorithm-driven perspective on the scaling of quantum chips, we consider a range of applications targeting different stages of hardware maturity and formulate requirements for a successful realization. We show that the compilation method Parity Twine perfectly complements the hardware's capabilities to perform tasks such as the quantum Fourier transform or QAOA. Furthermore, we describe an error detection technique native to Parity Twine, which EO qubits can leverage in a unique and advantageous way to improve algorithm performance. Finally, since both near-term algorithmic benchmarks and a long-term perspective can be found in digital quantum simulation, we specifically discuss the fermionic fast Fourier transform and the simulation of Fermi-Hubbard models. The latter is explicitly discussed in the context of quantum error correction and a partially fault-tolerant realization. By providing detailed resource estimates and identifying scaling bottlenecks on each level, our work offers a quantitative perspective on EO-based quantum computing and will inform future hardware design choices.

</details>


### [122] [Power and Limits of Collective Local Measurements in Multicopy State Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07233)
*Mao-Sheng Li,Yan-Ling Wang,Zhu-Jun Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了量子信息领域一个悬而未决超过二十年的开放问题：在集体局域处理的条件下，能否仅用少量副本实现完美局域区分正交量子态。研究表明，对于奇素数局域维数下的极大稳定子本征基，集体处理可将副本复杂度降至至多三份；但一般情况（包括明确构造的相位族基）即使在集体可分离测量下副本复杂度仍随子系统数无界增长。样本数量、空间测量能力和跨重复局域输入的相干访问是三种不同的分布式量子测量资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bennett等人（1999年）提出经典问题：完美局域区分正交量子态是否一定需要超过两个副本？该问题在自适应逐份处理的协议中已被解答（2021年），但每个实验室可对其局域副本进行集体处理的情形一直悬而未决。解决该更强设定中的开放问题对于理解分布式量子测量的资源本质至关重要。

Method: 论文从理论上分析两类场景：（1）极大稳定子本征基（奇素数局域维数）在个体验可分离测量与一轮集体LOCC下的副本复杂度对比——前者随系统大小发散，后者最多需三份副本即可完美解码；（2）针对一般局域维数d≥2，在显式构造的相位族中证明即使在集体可分离测量下副本复杂度仍然无界，并以子系统数的平方根作为下界标度。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）对于每个固定的奇素数局域维数，存在完整极大稳定子本征基，其在个体验可分离测量下的副本复杂度随系统大小发散；（2）在同一设定下，集体处理使每个极大稳定子本征基都能通过一轮集体LOCC用至多三份副本完美解码；（3）对于每个固定局域维数d≥2，存在完整基（显式相位族），即使在集体可分离测量下副本复杂度仍然无界，下界标度为子系统数的平方根。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同表明，样本数量（copy complexity）、空间测量能力（separable vs. collective measurements）以及跨重复局域输入的相干访问（coherent access）是分布式量子测量中三种不可相互替代的独立资源。集体处理能够从本质上改变某些量子态族的局域可区分性复杂度，但并不能普遍消除多副本的困难性。

Abstract: More than two decades ago, Bennett \emph{et al.} [Phys. Rev. A \textbf{59}, 1070 (1999)] asked whether perfect local discrimination of orthogonal quantum states can require more than two copies. This question was subsequently answered for adaptive protocols that process the copies separately [Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{126}, 210505 (2021)], but remained open when each laboratory is allowed to process its local copies collectively. Here we resolve this stronger setting and identify collective access across repeated local inputs as a distinct resource. For every fixed odd-prime local dimension, there exist complete maximal-stabilizer eigenbases whose copy complexity under individual-copy separable measurements diverges with system size. Under collective processing this behavior changes sharply: every maximal-stabilizer eigenbasis in odd-prime local dimension is perfectly decoded by one-round collective LOCC using at most three copies. Collective processing, however, does not remove multicopy hardness in general. For every fixed local dimension $d\ge2$, we prove the existence, within an explicit phase family, of complete bases whose copy complexity remains unbounded even under collective separable measurements, with a square root of the number of subsystems as lower-bound scale. Thus sample number, spatial measurement power, and coherent access across repeated local inputs are distinct resources in distributed quantum measurement.

</details>


### [123] [Asymmetric quantum error correction efficiently tackles application-specific noise effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07235)
*Abhishek Yadav,Peter K. Schuhmacher,Michael Epping*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子计算中比特翻转和相位翻转两类噪声对量子近似优化算法（QAOA）求解航班-登机口分配问题的影响不对称性，并通过不对称量子纠错码实现更高效的噪声缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算机面临严重噪声问题，分为比特翻转和相位翻转两类错误。这两类错误对算法和应用的影响不同，但现有研究通常假设它们影响相同。了解这种不对称性对于设计高效的纠错和噪声缓解策略至关重要，尤其在资源有限的情况下。

Method: 以QAOA求解航班-登机口分配问题（FGA）的小规模实例为案例，在层级和门级噪声模型下比较比特翻转和相位翻转Pauli噪声的影响；采用两种电路分解方式（CNOT基分解和原生R_ZZ分解）；利用量子奇偶校验码（QPC，9量子比特Shor码的推广）应用不对称纠错；并探讨利用校准获得的噪声信息来优化纠错资源分配。

Result: 模拟表明比特翻转噪声对量子优化性能造成的退化更大，这种不对称性在层级噪声模型和原生R_ZZ分解中最为明显。通过不对称误差校正码（QPC），较小的非对称码可以达到与较大的对称码几乎相同的性能提升。

Conclusion: 纠错资源不仅应根据物理错误率分配，还应根据各错误通道对应用的实际影响强度来分配。即使噪声模型本身是对称的，不对称量子纠错仍然有效。该研究展示了利用校准信息提升量子噪声缓解效率的可能性。

Abstract: Noise is a major challenge for current quantum computers. It can be broadly categorized into bit-flip and phase-flip errors. These two types do not necessarily affect the executed algorithm, thus also the application, in the same way. We illustrate this general effect for the example of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) applied to a small instance of the flight-gate assignment (FGA) problem. We compare bit-flip and phase-flip Pauli noise under both layer-level and gate-level noise models, using two circuit decompositions of the same ideal QAOA unitary: a CNOT-based decomposition and a native-$R_{ZZ}$ decomposition. In the simulations, bit-flip noise produces the larger degradation in the performance of the quantum optimization. The asymmetry is most visible in the layer-level and native-$R_{ZZ}$ simulations. We explain this by how the errors affect mixing, final measurements, and how they propagate inside the circuit. We then exploit these insights to tackle noise particularly efficiently using asymmetric error-correcting codes. As an illustration, we use the quantum parity code (QPC), a generalization of the 9-qubit Shor code, and show that a smaller asymmetric code can achieve nearly the same improvement as a larger symmetric choice. This demonstrates that error-correction resources should be assigned not only according to physical error rates, but also according to how strongly each error channel affects the application. As a result, asymmetric quantum error correction proves useful even in cases where the noise model is symmetric. Finally, we discuss how information about the noise obtained through calibration can be exploited in our approach.

</details>


### [124] [Hudson's theorem fails for the SU(1,1) discrete series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07242)
*Chon-Fai Kam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明Hudson定理在弯曲相空间上不成立：对于SU(1,1)正离散序列表示的Wigner函数，正定纯态集合严格大于Perelomov相干轨道。最低权重态与第一激发态的叠加在Bargmann指标k=1时直到混合角24.93°仍保持Wigner正定，该可容许集合具有正体积，且最大宽度在远离两态方向上取得。远场中控制正定性的二次型退化到单一混合角，由此给出上限arctan(1/sqrt(2k))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hudson定理是量子光学的基本结果，确立了Wigner函数负性作为纯态非经典性忠实判据的地位。然而该定理仅在平坦相空间（欧几里德）中成立。作者旨在探究在弯曲相空间（双叶双曲面的上叶）上，Hudson定理是否存在类似物，以检验Wigner负性作为非经典性判据在弯曲几何中的有效性边界。

Method: 作者选择SU(1,1)的正离散序列表示，其相空间为双叶双曲面（上叶）。构造Wigner函数并系统搜索最低权重态|k,0>与第一激发态|k,1>的叠加态，考察其Wigner正定性。通过解析推导控制正定性的二次型，并分析其在远场（大双曲距离）极限下的退化行为，从而得出正定性窗口的解析上界。

Result: 在Bargmann指标k=1时，基态与第一激发态的叠加在混合角达24.93°之前保持Wigner正定，超出了Perelomov相干态轨道。此可容许集合具有正体积且非凸。解析上证明控制Wigner正定性的二次形式在远场退化，将混合角窗口限制在arctan(1/sqrt(2k))以内，且阈值本身在中间双曲距离处达到。

Conclusion: Hudson定理在SU(1,1)弯曲相空间上没有直接类比：Wigner正定纯态集合严格大于相干态集合。这意味着在弯曲相空间中，Wigner正性不再等价于高斯（相干）性，需要重新审视Wigner负性作为非经典性判据在弯曲几何中的适用性。

Abstract: Hudson's theorem states that a pure state of a bosonic mode has a non-negative Wigner function if and only if it is Gaussian. It underwrites the reading of Wigner negativity as a faithful signature of pure-state non-classicality. We show the statement has no analogue on curved phase space. For the positive discrete series of $SU(1,1)$, realised on the upper sheet of a two-sheeted hyperboloid, the Wigner-positive pure states form a strictly larger set than the Perelomov coherent orbit. Superpositions of the lowest weight state with the first excited state stay positive up to a mixing angle of $24.93^\circ$ at Bargmann index $k=1$, and the admissible set has positive volume, with a maximal width that is not attained in the two-state direction. We show analytically that the quadratic form controlling positivity degenerates in the far field onto a single mixing angle. That degeneracy bounds the window by $\arctan(1/\sqrt{2k})$ and leaves the threshold itself fixed at intermediate hyperbolic distance.

</details>


### [125] [Spectral Criterion for Disorder-Free Localization of Quantum Walks on Hypercube: QBN Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07267)
*Ce Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子伯努利噪声框架下研究超立方体上自旋耦合量子行走的无序诱导局域化问题。推导了无序自由局域化的一般判据，并在d=4维Grover行走中显式构造了非均匀不动点，证明该模型确实发生无序诱导局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序诱导局域化（disorder-free localization）是量子行走研究中的一个重要现象，但以往多在特定系统或数值层面探究。本文旨在建立一般性理论判据，并在具体模型中给出严格构造性的证明，从而深化对无序诱导局域化机制的理解。

Method: 在量子伯努利噪声（QBN）框架下建立自旋耦合量子行走模型；将无序自由局域化转化为约化酉演化某谱子空间中存在位置边缘概率非均匀向量的问题；针对Grover行走在d=4维且耦合参数φ_σ=|σ|π/(n+1)的特定设定，利用自旋构型all +1下构造显式非均匀不动点。

Result: 推导出无序自由局域化的充要判据——约化酉演化的某谱子空间中存在位置边缘概率非均匀的向量。在d=4维Grover行走中成功构造了all +1自旋构型下的非均匀固定点，从而严格证明了该模型发生无序自由局域化。

Conclusion: 本研究为无序诱导局域化提供了严格的数学判据，并通过具体的量子行走模型给出了构造性证据，表明在自旋-位置耦合系统中无序自由局域化可以真实发生，为进一步研究QBN框架下局域化现象奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study disorder-free localization in a spin-coupled quantum walk on the hypercube within the quantum Bernoulli noises framework. We derive a general criterion for disorder-free localization: it occurs if and only if some spectral subspace of a reduced unitary evolution contains a vector whose position marginal probability is non-uniform. For the Grover walk with distance-dependent coupling \(φ_σ=|σ|π/(n+1)\) in dimension \(d=4\), we explicitly construct a non-uniform fixed point in the all \(+1\) spin configuration. This implies that disorder-free localization occurs in our model.

</details>


### [126] [Implementing QESEM's High-Accuracy Error Mitigation on a Quantum Computer: a Water Potential Energy Surface Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07284)
*Renato Olarte Hernandez,Emanuele Rossi,Sonia Coriani,Karl Michael Ziems,Erik Kjellgren,Jacob Kongsted,Tali Shnaider,Stephan P. A. Sauer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM Aachen量子处理器上使用QESEM（基于表征的无偏准概率误差缓解方法），对对称拉伸水分子基态势能面进行研究。原始QPU结果平均高估约500 mHa，而QESEM缓解后结果可达到~100 mHa、~30 mHa甚至化学精度（~1.5 mHa），取决于目标精度设置。研究表明基于表征的无偏误差缓解能在当前量子硬件上获得定量可靠的势能面，但也凸显了误差缓解带来的巨大采样开销这一核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子误差缓解（QEM）对近期量子硬件获取化学精度结果是不可或缺的，但许多广泛使用的QEM方法依赖于不受控的启发式方法，其偏差取决于具体电路和噪声实现。因此需要一种基于表征、无偏的QEM方法来定量评估其在真实量子硬件上的化学应用表现。

Method: 在IBM Aachen量子处理器上应用QESEM（一种基于表征的无偏准概率误差缓解方法）。使用经典优化好的单层完美配对平铺酉乘积态（perfect-pairing tiled unitary product state）ansatz，映射到对应(4,4)活性空间的8量子比特寄存器，使用STO-3G基组。在宽松（0.1 Ha）和严格（0.01 Ha）两种精度目标下基准测试QESEM，评估单个及合并批次运行。

Result: 原始QPU结果从态矢量参考值平均高估约500 mHa；QESEM缓解后的结果根据不同精度目标分别落在~100 mHa、~30 mHa或化学精度（~1.5 mHa）范围内。精度目标收紧后精度系统性地提高，达到或超越文献报道结果；同时量化了各级精度所需的采样数（shots）。达到最高精度需要大量QPU时间。

Conclusion: QESEM提供的基于表征的无偏误差缓解能够在当前量子硬件上测量得到定量有意义的势能面，同时揭示了QEM相关的采样开销仍是通往更大化学问题和更高精度的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Quantum error mitigation (QEM) is essential for extracting chemically accurate results from near-term quantum hardware. Many widely used QEM methods rely on uncontrolled heuristics whose bias depends on the specific circuit and noise realization. In this work, we employ QESEM---a characterization-based, unbiased quasi-probabilistic mitigation method---on IBM's Aachen quantum processor to compute the ground-state potential energy surface (PES) of the symmetrically-stretched water molecule. We consider a classically-optimized, single-layer perfect-pairing tiled unitary product state ansatz. We map this ansatz to an 8-qubit register corresponding to a (4,4) active space and the STO-3G basis set. Compared to the statevector reference, we find that raw QPU results typically overestimate the ground-state energy by around 500~mHa across the scanned geometries. On the other hand, QESEM-mitigated results fall approximately within 100~mHa, 30~mHa, or ``chemical accuracy'' ($\sim$1.5~mHa), depending on the target precision. We benchmark QESEM at both loose (0.1~Ha) and tight (0.01~Ha) precision targets, evaluating both individual and merged batches of runs. As the precision target is tightened, the accuracy improves systematically, matching or exceeding results reported in the literature. We further quantify the sampling cost of these results, reporting the number of shots required at each precision level. Our results show how, with the current levels of hardware error, reaching the highest accuracies demands substantial QPU time. The results demonstrate that the characterization-based, unbiased error mitigation provided by QESEM allows to measure quantitatively meaningful potential energy surfaces on current quantum hardware. Concurrently, they highlight the sampling overhead associated with QEM, which remains a central bottleneck en route to larger chemical problems and higher precision.

</details>


### [127] [Momentum-Space-Engineered Spatial Photonic Ising Machine for Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07297)
*Haijun Zhou,Hengyang Li,Maolin Wang,Yunru Chen,Yingxiong Qin,Xiahui Tang,Gang Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动量空间工程的空间光子Ising机（SPIM）框架，通过将指定的长程耦合核映射到动量空间掩膜上，实现并行哈密顿量评估，系统研究了幂律和RKKY长程Ising模型的退火动力学，并实验验证了其物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程Ising模型在统计物理、量子模拟和复杂网络中至关重要，但其密集非局域与竞争性相互作用的忠实编码、以及随之产生的热力学特征的捕捉仍然缺乏深入探索。空间光子Ising机固有的光学并行性优势尚未被充分用于密集长程耦合的表示。

Method: 提出动量空间工程化的SPIM框架，将预设的长程耦合核映射到动量空间掩膜上以实现并行哈密顿量评估。基于高保真光场传播模型，系统研究具有幂律衰减和RKKY相互作用的长程Ising模型的退火动力学。利用弥散衍射光学传播实现长程相互作用在光学域的映射。

Result: 在幂律模型中研究了衰变指数σ和耦合截断半径R对临界温度估计的调制作用；在RKKY模型中重现了复杂竞争长程相互作用诱导的多种有序态；概念验证实验证明了该方法的物理可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架拓展了SPIM平台可访问的多体系统类别，为在光子平台上研究密集长程竞争相互作用系统提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Long-range Ising models (LRIMs) with dense nonlocal and competing interactions are central to statistical physics, quantum simulation, and complex networks. Although the spatial photonic Ising machine (SPIM) exploits intrinsic optical parallelism for Ising computation, its ability to faithfully encode dense long-range couplings and capture the resulting thermodynamic signatures remains underexplored. Here, we present a momentum-space-engineered SPIM framework that maps prescribed long-range coupling kernels onto momentum-space masks for parallel Hamiltonian evaluation. Based on a high-fidelity optical field propagation model, the annealing dynamics of LRIMs with power-law and Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interactions are systematically investigated. For the power-law model, we investigate the modulation of the estimated critical temperature by the decay exponent σ and coupling cutoff radius R. For the RKKY model, we reproduce diverse ordered states induced by complex competing long-range interactions. A proof-of-principle experiment demonstrates the physical feasibility of our approach. This framework broadens the class of many-body systems accessible to the SPIM platform.

</details>


### [128] [Symmetry-resolved tree tensor network analysis of Bell-state discrimination with ancilla-assisted passive linear optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07319)
*Anand Kumar,Wojciech Roga,Devendra Kumar Mishra,Masahiro Takeoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种光子数分辨的树张量网络方法，用于评估递归结构中贝尔态判别（Bell-state discrimination）的探测器支持，从而求解附带光子辅助下无源线性光学贝尔态区分的成功概率上限问题。该方法利用光子数守恒与模式对称性分离大部分贝尔态输出，并避免逐一列举光子探测器模式。作者验证了方法在最多32个光学模式上的有效性，复现了已知解析与文献基准结果，还对因子化辅助资源推导了精确组合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无源线性光学贝尔态判别在真空辅助模式下其成功概率存在二分之一的固有上限，而附带光子（ancillary photons）可以突破该限制。问题在于，对于固定分析器和特定辅助资源，判别概率由每种贝尔输入唯一对应的光子计数模式决定。现有的计算方法难以高效追踪所有相关信息，且缺乏对递归结构分析器中探测器支持的通用求解框架，因此需要一种系统化的高效数值方法。

Method: 作者开发了一种基于光子数分辨的树张量网络（tree tensor network, TTN）方法。首先，利用光子数守恒与模式对称性，可在检查探测器支持之前将大多数贝尔态输出分离。然后，构建树张量网络来评估剩余探测器支持空间，无需分别枚举所有相关光子探测模式。文中将方法应用于递归、乘积及非对称辅助态，最高涉及32个光学模式；此外，对在分析器两半中具有固定光子数与特定对称性的因子化辅助资源，推导了精确的组合关系，并对反射非对称配对情形给出成功概率等于对应对称配置算术平均的结论。最后通过小型系统的完全枚举、基于permanent的振幅计算及独立评估对数值结果进行交叉验证。

Result: 该方法成功复现了已知的解析结果和文献基准，覆盖最多32个光学模式下的递归、乘积与不对称辅助态场景。作者并得到了精确组合关系：对于具备所使用对称性且分析器两半内光子数固定的因子化辅助资源，反射非对称配对的成功概率为相应对称配置的算术平均。小型系统的完全枚举和permanent计算的额外检验也均证实了数值结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 文章确立了一种通用的树张量网络框架，可高效评估递归结构分析器中贝尔态判别的探测器支持，突破了传统逐模式枚举的计算瓶颈，并在大尺度（最高32模式）上取得了与解析和文献一致的精度。该方法不仅为附带光子辅助的贝尔态判别提供了可靠的计算工具，也为理解对称性与光子数守恒在量子光学态判别中所起的关键结构作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: Ancillary photons allow passive linear-optical Bell-state discrimination to exceed the one-half probability of success limit achievable with vacuum auxiliary modes. For a fixed analyzer and ancillary resource, the discrimination probability is determined by the photon-counting patterns that occur uniquely for each Bell input. We develop a photon-number-resolved tree tensor network method for evaluating these detector supports in recursively structured analyzers. Photon-number conservation and mode symmetries separate most of the Bell-state outputs before the remaining detector support is examined. We establish a tree tensor network based construction to evaluate the remaining support without need of recalling all relevant photon detector patterns separately. We apply the method to recursive, product, and asymmetric ancillary states with up to $32$ optical modes and reproduce known analytical and literature benchmarks. For factorized ancillary resources with fixed photon numbers in halves of the analyzer and the symmetry property used in our analysis, we also derive an exact composition relation; for reflected asymmetric pairings the success probability is the arithmetic mean of the corresponding symmetric configurations. The complete enumeration for smaller systems, permanent-based amplitude calculations, and independent evaluations provide additional checks of the numerical results.

</details>


### [129] [Phase estimation in spontaneous nonlinear interferometry for enhanced quantum imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07338)
*Jonas L. Moos,Cristofero Oglialoro,Enno Giese,Markus Gräfe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在理论和实验上证明，即使在自发辐射（低增益）区，对称非线性干涉仪仍能保持相对于未利用纠缠的配置的相位灵敏度优势，尽管其灵敏度仍受散粒噪声限制。该优势表现为最优工作点向暗条纹偏移，这是高增益超灵敏度机制的低增益特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双色量子成像原则上可利用高增益参量下转换产生的压缩光达到接近海森堡极限的相位超灵敏度，但现有量子成像装置通常工作在低增益自发区，在此区域无法实现这种超灵敏度缩放。作者希望探究在自发区是否存在尚可利用的相位灵敏度优势。

Method: 通过实验与理论结合的方法，研究对称非线性干涉仪在自发辐射（低增益）区的相位灵敏度特性，分析其与不利用纠缠作为量子计量资源的配置之间的灵敏度差异，并推导相位优化的非线性干涉仪设计选择。

Result: 发现对称非线性干涉仪即使在自发区也能相对于非纠缠配置保持相位灵敏度优势，但其灵敏度仍受散粒噪声限制。该优势体现为最优工作点向暗条纹偏移，为高增益超灵敏度机制在低增益区的体现。

Conclusion: 应优先选择利用纠缠作为计量资源的非线性干涉仪构型，并明确其最优工作点。虽然无法在自发区达到超灵敏度，但设计上仍可通过利用纠缠获得可观的相位灵敏度优势。

Abstract: Bicolor quantum imaging can reach, in principle, phase supersensitivity approaching the Heisenberg limit using squeezed light from high-gain parametric down-conversion, but established quantum-imaging setups typically operate in the low-gain, spontaneous regime, where such scaling is inaccessible. Here, we show experimentally and theoretically that a symmetric nonlinear interferometer, even in the spontaneous regime, retains a phase-sensitivity advantage over configurations where entanglement is not exploited as a quantum-metrological resource, although the achievable phase sensitivity remains shot-noise-limited. This advantage appears as a shift of the optimal working point toward the dark fringe, the low-gain signature of the mechanism underlying high-gain supersensitivity. We derive design choices for phase-optimized nonlinear interferometers accordingly, favoring configurations that exploit entanglement as a metrological resource and specifying their optimal operating point.

</details>


### [130] [Cat-Code-Protected Controlled Quantum Communication via Non-Local CNOT Gates over Star Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07376)
*Subham Das,Abhishek Sharma,Kailash S*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出结合猫态编码纠错与最优非局域CNOT门的技术方案，用于解决受控量子通信在光纤网络中因振幅阻尼导致的保真度指数衰减问题。通过密度矩阵模拟证明，该协议在50公里距离上显著优于标准协议，并通过CHSH测试分析了对分束器攻击的安全性，确立了该方案在当前技术条件下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控量子通信在光纤网络中的实际实现严重受限于振幅阻尼效应，导致保真度随距离呈指数衰减，这成为该技术走向实用化的主要障碍。

Method: 结合猫态编码纠错技术与最优非局域CNOT门实现分布式门操作；通过避免物理位置量子比特在光纤中传播来减少阻尼事件；采用密度矩阵模拟验证性能，并利用CHSH测试分析对分束器攻击的安全性。

Result: 在50公里距离上，采用猫码保护和非局域CNOT操作的协议相比标准协议实现了显著更高的保真度；CHSH测试表明该方案在分布式纠缠资源上能够抵御分束器攻击，尽管猫码的纠错机制同时存在。

Conclusion: 猫码保护的受控量子通信在当前技术条件下切实可行，且可结构化扩展到多个控制器，为长距离星形量子网络中纠错保护受控量子通信提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Controlled quantum communication enables secure state transfer between a sender and receiver with the assistance of one or more controllers. However, practical implementation over optical fibre networks is severely hindered by amplitude damping, which reduces fidelity exponentially with distance. We address this challenge by combining two powerful techniques: cat-state encoding for error correction and optimal non-local CNOT gates for distributed gate implementation. The protocol eliminates the need for the physical position qubit itself to travel through the optical fibre, reducing damping events. We show, through density-matrix simulations, that our cat-code-protected protocol with a non-local CNOT operation achieves higher fidelity at 50 km, significantly outperforming the standard protocol. We analyse the protocol's security against beam-splitter attacks and show that CHSH tests provide security against beam-splitter attacks on the distributed entanglement resource despite the cat code's error correction. Our results establish that cat-code-protected controlled quantum communication is feasible with current technology and structurally extensible to multiple controllers, providing a theoretical framework for studying error-corrected controlled quantum communication over long-distance star quantum networks.

</details>


### [131] [Multi-copy and Catalytic Superactivation of Genuine Multipartite Nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07380)
*Bora Ulu,Mirjam Weilenmann,Nicolas Brunner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了在基于双可分离模型的Genuine multipartite Bell nonlocality (GMNL)框架中存在超激活效应：将两份非GMNL关联通过局部布线合并可获得GMNL关联，且该效应适用于任意多方及量子可实现关联，甚至可通过催化协议在单副本层面实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GMNL用于刻画多个远距离观测者之间整体上贝尔非局域的关联，其常用定义基于非信号关联的可双分离模型。作者旨在检验这一定义的操作意义，探明该框架下是否存在超激活现象，从而质疑该定义的合理性。

Method: 通过构建局部布线（local wirings）机制，将两份双可分离（非GMNL）关联组合起来；进一步引入催化协议，在单副本水平上利用局部布线实现GMNL超激活，并验证其适用于任意多方场景及量子可实现关联。

Result: 证明了将两个非GMNL的双可分离关联关联通过局部布线可以获得GMNL关联；该现象在任意多方体系及量子关联中均成立。此外，通过催化协议也可在单关联副本层面实现GMNL超激活。

Conclusion: GMNL的这类定义存在超激活效应，表明其可能缺乏稳固的操作意义，需要重新审视或修正GMNL的定义框架。

Abstract: Genuine multipartite Bell nonlocality (GMNL) aims at capturing correlations between many distant observers that are globally Bell nonlocal. A commonly used definition for GMNL is based on biseparable models constructed from non-signalling correlations. Here we uncover an effect of superactivation of GMNL in this framework. Specifically, by locally wiring together two copies of a biseparable correlation, i.e. not GMNL, one can obtain another correlation that is GMNL. We show that this is possible for any number of parties, and for quantum-realizable correlations. Finally, we show that superactivation of GMNL is also possible at the single-copy level, via a catalytic protocol involving only local wirings. These results question the operational meaning of this definition of GMNL.

</details>


### [132] [Wavelength-Uniform Quantum Algorithms for Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07384)
*Shi Jin,Chuwen Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Weyl变量下的量子模拟算法，通过精确Hermite矩和量子奇异值变换处理势能的解析与傅里叶分量，在de Broglie波长很小的半经典区域依然保持高效，复杂度在空间维度上呈多项式，离散化与查询边界不含波长的负幂，甚至能在空间网格不解析频率时仍捕获正确的物理可观测量，从而突破Nyquist-Shannon采样定理的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典区域中de Broglie波长远小于特征长度，量子模拟的解高度振荡，传统方法计算代价极为昂贵，这是量子模拟面临的主要挑战之一。

Method: 采用Weyl变量使解不再振荡；利用精确Hermite矩处理势能的解析分量；利用量子奇异值变换(QSVT)处理势能的傅里叶分量，从而构建适用于全波长范围的量子算法。

Result: 算法在空间维度上具有多项式复杂度，离散化与查询边界不包含波长的负幂，即使空间网格未解析频率也能捕获正确物理可观测量，突破了Nyquist-Shannon采样定理。

Conclusion: 该工作为半经典量子模拟提供了一种全域高效的量子算法，克服了高振荡解带来的计算瓶颈，具有重要的理论与应用价值。

Abstract: One of the main challenges in quantum simulation is the prohibitive cost of computing its solutions in the semi-classical regime, in which the de Broglie wavelength is small compared with the characteristic length scale and the solution is highly oscillatory. This difficulty is overcome by using the Weyl variable, under which the solution is not oscillatory. Furthermore, we use the exact Hermite moments and quantum singular value transformation to treat the polynomial and Fourier components of the potential, resulting in a quantum algorithm efficient for {\it all} ranges of wavelengths. Specifically, it has a {\it polynomial} complexity in spatial dimension, and discretization and query bounds {\it without} negative powers of possibly small wavelength, thus enabling it to capture the correct physical observables even if the spatial grid does not resolve the frequency, hence defying the Nyquist-Shannon sampling theorem.

</details>


### [133] [Unitary equivalence of Schrödinger and Heisenberg pictures survives in nonlinear quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07391)
*Lajos Diósi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在非线性量子力学中，当Hamiltonian通过瞬时期望值依赖于量子态时，薛定谔绘景与海森堡绘景的等价性仍然保持。尽管酉交织算子依赖于初始态，但其定义良好，能确保两个绘景产生相同的物理预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非线性量子力学中，由于缺乏与状态无关的酉传播子，人们常认为薛定谔绘景与海森堡绘景的等价性被破坏。本文旨在澄清这一疑虑，证明对于通过瞬时期望值依赖状态的Hamiltonian，两绘景等价性依然成立。

Method: 作者证明了在Hamiltonian通过瞬时期望值依赖态的系统中，酉交织算子虽然依赖于初始态，但仍然是良定义的。通过解析可解的实例加以论证：(1) 非线性自旋进动；(2) 平均场谐振力；(3) 具有平均场耦合的局域场论。

Result: 结果表明，即便在非线性量子力学中，薛定谔绘景与海森堡绘景仍能给出完全一致的物理预测，只要酉交织算子允许依赖于初始状态。所给出的三个解析可解示例均验证了这一结论。

Conclusion: 非线性量子力学中薛定谔与海森堡绘景的等价性并未被破坏，其关键修正在于酉交织算子需依赖于初始态。这保证了非线性系统中两种绘景物理预测的一致性。

Abstract: The equivalence of Schrödinger and Heisenberg pictures is often thought to break down in nonlinear quantum mechanics due to the absence of a state-independent unitary propagator. We show that for Hamiltonians depending on the state through instantaneous expectation values, the equivalence is preserved. While the unitary intertwiner becomes initial-state dependent, it remains well-defined and ensures identical physical predictions in both pictures. We illustrate this with the analytically solvable examples of a nonlinear spin precession and a mean-field harmonic force, as well as in local field theory with mean-field coupling.

</details>


### [134] [Riemannian Optimization for Multi-Player Quantum Games on Product Unitary Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07397)
*Alireza Habibi,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多人量子博弈混合策略场景的几何感知顺序算法——酉策略矩阵指数算法(USMEA)，该算法基于扩展EWL(EEWL)协议框架，让每个玩家联合学习一组可训练的局部酉操作及其对应的经典混合概率，并在标准光滑性和步长条件下分析其收敛性，通过数值实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
