Motivation: 量子博弈论将量子原理引入经典博弈论，EWL量子博弈通过将经典囚徒困境转化为量子囚徒困境来解决经典困境并实现更高收益的合作均衡。然而，现有研究对多人量子博弈混合策略场景的学习与优化方法探讨不足，需要一种能够将经典优化方法系统性地整合进量子策略互动设计与分析的算法。

Method: 首先讨论用于多人量子博弈混合策略的扩展EWL(EEWL)协议，其中每个玩家控制一组酉算子作为量子动作，并采用这些动作上的经典混合策略，收益定义为作用于共享量子态上的厄米奖励算符的期望值。随后提出酉策略矩阵指数算法(USMEA)，这是一种几何感知的顺序算法，每个玩家联合学习一组可训练的局部酉动作及相关经典混合概率，充当多智能体量子决策系统的学习与控制层。

Result: 在标准光滑性和步长条件下分析了USMEA的收敛性质，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，经典优化方法可以被系统性地整合到工程化量子策略互动的设计与分析中，为多人量子博弈混合策略场景提供了有效的学习与优化框架。

Abstract: Quantum game theory is an extension of classical game theory that uses quantum principles in game theory. The Eisert-Wilkens-Lewenstein (EWL) quantum game is an early example of the two-player classical Prisoner's Dilemma transformed into a quantum Prisoner's Dilemma. In the EWL game, the players choose pure quantum strategies represented by unitary matrices. This extension can resolve the classical dilemma by enabling cooperative equilibrium with higher payoff. In this paper, we first discuss the Extended EWL (EEWL) for multiplayer quantum games with mixed strategies. In EEWL, each player controls a set of unitary operators as quantum actions and uses a classical mixed strategy over these actions. The payoffs are defined as expectation values of Hermitian reward operators acting on a shared quantum state, which is generated and measured according to the EEWL protocol. We then propose the Unitary Strategy Matrix Exponential Algorithm (USMEA), a geometry-aware sequential algorithm for the EEWL mixed-strategy setting, in which each player jointly learns a trainable set of local unitary actions and the associated classical mixing probabilities. Thereby it acts as a learning-and-control layer for multi-agent quantum decision systems. We analyze the convergence properties of USMEA under standard smoothness and step-size conditions and validate the theory with numerical experiments. These results show how classical optimization methods can be systematically integrated into the design and analysis of engineered quantum strategic interactions.

</details>


### [135] [Photonic Time-Delayed Quantum Extreme Learning Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07416)
*Ekaterina Protsenko,Caterina Vigliar,Francesco Da Ros*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并数值模拟了一种基于时间延迟非线性干涉仪中非线性相互作用与光子数分辨检测的光子量子极端学习机（QELM）。通过二元分类非线性双月基准任务证明了该储备池的计算能力，并发现基于光子数关联的紧凑特征集可在降低测量复杂度的同时保持分类精度，且在损耗影响下系统能通过权重重分配维持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种实验上易于实现的光子量子极端学习机平台，利用时间延迟非线性干涉仪和光子数分辨检测来实现非线性信息处理，同时考察其在实际损耗条件下的鲁棒性和测量复杂度优化潜力。

Method: 采用时间延迟非线性干涉仪作为非线性储备池，结合光子数分辨检测构建QELM；通过数值模拟二元"双月"非线性分类基准来评估储备池计算能力；比较不同特征提取方案（包括学习的输出权重和替代量子读出）的分类性能；研究损耗条件下权重的自适应再分配机制。

Result: 数值模拟表明该QELM能成功完成非线性双月问题的二元分类；基于光子数关联的紧凑特征集能在降低测量复杂度的情况下保持分类准确率；在损耗存在时，系统通过将学习权重自然重新分布到更多可用特征上，维持了分类性能。

Conclusion: 时间延迟光子系统实现QELM具备鲁棒性，能够承受损耗效应，同时通过特征选择优化测量复杂度，是一个有前景且实验上可及的光子量子极端学习机平台。

Abstract: We propose and numerically simulate a photonic quantum extreme learning machine (QELM) based on non-linear interactions in a time-delay non-linear interferometer with photon-number-resolving detection. We theoretically demonstrate the reservoir's computational capability by binary classification of the non-linear two moons benchmark. Analysis of the learned output weights and alternative quantum readouts shows that compact feature sets based on photon-number correlations preserve classification accuracy while potentially reducing measurement complexity. We further show that the QELM maintains its classification performance under the effect of losses by naturally redistributing the learned weights across a larger set of available features. These results demonstrate the resilience of the time-delay photonic implementation and highlight its potential as an experimentally accessible QELM platform.

</details>


### [136] [High-purity fluorescence photon bundle emission from two separate emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07454)
*Zhicai Chen,Jun Xu,Deyi Kong,Xiangming Hu,Fei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从两个空间分离的二能级发射体中生成高纯度荧光双光子束的方案，其关联激发由强驱动的辅助发射体介导。与传统玻色模式方法不同，两个目标发射体的有限激发空间从本质上排除了高激发流形，从而消除了与不良高激发态相关的杂质光子。论文还解析表征了离共振单激发态的残余布居，并表明抑制相应泄漏可提高光子束纯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现高纯度量子光源是量子信息处理的关键需求。传统基于玻色子模式的方法难以避免高激发流形产生的杂质光子，这限制了光子束的纯度。寻找一种能够从本质上抑制杂质光子产生的方案，是推动量子光源实用化的核心动因。

Method: 利用强驱动辅助发射体作为中介，耦合两个空间分离的二能级发射体，实现它们之间的关联激发。由于二能级系统的有限激发空间（每个发射体最多一个激发量子数），系统固有地排除高激发流形，从而消除相应杂质光子。此外，通过解析分析离共振单激发态的残余布居，识别泄漏通道并加以抑制，以进一步提升光子束纯度。

Result: 该方案能够生成高纯度荧光双光子束。解析表征表明，离共振单激发态的残余布居是影响纯度的主要因素，抑制该泄漏可以进一步提升光子束的纯度。数值上验证了纯度可在有限激发空间条件下显著改善。

Conclusion: 该方案基于二能级发射体的有限激发空间和辅助发射体介导的关联激发，为构建高纯度量子光源提供了一条可行途径，在量子信息处理领域具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: We propose a scheme for generating high-purity fluorescence two-photon bundles from two spatially separate two-level emitters, whose correlated excitation is mediated by a strongly driven auxiliary emitter. Unlike bosonic-mode-based approaches, the finite excitation space of the two target emitters intrinsically excludes higher-excitation manifolds, and thus eliminates impurity photons associated with undesired higher-excitation states. We further analytically characterize the residual population of off-resonant single-excitation states and show that suppressing the corresponding leakage improves photon bundle purity. Our scheme offers a feasible pathway for constructing high-purity quantum light sources with promising applications in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [137] [Stationary states for a particle in a box with slanted walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07505)
*Nivaldo A. Lemos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有倾斜壁面的无限深势阱中的量子力学问题，通过Airy函数求解能量本征值，分析了能谱的渐近行为、Bohr对应原理的满足程度以及态的限制（束缚）程度，并发现了对称倾斜势阱中一个非平凡的现象：超过某个临界斜率后基态不再是受限最弱的本征态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准粒子限制在无限深方势阱或谐振子势中的情况已为物理学生熟知，但具有倾斜壁面的势阱涉及非标准的数学结构（Airy函数），能提供超出常规教材的新物理现象（如基态受限程度反转），同时帮助学生接触Airy函数这一不常出现在本科物理课程中的数学工具。

Method: 将Schrödinger方程转化为Airy微分方程，能量本征值由含第一类Airy函数的超越方程确定；通过图形法和数值法求解允许能级；利用Airy函数的基本性质（零点渐近行为）对能谱渐近性质进行论证；定义态的限制程度为在经典禁区找到粒子的概率，并比较不同本征态及与谐振子的对比。

Result: 数值和图形分析表明大量子数下相邻能级间距趋于零，该渐近行为由Airy函数零点性质严格确认，且Bohr对应原理的满足比标准方势阱和谐振子更精确。对于对称倾斜情况（左右壁斜率相等），存在一个临界斜率，超过该斜率后基态不再是受限最弱的态，这是一个出乎意料的结果。

Conclusion: 倾斜壁无限势阱问题不仅展现了丰富的物理特性（能谱渐近结构、Bohr对应原理的更精确实现、基态受限性质的意外反转），还是让物理本科生熟悉Airy函数的理想教学素材。

Abstract: The quantum mechanical problem of a particle in a infinite potential well with slanted walls is studied. The energy eigenvalues are determined by a transcendental equation involving the Airy function of the first kind. Two limiting cases are discussed. Next, the allowed energies are found by graphical and numerical methods. The graphical analysis hints that for large quantum numbers the consecutive energy levels get arbitrarily close together. This asymptotic behavior of the energy spectrum actually holds, as shown by means of an intuitive argument and confirmed by resorting to basic properties of Airy functions. In a sense, Bohr's correspondence principle is more accurately fulfilled than in the cases of the standard particle in a box or the harmonic oscillator. A state is regarded the more confined the less the probability of finding the particle in the classically forbidden region. For some stationary states the confinement degree is calculated and a comparison is made with the harmonic oscillator, for which the ground state is the least confined of all stationary states. In the symmetric case --- right and left walls of equal slope --- something unexpected happens: beyond a certain critical slope the ground state is no longer the least confined eigenstate. Over and above its interesting physical features, this problem provides students with the opportunity to get acquainted with Airy functions, which do not belong to the standard mathematical repertoire of physics undergraduates.

</details>


### [138] [Achieving the Quantum Limits with Twin-Field Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07517)
*Yaohua Li,Fan Yang,Klaus Mølmer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子实验中连续测量的信息提取难题，提出了一种"双场传感器"（TFS）方案：通过将原始传感器的信号与一个合适的系统副本进行联合测量，可以在广泛的传感器类别中饱和量子Fisher信息界。研究表明，从二能级原子到混合量子Rabi模型等多种实现中，简单的光子计数或零差探测即可达到理论量子极限，并进一步证明TFS在双参数联合传感中也能达到量子Fisher信息矩阵所定义的量子极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续测量在量子实验中扮演基础角色，但如何从连续探针信号中理解和提取最大可能的信息仍然是重大挑战。量子Fisher信息界定义了传感精度的理论极限，但实际测量方案往往难以饱和该界，需要有普适的测量策略来解决这一难题。

Method: 作者提出TFS方案，核心思想是同时测量原始传感器的信号与其一个合适系统副本的信号。通过这种联合测量策略，理论上证明对于一大类传感器可以饱和量子Fisher信息界。作者从物理机制层面阐明TFS的工作原理，并在多种具体系统中（二能级原子、混合量子Rabi模型等）验证其性能。

Result: 在所有考察的实现方案中，简单的光子计数或零差检测即可饱和理论量子极限。此外，作者进一步证明了TFS在双参数联合传感场景中，可以达到由量子Fisher信息矩阵量化的量子极限。

Conclusion: TFS提供了一种通用的量子测量策略，能够在广泛的传感器类别中通过简单的实际探测手段（光子计数或零差检测）饱和量子Fisher信息界，包括单参数和双参数联合传感场景，为量子增强传感和信息提取提供了新的理论框架和实用方案。

Abstract: Continuous measurements play a fundamental role in quantum experiments, yet understanding and extracting the maximal possible information in a continuous probe signal remains a significant challenge. In this work, we show that for a wide class of sensors, the quantum Fisher information bound can be saturated through a combined measurement of signals from the original sensor and a suitable system copy. We elucidate the physical mechanism of such a twin-field sensor (TFS) and study its performance across diverse implementations, ranging from two-level atoms to a hybrid quantum Rabi model. In all of these cases, we find that simple photon counting or homodyne detection saturates the theoretical quantum limit. We further prove that the TFS achieves the quantum limits for the joint sensing of pairs of parameters as quantified by the quantum Fisher information matrix.

</details>


### [139] [Toward Fault-Tolerant Variational Optimization: QAOA under [[4,2,2]] Error Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07537)
*Matteo Robert Child,Emanuele Dri,Giacomo Vitali,Chiara Vercellino,Alberto Leporati*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于$[[4,2,2]]$误差检测码的部分容错QAOA实现，用于求解方形图上的Max-Cut问题。核心贡献是提出一种新的辅助量子比特介导的逻辑$R_{ZZ}$门，实现了不同$[[4,2,2]]$块中量子比特间的相互作用。研究在五种噪声模型下、采用全连接和网格路由两种连接方式，通过Cirq和qsimcirq框架并行CPU执行评估了未编码和编码电路。稳定子测量的后选择始终能提高采样最优比特串的概率，其中五次测量效果最佳。结果表明误差检测是改善变分量子算法质量的实用近期策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法（如QAOA）在实际量子硬件上易受噪声影响，而完全容错量子计算在近期内难以实现。作者旨在探索利用轻量级误差检测码（而非完整纠错码）来提升近中期变分量子算法的输出质量，为无法等待完整容错实现的近期应用提供可行的降噪途径。

Method: 采用$[[4,2,2]]$误差检测码对QAOA电路进行编码，关键创新是设计了一个辅助量子比特介导的逻辑$R_{ZZ}$门，以实现在不同$[[4,2,2]]$块之间量子比特的纠缠操作。在五种噪声模型下，分别评估未编码与编码电路，并对比全连接与网格路由两种硬件拓扑，实验基于Cirq和qsimcirq框架并行CPU执行，利用稳定子测量的后选择技术来筛选有效结果。

Result: 在全部测试的噪声模型下，对稳定子测量进行后选择均能提升采样到最优比特串的概率，其中进行五次测量时获得的改善最为显著。这验证了编码方案在不同噪声环境下的鲁棒性以及后选择策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 误差检测作为一种实用且可扩展的近期策略，能够有效提升变分量子算法的输出质量；本文提出的辅助量子比特介导的逻辑$R_{ZZ}$门为基于$[[4,2,2]]$码的多块量子算法实现提供了关键的模块化构建方案。

Abstract: We present a partially fault-tolerant implementation of QAOA based on the $[[4,2,2]]$ error-detection code, targeting the Max-Cut problem on a square graph. Our main contribution is a novel ancilla-mediated logical $R_{ZZ}$ gate enabling interactions between qubits in different $[[4,2,2]]$ blocks. We evaluate unencoded and encoded circuits under five noise models, with both all-to-all and grid-routed connectivity, using the Cirq and qsimcirq frameworks with parallel CPU execution. Post-selection on stabilizer measurements consistently improves the probability of sampling optimal bitstrings, with five measurements providing the strongest benefit. These results support error-detection as a practical near-term strategy for improving the quality of variational quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [140] [Discrete Dirac equation on quantum graphs: A model of a Dirac particle in a branched lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07539)
*Mashrab Akramov,Carsten Trunk,Davron Matrasulov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了图（Graphs）上带离散边的一维定态离散Dirac方程，同时考察图的连续与离散表述，并以三边星形图为例，推导了本征态和能谱的精确解，与连续情形对比结果吻合良好，为纳米结构与量子器件设计中的相对论量子输运提供了系统性分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将相对论量子力学（Dirac方程）推广到分支结构（图/网络）上是一个具有物理意义的问题，尤其对于纳米结构和量子器件的设计。已有的连续图方法存在局限性，需要建立一个离散化的系统性框架来处理离散边上的Dirac方程，并与连续情形进行对比验证。

Method: 对一维定态离散Dirac方程在图结构上进行建模，分别考虑连续图和离散图两种表述形式。以三边星形图为具体算例，推导本征态和能谱的精确解，并与连续情形的解析结果进行数值对比。

Result: 获得了三边星形图上离散Dirac方程的本征态与能谱的精确解，并以表格形式与连续情形结果进行比较，显示出良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 该离散化方法为分析分支结构上的相对论量子输运提供了一套系统性的理论框架，对纳米结构和量子器件设计具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional time-independent discrete Dirac equation on graphs with discrete edges. Both continuous and discrete formulations of the graphs are considered, with particular focus on star graphs with three edges as a simple example. Exact solutions for eigenstates and spectra are derived and compared with the continuum case, showing good agreement in tabulated results. This approach offers a systematic framework for analyzing relativistic quantum transport on branched structures relevant to nanostructures and quantum device design.

</details>


### [141] [Geometry Dependence of Error Thresholds in Two-Dimensional Toric Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07579)
*D. Lessing,A. Langheld,C. Krämer,J. A. Koziol,K. P. Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过复本交换蒙特卡洛模拟，在Nishimori线上系统地计算了二维环形码在方、蜂窝、三角、骰子和kagome五种晶格上的最大似然最优错误阈值，发现最优阈值呈现围绕自对偶方格的双重性分裂，且晶格的平均配位数对阈值影响最大，但顶点和面元的详细排列也起重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究探讨了一般晶格几何在次优解码器（如最小权完美匹配）下的错误阈值，但随着近最优解码器的快速发展，解析最大似然意义上的终极码容量十分必要，需要明确晶格几何如何影响二维环形码的错误阈值。

Method: 将存在比特翻转或相位翻转错误的环形码映射到二维随机键伊辛模型（RBIM），使用复本交换蒙特卡洛模拟确定RBIM的临界行为，并在Nishimori线上对五种不同晶格（方、蜂窝、三角、骰子、kagome）计算最优（最大似然）错误阈值。

Result: 发现对偶晶格对之间的最优阈值呈现围绕自对偶方格的特征性分裂，这与次优解码器下观察到的定性行为一致；相位翻转阈值在给定晶格上等价于其对偶晶格的比特翻转阈值（格对偶性）。平均配位数对阈值影响最大，但晶格顶细节（顶点和面元的排列方式）对其精确值也有显著影响。

Conclusion: 晶格几何以两种尺度影响环形码的最优错误阈值：平均配位数决定阈值的主导水平，而顶点与面元的具体拓扑排列则在细微层面调节阈值的精确数值，二者共同决定了最大似然解码下各晶格的码容量差异。

Abstract: We investigate how the lattice geometry influences the error thresholds of two-dimensional toric codes. In the presence of bit- or phase-flip errors, the toric code maps onto the two-dimensional random-bond Ising model (RBIM). We determine the critical behaviour of the RBIM using replica-exchange Monte Carlo simulations. While previous studies have explored thresholds for general lattice geometries under suboptimal decoders such as minimum-weight perfect matching, the rapid development of near-optimal decoders makes resolving the ultimate, maximum-likelihood code capacity relevant. We compute these optimal error thresholds on the Nishimori line for the square, honeycomb, triangular, dice, and kagome lattices. Owing to lattice duality, the phase-flip threshold on a given lattice is equivalent to the bit-flip threshold on its dual. We find that the optimal thresholds of dual-lattice pairs display a characteristic duality-driven splitting around the self-dual square-lattice, mirroring the qualitative behaviour observed for suboptimal decoders. While the average coordination number has the strongest impact on the error threshold, our results demonstrate that the detailed arrangement of vertices and plaquettes also plays a significant role in determining its precise value.

</details>


### [142] [Topology Obstructs Pure Foundation Neural Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07591)
*Timothy Heightman,Elena Orlova,Philip Mantrov,Aleksei Ustimenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在自旋-1/2系统中，以哈密顿量参数为条件的纯态基态神经量子态模型（如神经网络波函数）存在拓扑障碍——对于任何具有非平凡基态丛的有能隙哈密顿量族，任何连续的归一化态矢量模型在某个参数点上与真实基态的重叠度为零，导致能量误差至少为一个谱间隙Δ。作者进一步证明该障碍同样适用于简并基态流形、时间动力学和具有混合时空拓扑的周期系统，并指出算子值模型（而非态矢量模型）可以规范地规避这些障碍，暗示基础神经量子态在表征上需要结构性改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型用于自旋-1/2系统基态是一种有前景的方法（涵盖量子化学到相图识别等应用），但目前几乎所有此类模型都是纯态模型（以哈密顿量参数为条件），作者想检验这种表征方式是否存在根本性的拓扑限制。

Method: 作者利用拓扑学/纤维丛理论分析：将纯态神经网络模型视为定义在哈密顿量参数空间上的连续归一化态矢量映射。通过构造非平凡的基态纤维丛（ground-state bundle），证明连续态矢量场无法全局非零地嵌入该丛——这与拓扑学中"连续矢量场必有零点"（如毛球定理推广）的经典结论相关。作者还推广了该分析到简并基态、含时演化和周期系统，并在单/双量子比特系统上给出具体示例验证。

Result: 1) 对于任何具有非平凡基态丛的有能隙哈密顿量族，任何连续归一化纯态模型都会在某个参数点与基态零保真度；2) 在该点附近能量误差至少为O(Δ)谱间隙量级；3) 该no-go结果对简并基态流形、含时动力学、时空混合拓扑周期系统同样成立；4) 该拓扑障碍会在保真度磁化率（fidelity susceptibility）上产生尖峰，形成没有相变却看似相变的数值伪特征；5) 算子值模型（operator-valued models）可以规范地规避这些障碍并保留拓扑信息。

Conclusion: 纯态（态矢量）表征方式对基础神经量子态模型存在内在的拓扑限制（no-go定理），而算子值模型是克服此障碍的结构性必要方向，这为神经量子态基础模型的架构设计提供了重要的理论指导。

Abstract: Foundation models for ground states in spin-1/2 systems are a promising method for problems ranging from quantum chemistry to identifying new phase diagrams. Nearly all such models are currently pure-states that condition on the Hamiltonian's parameters, whose Monte Carlo samples give energy estimates according to the variational principle. In this contribution, we show that this representation is topologically obstructed. For any gapped Hamiltonian family whose ground-state bundle is non-trivial, every continuous normalized state-vector model has zero fidelity with the ground state at some parameter value in the Hamiltonian family. For that value, the energy is at least one spectral gap, $Δ$, with an $O(Δ)$ gap in an open-neighbourhood of that point. We show that this is a sufficient no-go also in the case of degenerate ground-state manifolds, time dynamics, and periodic systems with mixed space-time topology, demonstrating these obstructions on one- and two-qubit systems. We discuss how this causes a spike in the fidelity susceptibility, giving a numerical signature of a phase-transition where there is none. We then show that operator-valued models canonically avoid these obstructions and preserve topological information, implying a structural necessity in representation for foundation neural quantum states.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum Approximate Counting with Bernoulli Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07604)
*Chengshen Gao,Yongzhen Xu,Lvzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究带伯努利oracle的量子计数问题，即给定m个伯努利分布（未知偏差p_i）和间隙参数Δ，估计正分布（p_i≥1/2+Δ）所占比例ρ。文中证明了查询复杂度的上界为Õ(√ρ/(Δε)+1/(Δ√ε))，下界为Ω(√ρ/(Δε))，实现了相对经典采样的二次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子计数算法假设成员关系oracle能精确标记每个元素，但实际场景中标签往往是概率性的（如噪声环境）。为弥合理论与实际应用的差距，需要研究带噪声或概率性oracle下的量子计数问题，分析其查询复杂度及其与理想情形的差距。

Method: 算法层面：首先利用量子奇异值变换（QSVT）在保持分布叠加态不一致的前提下相干放大偏差间隙；然后采用两阶段自适应振幅估计来估计ρ。下界层面：提出了量子adversary方法在布尔-平均情形方向上的新组合定理，用于推导匹配的下界。

Result: 给出上界Õ(√ρ/(Δε)+1/(Δ√ε))和近匹配下界Ω(√ρ/(Δε))，均优于经典采样复杂度Θ(ρ/Δ²ε²)。对于常数间隙Δ=Θ(1)（对应于每个查询以常数概率返回正确标签的有界误差oracle），上界和下界分别特化为Õ(√ρ/ε+1/√ε)和Ω(√ρ/ε)，完整刻画了该情形下的查询复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文首次刻画了带伯努利（有界误差）oracle的量子计数查询复杂度，证明了在概率性标签场景下量子算法依然能获得相对经典方法的二次加速，填补了量子计数在噪声标签场景下的理论空白。

Abstract: Quantum counting is a fundamental quantum algorithm that estimates the fraction of marked elements using a membership oracle, achieving a quadratic speedup over classical sampling. The membership oracle, however, assumes exact labeling of each element, but this assumption fails when the labels are inherently probabilistic. We study quantum counting with \emph{Bernoulli oracles}, where given $m$ Bernoulli distributions with unknown biases $p_1,\dots,p_m$ and a gap parameter $Δ$, the goal is to estimate the fraction $ρ$ of \emph{positive} distributions ($p_i\ge1/2+Δ$) to within additive error $ε$. We prove an upper bound of $\tilde{O}\!\big(\frac{\sqrtρ}{Δε}+\frac{1}{Δ\sqrtε}\big)$ queries, achieving a quadratic speedup over the classical sample complexity. % of $Θ(ρ/Δ^2ε^2)$.
  Our algorithm first uses the Quantum Singular Value Transformation (QSVT) to coherently amplify the bias gap without collapsing the superposition over distributions, and then applies two-stage adaptive amplitude estimation. We complement this upper bound with a near-matching lower bound of $Ω(\sqrtρ/(Δε))$ via a new composition theorem for the quantum adversary method in the Boolean-over-average-case direction. For the special case of a constant gap $Δ=Θ(1)$, which corresponds to the bounded-error oracle where each query returns the correct label with constant probability, our bounds specialize to $\tilde{O}\big(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε+\frac{1}{\sqrtε}\big)$ and $Ω(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε)$, thereby characterizing the query complexity of quantum counting with bounded-error oracles.

</details>


### [144] [Experimental realization of quantum-computing-enhanced sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07613)
*Yifang Xu,Hongwei Huang,Yilong Zhou,Yunlai Zhu,Chuanlong Ma,Lida Sun,Lintao Xiao,Ziyue Hua,Weiting Wang,Zijie Chen,Weizhou Cai,Hekang Li,Haohua Wang,Xiaoting Wang,Ming Li,Chang-Ling Zou,Luyan Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在自旋-振荡器架构中实现了量子计算增强的传感，将未知信号直接生成为相位oracle操作，在跨越128个光子的希尔伯特空间中实验演示了首次玻色子模Grover搜索，并观察到量子振幅放大效应。在相同传感迭代次数下，该方案比经典顺序搜索提取更多信息并分辨更多频率候选，确立了信号驱动oracle作为利用oracle量子算法实现量子计算增强传感的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法具有计算优势，但如何将量子计算融入量子传感领域，并将这种优势转化为增强的信息获取能力仍是一个重大挑战。现有方法缺乏量子计算与传感之间的自然物理接口。

Method: 采用自旋-振荡器架构：微波腔提供高维量子寄存器，耦合的超导量子比特作为灵敏传感器。未知信号直接产生相位oracle操作，建立量子计算与传感之间的自然物理接口。实验上在玻色子模中实现Grover搜索，并在超过128个光子的希尔伯特空间中观察量子振幅放大。

Result: 首次在玻色子模式中实验演示了Grover搜索，在超过128个光子的希尔伯特空间中观察到量子振幅放大。在相同的传感迭代次数下，该协议比经典顺序搜索能够提取更多信息并分辨更多频率候选。

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了信号驱动oracle作为利用oracle式量子算法实现量子计算增强传感的有效途径，为量子计算优势向量子传感领域转化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms offer computational advantages, yet incorporating them into quantum sensing and converting these advantages into enhanced information acquisition remains challenging. Here, we realize quantum-computing-enhanced sensing in a spin-oscillator architecture, where a microwave cavity provides a high-dimensional quantum register and a coupled superconducting qubit serves as the sensor. The unknown signal directly generates the phase oracle operation, establishing a natural physical interface between quantum computation and sensing. We experimentally demonstrate the first Grover search in a bosonic mode and observe quantum amplitude amplification in a Hilbert space spanning more than 128 photons. For the same number of sensing iterations, our protocol extracts more information and resolves more frequency candidates than classical sequential search. Our results establish signal-driven oracles as a route for harnessing oracle-based quantum algorithms and realizing quantum-computing-enhanced sensing.

</details>


### [145] [Benchmarking dynamical-structure-factor protocols in programmable neutral-atom geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07634)
*Matteo Grotti,Sergi Julià-Farré,Elisa Ercolessi,Alexandre Dauphin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过中性原子量子处理单元的数值仿真，评估了在更广泛的Ising类自旋系统中测量动力学结构因子的可行性。除了标准一维横场Ising链外，还验证了该协议在具有二聚化相互作用的一维链和二维横场Ising模型中的表现，并分析了其在有限仿真时间、脉冲调制、位置无序和激光噪声等实际实验条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学结构因子是凝聚态物理中的核心可观测量，传统上通过非弹性中子散射获取，近年可在量子模拟器中用动力学协议提取。但已有研究主要限于可积的一维模型，需要评估该方法在更广泛、更具挑战性的Ising类自旋系统（如二聚化链和二维模型）中的可行性，这些系统的谱性质在大尺度下难以用经典数值方法处理。

Method: 采用数值仿真方法对中性原子量子处理单元进行模拟。将标准的动力学协议应用于三类系统：一维横场Ising链（基准）、二聚化相互作用的一维链、以及二维横场Ising模型。同时模拟了真实实验条件下的各种噪声源（有限演化时间、脉冲调制、位置无序、激光噪声）以测试协议的鲁棒性。

Result: 结果表明中性原子量子模拟器能够在该协议下成功获取上述各系统的动力学结构因子信息，即使在二维模型和存在扰动的实验条件下也能保持有效。

Conclusion: 中性原子量子模拟器为在经典模拟日益困难的自旋系统中探测动力学响应函数提供了实用途径，为实验实现铺平了道路。该工作拓宽了量子模拟器测量动力学结构因子的适用范畴，超越了此前可积一维模型的局限。

Abstract: The dynamical structure factor is a central observable in condensed-matter physics, providing direct insight into the excitation spectrum and dynamical response of quantum materials. While it is traditionally accessed in real materials through inelastic neutron scattering, recent works have shown that analogous information can be extracted in quantum simulators using suitable dynamical protocols. So far, these approaches have been demonstrated mainly in paradigmatic integrable one-dimensional models. In this work, we use numerical emulations of a neutral-atom quantum processing unit to assess the feasibility of measuring the dynamical structure factor in a broader class of Ising-like spin systems. Beyond the standard one-dimensional transverse-field Ising chain, we benchmark the protocol in chains with dimerized interactions and in the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model, where spectral properties are difficult to access with classical numerical methods at large scales. We further analyze the robustness of the protocol under realistic experimental conditions, including finite simulation times, pulse modulation, positional disorder, and laser noise. Our results show that neutral-atom quantum simulators can provide a practical route to probing dynamical response functions in regimes where classical simulations become increasingly demanding, paving the way toward experimental implementation.

</details>


### [146] [Analog-to-digital conversion in the quantum regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07643)
*Thomas Pousset,Matteo Schiavon,Guillaume Ricard,Elie Awwad,Romain Alléaume,Nicolas Fabre*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对双零差探测的数字化链路（涵盖光学、电子及采样阶段）进行了严格的连续模式描述，推导出电子噪声、信号带宽、接收机电子带宽和采样率约束下的最大模式匹配系数，并提出了一个将奈奎斯特-香农准则推广至考虑量子涨落的维度定则，给出了针对加性白电子噪声的最优信噪比设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续变量量子光学中，相干探测与数字信号处理已成为核心工具，但此前研究多假设后测量处理可在光学域反向传播，缺乏对涵盖光学、电子和采样阶段的完整数字化链路进行严格理论处理，难以准确刻画实际接收机中各环节对探测性能的联合约束。

Method: 采用双零差探测的连续模式描述框架，系统建模从光学到电子再到采样的全链路数字信号处理入口；以最大模式匹配系数为优化目标，将电子噪声、信号带宽、接收机电子带宽和采样率作为约束参数；通过分析加性白电子噪声情形进行数值优化求解，并据此建立将奈奎斯特-香农准则推广至量子涨落情形的维度定则。

Result: 建立了推广的维度定则（一般化奈奎斯特-香农准则），它同时考虑信号带宽与量子涨落效应。数值结果表明：对于加性白电子噪声，最优信噪比在电子带宽足够通过信号但小于采样率时取得，该配置可抑制噪声放大，从而给出具体的相干探测设计准则。

Conclusion: 为连续变量量子光学相干探测的接收机设计提供了首个涵盖全数字化链路（光学-电子-采样）的严格理论框架，明确给出了电子带宽与采样率之间的最优权衡关系，为实际系统设计提供了可操作的指导原则。

Abstract: Digital signal processing has become essential in quantum optics, particularly for continuous-variable (CV) information encoding, enabling coherent detection schemes and mitigation of linear impairments. While linear signal processing in double homodyne detection has been studied under the assumption that all post-measurement processing can be backpropagated in the optical domain, a rigorous treatment of the digitization chain, spanning optical, electronic, and sampling stages, is still lacking. In this work, we consider the continuous mode description of double homodyne detection to fully capture the maximum mode-matching coefficient achievable as a function of electronic noise, signal bandwidth, receiver electronics bandwidth, and sampling rate, that is the entry point of digital signal processing. We establish a dimensioning rule that generalizes the Nyquist-Shannon criterion to account for quantum fluctuations of the measured signal, not merely its bandwidth. Analyzing the case of additive white electronic noise, we find numerically that optimal signal-to-noise ratio is achieved when the electronic bandwidth is large enough to pass the signal yet narrower than the sampling rate in order to mitigate noise amplification, yielding a concrete design criterion for coherent detection.

</details>


### [147] [Geometric inflation of deviations challenges neural quantum states in dynamics of quantum Ising models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07645)
*Wladislaw Krinitsin,Jonas B. Rigo,Mohammad Abedi,Markus Schmitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究神经量子态（NQS）在一维量子Ising模型淬火动力学中的模拟挑战，通过监督式状态重建证明表示能力并非主要障碍，而是发现量子几何张量（QGT）核空间的动力学旋转导致微小偏差被几何放大，从而限制了变分时间演化（TDVP）的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NQS虽为强关联量子系统非平衡动力学提供了强大框架，但在某些复杂度有限的物理区域中，精确的NQS模拟却意外困难。作者旨在澄清这一困惑的根源——究竟是表示能力不足，还是优化/演化算法本身存在未被认识的问题。

Method: 以周期性淬火的一维量子Ising模型为受控基准，采用监督式状态重建（supervised state reconstruction）估计所需参数数量的严格上界；随后分析无穷小时间相关变分原理（TDVP）的动力学行为，重点考察量子几何张量（QGT）的零空间旋转与参数偏差放大之间的关系，并与矩阵乘积态（MPS）解进行对比以验证敏感性来源。

Result: 监督重建给出比以往估计更紧的参数数上界，排除了表示能力作为关键瓶颈的可能；发现TDVP求解中QGT核空间随时间的旋转会突然使先前无关的参数微小偏差获得物理意义，引发几何性偏差膨胀。MPS方法求解相同TDVP具有稳定性，说明神经网络ansatz的非线性是敏感性的根源。

Conclusion: QGT零空间旋转是表征NQS动力学敏感性的几何诊断工具，并为改进TDVP算法提供了明确的目标方向。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) have emerged as a powerful framework for simulating non-equilibrium dynamics in strongly correlated quantum systems, offering scalable variational representations of highly entangled states. Yet, accurate NQS simulations have been found to be surprisingly challenging in some physical regimes of limited complexity. Here, we address paradigmatic quench dynamics of a one-dimensional quantum Ising model as a controlled benchmark. Through supervised state reconstruction we establish substantially tighter empirical upper bounds on the required parameter count than previous estimates, ruling out representational limitations as the key obstruction. Instead, we uncover a geometric inflation of small deviations as a hitherto overlooked challenge for accurate solutions of the infinitesimal time-dependent variational principle (TDVP): the dynamical rotation of the kernel of the quantum geometric tensor (QGT) can suddenly lend physical significance to previously irrelevant parameter deviations. The stability of matrix product state solutions of the same TDVP suggests that the non-linearity of the neural network ansatz is the origin of the sensitivity. These results identify QGT-null-space rotation as a geometric diagnostic of sensitive NQS dynamics and as a concrete target for improving TDVP algorithms

</details>


### [148] [Microwave-Free $^{13}$C Hyperpolarization of Diamond Particles Enabled by Magic Angle Spinning and NV Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07653)
*Rémi Blinder,Guzel Musabirova,Daehee Kim,Anshuman Nayak,Jason Twamley,Viateschlav N. Agafonov,Raiker Witter,Jörg Matysik,Fedor Jelezko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种无需微波照射、结合光激发与魔角旋转(MAS)的¹³C核超极化协议，在随机取向的金刚石颗粒（尺寸0.2–2 μm）中实现了高达280–411倍的¹³C极化增强，稳态绝对极化水平超过0.1%。该协议使超过99.9%的NV色心取向参与极化转移过程，显著缓解了NV自旋哈密顿量各向异性的影响，为将金刚石颗粒中核极化转移至外部核提供了第一步基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微波驱动的动态核极化（DNP）需要复杂的微波系统；而NV色心的基态自旋可通过光在室温下极化，使金刚石成为核超极化的候选平台。此前相关工作受限于NV自旋哈密顿量各向异性导致仅少数NV取向（<20%）能有效参与极化转移。本文旨在探索用光极化的NV色心结合MAS实现高效¹³C核超极化，缓解各向异性瓶颈。

Method: 使用随机取向的金刚石颗粒（尺寸0.2–2 μm），涵盖天然¹³C丰度（1.1%）和20%同位素富集样品；在7.1 T和9.4 T磁场下，结合连续光照射与魔角旋转（MAS，频率0–6 kHz可调），无需微波辐射。在7.1 T下系统研究MAS频率对¹³C极化的影响，并通过涉及光学泵浦NV中心、NV-替位氮(P1)-¹³C三自旋团簇中的能级避让交叉(LAC)附近自旋动力学的模型解释实验结果。

Result: 在7.1 T磁场下，富集样品获得280倍最大光诱导极化增强，天然丰度样品获得411倍增强。连续光照下稳态绝对¹³C极化超过0.1%。该协议使99.9%以上的NV取向可参与极化转移，极大缓解了NV哈密顿量各向异性的不利影响。

Conclusion: 该光驱动超极化协议在随机取向金刚石颗粒中实现了显著的¹³C核超极化，是无需微波的核极化方案的重要进展。这一成果代表了将金刚石颗粒中的核极化转移至外部核的第一步，有望应用于室温下高灵敏度、高分辨核磁共振（NMR）领域。

Abstract: Nuclear hyperpolarization from optically pumped color centers in solids offers an alternative to conventional microwave-driven dynamic nuclear polarization (DNP). Diamond can host the nitrogen vacancy (NV) center, whose ground spin state can be readily polarized by light at room temperature, making diamond a candidate platform for nuclear hyperpolarization. We report $^{13}{\rm C}$ nuclear hyperpolarization in randomly oriented diamond particles with sizes ranging from 0.2 to 2 $μ$m, both at natural $^{13}{\rm C}$ abundance (1.1 %) and at 20 % isotopic enrichment, at magnetic fields of 7.1 T and 9.4 T. The protocol combines optical illumination with magic angle spinning (MAS) and does not require microwave irradiation. By investigating the nuclear polarization as a function of the MAS frequency between 0 and 6 kHz at the magnetic field of 7.1 T, we find maximum light-induced polarization enhancements of $280$-fold for the isotopically enriched sample and $411$-fold for the natural abundance sample. Under continuous illumination, steady-state absolute $^{13}{\rm C}$ polarization levels above 0.1 % are reached. A model involving optical pumping of NV centers and spin dynamics near level anticrossings (LACs) in three-spin clusters formed by NV, a substitutional nitrogen (P1) and $^{13}{\rm C}$ is used to describe these findings. The protocol strongly mitigates the effect of the anisotropy of the NV spin Hamiltonian, allowing more than $99.9\%$ of NV orientations to participate in the polarization transfer process. These results represent a first step toward transferring nuclear polarization from diamond particles to external nuclei, with potential applications in sensitive and high-resolution NMR at room temperature.

</details>


### [149] [Dynamical Crossover of the Quantum Fisher Information in the Spin-Boson Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07674)
*D. Parlato,G. Di Bello,F. Pavan,G. De Filippis,C. A. Perroni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合到玻色环境的两能级系统的动力学量子Fisher信息，聚焦于量子比特能隙的估计。研究发现零温纯退相Ohmic环境下长时量子Fisher信息表现出耦合依赖的代数行为及动力学交叉（弱耦合时无界增长、交叉耦合时趋于有限值、强耦合时消失）；有限温度抑制长时增长并产生有限时极大值；非Ohmic浴中交叉现象在零温下消失；振幅衰减通道将无界增长替换为有限渐近值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解环境对量子计量学灵敏度的根本限制，特别是浴谱的低频结构如何决定动力学量子Fisher信息的渐近行为，以及热涨落和能量弛豫如何影响理想的纯退相动力学交叉现象。

Method: 结合解析计算与数值控制的矩阵乘积态（MPS）模拟。研究两能级系统耦合玻色环境，考虑纯退相Ohmic模型（可在零温精确求解）、非Ohmic浴（亚Ohmic与超Ohmic）、有限温度效应，以及额外引入振幅衰减（能量弛豫）通道的情形。

Result: 零温纯退相Ohmic模型：长时量子Fisher信息呈耦合依赖的代数行为，出现动力学交叉——弱耦合无界增长、交叉耦合处趋于有限渐近值、强耦合处消失。有限温度抑制长时增长并产生有限时极大值，保留零温交叉的清晰印记。非Ohmic浴零温下无交叉：亚Ohmic长时消失、超Ohmic发散。有限温度下超Ohmic二次浴出现温度依赖的交叉。增加振幅衰减后，无界长时增长被有限渐近值取代，纯退相交叉印记在早期动力学中保留。

Conclusion: 建立了浴的低频结构与动力学能隙传感渐近计量行为之间的直接联系，阐明了热涨落和能量弛豫如何规整理想的纯退相动力学量子Fisher信息交叉现象。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical quantum Fisher information of a two-level system coupled to a bosonic environment, focusing on the estimation of the qubit gap. We combine analytical calculations with numerically controlled matrix-product-state simulations. In the exactly solvable pure-dephasing Ohmic regime at zero temperature, the long-time quantum Fisher information displays a coupling-dependent algebraic behavior, leading to a dynamical crossover: it grows without bound at weak coupling, approaches a finite asymptotic value at the crossover coupling, and vanishes at strong coupling. At finite temperature, thermal fluctuations suppress the long-time growth and generate a finite-time maximum, whose dependence on the dephasing coupling retains a clear signa- ture of the zero-temperature crossover. We show that this crossover is absent at zero temperature in non-Ohmic baths: the long-time quantum Fisher information vanishes in the sub-Ohmic and diverges in the super-Ohmic regimes. At non-zero temperature instead, the crossover appears for a super-Ohmic quadratic bath and the crossover coupling becomes temperature-dependent. Moreover, in the zero-temperature Ohmic regime, we introduce an additional amplitude-damping system-bath coupling that induces energy relaxation. This relaxation channel replaces the unbounded long-time growth with a finite asymptotic quantum Fisher information associated with the reduced interacting ground state, while a signature of the pure-dephasing crossover persists in the early-time dynamics. These results establish a direct connection between the low-frequency structure of the bath and the asymptotic metrological behavior of dynamical gap sensing, and show how thermal fluctuations and energy relaxation regularize the ideal pure-dephasing dynamical crossover of the quantum Fisher information.

</details>


### [150] [Subsystem self-correction of the GKP qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07702)
*Brian Chung Hang Cheung,Lasse Bjørn Kristensen,Frederik Nathan,Michael Kastoryano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子信息（子系统的代码分解）角度，对理想GKP哈密顿量的自校正行为（逻辑寿命的Arrhenius型标度）提供了自洽的推导。当通过物理正交分量q和p耦合时，细致平衡跳变算子分解为仅作用于规范子系统的主导部分和作用于逻辑量子比特的边界项，后者被模块单元边界附近的吉布斯权重指数抑制。与自旋型表面码不同，GKP哈密顿量在模块相空间中实现了有效的弦张力，使得错误接近逻辑扇区边界时能量成本增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动量子纠错（自校正）是量子信息科学中的圣杯。GKP码被认为可能具备自校正性质，是自校正量子存储器的候选方案，亟需从量子信息视角给出严格的理论推导。

Method: 基于子系统代码分解的量子信息方法，从第一性原理推导理想GKP哈密顿量的自校正行为，分析细致平衡跳变算子在q和p物理正交分量耦合下的分解结构。

Result: 揭示了GKP哈密顿量的自校正机制：跳变算子分解为规范子系统主导项和逻辑量子比特边界项；边界项被吉布斯权重指数抑制；与自旋量子存储器（如二维表面码）不同，GKP在模块相空间中实现了有效弦张力。

Conclusion: GKP哈密顿量能够实现自校正量子记忆行为，其自校正性源于模块相空间中的有效弦张力机制，这一机制在逻辑寿命的Arrhenius标度中体现。

Abstract: Passive quantum error correction, also known as self-correction, is a holy grail in quantum information science. Recent theoretical advances suggest that the Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code can exhibit self-correction properties, positioning it as a candidate for the realization of self-correcting quantum memories. In this article, we provide a self-contained derivation of the self-correcting behavior of the ideal GKP Hamiltonian, manifested in the Arrhenius type scaling of the logical lifetime, from a quantum-information perspective based on the subsystem code decomposition. When coupling through the physical quadrature $q$ and $p$, the detailed-balance jump operators decompose into a dominant part acting only on the gauge subsystem and a boundary term that acts non-trivially on the logical qubit. The boundary term is then exponentially suppressed by the Gibbs weights near the edge of the modular cells. In contrast to spin-based quantum memories such as the two-dimensional surface code, the GKP Hamiltonian realizes an effective string tension in modular phase space, whereby the energy cost increases as an error approaches the boundary of a logical sector.

</details>


### [151] [Single- and Two-Qubit Gates Driven by an Integrated Photonic Laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07708)
*Chris Caron,Zhenyu Wei,Andrei Isichenko,David Heim,Meiting Song,Nick Montifiore,Kaikai Liu,Josiah Dill,Daniel J. Blumenthal,Robert J. Niffenegger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用集成可见光波段的布里渊激光器（稳定于集成线圈谐振器）驱动^88Sr^+光量子比特的相干单量子比特和双量子比特门操作，实现了99.61%的平均单比特保真度和92.35%的贝尔态保真度，首次展示了集成激光源满足囚禁离子相干量子逻辑的相位噪声要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 囚禁离子是量子计算的主导技术之一，但其依赖定制化的台式激光和光学系统，严重阻碍了系统的可扩展性和鲁棒性。集成硅氮化物激光器此前虽已实现频率选择性量子态制备、测量和光钟跃迁的询问，但尚未能驱动相干量子门操作，因为相干量子逻辑对激光的相位噪声和稳定性提出了更严格要求。本文旨在证明集成可见光窄线宽光子激光器能够满足囚禁离子相干量子逻辑对相位噪声的要求。

Method: 采用可见光波长集成的布里渊激光器，并将其稳定到集成线圈谐振器上，用于驱动^88Sr^+光量子比特的相干单量子比特和双量子比特门操作。使用随机基准测试（randomized benchmarking）测量单量子比特平均保真度；使用两层Mølmer-Sørensen相互作用生成纠缠贝尔态以验证双量子比特操作。

Result: 单量子比特平均Clifford门保真度为99.61% ± 0.03%；双量子比特Mølmer-Sørensen相互作用生成的贝尔态保真度为92.35% ± 1.50%。量子比特裸Ramsey相干时间为660 ± 9 μs，通过自旋回波可将相干时间延长至1.750 ± 0.033 ms。

Conclusion: 该工作首次证明集成可见光窄线宽光子激光器能够满足囚禁离子相干量子逻辑的相位噪声要求，为将光学系统单片集成于囚禁离子量子处理器提供了可行的技术路径，有望推动囚禁离子量子计算系统的可扩展化发展。

Abstract: Trapped ions are a leading technology for quantum computing, but their reliance on bespoke tabletop laser and optical systems remains a major obstacle to scaling and robustness. Integrated silicon nitride lasers, compatible with future monolithic integration with surface electrode ion traps, have recently demonstrated frequency-selective qubit state preparation and measurement as well as interrogation of an optical clock transition. However, coherent qubit gates driven by integrated laser sources have not yet been demonstrated because coherent quantum logic imposes substantially more stringent performance requirements on the laser. Here, we use a visible-wavelength integrated Brillouin laser stabilized to an integrated coil resonator to drive coherent single- and two-qubit gates with $^{88}$Sr$^+$ optical qubits. We measure an average single-qubit fidelity of 99.61% $\pm$ 0.03% per Clifford gate with randomized benchmarking and use a two-qubit Mølmer-Sørensen interaction to generate an entangled Bell state with a fidelity of 92.35% $\pm$ 1.50%. The qubit exhibits a bare Ramsey coherence time of 660 $\pm$ 9 $μ$s, extended to 1.750 $\pm$ 0.033 ms by spin echo. These results demonstrate that integrated visible-wavelength narrow-linewidth photonic lasers can meet the phase-noise requirements for coherent trapped ion quantum logic, providing a path for scalable optical systems integrated within trapped ion quantum processors.

</details>


### [152] [Propaq: A Python package for Heisenberg Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07730)
*Hrishikesh Belagali,Ryan LaRose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Propaq，一个用于经典模拟量子电路的高性能Python包，实现了海森堡（Heisenberg）传播技术。它兼容Qiskit和Cirq等主流量子软件包，提供任意基下的海森堡传播、混合薛定谔-海森堡模拟、自定义截断与噪声模拟等独特功能，并配备详细日志以分析模拟精度、运行时间和内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子电路对于量子算法的验证、调试和教学至关重要。海森堡传播（即保利传播/稳定器传播等）是一类重要的经典模拟技术，但现有的实现缺乏灵活性，且不能很好地与主流量子软件包集成。作者旨在开发一个兼顾性能、兼容性和功能拓展性的Python包，以满足不同模拟场景和研究的需要。

Method: Propaq是一个纯Python/Python生态下的开源包，后端实现了Heisenberg传播技术。其功能包括：(1) 支持任意基下的Heisenberg传播；(2) 混合Schrödinger-Heisenberg模拟，即在模拟中灵活选用不同传播模式；(3) 自定义截断策略以及噪声模拟方法；(4) 提供细粒度的日志系统，可监控模拟准确性、运行时间及内存占用。同时与Qiskit和Cirq等外部量子框架保持接口兼容。

Result: Propaq在性能上匹配或优于同类最先进的Heisenberg传播软件包。它与主流量子软件包兼容，并提供多种独特的仿真与研究功能，包括任意基传播、混合仿真、自定义截断与噪声模拟，以及详细的日志信息。

Conclusion: Propaq为量子电路经典模拟提供了一个性能强劲且高度灵活的工具，兼具兼容性和多功能性。作者详细描述了其特性、面向研究的使用方式以及后端实现细节，为量子计算领域的仿真与研究工作提供了新的实用方案。

Abstract: We introduce Propaq, a performant and flexible Python package implementing Heisenberg propagation techniques for classically simulating quantum circuits. Propaq is compatible with major quantum software packages like Qiskit and Cirq, and matches or outperforms similar state-of-the-art packages for Heisenberg propagation. Beyond this, Propaq has multiple distinguishing features for simulation and research. These include Heisenberg propagation in any basis, hybrid Schrödinger-Heisenberg simulation, custom truncation and noisy simulation methods, and detailed logging information for analyzing simulation accuracy, runtime, and memory usage. We describe these features, how to use Propaq for research, and its backend implementation.

</details>


### [153] [QROB: Quantifying Realization Overhead in Quantum Compilation via Reverse Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07776)
*Jintao Li,Kaiqi Li,Rui Wang,Yilun Zhao,Kaixuan Huang,Ying Wang,Jialin Zhang,Zheng-An Wang,Xiaoming Sun,Heng Fan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QROB是一种可扩展的反向构造方法，通过从可直接实现的配置反向生成编译实例，保留了可行的、与编译器无关的参考路径，为NISQ SWAP路由和容错晶格手术调度等量子编译任务提供统一的评估基准。实验表明现有编译器存在高达24.1x的SWAP代价差距和7.0x的makespan差距，且基于QROB参考训练的编译路由器优于Qiskit SABRE，在真实硬件上将镜像电路生存率提升至1.65倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编译需要协调程序的理想交互拓扑与硬件局部性约束，但在大规模评估中缺乏校准过的参考基准来衡量编译实现的额外开销（realization overhead）。现有评估无法公平、系统地量化编译器在布线路由和调度上的性能差距。

Method: QROB采用可扩展的反向构造方法：从可直接实现的配置（满足硬件约束的合法布局）出发反向生成编译实例，将逆路径保留为可行且与编译器无关的参考方案。该原则适用于NISQ SWAP路由、容错晶格手术调度，并扩展到容量受限的量子内存访问调度场景。评估覆盖9至156量子比特的系统。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1) 编译器表征方面，NISQ编译器相比参考产生了高达24.1倍SWAP代价，容错编译器产生高达7.0倍makespan；(2) 作为数据驱动编译的监督来源，在QROB参考上训练的路由器在84.8%的真实应用电路上优于Qiskit SABRE；(3) 在三个156量子比特IBM Heron-r2处理器上，QROB参考实现的中位镜像电路生存率是全量Qiskit O3编译的1.65倍。

Conclusion: QROB不仅提供了可扩展的量子编译基准框架，还证明了算法层面的编译差距会直接转化为物理层面的保真度损失，缩小这些差距能够带来实际的硬件端增益。该方法同时适用于NISQ和容错时代的不同编译调度问题。

Abstract: Quantum compilation reconciles a program's idealized interaction topology with hardware locality constraints, yet evaluations at scale lack calibrated references for realization overhead. We present QROB, a scalable reverse-construction methodology that generates compilation instances backward from directly realizable configurations, retaining the inverse paths as feasible, compiler-independent references. QROB provides a common evaluation substrate for NISQ SWAP routing and fault-tolerant lattice-surgery scheduling, while extending its reference-preserving principle to capacity-constrained quantum memory-access scheduling.
  Across systems ranging from 9 to 156 qubits, evaluations highlight QROB's utility as both a diagnostic benchmark and a data source. First, for compiler characterization, QROB reveals substantial realization gaps in existing tools, with NISQ compilers incurring up to 24.1x the reference SWAP cost and fault-tolerant compilers requiring up to 7.0x the reference makespan. Second, as a supervision source for data-driven compilation, a router trained on QROB references outperforms Qiskit SABRE on 84.8% of real-world application circuits. Finally, on real hardware, QROB reference realizations achieve a median mirror-circuit survival rate 1.65x that of full Qiskit O3 compilations across three 156-qubit IBM Heron-r2 processors, demonstrating that closing algorithmic compilation gaps translates directly into physical fidelity gains.

</details>


### [154] [Realization of decoherence-induced averaged symmetry-protected topological phases on quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07831)
*Ruizhe Shen,Xue-Jia Yu,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在可编程量子处理器上实验实现了退相干诱导的平均对称性保护拓扑（ASPT）序。通过从一维团簇态（cluster SPT）出发，利用辅助量子比特辅助量子电路工程化亚晶格选择性泡利-Z退相干，观测到对称性电荷的衰减所反映的对称性转换，并通过破坏性SWAP读取测量两副本Renyi-2弦关联子仍保持非平凡，揭示了密度矩阵层面编码的ASPT结构。该退相干还使谱权重在约化稳定子扇区间重新分布，同时保持半链纠缠谱的特征性两重配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SPT相是为精确对称性保护的纯态而定义的，但在开放量子系统中，退相干产生混合态系综，其平均对称性仅在对微观轨迹取平均后才可能涌现。作者旨在实验实现并验证这种退相干诱导的ASPT序，探索结构化退相干作为可编程资源以工程化混合态拓扑量子物质。

Method: 作者在一维团簇SPT态基础上，通过辅助量子比特（ancilla）辅助量子电路工程化亚晶格选择性的泡利-Z退相干。利用辅助测量后的量子态平均实现混合态系综，并采用破坏性SWAP读取方案测量Renyi-2弦关联子，同时分析退相干前后约化稳定子扇区的谱权重分布与纠缠谱配对结构。

Result: 实验观察到对称性电荷衰减表明对称性发生了转换（从精确对称性转为平均对称性）。两副本Renyi-2弦关联子在退相干下仍保持非平凡，证明ASPT结构在密度矩阵层面存在。工程化退相干将谱权重重分配至不同约化稳定子扇区，但半链纠缠谱的特征性两重简并配对仍被保持。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了门级量子线路工程化和探测ASPT序的可行路线，表明结构化退相干可用作可编程资源以实现混合态拓扑量子物质，拓展了拓扑量子物质在开放量子系统领域的研究范畴。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases are conventionally formulated for pure states protected by exact symmetries. In open quantum systems, however, decoherence generates mixed-state ensembles in which average symmetry can instead emerge only after averaging over microscopic trajectories. In this work, we realize decoherence-induced averaged SPT (ASPT) order on programmable quantum processors. Starting from a one-dimensional cluster SPT, we engineer sublattice-selective Pauli-$Z$ dephasing through ancilla-assisted quantum circuits. We observe the resulting symmetry conversion through the decay of the symmetry charge. Moreover, a two-replica Renyi-2 string correlator measured through destructive SWAP readout remains nontrivial, revealing the ASPT structure encoded at the density-matrix level. We further show that the engineered dephasing redistributes spectral weight among reduced-stabilizer sectors while preserving the characteristic twofold pairing of the half-chain entanglement spectrum. These results establish a gate-based route to engineering and probing ASPT order and demonstrate structured decoherence as a programmable resource for realizing mixed-state topological quantum matter.

</details>


### [155] [Restricted typicality in non-equilibrium quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07832)
*Konrad Pawlik,Piotr Sierant,Jakub Zakrzewski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了质量典型性可推广到受流体动力学瓶颈限制的量子多体系统中：通过仅以慢模式约束的最大熵Scrooge系综，能够系统性地重建全局时间演化态的复杂非线性量子性质（如纠缠熵和参与熵），这一现象被称为全局受限典型性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确模拟一般量子多体系统的时间演化需要指数级增长的计算资源，而流体动力学和统计力学虽能预测局域观测量期望值，但无法提供量子态的非线性特征信息。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，探索如何恢复宏观框架所丢失的量子信息。

Method: 针对具有时间尺度分离且动力学由守恒荷输运所主导的系统，采用仅由系统慢模式约束的最大熵Scrooge系综（Maximum-Entropy Scrooge ensemble）来重构全局时间演化的量子态，验证其能否准确再现半链纠缠熵和计算基下的参与熵等非线性量子性质。

Result: 研究表明，仅用慢模式约束的Scrooge系综即可精确重建全局时间演化态的非线性量子性质（包括半链纠缠熵和计算基参与熵），表明流体动力学瓶颈系统的全局态在希尔伯特空间的动力学受限子流形内表现为典型纯态。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典典型性范式进行了推广，提出"全局受限典型性"这一新概念，说明受流体动力学约束的量子系统在慢模式所定义的受限希尔伯特空间子流形中仍具有典型纯态行为，从而系统性地恢复了宏观框架中丢失的非线性量子信息。

Abstract: Characterizing the time evolution of generic quantum many-body systems is a fundamental challenge, as representing the exact state requires exponentially scaling computational resources. While hydrodynamics and statistical mechanics successfully simplify this task by predicting the expectation values of local observables, these macroscopic frameworks provide no information about nonlinear characteristics of the quantum state. In this work, we demonstrate that for systems exhibiting a timescale separation, with dynamics governed by the transport of conserved charges, this lost information can be systematically recovered. By constraining the maximum-entropy Scrooge ensemble solely by the system's slow modes, we accurately reconstruct complex, nonlinear quantum properties of the global time-evolved state, including the half-chain entanglement entropy and the participation entropy in the computational basis. Our results generalize the paradigm of canonical typicality, revealing that a hydrodynamically bottlenecked system behaves as a typical pure state within a dynamically restricted submanifold of the Hilbert space - a phenomenon we term global restricted typicality.

</details>


### [156] [Snakes on a Hyper-Plane: Dynamical and Ontological Locality in Everettian Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07860)
*Travis Norsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文质疑当前关于Everett量子力学（EQM）时空态实在论（SSR）版本与相对论性因果性兼容的共识。尽管该理论声称满足动力学局域性（局部区域的约化密度矩阵不受过去光锥外干预影响），但因其不可分离性而引入了非局域beables（非局部区域的RDM），作者认为这给动力学局域性的现有表述带来困难，暗示SSR EQM可能并不真正兼容相对论性因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在挑战新兴共识——即SSR EQM在具有本体论非局域性（非分离性）的同时，仍完全兼容相对论所要求的动力学局域性。作者认为该共识忽视了一个关键问题：理论中非局域beables的存在如何影响动力学局域性的定义与可行性。

Method: 通过对SSR EQM所设定的局部与非局部beables（RDM）进行概念分析，检查现有动力学局域性表述在处理非局域beables时的充分性，并据此论证非局域beables的引入会破坏现有动力学局域性框架的完备性。

Result: 发现SSR EQM虽然声称具有动力学局域性（局部RDM不受过去光锥外干预影响），但其非分离性必然涉及非局域RDM作为非局域beables。这些非局域beables的存在使现有动力学局域性表述复杂化，并对该理论与相对论性因果性的兼容性提出实质性质疑。

Conclusion: 作者认为，与新兴共识相反，SSR EQM可能不应被认为与相对论性因果性兼容；至少需要新的动力学局域性表述来处理非局域beables的问题，否则该理论的相对论兼容性主张难以成立。

Abstract: An apparently emerging consensus holds that Everettian Quantum Mechanics (EQM), in the Spacetime State Realism (SSR) version of Wallace and Timpson, involves ontological non-locality (i.e., non-separability) but is fully compatible with the dynamical notion of locality usually taken to be required by relativity. The claim of dynamical locality is based on the fact that the local beables postulated by the theory (namely, Reduced Density Matrices, RDMs, associated with localized regions) are not affected by interventions outside their past light cones. However, the theory's non-separability means that it posits non-local beables (namely RDMs for non-localized regions) in addition to the local ones. We argue here that the involvement of non-local beables introduces complications for extant formulations of dynamical locality and at least suggests, contrary to the emerging consensus, that SSR EQM should perhaps not be considered compatible with relativistic causality after all.

</details>


### [157] [Quantifying Margenau--Hill Nonclassicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07869)
*Sudip Chakrabarty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Margenau-Hill准概率（MHQ）分布的矩来量化非经典性，无需重构完整分布。作者引入对数MHQ负性作为非经典性量化指标，推导了基于低阶矩的严格下界层级，其中四阶矩界在偶矩界中最强；对qubit系统推导了精确MHQ负性的紧上界并识别了极值态，还获得了基于四阶矩的任意qubit可观测量对的最优正性阈值；并证明相关矩具有精确多副本表示，可通过干涉测量或经典阴影技术估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准概率分布通过偏离经典概率论来描述量子系统的非经典特征，但传统方法需要完全重构分布，代价高昂。作者希望建立不依赖完整分布重构、仅用低阶矩来量化非经典性的框架，以实现更高效的实验估计。

Method: 首先定义对数MHQ负性作为非经典性量化器，利用矩方法推导出由低阶矩构成的下界层级不等式。对qubit体系推导精确负性的紧上界并寻找极值态，以及基于四阶矩的最优正性阈值。最后构造矩的精确多副本表示，使其可通过干涉测量或经典阴影层析技术进行估计。

Result: 建立了严格的矩下界层级，证明四阶矩界是同偶阶矩中的最强界；对qubit给出紧上界和极值态；获得任意qubit可观测量的四阶矩正性最优阈值；证明相关矩具有精确多副本表示以支持实验估计。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于矩的方法论框架，使MHQ负性能够通过有限数量的低阶可观测量的矩获得定量信息，避免了完整分布重构，为量子非经典性的高效量化提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Quasiprobability distributions offer a useful way of describing nonclassical features of quantum systems through their departure from classical probability theory. In this work, we investigate the quantification of nonclassicality associated with the Margenau--Hill quasiprobability (MHQ) distribution using its moments, without requiring reconstruction of the full distribution. First we introduce the logarithmic MHQ negativity as a quantifier of nonclassicality, and then derive a hierarchy of rigorous lower bounds in terms of low-order moments. Within the resulting hierarchy, the fourth-moment bound is the strongest among the bounds based on even moments. For qubits, we further derive a tight upper bound on the exact MHQ negativity and identify the corresponding extremal state. We also obtain an optimal positivity threshold based on the fourth moment for arbitrary pairs of qubit observables. Finally, we show that the relevant moments admit exact multicopy representations, providing a route to their estimation using interferometry or classical shadow techniques. Our results establish a moment-based framework for extracting quantitative information about MHQ negativity from a finite set of low-order observables.

</details>


### [158] [Measurement-induced remote activation of nonclassicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07875)
*Sudip Chakrabarty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非经典性的远程激活：通过关联伙伴的局域测量，可以将原本经典约化态驱动的子系统转化为真正的非经典条件态。作者在双量子比特系统中给出了最优投影测量的精确闭式解，证明非酉局域噪声反而能增强效果而酉通道永远无益，并通过量子比特-振荡器混合模型验证了同一机制可在Wigner函数中产生负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非经典性作为量子优势的核心资源，通常通过Wigner函数或Kirkwood-Dirac分布的负性来定义，但如何在不直接作用于目标系统的前提下远程激活或增强其非经典性，此前缺乏系统性的理论刻画。

Method: 理论推导结合解析计算。在双量子比特系统中推导最大单分支效应及其概率加权平均的闭式表达式，并优化二输出投影测量；研究局域噪声（分酉与非酉）对该效应的影响，其中非酉通道需相对于噪声更新后的参考基进行评价；最后通过可精确求解的混合量子比特-振荡器模型验证机制的一般性。

Result: 在共享态无局域偏置时，二输出投影测量的最优性被证明；非酉且与目标轴失准的局域噪声能增强激活效应，而任何酉通道都不能起到帮助作用。该非经典条件态可使同一参考可观测量产生异常弱值（该弱值在未受操控的态上即使任意后选择也无法获得）。混合模型中同样机制可产生Wigner负性。

Conclusion: 测量诱导的量子操控（steering）被确立为远程激活非经典性的可操作资源，揭示了关联性在探测和激活量子非经典行为中的核心作用，为量子信息处理中的非经典资源生成提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Nonclassicality, defined through nonpositivity of quasiprobability distributions such as the Wigner and Kirkwood-Dirac distributions, underlies key quantum advantages. We show that it can be remotely activated: local measurements by a correlated partner alone can drive a party whose reduced state is classical into a genuinely nonclassical conditional state. For two-qubit systems we obtain closed forms for the maximal single-branch effect and for its probability-weighted average under the best two-outcome projective measurement, the latter proven optimal over all measurements when the shared state carries no local bias. Local noise on the steered party, evaluated relative to its own noise-updated reference basis, can enhance the effect when non-unital and misaligned with that party's axis, whereas no unital channel ever helps. This nonclassicality is shown to unlock, for the same reference observable, anomalous weak values unavailable to the unsteered state under arbitrary postselection. An exactly solvable hybrid qubit-oscillator model shows the same mechanism generates Wigner negativity. These results identify measurement-induced steering as an operational resource for remotely activating nonclassicality.

</details>


### [159] [Riemannian optimization for linear optical problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07890)
*Pablo V. Parellada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了定义在多端口干涉仪酉矩阵上的任意可微函数的黎曼梯度的闭合解析公式，并将其应用于黎曼优化算法（如梯度下降或BFGS）中搜索最优干涉仪配置，用于线性光学的态制备等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性光学中的许多量子科学应用需要搜索多端口干涉仪的最优配置，例如寻找最优的引导态制备方案。现有的优化方法效率较低，需要更快、更通用的方法来求解涉及更多模式和光子的线性光学问题。

Method: 推导了任意定义在干涉仪酉算子上的可微函数的黎曼梯度的闭合解析公式；将该梯度融入黎曼优化算法（梯度下降、BFGS等）中迭代搜索最优干涉仪配置。

Result: 成功改善了文献中NOON态和光子催化态制备的成功概率；所提出的优化器相比文献中其他方法快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 基于解析黎曼梯度的优化方法为线性光学问题提供了一种高效、通用的求解工具，开辟了处理涉及更多模式和光子的复杂线性光学问题的可能性。

Abstract: Many applications of linear optics in quantum science involve searching for some optimal configuration of a multiport interferometer. For example, to find heralded state preparations, one can optimize the interferometer to maximize the fidelity and success probability of the state preparation. In this paper, we derive a closed analytical formula for the Riemannian gradient of any differentiable function defined over the interferometer unitaries. We use this gradient in Riemannian optimization algorithms, such as gradient descent or BFGS, to find the optimal setup of the interferometer. As an interesting use case, we search for linear optical state preparations, improving some of the success probabilities reported in the literature for NOON or photon catalysis preparations. Finally, we show that our optimizer is orders of magnitude faster than other methods in the literature, opening the door for solving linear optical problems involving more modes and photons.

</details>


### [160] [Few-Body Decay Dynamics in Colloidal CsPbI3 Quantum-Dot Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07892)
*Emma Daggett,Christian M. Lange,Nicholas Favate,Ankit Kundu,Ishita Agarwal,Arya D. Keni,Adhyyan S. Mansukhani,Christina W. Li,Libai Huang,Jonathan D. Hood*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了CsPbI3钙钛矿量子点从单个发射体到含多达十个点的自组装团簇的光学行为。通过光子自相关、闪烁统计和发射亮度结合估计每个团簇的发射体数量，发现包含两个及以上发射体的团簇呈现双指数衰减，短寿命随发射体数量减少且存在额外长寿命组分。即使在低相干性区域也观察到依赖发射体数量的衰变动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体钙钛矿量子点兼具明亮发射和自组装成紧密结构的特性，使其能够研究从孤立发射体到少量子点团簇的辐射动力学，探索集体光学行为如何在单发射体与系综极限之间涌现。

Method: 采用光子自相关测量、闪烁统计和发射亮度相结合的方法来估算每个团簇的发射体数量；使用光子相关傅里叶光谱研究单点发射的相干性；比较孤立点与不同发射体数量团簇的荧光衰减动力学。

Result: 含两个及以上发射体的团簇呈现双指数衰减，短寿命随发射体数量增加而变短，并出现额外的长寿命组分；单点发射远未达到寿命限制性相干区域，但即使在低相干区域仍观察到依赖发射体数量的衰变动力学。

Conclusion: 自组装钙钛矿量子点团簇是研究集体光学行为如何在单发射体和系综极限之间涌现的有效平台。

Abstract: Colloidal perovskite quantum dots combine bright emission with the ability to self- assemble into closely spaced structures, enabling radiative dynamics to be studied from isolated emitters to few-dot clusters. Here, we characterize CsPbI3 perovskite quan- tum dots from isolated emitters to self-assembled clusters containing up to ten dots. We estimate the number of emitters in each cluster using a combination of photon autocorrelation, blinking statistics, and emission brightness. Compared with isolated dots, clusters containing two or more emitters exhibit biexponential decay, with a short lifetime that decreases with emitter number and an additional long-lived component. Photon-correlation Fourier spectroscopy shows that the single-dot emission remains far from the lifetime-limited regime. Nevertheless, we observe emitter-number-dependent decay dynamics even in this low-coherence regime. Together, these measurements es- tablish self-assembled perovskite quantum-dot clusters as a platform for studying how collective optical behavior emerges between the single-emitter and ensemble limits

</details>


### [161] [A Novel Steganography Scheme Using Quantum Hilbert Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07894)
*Nitin Jha,Abhishek Parakh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限维量子希尔伯特变换（QHT）的量子隐写方案，通过将经典比特以弱有符号相位扰动形式嵌入量子载体态中实现隐蔽通信，接收方通过二元态判别、块聚合和经典纠错解码恢复隐藏消息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐写术旨在将隐藏消息嵌入看似正常的通信中，但相位域的信息隐藏尚未在量子系统中得到充分探索，因此需要开发一种利用量子相位域进行信息隐藏的新方法。

Method: 本文首先基于量子傅里叶变换引入有限维量子希尔伯特变换（QHT）作为幺正相位算子，然后利用该算子将经典比特作为微弱的带符号相位扰动嵌入量子覆盖态中。接收端通过二元态判别、块聚合以及经典纠错解码三个步骤来恢复嵌入的隐藏消息。

Result: 成功构建了有限维QHT作为幺正相位算子，并基于此开发了一种完整的QHT量子隐写方案，该方案能够在量子系统中实现基于相位域的隐蔽信息传输。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地探索了量子系统中相位域的信息隐藏方法，通过QHT实现了经典比特在量子载体中的弱相位嵌入与可靠恢复，为量子隐写术开辟了新的技术路线。

Abstract: The main goal of steganography is to transmit hidden messages in legitimate-looking communication messages. Phase-domain information hiding, however, has not been fully explored for quantum systems. This work introduces a finite-dimensional Quantum Hilbert Transform (QHT) as a unitary phase operator based on the Quantum Fourier Transform. Using this construction, we develop a QHT-based quantum steganography scheme that embeds classical bits as weak signed phase perturbations of quantum cover states. Bob recovers the hidden message through binary state discrimination, block aggregation, and classical error-correcting decoding.

</details>


### [162] [Quantum Wavefunction Augmentation via Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07972)
*Sonaldeep Halder,Chayan Patra,Rahul Maitra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-WAVE方法，结合SQD采样的行列式与CISD行列式，通过β退火变分自编码器（VAE）迭代扩展子空间基础，在连续隐空间中学习波函数的主要支持结构并生成新的主导行列式，超越固定激发层级和原始硬件采样所能提取的范围，在H₂O和N₂解离中达到亚毫哈特里精度，并在52比特乙烯和60比特Cr₂体系上验证了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的量子对角化（SQD）在强关联体系中精度会退化，因为有限次采样无法覆盖相关的行列式空间。现有方法受限于固定激发层级或硬件采样能力，需要一种能超越这些限制的混合方法来构造更紧凑且更准确的波函数。

Method: Q-WAVE将SQD硬件采样的行列式（通过SqDRIFT Krylov电路生成）与CISD行列式结合作为种子，使用自定义β-annealed变分自编码器（VAE）在连续隐空间中学习波函数的主要支持结构，通过迭代方式生成新的主导行列式来扩展基础空间，直至逼近变分基态。该方法不受固定激发层级限制，能从融合的种子集中发现新的高权重行列式。

Result: 在H₂O和N₂解离问题中达到相对于全组态相互作用（FCI）的亚毫哈特里精度；在52比特乙烯体系上达到相对于CCSD(T)的亚毫哈特里精度、展现可扩展性；在高度关联的60比特Cr₂体系上通过最终微扰修正达到化学精度。

Conclusion: Q-WAVE通过生成式机器学习有效扩展了SQD的适用边界，生成的紧凑波函数优于单纯从原始硬件采样中提取的结果，为量子中心超级计算中的强关联体系处理提供了一条可行的混合经典-量子路径。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) has emerged as a promising route for quantum-centric supercomputing, relying on classical diagonalization of the molecular Hamiltonian within a hardware-sampled determinant subspace. However, its accuracy degrades in strongly correlated regimes where the relevant determinant space exceeds what finite-shot sampling can capture. In this work, we introduce Quantum Wavefunction Augmentation via Variational Autoencoders (Q-WAVE), a hybrid method that combines determinants sampled via SqDRIFT Krylov circuits and configuration interaction singles and doubles (CISD) determinants with generative machine learning. Using a custom $β$-annealed variational autoencoder (VAE) model, Q-WAVE iteratively expands this basis toward the variational ground state. The VAE learns the wavefunction's primary support structure from the combined hardware and CISD seed in a continuous latent space, generating new dominant determinants beyond any fixed excitation hierarchy. The resulting compact wavefunction exceeds what can be extracted from raw hardware samples alone. We demonstrate sub-millihartree accuracy compared to full configuration interaction for $\text{H}_2\text{O}$ and $\text{N}_2$ dissociation. Finally, we establish Q-WAVE's scalability on a 52-qubit ethylene system (achieving sub-millihartree accuracy versus CCSD(T)) and a highly correlated 60-qubit $\text{Cr}_2$ stress test that attains chemical accuracy upon a final perturbative correction.

</details>


### [163] [Learned Diffractive Optics for Quantum-Optimal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08014)
*Matthew J. Filipovich,Alexander Duplinskii,A. I. Lvovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用学习型衍射光学（衍射光学神经网络）来解决光子受限视觉传感中的量子最优测量问题。通过直接在任务品质因数上优化物理可实现的衍射光学元件，该方法在相干和衍射受限非相干光场的态判别与参数估计任务中，显著超越标准测量方案，并接近给定光子数下的量子极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学设定了光子受限传感的终极上限，但实际达到该上限的测量方案仅在特殊情况下已知。对于视觉传感问题——即从远处物体发射或反射光场的空间结构推断其特征——由于物体和光场结构的潜在复杂性，构造最优测量方案极具挑战性。

Method: 作者采用学习型衍射光学（物理可实现的衍射光学神经网络），直接针对每个任务的品质因数（figure of merit）进行优化，无需事先了解最优测量方案。该方法被应用于相干和衍射受限非相干光场的态判别和参数估计问题，并在受限光子预算下进行评估。

Result: 优化后的衍射光学神经网络在性能上大幅超越标准测量方案，并且接近给定光子数条件下的量子极限，同时在渐近极限下也能逼近量子极限。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过端到端优化的物理可实现的衍射光学元件，可以在没有最优测量先验知识的情况下，实现接近量子极限的光子受限视觉传感，为实际量子最优测量系统的构建提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Quantum mechanics sets the ultimate bounds on photon-limited sensing, yet practical measurements attaining these bounds are known only in special cases. This is particularly the case for visual sensing problems, where the goal is to infer features of a distant object based on the spatial structure of the light field it emits or reflects. Because of the potentially complex structure of such objects and fields, constructing optimal measurements on them is a challenging task. Here, we apply learned diffractive optics to state discrimination and parameter estimation of coherent and diffraction-limited incoherent light fields under a restricted photon budget. Optimized directly on each task's figure of merit, without prior knowledge of the optimal measurement, the physically realizable diffractive optical neural networks substantially outperform standard measurements and approach the quantum limits for a given number of photons as well as in the asymptotic limit.

</details>


### [164] [Sklar's Theorem and Quantum State Reconstruction from All-Context Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08035)
*Yi-Yu Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Sklar定理（将联合分布的边缘与依赖结构分离）应用于局部量子测量产生的Born统计量。研究发现，对于非直积双量子比特态，所有局部二元投影测量背景下，态由依赖核决定，至多存在离散的双自旋翻转模糊性。结合对应的标量优化与量子总关联，可复现全局量子失谐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典概率论中Sklar定理的视角（边缘与依赖结构分离）引入量子信息领域，探讨量子态的Born统计量是否也能以类似方式刻画，并试图用量子关联理论中的工具（如copula和依赖核）来重构量子态和量子失谐。

Method: 运用Sklar定理将两比特量子态在局部二元投影测量下生成的Born联合分布分解为边缘（单比特统计）与依赖核（copula）。对非直积双量子比特态，系统考察所有可能的局部二元投影测量上下文，证明依赖核集合足以确定密度矩阵（至多存在双自旋翻转的离散歧义）。将依赖核相关的标量优化与量子总相关性相结合，用于重建全局量子失谐。

Result: 证明了非直积双量子比特态可被全部局部测量上下文下的依赖核唯一确定（除双自旋翻转离散模糊外），且通过标量优化结合量子总相关可复现全局量子失谐。

Conclusion: 经典copula理论可以自然延伸至量子Born统计中，为量子失谐提供一种新的几何/依赖结构视角，揭示了量子态的信息几何与经典边缘-依赖分解之间存在深层联系。

Abstract: Sklar's theorem separates the marginals of a joint distribution from its dependence structure. We apply this viewpoint to Born statistics generated by local quantum measurements. For non-product two-qubit states, the dependence nuclei over all local binary projective measurement contexts determine the state up to at most a discrete double-spin-flip ambiguity. The corresponding scalar optimization, combined with the quantum total correlation, reproduces global quantum discord.

</details>


### [165] [Quantum magnonics with engineered dissipative and coherent interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08050)
*Debsuvra Mukhopadhyay,Jayakrishnan M. P. Nair,Girish S. Agarwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是对腔自旋波电子学（cavity magnonics）领域的前瞻性综述，探讨该领域如何超越传统相干自旋-光子杂化范式，转向耗散和相干相互作用的受控工程、Floquet动力学及量子涨落。文章指出这些要素重塑了集体动力学，并为量子态工程、输运和传感开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔自旋波电子学正在从一个以相干自旋-光子杂化为核心的领域，演变为一个更为广泛的研究平台。作者旨在揭示耗散耦合、反PT对称性、Floquet工程以及参量驱动等"非常规"要素如何拓展该领域的边界，使其超越传统极化激元物理，成为传感、换能、自旋输运和混合量子技术的多功能平台。

Method: 本文采用Perspective（前瞻性视角）的论述方式，系统性地组织并评述了以下几个前沿方向：(1) 耗散耦合与反PT对称性在实现长寿命集体模和增强频谱响应方面的作用；(2) Floquet工程用于构建非互易输运和相位控制相互作用；(3) 量子自旋波电子学进展，包括压缩和纠缠自旋波态（含热自旋波的压缩）以及自旋波薛定谔猫态制备方案；(4) 参量驱动和主动增益对混合磁化率及自旋波-光子响应的显著调制。

Result: 该Perspective表明，耗散和相干相互作用的受控设计、Floquet动力学、量子涨落、参量驱动与主动增益等元素显著扩展了腔自旋波电子学的功能范围，使其超越了传统的相干混合极化激元现象，能够支持新的量子态制备、非互易输运、增强传感与换能方案。

Conclusion: 作者总结认为，腔自旋波电子学已确立为一个超越传统极化激元物理的多功能平台，在传感、信号换能、自旋输运以及混合量子技术等多个方面具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Cavity magnonics is moving beyond the conventional paradigm of coherent spin--photon hybridization toward the controlled engineering of dissipative and coherent interactions, Floquet dynamics, and quantum fluctuations. This Perspective examines how these ingredients reshape collective dynamics and open new avenues for quantum state engineering, transport, and sensing. We discuss the role of dissipative coupling and anti-$\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry in enabling long-lived collective modes and enhanced spectral response, and highlight Floquet engineering as a means of engineering nonreciprocal transport and phase-controlled interactions. We then review recent progress in quantum magnonics, ranging from squeezed and entangled magnon states, including the squeezing of thermal magnons, to prospective schemes for preparing magnonic Schrödinger-cat states. We also discuss how parametric driving and active gain strongly modify hybrid susceptibilities and amplify magnon--photon response. These advances expand the scope of cavity magnonics beyond conventional polariton physics, establishing it as a versatile platform for sensing, transduction, spin transport, and hybrid quantum technologies.

</details>


### [166] [From Bits to Qubits: The Theory and Practice of Quantum Data Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08058)
*Xiao-Ming Zhang,Arthur G. Rattew,Bujiao Wu,Georgios Styliaris,Xiaoming Sun,Bálint Koczor,Xiao Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于量子数据编码的综合综述论文，系统回顾了将经典数据编码到量子系统中的核心概念、算法和实际考量，涵盖量子态制备、酉合成、QRAM和块编码等主流访问模型，并讨论了电路规模、深度、时空权衡及容错实现所需的非克利福德资源，同时面向稀疏数据、布尔函数和张量网络等结构化数据进行专门探讨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典数据编码为量子系统是几乎所有量子算法执行的基础步骤，也是实现实用化量子优势的关键瓶颈。随着量子计算从理论走向实践，需要一篇系统性的综述来梳理该领域从概念起源到最新进展的完整脉络，并连接理论与实践，为新手和活跃研究者提供参考。

Method: 本文采用综合性综述方法，从三个维度展开：（1）梳理量子数据编码从早期概念基础到近期进展的演进历程；（2）系统调查主流量子数据访问模型，包括量子态制备、酉合成、QRAM和块编码；（3）归纳分析电路规模、深度、时空权衡和非克利福德资源等容错实现指标，并对稀疏数据、布尔函数和张量网络表示的结构化数据场景进行专门讨论。

Result: 文章系统性地对比了不同量子数据访问模型在电路复杂度、资源消耗等方面的优劣，阐明了各类访问模型在量子算法中的角色分工，总结了结构化数据的编码策略，并指出了量子数据编码在推动量子优势实现中的关键作用及未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 量子数据编码是连接经典世界与量子计算的关键桥梁，未来需要在访问模型优化、资源效率提升和面向实用化场景的结构化数据编码等方面持续探索，才能充分发挥量子算法的潜力并最终实现实用化量子优势。

Abstract: Encoding classical data into quantum systems is a foundational step in the execution of nearly all quantum algorithms, and a critical bottleneck in realizing practical quantum advantage. This review provides a comprehensive account of the concepts, algorithms, and practical considerations associated with quantum data encoding. We trace the development from its early conceptual foundations to recent advances, considering commonly used access models, such as quantum state preparation, unitary synthesis, QRAM and block encoding. We survey the circuit size, depth, space-time tradeoffs, as well as non-Clifford resources required for fault-tolerant implementation. We also discuss the roles of different access models in quantum algorithms. Special attention is given to structured data, such as sparse data, Boolean functions and data represented by tensor networks. This review bridges theory and applications, serving both as a pedagogical guide for newcomers and as a reference for active researchers. We also highlight the pivotal role of quantum data encoding in quantum computing and provide insights into future directions that will enable quantum advantage.

</details>


### [167] [Bottom-Up Design of Quantum Optical Experiments Using Discrete Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08073)
*Isaac L. Huidobro-Meezs,Simón Paiva-Ortega,Rodrigo A. Vargas-Hernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Grinch框架，一种自底向上、奖励驱动的生成式方法，直接从保真度奖励中学习采样量子光学实验的光学图结构，无需预训练数据集，能够生成多种高保真度光学图，并找到渐近解和非局域Toffoli门构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计量子光学实验需要在离散电路拓扑和连续参数上进行搜索，且同一目标态往往有多种实现方式。现有的基于图的方法通常通过优化稠密图并剪枝为单一电路来解决，缺乏灵活性和对多种解的探索能力。

Method: Grinch是一种自底向上的奖励驱动生成框架，直接从保真度奖励中学习采样光学图的策略，不依赖预训练数据集。框架通过在离散图拓扑空间中探索，并可直接将硬件连接约束融入搜索过程。

Result: 在多部分纠缠态、图和簇态以及非局域光子门目标上获得了多种高保真光学图；识别了无法由图精确生成状态的渐近解；发现了仅需两个辅助量子位的非局域Toffoli门构造；引入硬件约束目标后获得了CNOT和Toffoli门的替代构造。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种奖励驱动的量子光学逆向设计方法，能在搜索过程中直接纳入连接性约束，探索替代电路拓扑，为量子光学实验设计提供新的范式。

Abstract: Designing quantum optical experiments requires searching over discrete circuit topologies and continuous parameters, often with multiple realizations of the same target state. Graph-based methods commonly address this problem by optimizing a dense graph and pruning it toward a single circuit. We introduce \texttt{Grinch}, a bottom-up reward-driven generative framework that learns to sample optical graphs directly from fidelity-based rewards without relying on a pre-existing training dataset. We demonstrate the framework on multipartite entangled states, graph and cluster states, and nonlocal photonic-gate targets, obtaining multiple high-fidelity optical graphs. We identify asymptotic solutions for states that cannot be generated exactly by graphs, as well as nonlocal Toffoli gate constructions requiring two ancillas. Our work presents hardware-constraint objectives, which lead to alternative constructions of CNOT and Toffoli gates when connections between ancilla nodes are restricted. These findings illustrate a reward-driven approach to quantum optical inverse design, exploring alternative circuit topologies while directly incorporating connectivity constraints into the search process.

</details>


### [168] [Spectral Sign Implication for Quantum Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08099)
*Kenji Tokuo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子逻辑中的谱符号蕴含（spectral sign implication），该操作通过从后件投影算子中减去前件投影算子所得算子的非负谱投影来定义。该操作在可交换投影上与经典物质蕴含一致，并通过算子的差谱结构比较任意投影对。它满足Hardegree的四条最小蕴含条件、逆否律及假性条件，且不同于标准多项式蕴含之处在于其结果不必属于由自变元生成的正交格。在Borel构造的均匀类中，满足蕴涵、逆否律和假性条件的操作恰好对应谱分支的可测选择；在连续子类中这三个条件唯一确定谱符号蕴含。在有限维情形，该操作还是Helstrom判别中先验与秩成正比的子空间态最优投影检验接受投影中的最大者。这些结果将量子蕴含与两投影算子理论和二元量子判别联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子逻辑中蕴含算子的构造长期以来存在困难，标准多项式蕴含不总能给出正交格中的元素，且在非交换情形下与经典蕴含的本质对应关系不明。本文旨在通过算子差值的谱结构定义一种新的量子蕴含，使其同时满足蕴含逻辑的基本条件，并与算子理论和量子判别建立深层联系。

Method: 定义谱符号蕴含为(Q−P)非负谱投影（即从后件投影Q减去前件投影P后取其非负谱部分）。系统验证其满足Hardegree的蕴含条件、逆否律和假性条件；在Borel构造的均匀类中对满足三条件的操作进行分类，证明其对应于谱分支的（可测）选择；在连续子类中证明唯一性；在有限维中分析其与Helstrom二元量子判别最优投影检验的关系。

Result: 谱符号蕴含在可交换投影上退化为经典物质蕴含；在一般Borel构造下，满足蕴涵、逆否律和假性条件的操作恰为谱分支的Borel选择；在连续谱分支子类中条件唯一确定该蕴含；有限维下其是Helstrom判别中接受投影的最大元素。

Conclusion: 谱符号蕴含为量子逻辑中的经典蕴含推广提供了基于谱分解的自然构造，同时建立了量子逻辑蕴含与两投影算子理论、二元量子判别之间的形式联系，揭示了蕴含逻辑条件与谱分支选择之间的精确对应关系。

Abstract: We study the spectral sign implication for quantum logic, defined by the nonnegative spectral projection of the operator obtained by subtracting the antecedent projection from the consequent projection. The construction agrees with classical material implication on commuting projections and compares arbitrary pairs of projections through the spectral structure of their difference. It satisfies Hardegree's four minimal implicative conditions, his law of contraposition, and a falsity condition. It differs from the standard polynomial implications in that its value need not belong to the ortholattice generated by its arguments. Within a uniform class of Borel constructions for pairs of projections, the operations satisfying entailment, contraposition, and the falsity condition correspond exactly to measurable choices of spectral branch. In the continuous subclass, these three conditions determine the spectral sign implication uniquely. In finite dimensions, the operation is also the largest among the acceptance projections of optimal projective tests for Helstrom discrimination between subspace states with priors proportional to rank. These results connect quantum implication with the operator theory of two projections and binary quantum discrimination.

</details>


### [169] [Quantum algorithm for PageRank computation through multistep quantum resonant transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08140)
*Chuqing Wang,Hefeng Wang,Hua Xiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多步量子共振跃迁(mQRT)的量子算法，用于获取编码Google矩阵PageRank向量的量子态。通过将网络图分解为嵌套结构的子图序列，构造中间哈密顿量演化路径，使系统从简单初始哈密顿量的基态逐步演化到问题哈密顿量的基态，从而高效获得PageRank向量对应的量子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PageRank向量在大规模网络分析中具有重要应用，但经典计算在大规模网络上效率低。现有量子算法在获取PageRank向量量子态时往往需要较多辅助资源或复杂的演化过程，因此需要一种更高效且资源开销更低的量子算法来获取PageRank向量的量子态。

Method: 1) 将PageRank向量编码为与Google矩阵相关联的问题哈密顿量的基态；2) 将Google矩阵对应的网络图划分为一系列具有嵌套结构的子图；3) 基于子图构造一组哈密顿量序列，形成从简单初始哈密顿量到问题哈密顿量的演化路径；4) 通过多步量子共振跃迁(mQRT)逐步经过中间哈密顿量的基态，最终到达问题哈密顿量的基态。

Result: 该算法仅需一个辅助量子比特(ancillary qubit)，算法运行时间与步数成正比，为高效获取大规模网络的PageRank向量量子态提供了一种新途径。

Conclusion: 该算法通过嵌套子图分解和mQRT机制，以较低的量子资源开销实现了PageRank向量量子态的高效制备，为大规模网络的量子信息处理提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for obtaining a quantum state that encodes the PageRank vector of the Google matrix through multistep quantum resonant transition (mQRT). In the algorithm, the PageRank vector is encoded in the ground state of a problem Hamiltonian associated with the Google matrix. By dividing the web graph corresponding to the Google matrix into a series of subgraphs with nested structure, we construct a sequence of Hamiltonians based on the subgraphs to form a Hamiltonian evolution path from a simple initial Hamiltonian to the problem Hamiltonian. The ground state of the problem Hamiltonian is obtained by going through ground states of the intermediate Hamiltonians via QRT step by step. This algorithm requires only one ancillary qubit, and the runtime of the algorithm is proportional to the number of steps. It provides a new way for efficiently obtaining the quantum state of the PageRank vector of large-scale networks.

</details>


### [170] [Quantum-state texture measure and texture transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08163)
*Yufan Lin,Yu Guo,Fei He,Shuanping Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统地扩展了量子态纹理（quantum-state texture）的资源理论，解决了纹理自由操作下纯态到任意态的转换问题及qubit情形下任意两态间的确定性变换问题，提出了两类新的纹理度量（基于凸函数的度量和通过纯态最小纹理成本定义的纹理代价），并证明了每个纹理单调量都可导出一个满足凸-roof扩展所需性质的函数（即提供了该构造的逆定理），从而给出了量子态纹理较为完整的资源理论表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态纹理量化了选定基下态的结构不规则性，已被证明是通用电路中门表征的宝贵资源。已有研究提出了基于收缩距离的纹理度量类及凸-roof扩展纹理度量的构造框架，但该理论仍有多个方向待发展，包括态转换问题的求解、新度量类别的建立以及凸-roof扩展构造的反向证明。

Method: 作者从资源理论的角度对量子态纹理进行了多方向扩展：(1)考察纯态在纹理自由操作下到任意态的可转换性，并完整求解了qubit情形下任意两态间的确定性变换问题；(2)提出了两类新的纹理度量：基于凸函数的度量以及通过纯态中纹理最小成本定义的纹理代价；(3)证明了每个纹理单调量都可导出一个非负函数，该函数满足与凸-roof扩展中使用的函数相同的性质，从而给出了该构造的逆命题。

Result: 该工作为量子态纹理提供了相对全面的资源理论表述：解决了纹理自由操作下的态转换问题（包括qubit全域确定性变换），建立了新的度量类别，并确立了凸-roof扩展构造的逆定理。

Conclusion: 本文的成果为量子态纹理建立了较为完整的资源理论框架，涵盖了转换、度量与扩展三方面的核心理论问题，为纹理作为门表征资源提供了更坚实和普适的理论基础。

Abstract: Quantum-state texture quantifies structural irregularities of a state in a selected basis and has emerged as a valuable resource for gate characterization in universal circuits. Consequently, a class of contractive distance based texture measures and a framework for constructing convex-roof extended texture measure have been proposed. Here, we extend this theory in several directions. We examine the convertibility of a pure state to any state under texture-free operations, and fully solve the deterministic transformation problem between any two states in the qubit case. We then propose two classes of texture measures: convex-function-based measures and texture cost defined via minimal cost of texture contained in pure states. Moreover, we show that every texture monotone gives rise to a non-negative function which admits the same properties as the function used in the convex-roof extension---thus providing a converse to that construction. Our work thereby offers a relatively comprehensive resource-theoretic formulation of quantum-state texture.

</details>


### [171] [Saving resources through repeat-until-success positive-operator-valued-measure measurements in quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08168)
*Hefeng Wang,Sixia Yu,Hua Xiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子计算方法，其中计算由POVM测量沿计算路径分多步引导进行。通过将辅助量子比特耦合到工作量子比特寄存器，并对辅助比特执行投影测量来实现工作比特上的POVM测量。该方法采用"重复直到成功"（repeat-until-success）策略，每步计算都能确定性地获得目标状态。研究表明，与将所有中间测量推迟到线路末端相比，该方法引入中间测量可在多项式和幺正操作数量上实现多项式级别的减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子计算常将测量推迟到线路末端（deferred measurement principle），但中间测量在某些场景下可能带来效率和资源上的优势。作者旨在证明在计算过程中引入中间POVM测量可以减少量子比特数量和幺正操作的数量，从而提升计算效率。

Method: 将单个辅助量子比特与工作量子比特寄存器耦合，通过在整个系统上施加幺正操作后再对辅助比特进行投影测量，实现对工作比特的有效POVM测量。每步采用重复直到成功的协议，使目标状态通过POVM测量在工作比特上确定性地获得。研究对比了引入中间测量与将测量推迟到线路末端两种策略的资源消耗。

Result: 证明在该方法中，通过计算过程中对辅助比特引入中间测量，可使所需的量子比特数量和幺正操作数量相对于将中间测量推迟到计算末端的情况产生多项式级别的缩减。同时提供了该方法的实现方案。

Conclusion: 中间POVM测量并不总是可以无损地推迟到线路末端——在某些情况下，引入中间测量反而能在资源（量子比特数和幺正操作数）上带来多项式级别的节省。这一发现对量子电路优化和量子计算资源调度具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We present a quantum computation approach in which computation is guided by positive-operator-valuedmeasure (POVM) measurements following a given computation path in multisteps. In this approach, one ancillary qubit is coupled to a register of working qubits, and a POVM measurement is implemented effectively on the working qubits by applying a unitary operation on the whole system followed by a projective measurement performed on the ancillary qubit. Each step of the computation is a repeat-until-success procedure such that the desired state of the step is obtained deterministically on the working qubits via POVM measurements. The principle of deferred measurement states that measurements can always be moved from an intermediate stage of a quantum circuit to the end of the circuit without affecting the efficiency of the computation.We demonstrate that in our approach, by introducing intermediate measurements on the ancillary qubit in the computation process, both the number of qubits and unitary operations can be reduced polynomially, compared to the case where the intermediate measurements are deferred to the end of the computation. We also provide a method for implementation of the approach.

</details>


### [172] [Classical Active-Space Hybrid Quantum Subspace Expansion (CASH-QSE): Quantum Corrections without Remeasuring the Classically Calculable Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08170)
*Artur F. Izmaylov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出CASH-QSE方法，将经典CASSCF参考态与量子制备态结合，利用占据数结构构造与参考态精确正交的量子态，从而无需重叠测量即可通过普通厄米特征值问题求解能量，避免近线性依赖引起的测量误差放大问题，在H₂O和N₂键拉伸基准上达到化学精度并显著降低采样成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE制备的波函数常含有大量经典可处理的成分，但其能量贡献仍需在量子设备上采样，造成不必要的量子资源开销。虽然可将波函数分解为经典参考态与量子态的组合以在经典上评估参考能量，但各分量间的近线性依赖会放大测量误差并使能量估计不稳定，亟需设计能避免该问题的混合量子子空间方法。

Method: CASH-QSE方法保留CASSCF参考态进行经典处理，利用占据数（occupation structure）约束构造与参考态之间以及彼此之间严格正交的量子态，从而将能量求解转化为无需重叠测量的普通厄米特征值问题；相同的占据约束还简化了待测量的算符。方法结合全组态相互作用（FCI）指导组分选择。

Result: 在H₂O与N₂沿键拉伸坐标（覆盖弱到强关联区域）的基准测试中，使用STO-3G基组以及H₂O的受限cc-pVDZ轨道空间，CASH-QSE达到了化学精度，且最大完整测量线路仅包含数百个全连接逻辑CNOT门。在有利情况下，相较于ADAPT-VQE，其理想化最终能量采样成本降低了数个数量级。

Conclusion: CASH-QSE通过经典-量子混合子空间分解配合占据约束实现正交化，有效规避了近线性依赖导致的测量误差放大，在保持化学精度的同时大幅降低电路深度与采样成本，为变分量子本征求解器提供了更高效的替代方案。

Abstract: The wave function prepared by a variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) can contain a substantial classically tractable component, yet its energy contribution is sampled on the quantum device.
