Representing the wave function as a combination of a classical reference and separately prepared quantum states allows the reference energy to be evaluated without quantum sampling. However, near-linear dependence among these components can amplify measurement errors and destabilize the resulting energy estimate. We introduce Classical Active-Space Hybrid Quantum Subspace Expansion (CASH-QSE), which retains a complete-active-space self-consistent field (CASSCF) reference classically and uses occupation structure to construct quantum states exactly orthogonal to the reference and to one another. The energy follows from an ordinary Hermitian eigenvalue problem without overlap measurements, while the same occupation constraints simplify the measured operators. Using full configuration interaction to guide component selection, we test CASH-QSE along H$_2$O and N$_2$ bond-stretching coordinates spanning weakly to strongly correlated regimes. The benchmarks use STO--3G for both molecules and a restricted cc-pVDZ orbital space for H$_2$O. CASH-QSE reaches chemical accuracy while limiting the largest complete measurement circuits to a few hundred all-to-all logical controlled-NOT gates. In favorable cases, it also reduces the idealized final-energy sampling cost by several orders of magnitude relative to VQE with an adaptive derivative-assembled pseudo-Trotter ansatz (ADAPT-VQE).

</details>


### [173] [Transversal Fanout for Fault Tolerant Distributed Quantum Computing: Analysis and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08233)
*Seng W. Loke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式容错量子计算中逻辑扇出（logical fanout）操作的资源高效实现方法，利用量子纠错码块上的横向（transversal）逻辑操作来减少分布式节点间的非局域通信开销。该方法基于双向Bicycle（BB）码编码的量子信息构建分布式扇出电路，并分析了物理门、纠缠、电路深度和辅助比特需求。此外，还研究了基于分布式扇出并发性的全局门GCZ的分布式实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算中，逻辑扇出操作涉及从公共控制量子比特到远程节点上多个目标量子比特的CNOT操作。如果直接在编码块之间实现该操作，会产生大量非局域通信开销。如何在保持逻辑动作正确性的前提下，利用编码块结构和横向逻辑操作来降低所需的非局域通信是核心动机。

Method: 利用编码量子块的内部结构和可用的横向逻辑操作（transversal gates），构造减少非局域操作数量的分布式扇出电路。该方法针对使用Bivariate Bicycle（BB）码编码的量子信息进行开发与演示，通过横向扇出操作的并发性实现分布式全局门GCZ。

Result: 给出了系统化的分布式逻辑扇出电路构造方法，并分析了所需物理门数量、纠缠资源、电路深度及辅助比特等开销指标。该方法适用于远距离容错量子处理器间的大规模逻辑扇出操作，同时验证了基于BB码的全局门GCZ的分布式实现可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种系统性的方法，可在分布式纠错量子处理器间高效实现大规模逻辑扇出操作，为分布式容错量子计算中非局域通信瓶颈问题提供了可行的资源优化路径。

Abstract: We study a resource-efficient approach for implementing logical fanout operations in fault-tolerant distributed quantum computing using transversal operations on quantum error-correcting code blocks. Logical fanout, comprising multiple controlled-NOT operations from a common control qubit to target qubits located at remote nodes, is an important primitive for distributed quantum computation but can require substantial non-local communication when implemented directly between encoded blocks. We exploit the structure of encoded blocks and the availability of transversal logical operations to construct distributed fanout circuits that reduce the required non-local operations while preserving the logical action of the fanout operation. The construction is developed for encoded quantum information and illustrated using Bivariate Bicycle (BB)-code blocks. We analyze the resulting physical gate, entanglement, circuit-depth, and ancilla requirements. The approach provides a systematic method for implementing large logical fanout operations across distributed error-corrected quantum processors. Also, we study a distributed implementation of the global gate GCZ involving logical qubits (encoded using BB-code blocks), exploiting the concurrency in transversal distributed fanouts.

</details>


### [174] [Scaling-Enhanced Rapid Readout of a Qubit Ensemble Assisted by High-Frequency Detector Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08374)
*Hiroki Nakabayashi,Yuichiro Matsuzaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究大型量子比特布居集体测量的读出时间随比特数N的标度关系，确立了无高频模式探测器的基准标度N^{-1/2}，并证明具有无界高频模式的探测器可超越该基准，获得特征时间标度N^{-1/(2-ν)}（0<ν<2），其中ν由探测器频谱结构决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型量子比特集合的测量通常通过将集合与探测器耦合再测量探测器来完成。尽管快速集体读出非常重要，但读出时间如何随N标度这一问题此前尚不清楚，需要从理论上加以阐明。

Method: 首先对一类广泛的、不含紫外高频模式的探测器建立N^{-1/2}的基准标度下界；随后分析具有无界高频模式的探测器，推导出其标度时间服从N^{-1/(2-ν)}（0<ν<2），其中ν为表征探测器频谱结构的指数。

Result: 证明了高频探测器模式是一种能够实现集体量子读出标度优势的资源，且探测器频谱直接控制读出时间的标度指数ν。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了探测器高频模式对集体量子测量读出速度的关键作用，确立了频谱结构与读出标度指数之间的直接对应关系，为设计更快速的集体读出方案提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Quantum measurements of large qubit ensembles are often performed indirectly by coupling the ensemble to a detector and subsequently measuring the detector. Despite the importance of rapid collective readout, it remains unclear what determines how the readout time scales with the number of qubits $N$. Here, we first establish a benchmark $N^{-1/2}$ for a broad class of detectors without ultraviolet high-frequency modes. We then show that the detector with unbounded high-frequency modes can surpass this benchmark and yield a characteristic time scaling $N^{-1/(2-ν)}$ for $0 < ν< 2$, where $ν$ characterizes the spectral structure of the detector. Our results establish high-frequency detector modes as a resource for achieving a scaling advantage in collective quantum readout, with the detector spectrum directly controlling the scaling exponent of the readout time.

</details>


### [175] [Non-Markovian dynamics of a qubit on the zigzag edge of photonic graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08382)
*Enrico Di Benedetto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用解析方法研究了一个量子比特与光子蜂窝晶格锯齿形边缘耦合的动力学行为，通过解析算子方法和精确晶格格林函数，刻画了量子比特-光子束缚态谱和复极点，揭示了量子比特占据数的长时间标度律。与体相石墨烯耦合情形不同，连接狄拉克点的零能平带抑制了由分支切割引起的长时间拖尾，转而驱动带阻尼的拉比振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子比特与光子结构耦合的动力学是量子光学和凝聚态物理交叉领域的重要问题。已有的研究多关注量子比特与石墨烯体相耦合的情形，而对于量子比特与光子蜂窝晶格边缘（尤其是锯齿形边缘）耦合的动力学行为尚未被系统解析地表征。理解边缘态和零能平带对量子比特动力学的影响，对于在光子平台上实现可控的量子态操控具有重要意义。

Method: 采用解析算子（resolvent operator）方法，配合精确的晶格格林函数（lattice Green's functions），解析地计算量子比特-光子束缚态的谱分布和复极点位置，进而推导量子比特占据数的长时间动力学标度律。

Result: 研究发现，与量子比特耦合到石墨烯体相（bulk）的情形在长时间尺度上呈现由分支切割（branch cut）导致的幂律拖尾不同，耦合到蜂窝晶格锯齿形边缘时，由于存在连接狄拉克点的零能平带（zero-energy flat band），这些分支切割诱导的长时间拖尾被抑制，取而代之的是带阻尼的拉比振荡（damped Rabi oscillations）行为。

Conclusion: 量子比特与光子蜂窝晶格边缘耦合的动力学由零能平带主导，表现出与体相耦合截然不同的长时间演化特征；该研究为在光子平台上利用拓扑/平带结构调控量子比特动力学提供了理论依据，并指出了潜在的可探测实验平台。

Abstract: We present an analytical study of the dynamics of a qubit coupled to the zigzag edge of a photonic honeycomb lattice. Leveraging a resolvent operator approach and exact lattice Green's functions, we map the spectrum of qubit-photon bound states and complex poles to uncover the long-time scaling laws of the qubit population. Unlike a qubit coupled to the bulk of graphene, the presence of a zero-energy flat band connecting the Dirac points suppresses long-time branch-cut-induced tails, instead driving damped Rabi oscillations. Potential experimental platforms are briefly discussed.

</details>


### [176] [Dynamics of entanglement in non-Hermitian system of nonreciprocal coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08388)
*Jiguo Wu,Shaojie Yang,Xiong Liu,Zhibo Jiang,Zhiyou Zhang,Rongchun Ge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验可实现的非互易耦合腔系统（基于二维可重构非厄米测量激光阵列），同时展示二阶和三阶例外点（EPs），观察到一种全新的纯量子连续纠缠相变，揭示了多EP相互作用如何在不同区域改变纠缠动力学同时保持加速产生，为高阶简并应用和量子系统动态相变的研究开辟新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米量子系统研究因实验进展而重获关注，但不同阶数例外点之间的相互作用尚缺乏系统研究。虽然调控EP阶数对量子控制（如加速纠缠产生）具有重要意义，但目前对多阶EP交互作用的初步探索仍然缺失。

Method: 采用非互易耦合腔系统，该系统可通过二维可重构非厄米测量激光阵列在实验上实现。通过精心设计，系统同时展现二阶和三阶例外点，观察纯量子连续纠缠相变的符号特征，分析多EP相互作用对纠缠动力学和产生加速的影响。

Result: 观察到一种全新的纯量子连续纠缠相变，揭示了多EP相互作用如何在不同区域中改变纠缠动力学，同时保持加速的纠缠产生。研究结果展示了高阶EP的真正量子非厄米物理特性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了高阶例外点的量子非厄米物理本质，为未来研究高阶简并的应用以及量子系统动态相变打开了新的方向。

Abstract: The exploration of the effect of non-Hermitian (NH) in quantum systems is gaining renewed momentum as a result of the recent progress in experiments. It has been indicated that the ability to engineer the orders of exceptional points (EPs) can be fundamental to its applications in quantum control, such as accelerating entanglement generation; however, an initial survey of the interactions between different orders of EPs is still missing. In this work, we try to partially address this issue by employing a non-reciprocally coupled cavity system which can be experimentally realizable with a two-dimensional reconfigurable NH gauged laser array. With deliberate design, our systems display both second and third orders of EPs. The sign of a novel, purely quantum continuous entanglement phase transition is observed, which reveals how multi-EP interactions alter entanglement dynamics across different regimes while maintaining accelerated generation. Our findings reveil the genuinely quantum NH physics of higher-order EPs, which has opened up a new avenue for future investigations of the higher-order degeneracy application and the dynamic phase transitions of quantum systems.

</details>


### [177] [A theoretical study of subcarrier-wave quantum key distribution system integration with an optical transport network utilizing dense wavelength division multiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08389)
*Fedor Kiselev,Natalia Veselkova,Roman Goncharov,Vladimir Egorov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在密集波分复用（DWDM）光传输网络中经典信道存在的情况下，子载波量子密钥分发（SCW QKD）的性能表现，考虑了自发拉曼散射噪声、四波混频和信道隔离效率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际光传输网络中部署量子密钥分发系统时，经典DWDM信道会引入噪声干扰量子信道，影响QKD系统性能和安全性。因此，需要评估经典信道共存条件下SCW-QKD协议的可行性与最优部署方案。

Method: 计算SCW-QKD协议在不同DWDM系统参数和量子信道分配方案下的量子误码率（QBER）以及安全密钥生成速率，综合考虑自发拉曼散射（SRS）噪声、四波混频（FWM）效应及信道隔离效率等因素。

Result: 计算结果表明，与C波段分配相比，量子信道分配在1310 nm波长处更为优选，尽管该波长处光纤衰减更大，但其非线性噪声更低。

Conclusion: 在实际DWDM网络中共存部署SCW-QKD时，将量子信道分配至1310 nm波长比C波段更有利，这是非线性噪声减少与光纤衰减增大之间权衡的最优结果。

Abstract: In this paper we study the performance of subcarrier-wave quantum key distribution (SCW QKD) in the presence of classical channels of optical transport network utilizing dense wavelength division multiplexing (DWDM). We consider the impact of spontaneous Raman scattering noise as well as the four-wave mixing and channel isolation efficiency. We calculate quantum bit error rate as well as the secure key generation rate of SCW-QKD protocol for different parameters of DWDM system and quantum channel allocations. Our calculations show, that quantum channel allocation at the wavelength of 1310 nm is preferable to allocation at C-band due to lower nonlinear noise, despite fiber's larger attenuation at this wavelength.

</details>


### [178] [Performance of the subcarrier-wave quantum key distribution in the presence of spontaneous Raman scattering noise generated by classical DWDM channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08406)
*Fedor Kiselev,Roman Goncharov,Natalia Veselkova,Eduard Samsonov,Alexei D. Kiselev,Vladimir Egorov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了单模光纤中密集波分复用（DWDM）经典信道产生的自发拉曼散射（SpRS）噪声对副载波量子密钥分发（SCW QKD）系统性能的影响，提出了考虑SpRS噪声的量子误码率（QBER）和安全密钥生成率的数学模型，并比较了连续波与脉冲两种工作模式下的系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光纤网络中，DWDM经典信道产生的SpRS噪声是影响QKD系统性能的重要干扰源，需要量化评估其对SCW QKD系统的影响，以确定系统在实际部署中的可行性与性能边界。

Method: 提出了考虑SpRS噪声的数学模型，用于评估QBER和安全密钥生成率；针对SCW QKD系统的两种工作模式（连续波模式和脉冲模式），在不同接收机灵敏度的经典DWDM条件下进行性能分析与对比。

Result: 研究发现脉冲模式在安全密钥生成率和最大可实现传输距离两方面均优于连续波模式。

Conclusion: 在DWDM网络共存条件下，SCW QKD系统的脉冲模式相比连续波模式具有更优的抗SpRS噪声性能，能够实现更高的安全密钥生成率并支持更长的传输距离。

Abstract: In this paper we study performance of the subcarrier-wave quantum key distribution system (SCW QKD) in the presence of spontaneous Raman scattering (SpRS) noise generated by classical channels of dense wavelength division multiplexing (DWDM) network within a single-mode optical fiber. We present the mathematical model for evaluation of the quantum bit error rate (QBER) and the secure key generation rate with the SpRS noise taken into account. We consider two regimes of the SCW QKD system: the continuous wave regime that uses continuous wave laser and the pulsed regime. For these regimes, performance of the system is analyzed depending on receiver sensitivity of classical DWDM. It is found that the pulsed regime outperforms the continuous wave regime in both the secure key generation rate and the maximum achievable distance.

</details>


### [179] [Efficient quantum state preparation on Quantinuum hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08414)
*Archie Butterworth,Josh Green,Yusen Wu,Jie Pan,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端框架，将资源高效的量子态制备与快速鲁棒的保真度验证相结合，在Quantinuum H2-1离子阱平台上实验制备并验证了编码数字声学信号的结构化复数量子态，硬件保真度达0.929，仅需1000次测量即可完成稳健验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子设备需要制备特定量子态以实现相对于经典计算的优越性，但当前在近端量子硬件上实现高效态制备与可靠保真度验证仍面临挑战。特别是对于结构化量子态，现有验证方法（如shadow overlap）的样本复杂度可能过高，难以在实际噪声和测量预算下实现。

Method: 提出一个集成框架：(1) 资源最小化电路设计，针对NISQ时代和早期容错设备进行优化；(2) 引入预测量基变换技术，将验证参数τ降低超过10个数量级，强化shadow overlap方法的理论认证保证；(3) 将张量网络态制备与shadow验证整合为统一工作流；(4) 在Quantinuum H2-1俘获离子平台上进行实验验证。

Result: 在Quantinuum H2-1平台上成功实验制备和验证了编码数字化声学信号的结构化量子态，硬件保真度达到F_hw = 0.929，无需理想化假设或深度容错开销。通过预测量基变换，将验证参数τ降低了超过10个数量级，并将稳健验证过程压缩至仅1000次测量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在真实噪声和有限测量预算条件下，如何将结构化经典数据高效映射到量子硬件并进行可靠验证，提供了即时的、可扩展的基准测试标准，对NISQ时代及早期容错设备上的量子态验证具有范式性意义。

Abstract: Preparation and verification of specific quantum states is an important capability for quantum devices to realise advantages over classical computations and algorithms. In this work, we have demonstrated an end-to-end framework that combines resource-efficient quantum state preparation with rapid, robust fidelity verification on near-term quantum hardware. By experimentally preparing and validating a structured complex quantum state encoding a digitized acoustic signal on the Quantinuum H2-1 trapped-ion platform, we achieved a high hardware fidelity of $F_{\mathrm{hw}} = 0.929$. Crucially, this milestone was realized without relying on idealized assumptions or deep fault-tolerant overhead, but rather through resource-minimal circuits optimized for NISQ-era and early fault-tolerant devices.
  Furthermore, we addressed a key limitation in current quantum state certification. While validation methods like shadow overlap work well for random states, their sample complexity can become prohibitively high for the structured states used in practical algorithms. We mitigate this by introducing a pre-measurement basis-change technique that reduces the verification parameter, $τ$, by over 10 orders of magnitude for structured targets. This approach tightens the theoretical certification guarantees of the shadow overlap method and integrates tensor-network preparation and shadow validation into a unified workflow. These results shift the paradigm of how structured classical data can be mapped to and verified on quantum hardware under realistic noise and measurement budgets. By compressing a robust verification procedure to just 1,000 measurement shots, this framework offers an immediate, scalable benchmarking standard.

</details>


### [180] [Time-Dependent Quantum Monte Carlo for fermions: from Bayesian conditioning to spinor dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08415)
*Ivan P. Christov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了含时量子蒙特卡洛方法中的条件相互作用。该方法用walker系综在物理空间中采样多电子态，通过贝叶斯定理和单一经验替换得到Nadaraya-Watson形式的条件相互作用，赋予非局域长度以条件宽度的物理含义，并推广到自旋情形，涵盖费米子交换、正交归一化约束及无符号问题等关键特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时量子蒙特卡洛方法此前以启发式方式使用条件相互作用，缺乏严格推导；该方法此前多应用于反平行自旋电子体系（交换作用休眠而非缺失），需要形式化处理交换活性（平行自旋）的费米子体系，并为非局域长度、对极限与平均场极限等提供一致的物理基础。

Method: 将walker视为电子在有限分辨率下的局域化体现，利用贝叶斯定理并结合单一经验替换，推导出Nadaraya-Watson形式的条件相互作用。非局域长度被解释为条件化宽度而非拟合耦合参数。由于条件化消除了Hartree-Fock中消除正交归一化乘子的规范自由度，正交归一化通过约束导出的项来强制实施。整个过程推广到旋量(spinor)描述，其中泡利重合抑制由局域自旋排列分级调节。

Result: 条件相互作用从推导而非假设中获得Nadaraya-Watson形式；成对极限与平均场极限由同一构造自然得出。费米子交换由walker承载不了而停留在波函数部分，使正walker采样免于符号问题。正交归一化由约束推导的项强制执行。推广到旋量后，泡利抑制按局域自旋排列分级，并通过共线及平均场极限约化为已知的双粒子自旋方程。

Conclusion: 该方法为含时量子蒙特卡洛的条件相互作用提供了严格的理论推导，将其从启发式构造提升为基于贝叶斯推理的第一性原理形式，同时扩展至具有活性交换的费米子体系及旋量情形，为无符号问题的正walker采样提供了理论保障。

Abstract: The time-dependent quantum Monte Carlo method represents a many-electron state by an ensemble of replicas, in which each electron is described by a cloud of walkers which samples its density in physical space, one walker and one guide wave per replica, and it replaces the Hartree potential by a conditional interaction built from the walker positions. The sampling is in physical space rather than in configuration space, which is what keeps the cost polynomial. Here that conditional interaction is derived rather than postulated. The method has so far been applied mostly to opposite-spin electrons, where the exchange is dormant rather than absent, and the present formulation addresses the regime in which it is active. Treating the walker as a localization of its electron to a finite resolution, Bayes theorem and a single empirical substitution yield the Nadaraya-Watson form which the method has used heuristically. The nonlocality length thereby acquires a meaning as the width of the conditioning rather than as a fitted coupling, and the pair and mean-field limits follow from one construction. For fermions, exchange cannot be carried by the walkers and stays in the wave sector, which is what leaves the positive walker sampling free of the sign problem. Because the conditioning removes the gauge freedom which eliminates the orthonormality multipliers in Hartree-Fock, orthonormality is enforced here by a term derived from the constraint. The formulation is generalized to spinors, where the Pauli suppression at coincidence becomes graded by the local spin alignment, and it reduces through the collinear and mean-field limits to the known two-particle spin equations.

</details>


### [181] [Trapdoor Functions with Secure Key Leasing and Copy Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08423)
*Fuyuki Kitagawa,Ryo Nishimaki,Yuki Shirakawa,Takashi Yamakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了量子密码学中的陷门函数（TDF），分别在有安全密钥租赁（TDF-SKL）和防拷贝（TDF-CP）两种设置下给出了定义、构造及应用。基于LWE假设构造了无域采样器的TDF-SKL，基于任何标准公钥加密方案结合hinting伪随机生成器构造了带域采样器的TDF-SKL；基于不可区分混淆和LWE假设构造了TDF-CP。此外，利用TDF-SKL和TDF-CP分别构造了具有鲁棒量子解密密钥（可抵御恶意密文攻击后可重用）的PKE-SKL和SDE方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受量子理论中不可克隆定理启发，量子密码原语（如安全密钥租赁和防拷贝）引起了广泛关注，但作为公钥密码学基础原语的陷门函数在这些新兴量子设置中尚未被深入研究。同时，现有的PKE-SKL和SDE构造存在关键漏洞：量子解密密钥在解密恶意选择的密文后可能被破坏而失效。这些问题亟待解决。

Method: 作者首先分别形式化定义了有无域采样器两种版本的TDF-SKL，并给出两种构造：基于LWE假设的无需域采样器构造，以及基于任何标准PKE加上hinting PRG的带域采样器构造；然后定义了TDF-CP，并遵循Ananth和Behera在CRYPTO 2024中提出的模块化防拷贝框架，基于不可区分混淆和LWE假设给出构造；最后从TDF-SKL和TDF-CP出发构造具有鲁棒可重用量子解密密钥的PKE-SKL和SDE方案。

Result: 成功给出了TDF-SKL（有无域采样器两种情况）和TDF-CP的定义与构造；证明基于LWE和/或iO等标准假设即可实现这些原语；并利用它们解决了现有PKE-SKL和SDE中量子解密密钥易被恶意密文销毁的问题，构造出解密任意密文后仍可复用的鲁棒方案。

Conclusion: 本文首次在安全密钥租赁和防拷贝框架下系统研究了陷门函数，填补了这一基础公钥密码原语在量子不可克隆设置中的空白，并利用所构造的TDF-SKL与TDF-CP解决了现有PKE-SKL和SDE的量子密钥鲁棒性问题，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Inspired by the no-cloning theorem in quantum theory, a variety of quantum cryptographic primitives with unclonable functionalities, such as secure key leasing and copy protection, have been proposed and attracted significant attention. However, trapdoor functions (TDFs), fundamental primitives in public-key cryptography, have not been extensively studied in these areas. In this work, we initiate a study of TDFs in both secure key leasing and copy protection settings. We first introduce the definition of TDFs with secure key leasing (TDF-SKL), which enables leasing and deleting of quantum trapdoors. We formalize TDF-SKL both with and without domain sampler, and give a construction of TDF-SKL without domain sampler based on the LWE assumption and a construction of TDF-SKL with domain sampler based on any standard PKE schemes combined with hinting pseudorandom generators [Koppula and Waters, CRYPTO 2019]. Next, we define TDFs with copy protection (TDF-CP), where the inversion functionality is copy protected by a quantum trapdoor. We establish a construction of TDF-CP assuming indistinguishability obfuscation and the LWE assumption, following a modular framework of copy protection proposed by Ananth and Behera [CRYPTO 2024]. We also present applications of TDF-SKL and TDF-CP. Existing constructions of public-key encryption with secure key leasing (PKE-SKL) and single-decryptor encryption (SDE) suffer from a critical vulnerability: quantum decryption keys may be destroyed after decrypting maliciously chosen ciphertexts. We construct PKE-SKL schemes and SDE schemes with robust quantum decryption keys that remain reusable after decrypting arbitrary ciphertexts from TDF-SKL and TDF-CP, respectively.

</details>


### [182] [Explicit block encodings of rate matrices for simulating polymerization kinetics on quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08432)
*Yuhei Ikeda,Hokuto Iwakiri,Soichiro Nishio,Kentaro Matsumoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种活性聚合动力学模型（单单体聚合Model A和双单体共聚Model B）的量子区块编码电路构造方案，通过稀疏预言机和线性组合酉算子（LCU）分解将非酉速率矩阵嵌入酉量子电路，实现聚合物分子量分布和序列演化的量子模拟。数值模拟验证了稀疏预言机编码能复现经典时间演化，资源估计显示仅需O(log N)个量子比特，为在容错量子硬件上模拟聚合动力学奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典聚合物动力学模拟面临分子分辨率与计算成本的根本权衡：共聚物可区分的种类数随链长呈指数增长，导致经典态空间爆炸。量子计算有望解决此问题，但其关键在于将控制动力学的非酉速率矩阵嵌入酉量子电路（即区块编码），目前尚缺乏针对聚合反应动力学模型的明确区块编码构造方案。

Method: 针对两种活性聚合动力学模型分别构造显式区块编码电路：（1）Model A（单单体聚合），其下双对角速率矩阵通过稀疏预言机构造和两项LCU分解实现编码；（2）Model B（双单体共聚），利用m-索引对聚合物种类进行双射整数标记，将动力学矩阵化为结构化稀疏矩阵，通过五项LCU或稀疏预言机构造进行编码。数值模拟选取报道的烯烃共聚体系反应活性比，涵盖近无规（r1r2≈1）和嵌段状（r1r2>1）微结构。

Result: 数值模拟表明，稀疏预言机编码能精确复现经典时间演化结果；LCU编码通过显式重构编码矩阵块得到验证。资源估算显示两种实现均仅需O(log N)个量子比特（相对经典态空间呈指数级内存节省），门计数随系统规模平缓增长，在10^3个系统量子比特时达到10^4至10^5个门。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了模拟聚合动力学的具体量子电路基础，是首次利用指数级态空间压缩处理高维聚合物反应网络的尝试，为未来在容错量子硬件上实现聚合过程的全量子模拟铺平了道路。

Abstract: Predicting how molecular weight distribution and monomer sequence evolve during polymerization is central to polymer science, yet classical approaches face a trade-off between molecular resolution and computational cost: for copolymers, the number of distinguishable species grows exponentially with chain length. Quantum computing offers a potential alternative, provided the non-unitary rate matrices governing the kinetics can be embedded into unitary quantum circuits, a task known as block encoding. Here we construct explicit block-encoding circuits for two kinetic models of living polymerization: Model A, single-monomer polymerization, whose lower-bidiagonal rate matrix is encoded via a sparse-oracle construction and a two-term linear combination of unitaries (LCU) decomposition; and Model B, two-monomer copolymerization, where a bijective labeling of polymer species by an integer index (the m-index) yields a structured sparse matrix encoded via either a five-term LCU or a sparse-oracle construction. Numerical simulations with the sparse-oracle encodings reproduce the classical time evolution for reactivity ratios drawn from reported olefin copolymerization systems spanning near-random ($r_1 r_2 \simeq 1$) and blocky ($r_1 r_2 > 1$) microstructures, and the LCU encodings are verified by explicit reconstruction of the encoded matrix block. Resource estimation shows that both implementations require only $O(\log N)$ qubits in the matrix dimension $N$ (an exponential memory saving over the classical state space), with gate counts growing gradually, reaching $10^4$ to $10^5$ gates at $10^3$ system qubits. These results establish a concrete quantum circuit foundation for simulating polymerization kinetics on fault-tolerant quantum hardware, and a first step toward exploiting exponential state-space compression for high-dimensional polymer reaction networks.

</details>


### [183] [The Forgotten History of Wave Function Variance Optimization and its Relevance for Neural-Network VMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08453)
*Dario Bressanini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）和神经网络VMC中"何种局域能量泛函能最优优化试探波函数"这一近百年历史的问题。由于神经网络波函数的局域能量分布具有重尾特征，基于L2范数（高斯假设）的方差最小化不再适用。作者提出并评估了MAD（L1）、Cauchy损失和L-4泛函等鲁棒代价函数，并在H2+全势能曲线上使用Guillemin-Zener波函数进行基准测试，发现方差最小化在所有R下均被替代泛函超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分蒙特卡洛和神经网络VMC中，波函数优化的核心是选择何种局域能量的代价函数。传统的方差最小化假设局域能量服从高斯分布（L2范数），但现代神经网络波函数面临高统计噪声和重尾分布，这一假设被打破。此外，库仑体系的局域能量呈E^-4幂律尾，导致中心极限定理对方差估计无效。作者回顾了这一被遗忘的历史，旨在为现代NQS框架寻找更稳健的优化代价函数。

Method: 论文采用历史回顾与数值基准相结合的方法。首先追溯方差优化自1930年代以来的发展历史。随后理论分析库仑体系中局域能量分布的E^-4幂律尾行为及中心极限定理失效的原因。方法上，提出将方差最小化替换为鲁棒的代价函数——MAD（L1范数）、Cauchy损失和L-4泛函（尾部指数与分析匹配E^-4）。选择H2+这一在每个核间距下都可精确求解的体系作为基准，使用Guillemin-Zener试探波函数在全势能曲线上分别测试这些代价函数的表现。

Result: 结果表明，能量最小化按构造达到最低能量；方差最小化在所有核间距R下均被替代泛函超越。具体而言，在成键区MAD表现最优，而在解离区L-4泛函表现最好，显示出不同物理区域需要不同鲁棒代价函数的适配性。

Conclusion: 这表明对于库仑体系，基于高斯假设的方差最小化并非最优的波函数优化策略。采用与局域能量真实幂律尾部相匹配的鲁棒代价函数（如针对成键区的MAD和解离区的L-4）可以更可靠地优化波函数，这一发现对现代神经网络量子态的优化具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: We revisit the almost century-old question of which functional of the local energy best optimizes a trial wave function, a problem of central importance in Variational Monte Carlo (VMC) and, more recently, in Neural-Network VMC (NN-VMC). While variance optimization dates back to the 1930s, the high statistical noise and heavy-tailed local energy distributions inherent to modern neural-network wave functions have renewed interest in this approach. We retrace its long and largely forgotten history here, showing its direct relevance to modern Neural Quantum States (NQS) frameworks. Minimizing the variance (an $L^2$ norm) implicitly assumes a Gaussian local energy distribution: an unjustified assumption. For Coulombic systems, the local energy distribution exhibits $E^{-4}$ power-law tails, causing the Central Limit Theorem to fail for the variance estimator. This instability can be mitigated by robust cost functions: the Mean Absolute Deviation (MAD, an $L^1$ norm), the Cauchy loss, or the $L_{-4}$ functional, which features a tail analytically designed to match the $E^{-4}$ exponent. We benchmark these functionals on $H_2^+$, an exactly solvable system at every internuclear distance, using the Guillemin-Zener wave function across the full potential energy curve. While energy minimization by construction yields the lowest energy, variance minimization is surpassed at every $R$ by alternative functionals: MAD proves superior in the bonding region, while $L_{-4}$ performs best in the dissociation regime.

</details>


### [184] [Deriving the Kijowski Arrival-Time POVM from the Schrödinger Current: Minimal Positivity and Uniqueness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08458)
*Avi Marchewka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子背流现象（量子回退），即动量支持完全为正的态却出现负的薛定谔流。作者寻求对自由薛定谔流的最小修正，使其对所有此类状态非负且保持每个动量分量各自的流。结果表明最小修正将动量核从 (p+p')/2m 改为 √(pp')/m，所得流为正且归一化，定义一个到达时间POVM。进一步将无背流要求扩展到含正负动量的态，迫使正负动量跨界核为零，最终得到的POVM恰好是Kijowski到达时间POVM。在对角保持的逐对最小构造下结果是唯一的，且构造本身不施加首达条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子背流现象表明，即使动量支撑完全为正的波函数，其薛定谔概率流也可能出现负值，这与直觉相悖且对定义粒子到达时间等物理量构成障碍。作者希望找到一个修正后的概率流当前表达式，使其在保持每个动量分量流的前提下对所有正动量态非负，从而为到达时间POVM提供基于概率流的最小物理动机。

Method: 作者在动量空间框架中对自由薛定谔流进行最小修正，要求满足两个约束：(1) 对动量完全为正的任何态，修正后流非负；(2) 保持每个单一动量分量的流不变。通过核函数从 K_Sch=(p+p')/2m 到 K_min=√(pp')/m 的几何平均替换实现最小化。随后考察正负动量混合态的无背流条件，证明跨界核必须为零，从而将总流分解为两个独立的定向贡献，得到Kijowski到达时间POVM。构造限定在对角保持的逐对最小方案内，并证明唯一性。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 最小非负修正是将动量核替换为几何平均 √(pp')/m，所得流为正、归一化，可定义到达时间POVM；(2) 扩展到包含正负两种动量符号的态时，定向无背流要求迫使跨界核为零，总流成为两个独立定向部分之和；(3) 该POVM与Kijowski到达时间POVM完全一致；(4) 在对角保持的逐对最小构造约束下，结果唯一。

Conclusion: 作者通过从量子背流的角度出发，用最小修正构造的方式为Kijowski时间到达POVM提供了基于概率流物理动机的推导：其方向性核结构及其分离（正负动量各自独立的贡献）正是无背流要求的最小自然结果。构造具有唯一性，且不依赖于首达条件假设。

Abstract: Quantum backflow refers here to the appearance of a negative Schrödinger current for a state whose momentum support is entirely positive. We ask for the smallest modification of the free Schrödinger current that makes it nonnegative for every such state, while preserving the current of each individual momentum component. We show that the required minimal modification changes the free-particle momentum kernel according to \[ K_{\rm Sch}(p,p')=\frac{p+p'}{2m} \;\longrightarrow\; K_{\min}(p,p')=\frac{\sqrt{pp'}}{m}. \] The resulting current is positive and normalized and therefore defines an arrival-time POVM. Extending the directional no-backflow requirement to states containing both momentum signs forces the cross-sector kernel to vanish, \[ K_{\min}^{+-}=K_{\min}^{-+}=0, \] so that the full current is the sum of two independent directional contributions. The resulting POVM is exactly the Kijowski time-of-arrival POVM, providing a current-based physical motivation for both its directional kernels and their separation. Within the diagonal-preserving pairwise-minimal construction considered here, the result is unique. The construction itself does not impose a first-arrival condition.

</details>


### [185] [Kinematic Control and Dephasing Dynamics of Quantum Resources in $e^{+}e^{-}\toτ^{+}τ^{-}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08555)
*Elhabib Jaloum,Omar Bachain,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了e⁺e⁻湮灭产生的τ⁺τ⁻自旋对所携带的量子资源（贝尔非局域性、可操控性、纠缠形成度与相干性）。在Belle-II能区（√s=10.579 GeV），这些资源受产生角度调控，在横向发射（θ=π/2）时达到最大。作者通过将自旋态表达为可分离分量与最大纠缠分量的速度及角度相关混合，揭示了其共同的运动学起源。随后，通过唯象相关退相位信道传播双自旋态，研究了环境记忆效应与信道间经典关联对量子资源的影响，发现记忆效应可产生马尔可夫演化中不存在的坍塌与复兴现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子资源（纠缠、非局域性、可操控性、相干性）在高能粒子物理中的表征与应用具有重要意义，但此前缺乏对τ⁺τ⁻产生过程中这些资源系统性特征化的研究，特别是在Belle-II能区及实际噪声环境下的行为。

Method: 作者在Belle-II能区（√s=10.579 GeV）参数化τ⁺τ⁻对的自旋态，将其表示为速度与角度依赖的可分离与最大纠缠混合态，分析各类量子资源随产生角度的变化；进一步将双自旋态通过具有记忆效应的相关退相位信道传播，评估环境记忆与信道间经典关联对资源演化的影响。

Result: 在√s=10.579 GeV下，贝尔非局域性、可操控性、纠缠形成度和相干性均由产生角度决定，在横向发射θ=π/2时取最大值。混合态表示揭示了从阈值态（√s=2m_τ，弱关联）到超相对论极限下贝尔态逼近的共同运动学起源。噪声信道分析表明，记忆效应产生马尔可夫演化中不存在的坍塌与复兴现象，且信道间经典关联影响资源存续。

Conclusion: 该工作将产生自旋关联的运动学机制与噪声控制其存续的特性进行了分离分析，为高能对撞机中τ轻子量子资源的产生机理与耗散特性提供了统一的理解框架。

Abstract: We characterize several quantum resources carried by the spins of the $τ^{+}τ^{-}$ pair produced in $e^{+}e^{-}$ annihilation. At the Belle-II energy, $\sqrt{s}=10.579\,\mathrm{GeV}$, Bell nonlocality, steerability, entanglement of formation, and coherence are governed by the production angle and are largest for transverse emission, $\vartheta=π/2$. Their common kinematic origin is exposed by expressing the spin state as a velocity and angle-dependent mixture of a separable contribution and a maximally entangled component. Within the physical production domain, this representation connects the weakly correlated threshold state at $\sqrt{s}=2m_τ$ to the Bell-state limit approached at ultrarelativistic energies. We then propagate the two-spin state through a phenomenological correlated-dephasing channel to determine how environmental memory and inter-channel classical correlations affect the available resources. Memory effects generate collapses and revivals that are absent from the monotonic Markovian evolution. The analysis therefore separates the kinematic mechanism that creates the spin correlations from the noise properties that control their subsequent survival.

</details>


### [186] [Multimode squeezed light generation and characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08591)
*Alexander Chudakov,Vladislav Severin,Anastasia Poshevkina,Danil Malyshev,Kirill Kuznetsov,Olga Tikhonova,Dmitry Kalashnikov,Polina Sharapova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对基于连续变量的量子计算与通信中多模压缩光进行了理论与实验研究，重点关注0型和II型光学参量放大器（OPA）产生的多模压缩光，并利用脉冲整形技术研究本振光光谱轮廓对压缩度的影响，基于施密特模理论进行理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子计算与通信因系统维度扩展而备受关注，其进展主要归功于基于压缩光的多模系统实现。然而，零差探测要求本振光轮廓与测量模式精确匹配，实际中往往不满足，因此需要正确处理多模压缩问题。

Method: 作者在理论和实验两个层面研究了由0型和II型OPA产生的多模压缩光，使用脉冲整形技术调控本振光光谱轮廓，并基于施密特模理论对压缩光源及压缩度随光谱轮廓的变化进行理论分析。

Result: 研究表征了两种OPA产生的多模压缩光源，并揭示了本振光光谱轮廓对压缩度的影响规律，理论分析与实验结果相互印证。

Conclusion: 该工作对多模量子协议的实际实现具有重要影响，为多模压缩光在量子计算与通信中的应用提供了理论与实验基础。

Abstract: Nowadays, the realization of quantum computations and communications based on continuous variables has attracted a significant attention due to a substantial expansion of the system dimensionality. The main progress in this area is attributed to the implementation of multimode systems based on squeezed states of light. One of the simplest ways to generate such states relies upon their producing in a single-pass optical parametric amplifier (OPA) using ultrafast pumping. However, for homodyne detection of such multimode states, the profile of the local oscillator (LO) must perfectly match the profile of the measured mode. Usually, this is not the case; therefore a proper treatment of multimode squeezing is required. In this work, we study both theoretically and experimentally the multimode squeezed light generated in type-0 and type-II OPA. We characterize such sources and investigate the degree of squeezing in dependence on the LO spectral profile, employing a pulse shaping technique. The theoretical analysis is performed using the Schmidt-mode theory. This work might have a significant impact on the realization of multimode quantum protocols

</details>


### [187] [Recovering Readout-Limited Fisher Information in Superconducting-Qubit Magnetometry with Squeezed Microwaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08598)
*M. -R. Yun,Y. -J. Qu,Zheng Shan,L. -L. Yan,Yu Jia,S. -L. Su*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文量化了压缩微波辅助色散读测如何恢复超导量子比特磁力计在量子态预分配过程中丢失的磁场信息。通过建立有效探测模框架，连接投影正交噪声、态分配误差与二元读测结果的经典Fisher信息，发现有限的正交失配会产生最优压缩强度，代表参数下可将读测限制的磁场灵敏度界提升27.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子比特磁力计的性能不仅依赖于Ramsey询问期间的磁场编码，还取决于读测阶段对已编码信息的高效恢复。以往研究主要关注提升磁场编码的灵敏度，而读测阶段的信息损失往往被忽视。作者希望量化压缩微波辅助散粒色散读测如何恢复量子态分配过程中丢失的磁场信息。

Method: 作者建立了一个有效探测模（effective detected-mode）理论框架，将投影正交噪声、态分配误差与二元读测结果的经典Fisher信息联系起来。该框架考虑了压缩正交与辨别轴之间的有限失配，揭示了压缩与反压缩涨落之间的竞争如何产生最优压缩强度。

Result: 对于代表性参数，压缩读测可将读测限制的磁场灵敏度界降低27.3%。性能提升源于在读测阶段恢复被丢失的信息，而非增加Ramsey询问期间编码的信息量。

Conclusion: 压缩微波辅助色散读测为缓解超导量子传感中测量阶段信息损失提供了一条实用路径，表明优化读测策略本身即可显著提升磁力计的整体灵敏度。

Abstract: The performance of superconducting-qubit magnetometers depends not only on magnetic-field encoding during Ramsey interrogation, but also on how efficiently the encoded information is recovered during readout. Here we quantify how squeezed-microwave-assisted dispersive readout can recover magnetic-field information lost during qubit-state assignment. We develop an effective detected-mode framework linking projected quadrature noise, state-assignment error, and the classical Fisher information accessible from binary readout outcomes. A finite mismatch between the squeezed quadrature and the discrimination axis produces an optimal squeezing strength through the competition between squeezed and anti-squeezed fluctuations. For representative parameters, squeezed readout reduces the readout-limited magnetic-field sensitivity bound by $27.3\%$. This improvement arises from recovering information lost in the readout stage rather than from increasing the information encoded during Ramsey interrogation. These results may provide a practical route for mitigating measurement-stage information loss in superconducting quantum sensing.

</details>


### [188] [Finite-size Entanglement Certification via Third-Order Local Randomized Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08635)
*Giovanni Scala,Corrado Cosimo Mattiacci,Dorota Pietryka,Gniewomir Sarbicki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三阶局部随机测量的有限尺寸纠缠证明方法，相较于传统的二阶相关测量，能够检测到更深层的纠缠信息，同时通过维度无关的集中界限严格控制有限样本误差，避免了全状态断层扫描带来的维度相关开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维系统中的纠缠认证通常需要全状态断层扫描，其成本随系统维度快速增长。虽然局部随机测量提供了可扩展的替代方案，但基于二阶相关性的现有测试只能获取状态有限的信息，无法检测某些纠缠态。

Method: 将局部随机测量扩展到三阶，推导出一个有限尺寸纠缠证明方案，利用三阶信息揭示二阶测量无法检测的纠缠，并建立维度无关的浓度界限以严格控制有限样本误差。

Result: 该方法能够在二阶检测失效的情况下仍然检测到纠缠，同时保持维度无关的误差控制，为实验平台提取更强的纠缠信息提供了可行的路径。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种实用且可扩展的高维纠缠认证方法，在不引入状态断层扫描的维度相关开销的前提下，实现了比二阶方法更强的纠缠检测能力。

Abstract: Certifying entanglement in high-dimensional systems usually requires full state tomography, whose cost grows rapidly with the system dimension. Local randomized measurements offer a scalable alternative, but existing tests based on second-order correlations access only limited information about the state. Here, we derive a finite-size entanglement certificate that extends local randomized measurements to third order. The additional third-order information reveals entanglement that remains undetected at second order, while a dimension-independent concentration bound provides rigorous control of finite-sample errors. Our result opens a practical route to extracting stronger entanglement information from experimental platforms without the dimension-dependent overhead of state tomography.

</details>


### [189] [Causal-Class Hierarchies in Coherence-Constrained Channel Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08639)
*Lin Zhu,Benchi Zhao,Xuanqiang Zhao,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang,Shenggen Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了受相干性资源理论约束的高阶量子变换中，因果结构（并行、固定顺序序列、一般高阶过程）是否能提升通道变换性能。针对双量子比特振幅阻尼通道，证明了在最大不相干超通道（MISC）和退相协变不相干超通道（DISC）下，任意非平凡阻尼强度都存在严格的因果层级；而混合泡利通道因可进行远程传输模拟（将通道依赖性转移到并行制备的Bell对角程序态），导致各类因果策略达到相同最优误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶量子变换（如并行、固定顺序序列、一般高阶过程）已被广泛研究，但当高阶操作受资源理论（如相干性资源理论）约束时，因果自由度是否能提升通道变换性能的问题尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用统一的半定规划（SDP）框架研究动力学相干性资源理论；具体分析了两类受约束的超通道——最大不相干超通道（MISC）和退相协变不相干超通道（DISC），对比并行、固定顺序序列和一般高阶因果策略下的通道变换最优误差。

Result: （1）对于双量子比特振幅阻尼通道和目标为恒等通道的情形，在MISC和DISC两种约束下，任意非平凡阻尼强度均存在严格的因果层级（并行&lt;固定顺序&lt;一般高阶）；（2）混合泡利通道可通过公共的远程传输模拟将通道依赖转移到并行制备的Bell对角程序态上，其余处理可吸收到单个量子演算中，因此并行、固定顺序序列和一般高阶策略可实现相同的最优误差。

Conclusion: 识别出自由程序态并行化是因果增强的结构性障碍：当通道允许公共的远程传输模拟且程序态可并行制备时，不同因果架构的性能差异消失；反之，振幅阻尼通道在相干性资源约束下展现严格的因果层级，表明因果自由度确实可在受资源约束的高阶变换中发挥增强作用。

Abstract: Higher-order quantum transformations allow multiple channel uses to be combined through different causal architectures, from parallel and fixed-order sequential networks to general higher-order processes. Whether this causal freedom improves channel transformation when the higher-order operation is also constrained by a resource theory remains largely unexplored. We study this question in the dynamical resource theory of coherence using a unified semidefinite-programming framework. For two qubit amplitude-damping channels and the identity target, we prove a strict causal hierarchy at every nontrivial damping strength under both maximally incoherent superchannels (MISC) and dephasing-covariant incoherent superchannels (DISC). In contrast, mixed-Pauli channels admit a common teleportation simulation that transfers the channel dependence to Bell-diagonal program states prepared in parallel. The remaining processing can then be absorbed into a single quantum operational, so parallel, fixed-order sequential, and general higher-order strategies achieve the same optimal error for any target. These results identify free program-state parallelisation as a structural obstruction to causal enhancement.

</details>


### [190] [Closing the Loop: Non-Causal Computation, Partial Traces, & Postselected Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08716)
*Mark Carney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为Baumeler & Wolf的逻辑自洽非因果电路提供了范畴论解释，将其与后选择量子隐形传态联系起来。通过在非负矩阵范畴中表示循环反馈为迹，证明了当诱导的循环转移矩阵对每个外部输入都有迹为1时过程是随机的，这等价于确定性电路每个输入对应唯一不动点。经典非因果电路被表示为利用最大纠缠贝尔态与后选择、含内部寄存器的测量-制备量子信道。核心结果表明经典逻辑自洽等价于后选择贝尔结果在循环维度d下对每个经典输入分布的概率恰好为1/d²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为理论上允许的"非因果电路"（信息可在时间或因果方向上循环流动）建立更严格的数学基础。Baumeler & Wolf提出的逻辑一致性条件虽然解决了非因果电路中悖论的问题，但其数学结构的本质尚未被充分理解。通过范畴论和量子信息论的视角重新解读，可以揭示这类电路与量子物理学中已知现象（如量子隐形传态中的后选择）之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者在非负矩阵范畴中使用范畴论迹来表示环路反馈结构。经典非因果电路被建模为"测量-制备"量子信道，其中包含内部寄存器并利用最大纠缠贝尔态进行后选择。通过将经典逻辑一致性条件（每个输入对应唯一不动点、循环转移矩阵迹为1）与量子后选择贝尔结果的概率（1/d²）建立等价关系，证明了规范化的条件输出与经典范畴迹完全一致。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 在非负矩阵范畴中被迹化的过程是随机的，当且仅当诱导的循环转移矩阵对每个外部输入的迹均为1；(2) 该条件等价于确定性电路对每个输入存在唯一不动点；(3) 经典逻辑自洽性等价于后选择贝尔结果在后选择前对每个经典输入分布的概率恰好为1/d²；(4) 规范化的条件输出与经典范畴迹精确吻合。

Conclusion: 本文通过连接经典非因果电路与后选择量子隐形传态，识别出一类贝尔后选择量子构造，其条件演化保持对经典输入分布的线性依赖。这种联系不仅为Baumeler & Wolf的逻辑一致性框架提供了全新的数学结构视角，也为后续研究非因果量子过程在理论上的可行性及边界条件奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper gives a categorical interpretation of Baumeler \& Wolf's logically consistent non-causal circuits, connecting them to postselected quantum teleportation. Looped feedback is represented by a trace in the category of non-negative matrices, and it is shown that the traced process is stochastic precisely when the induced loop transition matrix has trace $1$ for every external input, a condition shown to be equivalent to a unique fixed point for the loop for each input to a deterministic circuit. A classical non-causal circuit is represented by a measure-and-prepare quantum channel with an internal register utilising a maximally entangled Bell-state with postselection. The main result is that classical logical consistency is equivalent to the postselected Bell outcome having, for loop dimension $d$, probability exactly $1/d^2$ for each classical input distribution. The subsequent normalised conditional output then agrees exactly with the classical categorical trace. This work identifies a class of quantum Bell-postselection constructions whose conditional evolution maintains linear dependence on classical input distributions.

</details>


### [191] [Adaptive AI for Pulse-Level Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08727)
*Sanjeev Shapkota,Yayu Mo,Sanjaya Lohani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度Q学习网络的强化学习方法，用于替代传统控制变分量子本征求解器（ctrl-VQE）中的连续脉冲优化，实现超导量子平台上高保真度的量子态制备，并以氢分子基态制备为概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ctrl-VQE直接优化微波脉冲以实现更快、更低误差的量子态制备，但其连续控制景观需要高效的搜索策略。现有的优化方法在连续控制空间中效率低下，需要一种能够自主发现高性能脉冲序列的智能搜索方法。

Method: 采用基于深度Q学习网络的强化学习智能体，仅使用系统参数和奖励函数即可自主发现高性能脉冲序列。该方法完全适用于超导量子比特平台，无需ansatz，且工作在纳秒级分辨率，兼容硬件约束。在模拟超导设备上对氢分子基态制备进行概念验证。

Result: 智能体能够持续识别出优化的控制序列，实现高保真度，并优于随机搜索基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明自适应学习是面向硬件就绪的脉冲级量子控制的可行框架，为量子态制备中的脉冲优化提供了一种无需先验假设的有效途径。

Abstract: The Control Variational Quantum Eigensolver (ctrl-VQE) directly optimizes microwave pulses to enable faster and lower-error quantum-state preparation, but its continuous control landscape re- quires efficient search strategies. We demonstrate that a reinforcement-learning agent based on a deep Q learning network can autonomously discover high-performance pulse sequences using only system parameters and a reward function. The approach is fully general for superconducting qubit platforms, requires no ansatz, and operates at nanosecond resolution compatible with hardware con- straints. As a proof of concept, we apply the method to ground-state preparation of the Hydrogen molecule on a simulated superconducting device. The agent consistently identifies optimized control sequences that achieve high fidelity and outperform random-search baselines. These results highlight adaptive learning as a promising hardware-ready framework for pulse-level quantum control.

</details>


### [192] [On the Power of Adaptivity in Testing Quantum States in Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08733)
*Jan Seyfried,Sayantan Sen,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子状态认证、等价测试和独立性测试在保真度（fidelity）决策间隙下的样本复杂度问题。作者证明：对于秩为r的状态σ，认证任务不受自适应性的帮助，需要Θ̃(r^{3/2}/ε)个样本；而等价测试和独立性测试则可以通过自适应算法获得改进。关键技术是一种结合部分学习和ℓ₂距离测试的框架，改编自经典分布测试文献。作者还证明了在保真度下等价测试中自适应性能带来收益——即使对于量子比特，非自适应情况也需要Ω̃(1/ε²)个样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在迹距离（trace distance）下，量子状态认证、等价测试和独立性测试这三个问题的样本复杂度已被充分理解，且均可通过同一非自适应方法以Θ(d^{3/2}/ε²)个样本解决。然而在保真度（fidelity）作为决策距离时，这些问题的复杂度尚未被充分研究。本文的核心动机是探究在保真度度量下这些任务之间是否存在复杂度的分离，以及自适应测量策略能否带来收益。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于部分学习和ℓ₂距离测试的通用框架。该框架分为两步：首先对未知量子状态进行部分学习（partial learning），获取其重要特征的近似信息；然后将原有的保真度测试问题转化为在ℓ₂距离下的测试问题。作者将此框架分别应用于等价测试和独立性测试，并针对不同参数区间选择不同的学习与测试策略来优化样本复杂度。此外，通过构造信息论下界来证明认证问题的非自适应最优性以及等价测试中自适应性的必要性。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 在保真度下，秩为r的状态的认证不需要自适应，样本复杂度为Θ̃(r^{3/2}/ε)；(2) 等价测试的样本复杂度为Õ(min{d^{3/2}/ε², d^{9/4}/ε})；(3) 独立性测试（d_A≥d_C）的样本复杂度为Õ(min{(d_Ad_C)^{3/2}/ε², d_A^{9/4}d_C^{3/4}/ε})；(4) 在保真度下证明等价测试中存在非自适应与自适应的分离：即使对于量子比特，非自适应情况下也需要Ω̃(1/ε²)个样本。

Conclusion: 在保真度度量下，量子状态认证、等价测试和独立性测试呈现出不同于迹距离情形的新特性。认证在保真度下与迹距离下的最优样本复杂度结构不同（依赖状态σ的秩r而非全维度d），且不受自适应性影响；但等价测试却能从自适应测量中获益，在非自适应与自适应之间存在分离。这揭示了不同问题在保真度度量下的根本差异，并为量子假设检验提供了新的理论边界。

Abstract: We study the problems of quantum state certification, equivalence testing and independence testing. In certification, given samples of an unknown quantum state $ρ$ and the description of a state $σ$, the goal is to test whether $ρ=σ$, or whether $ρ$ and $σ$ are far in a given distance measure. In equivalence testing, $σ$ is also unknown and only accessible via samples. Independence testing decides whether $ρ_{AC}=ρ_A\otimesρ_C$, or is far from being a product. The sample complexities of these problems are now well-understood for a decision gap $\varepsilon$ in trace distance: in the single-copy measurement setting with $d$-dimensional states, all three tasks can be solved using the same non-adaptive approach, which uses $Θ(d^{3/2}/\varepsilon^2)$ samples and is optimal in general, even without adaptivity.
  In this work, we consider decision gaps expressed in fidelity and study possible separations between these problems and how adaptivity can help. We prove that certification with respect to fidelity for a state $σ$ of rank $r$ does not benefit from adaptivity and requires $\widetildeΘ(r^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$ samples. For equivalence testing and independence testing, we provide adaptive algorithms using $\widetilde{O}(\min\{d^{3/2}/\varepsilon^2,d^{9/4}/\varepsilon\})$ and $\widetilde{O}(\min\{(d_Ad_C)^{3/2}/\varepsilon^2,d_A^{9/4}d_C^{3/4}/\varepsilon\})$ samples, for $d_A\geq d_C$, respectively. Our main technique is a framework that uses partial learning and a reduction to testing in $\ell_2$-distance, adapted from the distribution testing literature. We show that adaptivity matters for equivalence testing in fidelity by proving that $\widetildeΩ(1/\varepsilon^2)$ samples are necessary in the non-adaptive case even for qubits, showing a separation from certification.

</details>


### [193] [Uniqueness and Cramér-Rao Efficiency of Quantum U-Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08745)
*Ayanava Dasgupta,Naqueeb Ahmad Warsi,Premanshu Chatterjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于独立量子态副本对量子态标量多项式泛函进行无偏估计的理论框架，建立了置换不变有限副本可观测量的边际与泛函梯度之间的等价关系，并证明量子U统计量是任意副本数下置换不变无偏估计的唯一扩展。文章推导了普适方差展开，其中主导的1/n项由泛函梯度的方差决定，高阶项为O(1/n²)。该主导方差与多参数量子Cramér-Rao极限一致，确立了量子U统计量的渐近有效性。文章还刻画了梯度一阶方差为零时的高阶标度行为，并以Bures χ²散度为例进行了应用分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态标量多项式泛函的无偏估计是量子信息与量子计量学中的基础问题。现有研究缺乏对这类泛函在独立副本设置下的系统性估计理论，特别是关于估计子的最优性、唯一性以及渐近效率的完整刻画。

Method: 本文通过建立置换不变可观测量的边际与泛函梯度的等价关系，利用量子U统计量作为无偏估计子的自然拓展框架，推导普适的方差展开，并与量子Cramér-Rao界限进行对照分析。

Result: 获得了四个主要理论结果：(1) 一阶边际与泛函梯度等价；(2) 量子U统计量是置换不变无偏估计的唯一任意副本扩展；(3) 普适方差展开中主导项为O(1/n)，与多参数量子Cramér-Rao极限一致，确立渐近效率；(4) 在梯度一阶方差消失处刻画了高阶O(1/n²)标度行为。应用层面，对Bures χ²散度的分析表明参考态的光谱下界是方差有界估计的充分但非必要条件。

Conclusion: 量子U统计量在无偏置换不变估计框架下具有唯一性和渐近最优性（达到量子Cramér-Rao界），形成了系统的理论体系。Bures χ²散度的例子揭示了已有条件判断的局限性，为一般量子泛函估计问题提供了新的理论工具和深入见解。

Abstract: We study unbiased estimation of scalar-valued polynomial functionals of quantum states from independent copies. We establish an equivalence between the first-order marginal of a permutation-invariant finite-copy observable and the functional gradient. We then prove that, among unbiased permutation-invariant estimators, the quantum U-statistic is the unique extension to an arbitrary number of copies. We further derive a universal variance expansion in which the leading $1/n$ term is determined by the variance of the functional gradient, while higher-order contributions are of order $O(1/n^2)$. This leading variance coincides with the multiparameter quantum Cramér-Rao limit, establishing asymptotic efficiency of quantum U-statistics. We also characterize the higher-order scaling at points where the variance of the first-order gradient vanishes. As an application, we analyze the Bures $χ^2$-divergence and show that a spectral lower bound on the reference state is sufficient but not necessary for bounded-variance estimation.

</details>


### [194] [Quantum speed limits based on quantifiers of quantum-state texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08752)
*Yuhang Xie,Yanjun Chu,Chenyang Cui,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子速度极限与量子态纹理（quantum-state texture）之间的相互关系，通过迹距离、态粗糙度和Jensen-Shannon散度等量化指标，分析了系统在退相和耗散动力学下的最小演化时间，并探讨了由完全正保迹映射描述的非幺正动力学。结果表明，基于量子态纹理导出的量子速度极限能够捕捉量子演化速度的基本约束，在量子计算、量子控制和量子计量等领域具有潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子速度极限定义了量子系统演化的最短时间下限，是量子理论中的基本概念；而量子态纹理作为量子资源理论中的一个新兴范式，近年来引起了广泛研究兴趣。然而，两者之间的关系尚未被系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白，探索量子态纹理如何刻画量子演化的速度约束。

Method: 研究选取了三种典型的量子态纹理度量——迹距离（trace distance）、态粗糙度（state rugosity）和Jensen-Shannon散度——作为量化工具，系统推导相应的量子速度极限下界。通过在退相（dephasing）和耗散（dissipative）动力学下分析物理系统的最小演化时间，验证所提边界的有效性；对于Jensen-Shannon散度情形，进一步研究了由完全正保迹（CPTP）映射描述的非幺正动力学，并以振幅阻尼信道为典型实例进行详细分析，同时评估了这些界在相应动力学模型中的紧致性。

Result: 研究表明，基于量子态纹理构建的量子速度极限可以有效地刻画量子态演化的最短时间尺度。针对Jensen-Shannon散度在振幅阻尼信道中的分析验证了该度量在CPTP映射下的适用性。此外，对各动力学模型下的时间界进行了紧致性评估，揭示了不同纹理度量所导出的速度极限的松紧程度差异。

Conclusion: 量子速度极限与量子态纹理之间存在深刻的联系，前者可以视为后者在动力学层面上的体现。基于量子态纹理导出的速度极限能够捕捉量子系统演化的根本约束，为量子计算、量子控制和量子计量等领域提供了新的理论工具和应用前景。

Abstract: Quantum speed limits impose intrinsic lower bounds on the shortest time scale for quantum system evolution. As an emerging paradigm in quantum resource theory, quantum-state texture has attracted research interest amid the rapid advancement of quantum theory. Herein, we investigate the interplay between quantum speed limits and quantum-state texture via several canonical quantifiers, including trace distance, state rugosity and Jensen-Shannon divergence. To demonstrate our findings, we analyze the minimum evolution time of physical systems subject to dephasing and dissipative dynamics. For the Jensen-Shannon divergence, we further explore nonunitary dynamics described by completely positive and trace-preserving maps, taking the amplitude damping channel as a typical example. In addition, we explore the tightness of these bounds in the considered dynamical models. Our results reveal that quantum speed limits derived from quantum-state texture capture the fundamental constraints on quantum evolutionary speed, with promising applications in quantum computing, quantum control and quantum metrology.

</details>


### [195] [Dual-Trigger of Series Nanowire Detector for Event-Based Photon Number Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08811)
*Stefanie Grotowski,Daniel Landes,Fabian Wietschorke,Paul Pucknus,Rasmus Flaschmann,Torsten Langer,Torsten Krause,Kai Müller,Jonathan J. Finley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双触发读出方法，应用于三像素串联超导纳米线单光子探测器（SNSPD），通过同时触发检测脉冲的上升沿和下降沿，将光子数信息映射到上升沿与下降沿之间的时间间隔上，从而在保持高精度到达时间的同时实现光子数分辨（n=1,2,3），三类事件的后验置信度均达到99%。研究还发现定时抖动随光子数n增加而减小（n≥2时低于41 ps），并比较了边缘触发与恒定分数甄别（CFD）在到达时间提取方面的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单通道边缘触发读出方式难以实时分辨光子数n，也无法表征定时抖动对n的依赖关系。然而光子数分辨（PNR）探测器是光子量子技术中的关键组件，因此需要一种既能保持高精度到达时间又能实时分辨光子数的读出方案。

Method: 采用双触发方法，在三像素串联SNSPD上同时触发检测脉冲的上升沿和下降沿。上升沿提供精确的光子到达时间，而上升沿与下降沿之间的时间间隔映射光子数n，从而实现事件分类。同时将双触发边缘读出与恒定分数甄别（CFD）方法在到达时间提取性能上进行对比。

Result: 该方法成功区分n=1,2,3三类光子事件，每类后验置信度达99%。定时抖动随光子数n增加而降低，n≥2时抖动低于41 ps。CFD方法对单光子事件产生更低的抖动以及几乎恒定的平均到达时间，优于边缘触发方法。

Conclusion: 双触发读出被证实是一种稳健、低延迟的PNR探测器读出方案，同时揭示了定时抖动对光子数的依赖关系，这对预置光子量子应用中可实现的定时精度具有重要意义。

Abstract: Photon-number-resolving (PNR) detectors are essential components of photonic quantum technologies. However, conventional single-channel edge-triggered readout struggles to resolve the photon number $n$ in real time or to characterize how timing jitter depends on $n$. In this work, we use a dual-trigger method on a three-pixel series-connected superconducting nanowire single-photon detector (SNSPD) that triggers on both the rising and falling edges of the detection pulse. By doing so, we preserve the precise arrival time of the detection event while mapping the photon number onto the time interval between the rising and falling edges, allowing clear separation of the events. Using this technique, we assign each detection event to $n = 1, 2,$ or 3 photons with $99\,\%$ posterior confidence across all three classes. The timing jitter decreases as $n$ increases, reaching values below $41\, \text{ps}$ for $n \geq 2$. Comparing edge-triggering with constant-fraction discrimination (CFD) for arrival-time extraction, we find that CFD yields lower jitter for single-photon events and a nearly constant mean arrival time. Altogether, our results establish dual-triggering as a robust, low-latency readout scheme for PNR detectors, while revealing a photon-number dependence of the timing jitter relevant to timing precision achievable in heralded photonic quantum applications.

</details>


### [196] [Distributed Quantum Property Testing with Quantum Carrier Pigeons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08864)
*Kenny Chen,Mina Doosti,Ryan Sweke,Chirag Wadhwa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通信受限下的分布式量子推断框架，其中m个分布式节点各自接收一个未知d维量子态ρ的副本，通过受限的单向通信信道与中心节点通信，中心节点旨在推断ρ的某些性质。作者聚焦于量子态认证问题，证明了在共享随机源下拷贝复杂度为Θ(d²/(2^{n_q}2^{n_c/2}ε²))，并证明共享随机性是必要的，私有硬币设置下界为Ω(d³/(4^{n_q}2^{n_c}ε²))，同时给出了匹配至√log d因子的私有硬币算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将Acharya、Canonne和Tyagi在经典分布式推断框架推广到量子领域，考虑量子通信和共享纠缠等量子资源，以在通信受限的条件下建立分布式量子推断的一般性理论框架，并刻画分布式量子态认证问题的复杂度特征。

Method: 本文建立了一个分布式量子推断框架，其中m个节点各持有一个量子态副本，经受限信道（n_c比特+n_q量子比特，且n_c+n_q≤log d）传输给中心节点。针对量子态认证问题，作者分别在公共硬币（共享随机源）和私有硬币两种设置下分析通信约束，推导拷贝复杂度的上下界，并设计相应的算法与下界证明技术。

Result: 在共享随机源下，分布式量子态认证的拷贝复杂度为Θ(d²/(2^{n_q}2^{n_c/2}ε²))。证明共享随机性在此复杂度中不可或缺，私有硬币设置下界为Ω(d³/(4^{n_q}2^{n_c}ε²))。此外，作者还构造了一个私有硬币算法，其复杂度与该下界在√log d因子内匹配，证明该复杂度接近最优。

Conclusion: 本文建立了通信约束下分布式量子推断的一般框架，完整刻画了分布式量子态认证在有限通信条件下的复杂度，揭示了共享随机性和量子通信资源对拷贝复杂度的深刻影响，并为后续分布式量子推断研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce a framework for distributed quantum inference under communication constraints. In our model, $m$ distributed nodes each receive one copy of an unknown $d$-dimensional quantum state $ρ$, before communicating via a constrained one-way communication channel with a central node, which aims to infer some property of $ρ$. This framework generalizes the classical distributed inference framework introduced by Acharya, Canonne, and Tyagi [COLT 2019], by allowing quantum resources such as quantum communication and shared entanglement.
  Within this setting, we focus on the fundamental problem of quantum state certification: Given a complete description of some state $σ$, decide whether $ρ=σ$ or $\|ρ-σ\|_1\geq ε$. Additionally, we focus on the case of limited communication between distributed nodes and the central node: we assume each communication channel is limited to only $n_c$ bits and $n_q$ qubits with $n_c + n_q \leq \log d$. When all nodes can make use of a shared source of randomness, we show that the copy complexity of distributed state certification is $Θ(\frac{d^2}{2^{n_q} 2^{n_c/2}ε^2})$. We further demonstrate that shared randomness is necessary to achieve the above complexity, by proving an $Ω(\frac{d^3}{4^{n_q} 2^{n_c} ε^2})$ lower bound in the $\textit{private-coin}$ setting. Moreover, we develop a private-coin algorithm that matches this bound up to a $\sqrt{\log d}$ factor, showing this complexity is near-optimal. Together, our work establishes a general framework for distributed quantum inference with communication constraints and characterizes the complexity of distributed state certification with limited communication.

</details>


### [197] [A Platform-aware Compilation Framework for Fault-tolerant Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08908)
*Srushti Patil,Susan X. Chen,Andreas Juul Bay-Smidt,Stefan Alaric Schäffer,Peter Krogstrup,Stefano Paesani,Gemma C. Solomon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个平台感知的量子编译框架，能将量子电路重编译为特定硬件兼容的指令集与容错操作，并提供端到端资源估算（物理量子比特数、求解时间、经典处理时间）。框架引入了面向长程逻辑连通性平台的transversal active volume (t-AV)编译架构，并针对2D Fermi Hubbard模型哈密顿模拟和三亚甲基甲烷分子特征能量估计进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化算法的编译结果会随物理硬件平台和纠错模型的不同而显著变化，然而现有编译框架通常只针对单一的架构-硬件配置进行优化，难以跨平台评估资源需求，限制了不同硬件方案的公平比较与资源规划的准确性。

Method: 提出平台感知的编译框架，将量子电路重编译为与硬件兼容的指令集及容错操作，提供物理量子比特数、时间到解和经典处理时间三个维度的端到端资源估算。引入transversal active volume（t-AV）编译架构以支持具备长程逻辑连通性平台的容错操作高效执行。针对不同硬件模态（连通性、时钟速度、噪声模型）定制不同的编译器进行基准测试。

Result: 针对2D Fermi Hubbard模型的哈密顿模拟以及三亚甲基甲烷分子特征能量估计（早期容错量子化学演示候选）进行基准测试，后者表明端到端量子模拟仅需约10^4个物理量子比特，运行时间从10^2毫秒（光子学、超导平台）到10^5毫秒（中性原子平台）不等。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了跨不同硬件平台进行端到端资源估算的可行性，揭示了硬件选择对量子算法资源需求的关键影响，为早期容错量子计算的应用演示提供了实用的规划工具。

Abstract: The compilation of an algorithm can vary significantly with the choice of physical hardware platform and error correction model. Yet, current compilation frameworks typically commit to a single architecture-hardware configuration, making it difficult to assess resource estimates across platforms. We present a platform-aware compilation framework that re-compiles a quantum circuit into a hardware-compatible instruction set as well as fault-tolerant operations and provides end-to-end resource estimates in terms of physical-qubit count, time-to-solution, and classical processing time. We benchmark the framework by obtaining end-to-end resource estimates for different compilers, each tailored to the functionalities of specific hardware modalities: connectivity, clock speed, and noise model. As part of this framework, we introduce a transversal active volume (t-AV) compilation architecture designed for the efficient execution of fault-tolerant operations in platforms supporting long-range logical connectivity. We benchmark the framework for Hamiltonian simulation of the 2D Fermi Hubbard model as well as for eigenenergy estimation of a small molecule (trimethylenemethane) as a candidate for early fault-tolerant demonstration of quantum chemistry. For the latter, we show that end-to-end quantum simulations can be achieved with $\sim10^4$ physical qubits and runtimes ranging from $10^2$ ms (photonics, superconducting) to $10^5$ ms (neutral atoms).

</details>


### [198] [Resilience Beyond the Light Cone: Error-Detected Primitives for Practical Dynamic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08925)
*Kevin C. Smith,Bibek Pokharel,Satvik Maurya,Maika Takita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对动态电路原语的通用错误检测方案，通过权衡保真度与后选择开销（无需额外辅助量子比特），统一并升级了包括扇出门、多量子比特泡利旋转、W态与Dicke态制备等在内的多类基元，并在超导量子处理器上实验验证了该方案的实际效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态电路通过中间测量与经典前馈可以突破酉电路的"光锥"限制，在恒定深度下生成长程纠缠，但代价是需要数量随系统尺寸呈线性增长的中间测量，且测量与前馈延迟会引入错误，破坏长程纠缠的保真度。本文旨在缓解这一矛盾，找到无需额外辅助量子比特即可检测错误、提升保真度的通用方案。

Method: 作者将多类动态电路基元统一到一个共同框架中，证明它们可以内置错误检测机制：通过将"不忠实的实现"转化为可检测的错误（以保真度为代价换取后选择开销），无需额外辅助量子比特即可实现错误检测。框架覆盖扇出门、多量子比特泡利旋转、W态及更高权重Dicke态制备、特定非正态矩阵乘积态制备等；同时引入了一种降低深度的错误检测Hadamard测试实现。

Result: 在IBM超导量子处理器上的实验表明：(1) 错误检测方案成功制备了横跨100量子比特链的长程纠缠Bell对，保真度F=0.59±0.02，超过纠缠认证阈值F>0.5，而基线动态电路实现仅为0.39±0.01未达阈值；(2) 通过消耗最多40量子比特的GHZ态，实现了最多20量子比特W态的恒定深度制备，最大尺寸下绝对保真度提升约ΔF≈0.2。

Conclusion: 该框架使低深度动态电路基元在实际量子硬件上变得切实可行，为克服动态电路因测量错误导致的性能退化提供了通用且高效的实用路径。

Abstract: Dynamic circuits, which augment unitary operations with mid-circuit measurements and classical feedforward, can generate long-range entanglement in constant depth, enabling low-depth primitives ranging from nontrivial state preparation to many-qubit entangling gates. Escaping the light-cone constraints of unitary circuits, however, comes at a cost: these primitives typically require a number of mid-circuit measurements that scales with system size and that, together with feedforward latency, can introduce errors that degrade the long-range entanglement they rely on. Here, we alleviate this tension by showing that many such primitives, when cast into a common framework, admit an error-detection scheme that trades infidelity for postselection overhead with no additional ancillas. Our framework thus unifies and upgrades a broad class of primitives including fan-out gates, multi-qubit Pauli rotations, the preparation of W and higher-weight Dicke states, and certain non-normal matrix product states. We also introduce a reduced-depth, error-detected implementation of the Hadamard test, extending the use cases of dynamic circuits to a key algorithmic primitive. Finally, we establish the practical utility of our scheme through experiments on a superconducting quantum processor. We demonstrate the error-detected preparation of a long-range entangled Bell pair spanning a 100-qubit chain with fidelity $F=0.59\pm0.02$, surpassing the entanglement-certification threshold $F>0.5$ that the baseline dynamic-circuit implementation fails to reach ($0.39\pm0.01$). Separately, we demonstrate constant-depth preparation of W states of up to 20 qubits by consuming GHZ states of up to 40 qubits, finding absolute fidelity improvements of $ΔF\approx 0.2$ across the largest sizes studied. Altogether, these results bring low-depth dynamic-circuit primitives within practical reach on present-day hardware.

</details>


### [199] [First fault-tolerant quantum memory demonstration for a generalized superfast encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08957)
*James Brown,Kenny Heitritter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义超快速编码（GSE）的一种偶数距离d的常数量子位权稳定子构造，其中每个费米子模被分配到一个d量子位环状块上。该构造的稳定子生成元权重恒为4或6，整个稳定子集可划分为四个量子位对易的群，便于紧凑的伴随式提取调度。通过对[[48,8,6]]和[[64,8,8]]两种码的量子内存实验仿真，观察到约4×10⁻³的阈值，这是首次对费米子映射实现具有阈值行为容错量子内存的表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义超快速编码（GSE）是一种具有纠错/检错性质的费米子到量子位映射，但此前所有演示仅限于检错层面，在电路级噪声下从未观察到容错性。作者旨在通过新的构造使GSE具备真正的容错能力，从而弥合费米子编码与稳定子容错间的鸿沟。

Method: 引入偶数距离d的常权重GSE构造：将N个模各分配到一个d量子位环状块上，得到权重恒为4或6的稳定子生成元（对任意偶数d）。将完整稳定子集划分为四个两两量子位对易的群以简化伴随式提取调度。随后在电路级去极化噪声下对[[48,8,6]]和[[64,8,8]]两种码实例进行量子内存实验仿真。

Result: 两种码实例的量子内存实验仿真显示阈值为约4×10⁻³，这是首次在费米子映射类编码中观察到具有阈值缩放行为的容错量子内存指标。

Conclusion: 该工作首次证明了费米子-量子位映射（GSE）可在电路级噪声下实现真正的容错性能，其常权重稳定子结构与四群划分特性为实际容错实现提供了可行路径，为费米子编码在量子纠错中的应用开辟了新方向。

Abstract: The Generalized Superfast Encoding (GSE) is a fermion-to-qubit mapping that has error-correcting/detecting properties. To this point, all demonstrations have been relegated to error-detection only, as no fault-tolerance under circuit-level noise has been observed. Here, we introduce an even-distance $d$ constant stabilizer-weight GSE where each of $N$ modes is assigned a $d$-qubit block arranged on a ring. The resulting stabilizer generators have constant weight 4 or 6 for any even distance $d$. Furthermore, the full stabilizer set of this construction can always be partitioned into four qubit-wise commuting groups, which enables compact syndrome-extraction scheduling. We simulate quantum memory experiments under circuit-level depolarizing noise for two instances of this code, $[[48,8,6]]$ and $[[64,8,8]]$ and the threshold is observed to be $\approx 4\times10^{-3}$. This is, to our knowledge, the first fault-tolerant quantum-memory characterization of a fermion-mapping with threshold-like scaling.

</details>


### [200] [Restart and first detection in a lackadaisical quantum walk with flat-band localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08973)
*Debraj Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维"懒散"离散时间量子行走（含自环权重ℓ）在随机重启和锐利重启下的动力学。发现平坦带活性态与平坦带暗态在重启下表现出截然不同的行为：对于几何随机重启，平稳均方位移以q⁻²发散；平坦带活性态的占据概率趋向固有局域值，而暗态则按q ln(1/q)衰减。对于幂律重启，仅当s>2时存在归一化平稳占据分布。此外，研究了带锐利重启的监测首次探测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子行走是量子信息与量子模拟的重要工具，而重启机制已被证明可以改变量子系统的统计特性。本文关注的"懒散"量子行走具有平坦带导致的固有局域化特性，这一结构使得重启效应与以往研究的能带结构不同。此前重启研究多集中于简单量子行走，缺少对平坦带系统——尤其是平坦带活性态与暗态的区分——在重启下的系统分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用解析方法研究一维懒散离散时间量子行走在两种重启协议下的行为：（1）几何随机重启，每步以概率q重启；（2）幂律重启，等待时间分布为p_m∝m⁻ˢ；（3）锐利重启下的监测首次探测，即连续r次未探测到则重新初始化。作者选取两个初始态：平坦带活性态（具有有限平坦带重叠）和平坦带暗态（平坦带重叠为零），分析其平稳占据分布、空间矩和首次探测时间等统计量。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）几何随机重启下，平稳均方位移在q→0时以q⁻²发散；平坦带活性态的重启位占据概率趋于无重启时的固有局域化值，而暗态以q ln(1/q)趋于零；（2）幂律重启下，平稳占据分布的标准化仅对s>2成立，p阶空间矩仅在s>p+2时有限；在1<s≤2区间，活性态在每格点的占据收敛到固有平坦带分布，暗态趋于零；（3）在固定r的锐利重启首次探测中，平坦带活性态的均值首次探测时间存在自环权重ℓ的极小值，而暗态在ℓ→∞时趋于弹道探测极限。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了平坦带背景下的量子行走在重启下呈现丰富的统计行为，最重要的是发现平坦带活性态（由固有局域化主导）与平坦带暗态（仅由扩散带贡献）在重启下表现出截然不同的标度行为，这表明平坦带的内在结构对重启稳定性有着本质性的影响，为理解和控制量子行走的局域化提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study stochastic and sharp restart in a one-dimensional lackadaisical discrete-time quantum walk with self-loop weight $\ell$. In the absence of restart, the dynamics has a flat band responsible for intrinsic localization and two dispersive bands supporting ballistic propagation. We compare two initially localized benchmark states: a flat-band-active state with finite flat-band overlap and a flat-band-dark state with zero flat-band overlap. For geometric stochastic restart with per-step restart probability $q$, the stationary mean-squared displacement scales as $q^{-2}$ as $q\to0$. In the same limit, the restart-site occupation probability approaches the restart-free intrinsic localized value for the flat-band-active state, whereas for the flat-band-dark state it vanishes as $q\ln(1/q)$. For power-law restart, where $p_m\propto m^{-s}$ is the probability that the waiting time to the next restart is $m$ steps, a normalized stationary site-occupation distribution exists only for $s>2$, while the stationary absolute spatial moment of order $p$ is finite only for $s>p+2$. In the regime $1<s\leq2$, at every fixed lattice site, the flat-band-active occupation converges to the intrinsic flat-band profile, while the flat-band-dark occupation tends to zero. We also consider monitored first detection with sharp restart, in which the walk is reinitialized after a fixed number $r$ of consecutive unsuccessful measurements. For fixed $r$, the mean first-detected-passage time of the flat-band-active state exhibits a minimum at an intermediate self-loop weight, whereas the flat-band-dark state approaches a ballistic detection limit as $\ell\to\infty$.

</details>


### [201] [No information transmission through quantum channels above capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08998)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意有限维无记忆量子信道上，无辅助量子通信和经典通信的指数级强逆定理，表明信道容量是可靠传输与灾难性失败的相变边界，超出容量时保真度和成功概率随信道使用次数指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息论中Shannon容量已确立了可靠通信的极限，但量子信道是否具有类似的强逆性质（即在超过容量时即使允许任意大的错误也无法传输信息）尚不清楚。作者希望严格证明量子信道容量不仅界定了可行与不可行区域，而且不可行区域是"灾难性"退化的。

Method: 采用经典Arimoto策略，并引入关键新工具：Rényi信息度量的积分表示，由此推导出Rényi容量的渐近连续性界。该方法将指数级强逆定理的证明归结为对Rényi容量的精细分析。

Result: 获得了任意有限维无记忆量子信道的无辅助量子通信（纠缠产生保真度）和经典通信（成功概率）的指数衰减上界。在超过相应容量的任意速率下，传输质量以信道使用次数的指数函数形式趋近于零。

Conclusion: 量子信道容量确实表征了一个尖锐的相变：容量以下可靠传输可行，容量以上即使容忍任意大的容许误差也不能成功，且失败按照指数速率发生。这排除了任何在容量以上实现非平凡传输的可能性，强化了量子信道容量的操作意义。

Abstract: We show that the capacity of a quantum channel demarcates a phase transition: while reliable transmission below capacity is always possible, any attempt to transmit information above it fails catastrophically. Specifically, we prove exponential strong converse theorems for unassisted quantum and classical communication over arbitrary finite-dimensional memoryless quantum channels. At rates beyond the respective capacity, the entanglement-generation fidelity and the success probability for classical communication decay exponentially with the number of channel uses. This rules out transmission above capacity even when one tolerates arbitrarily large errors. Our proof follows the classical Arimoto strategy, augmented by a crucial new ingredient: integral representations of Rényi information measures that lead to asymptotic continuity bounds for Rényi capacities.

</details>


### [202] [Preparation of a Bell state in an analogue device hosting the transverse-field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09013)
*Ana Palacios*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在模拟量子设备上制备单重态贝尔态（singlet Bell state）的协议，利用横向场伊辛模型的哈密顿量直接控制来实现，通过绝热插值制备基态后结合共振布居转移，在约100纳秒时间尺度内达到与门基制备相当的高保真度，并可在适度延长时长下将不保真度降至10^-4量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无噪声中型量子（NISQ）时代，模拟量子模拟器相比门基量子计算在某些任务上更具优势，但如何利用模拟设备的哈密顿量直接制备特定量子态（尤其是纠缠态如贝尔态）仍缺乏一般性协议。作者希望绕过门基电路编译的复杂性，直接通过对哈密顿量项的控制来实现高质量的单重态贝尔态制备。

Method: 协议利用单重态是对称哈密顿量家族第一激发态这一事实，分两步实现：(1) 通过绝热插值制备该对称哈密顿量的基态；(2) 在两个最低能级之间进行共振布居转移（resonant population transfer），将该基态转移至第一激发态（即单重态）。方案仅需直接控制横向场伊辛模型的项（ZZ相互作用及局部X和Z场），可在超导平台等系统中实现。

Result: 在现实参数下，该协议实现的保真度与标准门基制备方法相当，时间尺度约为100纳秒；适度延长协议时长可将不保真度降低至10^-4量级。对系统性控制误差的鲁棒性分析表明，在实现不完美的情况下仍能维持高保真度。

Conclusion: 该协议展示了在模拟量子设备上无需编译成量子门即可高效制备纠缠态的可能性，为模拟平台上量子态制备提供了一种可行的替代方案，兼具高保真度与对控制误差的鲁棒性，具有实际应用的潜力。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for preparing the singlet Bell state in an analogue device implementing the transverse-field Ising model with ZZ interactions and local X and Z fields, which can be engineered within the superconducting platform, among others. This protocol is performed by direct control of the terms of the Hamiltonian alone. The method exploits the fact that the singlet state is the first excited state of a symmetric Hamiltonian family, and comprises two steps: an adiabatic interpolation preparing the ground state of said symmetric Hamiltonian, followed by the resonant population transfer between its two lowest energy levels. For realistic parameters, the protocol achieves fidelities comparable to standard gate-based preparation within similar time scales ($\sim$100 ns), and can reach infidelities on the order of $10^{-4}$ with a moderately increased duration. We further analyse robustness against systematic control errors and show that high fidelities are maintained under implementation imperfections.

</details>


### [203] [Modular commutator as a robust topological invariant and approximate Markovianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09019)
*Tai Hsuan Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究模交换子（modular commutator）作为手性中心荷探测量的鲁棒性。作者在有限维Hilbert空间中证明：近似局部量子马尔可夫性质（即条件互信息I(A:C|B)很小但不为零）足以保证模交换子在拓扑保持形变下渐近不变；并证明模交换子的迹范数连续性，从而结合形变不变性建立其在满足近似局部量子马尔可夫条件、由准局域酉路径连接的无隙量子相内的渐近不变性。反过来，作者证明非零模交换子要求条件互信息随条带宽度B按指数衰减的速度不会快于指数（在纠缠面积律下），将精确的no-go定理推广为定量有限界。上述结论同样适用于Hall电导估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅在局部量子马尔可夫条件（条件互信息严格为零）下证明了模交换子的形变不变性，但该条件同时迫使模交换子本身为零，使得探针在物理上相关的近似情形下是否仍然稳健成为悬而未决的问题。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，即放宽严格马尔可夫条件至近似情形，并考察模交换子的鲁棒性。

Method: 本文采用三种技术手段：(1) 在有限维Hilbert空间中对近似局部量子马尔可夫条件建立形变不变性的渐近版本；(2) 证明模交换子在迹范数下的连续性；(3) 对由模Hamilton量生成的有限时间动力学进行分析，推导条件互信息下界的定量估计，并与纠缠面积律相结合。

Result: (1) 近似局部量子马尔可夫性质蕴含模交换子在拓扑保持形变下渐近不变；(2) 模交换子具有迹范数连续性；(3) 模交换子在与准局域酉路径相连接的、保持近似马尔可夫条件的无隙量子相内渐近不变；(4) 当态满足纠缠面积律时，非零模交换子所需的I(A:C|B)随条带宽度B的衰减不能快于指数衰减；(5) 所得结论对Hall电导估计量同样成立。

Conclusion: 本文从正反两方面完整刻画了模交换子在近似条件下的鲁棒性：正向证明了近似局部量子马尔可夫条件足以维持其作为拓扑不变量的渐近行为；反向给出了非零模交换子所需条件互信息的最小衰减速率（指数下界）。这为模交换子在实际物理态（马尔可夫性质仅近似成立）中的可用性提供了严格的理论基础，并将结论推广至Hall电导估计量。

Abstract: The modular commutator provides a bulk, local, single-wave-function probe of the chiral central charge $c_-$ for gapped ground states. Its invariance under deformations was previously established under a local quantum Markov condition---namely, the conditional mutual information $I(A:C|B)$ is zero for all tripartitions of a disk into three consecutive strips A, B, and C \cite{Modular-commutator-Gapped}. However, the local quantum Markov condition also forces the probe to vanish, leaving open whether modular commutator remains robust in physically relevant states where the Markov property holds only approximately. In this paper, we resolve this tension for finite-dimensional Hilbert spaces: the approximate local quantum Markov property implies the change of the modular commutator under topology-preserving deformations vanishes asymptotically. We next prove trace-norm continuity of the modular commutator. Combining deformation invariance with trace-norm continuity, we establish that the modular commutator remains asymptotically invariant within gapped quantum phases connected by quasi-local unitary paths that preserve the approximate local quantum Markov condition. Conversely, by analyzing finite-time dynamics generated by modular Hamiltonians, we derive a quantitative lower bound on the conditional mutual information required for a non-zero modular commutator: $I(A:C|B)$ across such strip tripartitions cannot decay faster than exponentially with the width of B given that the state satisfies entanglement area law, extending the exact no-go theorem of \cite{strict-J-2024} to a quantitative finite bound . Finally, we demonstrate that those conclusions apply equally to the Hall conductance estimator \cite{FanSahayVishwanath2023}.

</details>


### [204] [Semidefinite extension complexity of the separable set, with applications to approximate disentanglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09033)
*Sevag Gharibian,Carsten Hecht,Dorian Rudolph*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了\mathbb{C}^d⊗\mathbb{C}^d上量子可分态集合的半定扩展复杂度的定量下界。在Harrow-Natarajan-Wu扩展公式模型中，对于任意0<θ<2/7，存在常数使得任何具有均匀加性误差a的SDP规模至少为d^{c_θ·min{a^{-1/3},d^θ}}。该界在足够小的常数误差下有效，当a=o(1)时关于d是超多项式的，并改进了HNW在平方反比误差下的拟多项式界。证明结合了LRS定量伪密度定理、显式块正算子和Chebyshev放大，并由Lean形式化证明支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QMA(2)（两个证明者的量子验证）的核心优化问题是在可分态上最大化测量的接受概率。可分态集合的刻画极为困难，因此需要研究其半定规划近似的复杂度下界。此前Harrow、Natarajan和Wu的结果已证明拟多项式的下界，而Fawzi证明了可分态集合没有精确的半定表示，但缺乏定量方面的结果。本工作旨在给出定量下界，从"无法精确表示"推进到"即使近似表示也需要极大规模"，为QMA(2)问题的内在困难提供更强的证据。

Method: 论文将Lee-Raghavendra-Steurer的定量伪密度定理与显式构造的块正算子及Chebyshev多项式放大技术相结合。核心思想是构造一批可分态上接受概率极低但在某些纠缠态上接受概率极高的测量，通过Chebyshev放大增强区分度，再利用伪密度定理建立SDP扩展复杂度的下界。所有关键结果均辅以Lean定理证明器验证的形式化证明。

Result: 对于任意0<θ<2/7，存在常数c_θ,a_θ>0，使得对充分大的d，任何在HNW模型中带均匀加性误差a≤a_θ的SDP规模至少为d^{c_θ·min{a^{-1/3},d^θ}}。当误差a=o(1)时该界关于d为超多项式；当a≤d^{-3θ}时下界退化为d^{Ω(d^θ)}。该结果改进了HNW在逆平方误差下的拟多项式界，且对任何包含可分态且在迹距离a内的SDP可表示凸集同样适用。

Conclusion: 该工作为可分量子态集合的半定扩展复杂度提供了首个定量超多项式下界（在小常数误差区域），给出了Fawzi不可表示性定理的定量对应版本。这进一步确证了QMA(2)中优化问题的计算困难性，并通过Lean形式化验证增强了结果的可信度。

Abstract: We prove quantitative lower bounds on the semidefinite extension complexity of the set of separable quantum states on $\mathbb{C}^d\otimes\mathbb{C}^d$. We consider semidefinite programs (SDPs) that approximate the maximum acceptance probability of a measurement over separable states, the optimization problem underlying QMA(2). In the extended-formulation model of Harrow, Natarajan, and Wu (HNW), all measurements share a common feasible region and an objective-independent embedding of product states that exactly reproduces their acceptance probabilities. For every $0<θ<2/7$, there are constants $c_θ,a_θ>0$ such that, for sufficiently large $d$, any such SDP with uniform additive error $0<a\le a_θ$ has size at least $d^{c_θ\min\{a^{-1/3},d^θ\}}$. The bound applies at sufficiently small constant error, is superpolynomial in $d$ whenever $a=o(1)$, and becomes $d^{Ω(d^θ)}$ when $a\le d^{-3θ}$, improving HNW's quasipolynomial bound at inverse- square error. The same bound holds for any SDP-representable convex set of states that contains all separable states and lies within trace distance $a$ of them, giving a quantitative counterpart to Fawzi's theorem that the separable set has no exact semidefinite representation. Our proof combines the quantitative pseudo-density theorem of Lee, Raghavendra, and Steurer with explicit block-positive operators and Chebyshev amplification. Our main results are supported by Lean proofs.

</details>


### [205] [Near-Optimal Quantum Lower Bounds for Convex Optimization via Fourier Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09035)
*Brandon Augustino,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Enrico Fontana,Dylan Herman,Junhyung Lyle Kim,Guneykan Ozgul,Nadezhda Voronova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了高精度凸优化问题上的近线性量子查询下界：在显式构造的n维椭球体族上，任何算法在单位线性目标下返回精确可行点且附加目标误差为Θ(n^{-2})时，需要Ω(n/(log n·log log n))次成员查询。该结果在log因子内解决了Chakrabarti等人和van Apeldoorn等人提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度凸优化的查询复杂度此前存在未解决的开放问题，关于量子设置下凸优化的查询下界尚不清晰。作者希望通过建立近线性下界，在log因子内紧致刻画该问题的查询复杂度，从而弥合已有上下界之间的差距。

Method: 证明的核心是围绕行列式计算的下界展开，通过一种新颖的基于傅里叶秩（Fourier-rank）的多项式方法推导。在连续矩阵相位查询模型中，计算实n×n矩阵的行列式至少需要n/2次矩阵-向量乘积查询。这一构造还可推广用于最小特征值估计和光滑强凸函数优化的梯度查询下界。

Result: 在高精度凸优化中，返回精确可行点或近似可行点（与可行集距离在Θ(n^{-2})内）均需Ω(n/(log n·log log n))次成员查询；在相位查询模型中，行列式计算需至少n/2次矩阵-向量乘积查询，最小特征值估计需Ω(n)次相位查询；同时获得常数精度光滑强凸函数优化的近最优梯度查询下界。

Conclusion: 该结果在多项式因子内解决了一直悬而未决的量子凸优化查询复杂度问题，将Childs、Hung和Li的行列式与最小特征值下界从有限域扩展到了实数值设定，实现了对高精度凸优化查询复杂度的紧密刻画。

Abstract: We establish a near-linear quantum query lower bound for high-accuracy convex optimization over an explicit family of $n$-dimensional ellipsoids. We focus on linear optimization with an explicitly given objective, where the feasible set is accessed through a membership oracle. We show that any algorithm that, for every unit linear objective, returns an exactly feasible point with additive objective error $Θ(n^{-2})$ requires $Ω\!\left(\frac{n}{\log n\,\log\log n}\right)$ membership queries. The same lower bound can be shown to hold if the returned point is only required to be approximately feasible, within $Θ(n^{-2})$ distance from the feasible set. This resolves, up to logarithmic factors, an open question posed by Chakrabarti, Childs, Li, and Wu~(\textit{Quantum}, 2020) and by van Apeldoorn, Gilyén, Gribling, and de Wolf~(\textit{Quantum}, 2020). Coupled with the upper bounds in these papers, the query complexity of high-accuracy convex optimization is characterized tightly up to logarithmic factors. The proof is built around a lower bound for determinant computation that is derived via a novel polynomial method based on Fourier-rank. In the continuous matrix phase-query model, computing the determinant of a real $n\times n$ matrix requires at least $n/2$ matrix-vector product queries. The construction also yields an $Ω(n)$ phase-query lower bound for estimating the minimum eigenvalue of a real symmetric $n\times n$ matrix to additive accuracy $Θ(n^{-2})$. These results extend the determinant and minimum-eigenvalue lower bounds of Childs, Hung, and Li~(ICALP 2021) from finite fields to the real-valued setting. Based on the same constructions, we also prove a near-optimal gradient-query lower bound for constant-accuracy optimization of smooth and strongly convex functions.

</details>


### [206] [Impossibility of One-Way One-Round Quantum 4-Coloring via Matrix-Space Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09091)
*Tom Gur,Longcheng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一轮单向量子LOCAL算法无法以高概率对4色有向环进行着色，即使允许无界局部计算和量子消息长度。这是高概率量子LOCAL设置中首个超越非信号和有界依赖模型的下界，揭示了分布式量子算法的结构特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高概率量子LOCAL模型下建立下界，突破以往仅适用于非信号模型和有界依赖模型的限制，深入理解分布式量子算法的计算能力边界。

Method: 将分布式量子计算与非交换极值组合学联系起来：将局部碰撞概率与矩阵空间分解的加权乘性能量对应，并通过证明有向非交换Mantel定理的维无关加权稳定性定理来获得下界。

Result: 证明了一轮单向量子LOCAL算法无法高概率4色有向环，这是该设置中首个超越非信号和有界依赖模型的下界结果。

Conclusion: 通过量子分布式计算与矩阵分解能量之间的新联系，建立了一个强于现有模型的下界，暗示量子LOCAL模型具有超越经典与量子通信理论界限的独特结构限制。

Abstract: We show that one-way one-round quantum LOCAL algorithms cannot $4$-color directed cycles with high probability, even with unbounded local computation and quantum message length. This is the first lower bound in the high-probability quantum LOCAL setting that goes beyond the non-signaling and bounded-dependence models, exploiting the structure of distributed quantum algorithms.
  Our proof connects distributed quantum computing with noncommutative extremal combinatorics by identifying local collision probabilities with the weighted multiplicative energy of matrix-space decompositions. We obtain our lower bound by proving a dimension-independent weighted stability theorem for a directed noncommutative analogue of Mantel's theorem.

</details>


### [207] [Near-optimal synthesis of non-Gaussian phase gates via qubit-oscillator Rabi control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09132)
*Zhen Yang,Shan Jin,Zi-Wen Liu,Xiaoting Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于连续变量（CV）量子计算中多项式相位门的量子比特-振荡器Rabi合成方案，其总交互时间关于目标误差ε的逆以多对数尺度缩放，并给出了近乎最优的下界证明，可用以模拟CV量子动力学和求解线性偏微分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯门仍然是通用连续变量（CV）量子计算的关键瓶颈，因为其所需要的非线性效应难以工程实现。现有的方法往往依赖数值优化，难以扩展到大规模多模系统。因此，亟需一种高效、可分析构造且近最优的非高斯门合成方案。

Method: 作者开发了量子比特-振荡器Rabi合成方案用于逼近多项式相位门。通过解析方式构造Rabi序列，利用可解析构造的序列实现degree-R相位门的近似；该构造无需数值优化，因此可自然扩展到任意大的多模系统。总时间标度为O(log^{(R-1)/2+o(1)}(1/ε))。

Result: 建立了总时间的上界O(log^{(R-1)/2+o(1)}(1/ε))和下界Ω(log^{(R-1)/2}(1/ε))，证明该合成方案是近乎最优的。作为应用，该方案被用于模拟代表性的CV量子动力学，并实现了一个用于求解线性偏微分方程的CV量子算法。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了量子比特-振荡器Rabi控制作为CV量子信息处理中一种高效、可解析编译且近最优的原语，解决了非高斯门合成的可扩展性问题，并展示了其在动力学期模拟和偏微分方程求解等实际应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian gates remain a key bottleneck for universal continuous-variable (CV) quantum computation because the nonlinearities they require are difficult to engineer. To address this challenge, we develop an efficient qubit-oscillator Rabi synthesis scheme for polynomial phase gates, with a total interaction time that scales polylogarithmically with the inverse target error \(\varepsilon\). Specifically, for a class of readily preparable initial states, we show that a degree-\(R\) phase gate can be approximated by an analytically constructed Rabi sequence with total time \(O(\log^{(R-1)/2+o(1)}(1/\varepsilon))\). This construction requires no numerical optimization and therefore extends naturally to arbitrarily large multimode systems.
