We further establish a total-time lower bound of \(Ω(\log^{(R-1)/2}(1/\varepsilon))\), showing that the synthesis is near optimal. As applications, we use this scheme to simulate representative CV quantum dynamics and implement a CV quantum algorithm for solving linear partial differential equations. These results establish qubit-oscillator Rabi control as an efficient, analytically compilable, and near-optimal primitive for CV quantum information processing.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Novel Transition Mechanisms of Vector Localized Waves Induced by the Fourth-Order Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07335)
*Rong Han,Muchen Dong,Lei Wang*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合Lakshmanan-Porsezian-Daniel (LPD)方程中的新型矢量局域波解，该方程描述了海森堡铁磁自旋链的动力学。作者在简并区和非简并区中给出了Tajiri-Watanabe呼吸子、流氓波和共振模式，以及简并拍频孤子。研究发现四阶效应在两个区域中均诱导态转变，尤其是简并呼吸子可转化为孤子（这在耦合Hirota方程中不存在），拍频孤子仅能在简并区转化为稳定孤子（这在Manakov系统中未发现）。作者还揭示了共振模式的态转变并推导了各分支的转变条件，通过物理谱在谱域中识别了态转变条件，最终通过直接数值模拟验证了解析解的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索耦合LPD方程中超越先前已知的局域波结构，理解四阶效应对非线性波态转变的影响，并揭示其与耦合Hirota方程及Manakov系统在动力学行为上的本质差异，从而更深入地理解海森堡铁磁自旋链中的非线性波现象。

Method: 采用解析与数值相结合的方法：通过求解耦合LPD方程导出Tajiri-Watanabe呼吸子、流氓波、共振模式及拍频孤子的精确表达式；针对简并区与非简并区分别推导各模式的状态转变条件；构建物理谱并在谱域中识别态转变判据；运用直接数值模拟验证所获精确解的可靠性。

Result: 在简并区和非简并区均获得了多种新型矢量局域波解，并发现四阶效应可诱导呼吸子向孤子的转变，该现象在耦合Hirota方程中不存在；拍频孤子仅在简并区能够转化为稳定孤子，这在Manakov系统中未观察到；对共振模式的各种分支推导了相应的态转变条件，并在谱域中给出了补充性表征；所有解析结果均通过数值模拟得到验证。

Conclusion: 耦合LPD方程中的四阶色散/非线性效应显著丰富了局域波的态转变动力学，产生了不同于耦合Hirota方程与Manakov系统的新颖转变行为。所识别的谱域转变条件为实验观测和调控这些非线性波过渡提供了理论工具。精确解与数值模拟的一致性确认了解析框架的正确性。

Abstract: We investigate novel vector localized wave solutions in the coupled Lakshmanan-Porsezian-Daniel equa?tions, which describe the dynamics of the Heisenberg ferromagnetic spin chain. We present Tajiri-Watanabe breathers, rogue waves and resonant modes in both the degenerate and non-degenerate regions, together with the degenerate beating solitons. The fourth-order effect induces state transitions in both regions. In particular, the degenerate breathers can be transformed into solitons, whereas such transitions are absent in the coupled Hirota equations. Moreover, beating solitons can be converted into stable solitons only in the degenerate region, the phenomena not found in the Manakov system. We further uncover the state transitions of the resonant modes and derive the corresponding transition conditions for each branch. We derive the physical spectra and subsequently identify the state transition conditions in the spectral domain for both the degenerate and non-degenerate cases. These spectra provide an additional characterization of the transition dynamics. Finally, direct numerical simulations are performed to verify the validity of the exact solutions.

</details>


### [209] [Breathers in solitonic room-temperature superlattice-induced superfluorescence in quasi-2D perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08513)
*A. A. Gladkij,N. N. Rosanov,B. D. Fainberg*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文将描述准二维金属卤化物钙钛矿超晶格中Wannier激子-LO声子相互作用的二维非局域非线性薛定谔方程推广到三维超晶格结构，证明层间隧穿会导致混合坐标-动量空间中的呼吸子动力学，对应稳定的基本孤子，并建立了层间隧穿、微带形成与孤子动力学之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究提出薄钙钛矿薄膜室温超荧光中的孤子机制，同时超晶格结构有望增强超荧光。近期实验观测到准二维金属卤化物钙钛矿周期超晶格中的室温超荧光现象，需要理论模型来解释其中孤子的行为与稳定性。

Method: 将描述Wannier激子-LO声子相互作用的2D非局域非线性薛定谔方程扩展到超晶格结构，推导出三维非局域非线性薛定谔方程，并在混合坐标-动量空间（坐标平行于层、动量垂直于层）中分析孤子与呼吸子动力学。

Result: 层间隧穿导致微带形成，引发孤子的动量依赖相位调制，从而产生与稳定基本孤子对应的呼吸子动力学；在无层间隧穿时，呼吸子动力学消失，孤子变为静止态。

Conclusion: 研究确立了层间隧穿、微带形成与孤子动力学之间的直接关联，表明呼吸子行为可作为准二维钙钛矿超晶格中层间隧穿存在的特征信号。

Abstract: Recently, a soliton mechanism for room-temperature superfluorescence in thin perovskite films has been proposed, with a fundamental soliton predicted to remain stable under LO phonon--exciton interactions. At the same time, superlattice architectures offer a route to enhancing superfluorescence in perovskites. Motivated by recent observations of room-temperature superfluorescence in periodic superlattices of quasi-2D metal-halide perovskites, we extend the 2D nonlocal nonlinear Schrödinger equation describing Wannier exciton--LO phonon interactions to superlattice structures, obtaining a 3D nonlocal nonlinear Schrödinger equation. We show that interlayer tunnelling gives rise to breather dynamics corresponding to a stable fundamental soliton in mixed coordinate--momentum space, with the coordinate parallel to the layers and the momentum perpendicular to them. The breather dynamics originate from miniband formation, which induces a momentum-dependent phase modulation of the soliton. In the absence of interlayer tunnelling, the breather dynamics disappear and the soliton becomes stationary. These results establish a direct connection between interlayer tunnelling, miniband formation and soliton dynamics, suggesting that breather behavior can provide a signature of interlayer tunnelling in quasi-2D perovskite superlattices.

</details>


### [210] [Reduction Based Dynamical Systems Analysis of Nonlinear Wave Equations: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09050)
*Naresh Saha,Arnob Ray*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文综述了一种基于约化的方法论框架，将非线性偏微分方程（PDEs）系统性地与约化动力系统、不变相空间结构、精确波形的重构及稳定性分析联系起来，并系统考察了其在不同类型非线性波动方程中的适用性与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上构造精确行波解虽然能提供解析基准，但仅凭解的构造无法揭示相空间结构、分岔行为、稳定性以及对外部扰动的响应。因此需要对非线性波动方程建立一种更深层的、从PDE约化到动力系统的分析框架。

Method: 通过约化方法将PDE转化为低维动力系统，借助不变相空间结构（如平衡点、周期轨道、同宿轨道和异宿连接）对精确波形进行几何表征与重构，并在原PDE层面进行稳定性分析和数值验证，综合使用互补的诊断工具（而非仅依赖相图或有限时间指标）来区分不变结构的存在性与混沌的涌现。

Result: 该方法框架在Schrödinger型、耦合、非傍轴、耗散、磁性、浅水波及分数阶波动方程等多种体系中得到适用性验证；同时识别出若干关键方法缺口，包括参数空间的表征不完整、约化模型与全PDE动力学之间的弱对应、广义与分数阶公式的模糊性、鲁棒性分析不足、数值可复现性不充分以及与实验可测量的关联不足。

Conclusion: 该框架将研究优先性置于物理可容许性、稳定性、鲁棒性和预测相关性之上，而非追求更多形式解的生成，旨在为流体、光学、等离子体等非线性物理系统中的波动预测、稳定性评估、控制与系统设计提供可靠支撑。

Abstract: Nonlinear wave equations exhibit rich interplay among dispersion, nonlinearity, coupling, dissipation, and external forcing, producing diverse coherent and complex wave structures. Although exact travelling-wave solutions provide analytical benchmarks, their construction alone does not reveal underlying phase-space, bifurcations, stability, or responses to perturbations. This review presents a reduction-based methodological framework connecting nonlinear partial differential equations(PDEs) to reduced dynamical systems, invariant phase-space structures, exact waveform reconstruction, stability analysis, and verification in PDEs. Its applicability and limitations are examined across Schr{ö}dinger-type, coupled, nonparaxial, dissipative, magnetic, shallow-water, and fractional wave equations. Particular emphasis is placed on correspondence between analytical waveforms and invariant orbits. Equilibria, periodic trajectories, homoclinic orbits, and heteroclinic connections geometrically represent constant, periodic, localized, and front-like structures, respectively. This review distinguishes existence and stability of invariant structures from onset of chaos, emphasizing complementary diagnostics rather than reliance on phase-portraits or finite-time indicators alone. Major methodological gaps include incomplete parameter-space characterization, weak correspondence between reduced models and full-PDE dynamics, ambiguities in generalized and fractional formulations, limited robustness analysis, inadequate numerical reproducibility, and insufficient links to experimental observables. The framework prioritizes physical admissibility, stability, robustness, and predictive relevance over generation of additional formal solutions. It supports reliable nonlinear wave prediction, stability assessment, control, and system design in fluid, optical, plasma, and other nonlinear physical systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [What Did I Just Say? Self-Listening for Full-Duplex Speech Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05592)
*Xuanning Zhou,Junyi Ao,Xiaotong Liu,Tom Ko,Benyou Wang,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Listening全双工语音建模方法，通过将模型实际播放的语音反馈为输入流来解决全双工语音语言模型中的"锚定中断"问题，并引入AnchorSpeech数据集进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音语言模型能同时听和说，但文本生成、语音合成和音频播放是异步进行的，导致模型认为自己说过的话与用户实际听到的不一致。在遭遇中断后，模型需要从中断中恢复并保持对"实际已播报语音"的感知，这一"锚定中断"问题亟待解决。

Method: 提出Self-Listening机制，一种全双工建模方法，将用户语音、模型文本和模型播放的语音交错交织。核心创新在于将模型实际播放的语音（realized speech）作为输入流反馈给模型，使中断恢复锚定在用户真正听到的内容之上。同时构建AnchorSpeech数据集，包含同构的训练和测试划分，用于追踪结构化有序回复中哪些条目已被实际说出。

Result: 在AnchorSpeech测试集上，配备了Self-Listening机制的模型相较于全双工基线模型，在锚定性能上表现更优，即能更好地在中断后与最后一个已完成条目保持一致并做出响应。

Conclusion: Self-Listening通过将实际播放语音反馈回模型输入，有效解决了全双工语音模型在中断恢复时对自身已实现语音的感知问题，为构建更自然的全双工对话系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken language models can listen and speak simultaneously, enabling them to handle interruptions and backchannels in human conversation. However, text generation, speech synthesis, and audio playback proceed asynchronously. As a result, what a model believes it has said may not match what has actually been played to the user. We refer to the problem of recovering from an interruption while remaining aware of the model's realized speech as anchor interruption. To address this problem, we propose Self-Listening, a full-duplex modeling approach that interleaves user speech, model text, and the model's played speech. By feeding the realized speech output back to the model as an input stream, self-listening grounds interruption recovery in what the user has actually heard. We further introduce AnchorSpeech, a collection with homogeneous training and test splits for tracking which items of structured ordered responses have actually been spoken. AnchorSpeech-test evaluates whether a model can respond consistently with the last completed item before an interruption. Experiments show that, compared with full-duplex baselines, models equipped with self-listening mechanism achieve better anchoring performance.

</details>


### [212] [Emotion as a Distribution: Joint Valence-Arousal Probability Learning for Speaker-Independent Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05755)
*Tingyi Lin,Wen-Ren Yang,Kuanwei Chen*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种文本+语音的多模态情感识别系统，输出9×9的效价-唤醒概率矩阵（双高斯软标签+KL/交叉熵目标），在IEMOCAP上经严格的speaker-independent留一会话验证，达到73.0%±0.3的UA（Transformer融合基线上提升3.0点）；将前端换成冻结WavLM-Large特征后提至76.6%±1.3。系统输出的情感概率分布能恢复circumplex结构，且其熵与分类标注歧义弱相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类情感是渐变且常混合的，但多数多模态识别器将其压缩为单一硬标签。作者主张识别器应输出情感空间上的分布而非单一类别，以更好地服务咨询支持场景。

Method: 采用文本+语音双模态系统，在类别决策之外输出9×9效价-唤醒平面概率矩阵；使用二维高斯软目标配合KL/交叉熵损失训练；在固定编码器-融合-头框架下比较Transformer与状态空间模型（Mamba-1/2/3）骨干，于两个参数量级（约550与约2750参数）下评测；实验采用speaker-independent的5折leave-one-session-out IEMOCAP协议，仅报告留出会话上的指标。

Result: 双头系统达到73.0%±0.3 UA（三种子，独立复跑为72.1%），较Transformer融合基线高3.0 UA点（会话bootstrap 95% CI [1.0,4.7]）；替换前端为冻结WavLM-Large特征后同架构升至76.6%±1.3；简化效价-唤醒辅助任务可复现分类增益，专用回归头对连续评分的追踪略优；分布质心追踪效价/唤醒CCC达0.66/0.66（以类间结构为主），分布熵与标注者歧义弱但一致相关。

Conclusion: 系统头的独特价值在于输出归一化情感分布本身：它有效恢复circumplex（circumplex model of affect）结构，并蕴含与分类不确定性相关的信息；该分布输出可为咨询支持等需要细粒度情感理解的应用提供超越硬标签的额外价值。

Abstract: Human emotion is graded and frequently mixed, yet most multimodal recognizers collapse it onto a single hard label. We argue the recognizer should instead expose a distribution over affective space. Our text+speech system, alongside its categorical decision, emits a $9\times9$ probability matrix over the Valence-Arousal plane, trained with a two-dimensional Gaussian soft target under a Kullback-Leibler/cross-entropy objective, aimed at counseling support. Evaluation is strict: speaker-independent 5-fold leave-one-session-out IEMOCAP with rotating-session inner validation, headline metrics only on the held-out session. Within one fixed encoder-fusion-head pipeline we compare Transformer and state-space (Mamba-1/2/3) backbones at matched depth and width, at two operating points ($T\approx550$, $T\approx2750$). The featured dual-head system reaches 73.0% $\pm$ 0.3 unweighted accuracy over three seeds (separate rerun: 72.1%), exceeding the Transformer fusion baseline by 3.0 UA points (95% session-bootstrap CI [1.0,4.7]; significant under paired t-test and session-level bootstrap), with no latency or memory advantage at these lengths; swapping the ~1M trainable front-end for frozen WavLM-Large features (learnable layer weights) lifts the same architecture to 76.6% $\pm$ 1.3. Pre-specified controls scope the claims honestly: simpler valence-arousal auxiliaries reproduce the classification lift within noise, and a dedicated regression head tracks the continuous ratings slightly better, so the head's specific value is the normalized affect distribution itself. That distribution recovers the circumplex: its center of mass tracks valence and arousal (CCC 0.66/0.66; predominantly between-class structure, weaker within-class tracking), and its entropy is weakly but consistently linked to categorical rater ambiguity, not dimensional spread.

</details>


### [213] [Where Does the Sound Go? Tracing Acoustic Information Loss in Audio-Conditioned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05871)
*Song-ha Jo,Sehyun Lee,Soyoon Kim,Jaesik Choi,Sanghyuk Choi*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文探讨音频条件语言模型（audio-LM）未能充分利用声学线索的问题，发现问题根源不在于ASR预训练编码器的信息丢失，而在于LM头部的读出一致性（readout alignment）存在瓶颈。研究者通过对比不同前端（Whisper、EnCodec、DAC-VAE、WavTokenizer）在共享Qwen3.5-4B音频LM管线上的表现，并结合线性探针、几何分析和LogitLens干预等诊断手段，揭示尽管最终LM层仍保留可恢复的判别性声学结构（MCQA准确率比探针最高低83个百分点），但模型在内容特定读出阶段出现失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频条件语言模型常常未能充分利用韵律、情感和非语音声音等声学线索，作者想厘清这一缺陷是否源于ASR监督训练的前端编码器在信息到达LM之前就已丢弃了相关声学信息。

Method: 在共享的Qwen3.5-4B音频LM管线中，将ASR预训练的Whisper-Tiny和Whisper-Small与预训练音频编码器EnCodec、DAC-VAE及WavTokenizer进行对比，涵盖ASR、情感识别和声音字幕三项任务。通过更换前端编码器、线性探针、几何分析和LogitLens分析追踪任务相关信息在编码器、投影层、LM各层及LM头部中的分布，并对LM头进行针对性干预实验来验证读出瓶颈假说。

Result: 单纯更换编码器并不能解决声学信息利用不足的问题：Whisper变体在包括情感识别和环境声音字幕在内的所有任务上仍表现最强。线性探针与几何分析表明，即使在最终LM层，判别性声学结构仍然可被恢复，但MCQA准确率相比探针准确率最高落后83个百分点的差距揭示出读出失败才是关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 声学信息利用不足并非仅由前端编码器的信息丢失所导致；LM头的读出一致性（readout alignment）可能是占主导地位的瓶颈，这为改进音频语言模型提供了新的诊断视角和干预方向。

Abstract: Audio-conditioned language models often underuse acoustic cues such as prosody, emotion, and non-speech sounds, raising the question of whether ASR-supervised frontends discard this information before it reaches the LM. We test whether the frontend is responsible by comparing Whisper-Tiny and Whisper-Small with EnCodec, DAC-VAE, and WavTokenizer in a shared Qwen3.5-4B audio-LM pipeline on ASR, emotion recognition, and sound captioning. Encoder replacement alone does not resolve this underuse: Whisper variants remain strongest overall, including on emotion and environmental sound captioning. To localize the failure, we trace task-relevant information through the encoder, projector, LM layers, and LM head. Linear probes and geometric analyses show that discriminative acoustic structure remains recoverable at the final LM layer, even when MCQA accuracy trails probe accuracy by up to 83 points. Because the answer format and decoding procedure are controlled, this task-dependent gap points to content-specific readout failure rather than generic format bias. LogitLens analyses and a targeted LM head intervention support the conclusion that acoustic underuse is not explained solely by encoder-side information loss and that readout alignment can be a dominant bottleneck.

</details>


### [214] [Lead Vocal Separation from Vocal Ensemble Mixtures Using Phoneme Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06488)
*Yuma Narahata,Tomohiko Nakamura,Yuki Saito,Hiroshi Saruwatari*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用主唱（Vo）音素对齐作为辅助信息的人声分离模型。该模型基于具有SOTA性能的BS-RoFormer音乐源分离架构，并通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）将帧级音素标签注入中间表示。实验证明，音素对齐条件化比仅基于音频的基线以及仅基于Vo歌唱/静音活动条件化能带来更大的分离性能提升，且当伴奏声部与Vo音素不一致时优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代无伴奏合唱（a cappella）常采用主唱-伴奏织体，主唱声部（Vo）承载主旋律而其他声部提供伴奏。由于声学差异小且时间上常重叠，目标源与干扰源均为相似的人声音色，使Vo分离任务极具挑战性，需要引入额外声学信息（如音素对齐）来辅助分离。

Method: 提出的Vo分离模型基于带划分RoPE Transformer（BS-RoFormer）——一种当前最先进的音乐源分离模型。模型利用帧级Vo音素标签，通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）机制将音素对齐信息注入中间特征表示，以实现主唱声部从其他声部中的分离。

Result: 实验结果显示，音素对齐条件化显著提升了Vo分离性能，优于仅音频的基线；相比仅以Vo歌唱/静音活动为条件的设置，音素标签带来更大的平均性能增益。进一步分析表明，当伴奏声部与Vo共享相同音素的声部较少时，音素标签信息的优势更大。

Conclusion: 音素对齐作为辅助信息能有效提升无伴奏合唱中主唱声部的分离效果，且其增益来源与声部间音素差异的相关性表明该方法可通过引入语音学信息弥补仅依靠声学线索的不足。

Abstract: Contemporary a cappella singing often has a lead-and-accompaniment texture, where the lead vocal (Vo) part carries the main melody and the remaining vocal parts provide accompaniment. Owing to their distinct roles, separating the Vo part from the remaining vocal parts, referred to as Vo separation, enables downstream applications such as lyric recognition and minus-one accompaniment generation for vocal ensemble music. Despite these potential applications, acoustic cues for this task are limited because the target and interfering sources are all singing voices with similar acoustic characteristics and often overlap in time, making Vo separation challenging. In this paper, we propose a Vo separation model that uses phoneme alignment of the Vo part as auxiliary information. The proposed model is based on band-split RoPE Transformer (BS-RoFormer), a state-of-the-art music source separation model, and introduces frame-level phoneme labels into its intermediate representations using feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM). Experimental results show that phoneme-alignment conditioning improves Vo separation performance over an audio-only baseline and yields larger average gains than conditioning only on Vo singing/silence activity. Further analysis suggests that the advantage of phoneme-label information is larger when fewer remaining vocal parts share the same phoneme as Vo.

</details>


### [215] [BinauralVAE: Spatial Audio Reconstruction For World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06837)
*Luis Vitor Zerkowski,Luiz Velho*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BinauralVAE，一个开源的双耳空间音频重建管线，评估多种VAE架构（包括复值变体）来学习双耳信号的鲁棒潜在表征，为未来基于音频的世界模型奠定状态表征基础，使声音成为空间知识获取的补充分模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身人工智能长期依赖视觉感知构建世界模型，但在视觉遮挡、低光照或停电等场景中视觉不可靠，而声学信息可作为空间感知的关键替代模态。然而现实空间音频研究及音频中心世界模型的探索仍然匮乏。

Method: 提出了BinauralVAE开源管线，评估从基础基线到先进数学架构多种VAE变体（包括复值VAE），以学习双耳空间音频信号的鲁棒潜在表征。与AudioWorldSim协同开发，利用模拟机器人导航环境中采集的真实声学数据进行训练和验证，建立动作与声学结果之间直接因果关系的状态表征。

Result: 建立了从基础到复杂的多种VAE架构在空间化音频重建上的评估体系，并开发了完整的开源管线（BinauralVAE）与配套的AudioWorldSim仿真环境，初步实现了双耳信号的潜在表征学习，为音频领域世界模型提供了基础设施。

Conclusion: 该管线为未来基于音频的世界模型奠定了基础，旨在将导航动作与声学后果直接映射，使声音成为空间知识获取中视觉模态的重要补充方案。

Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence has historically very much relied on visual perception, leading to a proliferation of multiple vision-centric world models. However, this reliance fails to capture spatial understanding in its entirety and can even present vulnerabilities in environments with visual occlusions, low-light conditions, or blackouts-scenarios, where acoustic information becomes a critical alternative for spatial awareness and navigation. Despite its potential, research into realistic spatial audio and particularly the development of audio-centric world models remains sparse. In this technical report, we introduce BinauralVAE: a flexible, open-source pipeline (https://github.com/Luizerko/BinauralVAE) that explores multiple models for spatialized audio reconstruction, progressing from fundamental baselines to advanced, mathematically grounded architectures. Our approach evaluates various Variational Autoencoder architectures -- including complex-valued variants -- to learn robust latent representations of binaural signals. Developed alongside AudioWorldSim, our methodology leverages realistic acoustic data captured as a simulated robot navigates an environment. This pipeline establishes a foundation for state representation in a future audio-based world model, designed to map the direct causal connection between navigational actions and their resulting acoustic consequences, and helping to enable sound as an essential complementary modality for spatial knowledge acquisition.

</details>


### [216] [Frequency Estimation Based on SNR-adaptive Frequency Estimator Under Wide SNR Range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07034)
*Hee-Yang Jung,Dong-Hee Paek,Woo-Jin Jung,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种SNR自适应的频率估计器（SAFE），用于解决多音调正弦信号在低信噪比环境下频率估计困难的问题。SAFE由时间-频率图像神经网络（TFINet）和基于SNR的频率选择器（SFS）两部分组成，前者增强低SNR下的弱音调频率分量，后者根据估计的SNR选择鲁棒或超分辨频率估计器，从而在低SNR下保持鲁棒性同时在高SNR下保留高精度。仿真结果表明SAFE在-10dB至0dB范围内FNR为13.00%（较最优方法提升13.04%），NN-RMSE降低56.67%，并在真实数据实验中展现出稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频率估计方法在低信噪比环境下难以准确估计音调数量及个体频率，因为弱频率分量被噪声淹没；同时现有方法普遍存在低SNR鲁棒性与高SNR估计精度之间的权衡问题，难以在宽SNR范围内保持一致优越的性能。

Method: 提出SAFE框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) TFINet（时间-频率图像神经网络），用于在低SNR下增强微弱音调频率分量；(2) SFS（基于SNR的频率选择器），用于估计每个音调频率的SNR，并根据估计的SNR在鲁棒频率估计器与超分辨率频率估计器之间进行选择，实现自适应的频率估计策略。

Result: 仿真结果显示：在-10dB至0dB的SNR范围内，SAFE的FNR为13.00%，较现有最优方法提升13.04%；NN-RMSE较现有最优方法降低56.67%，表明其频率估计精度更高。真实数据实验进一步验证了SAFE在存在杂波的实际环境中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SAFE通过深度学习与自适应选择策略的结合，有效克服了传统方法在低SNR下的脆弱性与高SNR下的精度不足之间的权衡矛盾，实现了宽SNR范围内的鲁棒且精确的频率估计性能。

Abstract: Frequency estimation is the problem of estimating individual tone frequencies from noisy multi-tone sinusoidal signals. Existing frequency estimation methods have difficulty accurately estimating both the number of tone frequencies and the individual tone frequencies in low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) environments, because weak tone frequency components are buried in noise. In addition, existing methods generally exhibit a trade-off between robustness at low SNR and frequency estimation precision at high SNR, making it difficult to achieve consistently superior frequency estimation performance over a wide SNR range. To overcome these limitations, this paper proposes an SNR-adaptive frequency estimator (SAFE). SAFE consists of a time-frequency image neural network (TFINet), which enhances weak tone frequency components at low SNR, and an SNR-based frequency selector (SFS), which selects an appropriate frequency estimator according to the SNR of the estimated tone frequencies. TFINet enhances tone frequency components even in the low-SNR range, while SFS estimates the SNR of each tone frequency and selects either a robust frequency estimator or a super-resolution frequency estimator according to the estimated SNR. This enables SAFE to achieve robustness at low SNR while preserving high precision at high SNR. Simulation results show that SAFE achieves an False Negative Rate (FNR) of 13.00% over the SNR range from -10 dB to 0 dB, corresponding to an 13.04% improvement over the state-of-the-art method. In addition, SAFE reduces the Nearest Neighbor-Root Mean Squared Error (NN-RMSE) by 56.67% compared with the state-of-the-art method, demonstrating that SAFE performs more accurate frequency estimation. Furthermore, experiments using real-world data demonstrate that SAFE provides robust frequency estimation performance even in practical environments with clutter.

</details>


### [217] [Comparing Self-Supervised and Domain-Invariant Features for Cross-Domain Voice Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07079)
*Jeongmin Lee,Seung Yun,Minkyu Lee,Ran Han,Yoonkyu Woo,Jinxia Huang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文探讨语音钓鱼检测面临的三大挑战（真实犯罪录音稀缺且涉及隐私、样本量不足无法微调、需要轻量级声学检测），通过跨领域评估对比领域不变韵律特征与自监督表示（HuBERT、wav2vec2.0）。结果表明领域不变韵律特征支持零样本部署（69.5% F1），HuBERT在5样本下性能最优（94.2% F1），wav2vec2.0呈现高精度检测特征（99.4%精度），揭示了零样本可用性与高性能之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音钓鱼（vishing）检测面临三大关键挑战：隐私约束导致真实犯罪录音不可获得；即便有数据，样本也极少不足以微调；需要轻量级纯声学检测方案来替代大型自监督模型。作者旨在探索在这三类约束下可行的检测方案，并通过跨领域训练-测试范式评估不同声学特征表示的泛化能力。

Method: 采用跨领域评估方法：在基于场景的演员录音上训练，在真实犯罪通话上进行测试。对比两类特征方案：(1) 领域不变的韵律特征（prosodic features）；(2) 自监督表示模型（HuBERT、wav2vec2.0）。评估设置涵盖零样本（zero-shot）和5样本（5-shot）两种学习场景。

Result: 领域不变韵律特征在零样本下达到69.5% F1，在5样本学习中达到71.0% F1；HuBERT在5样本设置下达到最高性能94.2% F1；wav2vec2.0呈现精度导向的检测特征，5样本下F1达90.2%，精度高达99.4%。

Conclusion: 研究发现存在根本性权衡：领域不变韵律特征可在无真实数据时支持零样本部署，而自监督学习（SSL）方法虽能达到更高性能，但依赖真实样本且需要更多计算资源。选择何种方案取决于实际场景中数据可用性与计算资源的约束条件。

Abstract: Voice phishing detection faces three critical challenges: real criminal recordings are unavailable due to privacy constraints; when available, only a handful of samples exist, insufficient for fine-tuning; and lightweight acoustic-only detection is needed as an alternative to large self-supervised models. We compare domain-invariant prosodic features and self-supervised representations (HuBERT, wav2vec2.0) through cross-domain evaluation-training on scenario-based actor recordings and testing on authentic criminal calls. Domain-invariant prosodic features achieve 69.5% F1 zero-shot and 71.0% with 5-shot learning. HuBERT achieves highest performance (94.2% F1, 5-shot), while wav2vec2.0 exhibits a precision-oriented detection profile (90.2% F1 with 99.4% precision, 5-shot). These findings reveal fundamental trade-offs: domain-invariant features enable zero-shot deployment when no real data exists, while SSL methods achieve higher performance but require real samples and compute.

</details>


### [218] [KABURI-TTS: Phoneme-Keyed Activity-conditioned Bi-channel Utterance Rendering for Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07200)
*Ryuichiro Higashinaka,Shinnosuke Takamichi,Tetsuji Ogawa*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出KABURI-TTS，一种用于全双工对话语音合成的模型。它以每说话人的音素光栅图为输入，在基于帧的音素及其导出的语音活动条件下，将两个说话人的语音渲染到独立声道上，从而可控地生成双人、每声道一人的对话语音。用户评估显示其在话语和交互层面的自然度均优于强基线，并能产生更自然的重叠与更频繁的轮流说话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现全双工口语对话需要大量每声道一说话人的双通道对话语音数据。现有的对话式文本到语音（TTS）引擎在处理双方同时发生的现象（如反馈语、打断、重叠）时鲁棒性不足，难以再现人类式的重叠语音。因此需要一种可控且能生成自然重叠的对话语音合成方法。

Method: KABURI-TTS以每说话人音素光栅作为输入，将两个说话人的语音分别渲染到不同声道上，并以逐帧音素及其推导出的语音活动（voice activity）作为条件。由于音素光栅由独立模块提供，该方法支持可控地生成每声道一说话人的双人对话语音。

Result: 用户评估表明，与强基线相比，KABURI-TTS在话语层面和交互层面均获得更高的自然度。语音活动分析进一步确认，该方法生成的语音包含更多重叠以及更频繁的轮流说话现象。

Conclusion: KABURI-TTS能够有效生成具有人类式重叠和自然轮流说话特性的全双工双人对话语音，为会话语音合成提供了一种可控且自然度更高的解决方案。

Abstract: Realizing full-duplex spoken dialogue requires large amounts of two-channel, one-speaker-per-channel conversational speech data. Although conversational text-to-speech (TTS) engines have been developed, they are not necessarily robust to two-party simultaneous phenomena such as backchannels, interruptions, and overlaps that occur while the interlocutor is speaking. In this work, aiming at conversational speech synthesis that reproduces human-like overlap, we propose KABURI-TTS. KABURI-TTS takes a per-speaker phoneme raster as input and renders the speech of the two speakers on separate channels, conditioned on the per-frame phonemes and the voice activity derived from them. Because the phoneme raster is supplied by a separate module, the proposed method enables controllable generation of one-speaker-per-channel, two-party spoken dialogue. A user evaluation shows that, compared with strong baselines, the proposed method attains higher naturalness at both the utterance and the interaction level. Furthermore, an analysis of voice activity confirms that the proposed method produces more overlap and more frequent turn-taking.

</details>


### [219] [From Scores to Evidence: Auditable Decisions Can Improve Speech Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08899)
*Mengzhe Geng,Yujia Lu,Patrick Littell,Manuela Kunz,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可审计的语音深度伪造检测决策记录方法，在保持单一评分输出的同时，通过引入被动检测分数、条件键控探针分数、检索支持和说话人画像边际四类对齐线索及显式不一致坐标，使模型具备可解释性和可审计性，在ASVspoof 5 Track 1匹配子集上达到8.43%的等错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音深度伪造检测器通常为每个语音片段输出一个标量分数，该分数对排序系统有效，但无法解释边界样本为何应被信任、延迟处理或人工复审。两个分数相近的语音可能因完全不同的原因落入同一分数带（如被动检测与检索证据不一致，或关键探针不可用）。作者希望在保持标量决策输出的同时，不丢失决策背后的溯源信息。

Method: 提出一个可审计的决策记录框架，融合四条对齐线索进入延迟校准步骤：(1)被动检测器分数；(2)基于标记导数的条件键控探针分数；(3)检索支持；(4)说话人画像边际，并附带显式的不一致坐标。该框架将被动证据与检索增强证据结合，通过固定规则和晚期校准两种方式融合多线索。

Result: 在ASVspoof 5 Track 1的4,080条匹配子集上：固定检索增强规则将等错误率从纯检索的15.84%降至11.91%；基于完整记录进行晚期校准达到8.43%等错误率。在33.75%的复审预算下，暴露的线索并集覆盖了校准模型82.85%的误差。最佳被动WavLM基线仍达6.71%等错误率。

Conclusion: 该决策记录并非作为更强的独立检测器提出（最佳被动基线6.71% EER仍优于其8.43%），其核心贡献在于在产生单一操作分数的同时保留了每个被标记语音背后的证据来源，支持阈值判定与人工复审流程，实现了检测准确性与决策可审计性之间的平衡。

Abstract: Speech deepfakes can mimic a speaker's voice convincingly enough to deceive listeners and automated systems. This has driven strong progress in speech deepfake detection, but most detectors still end with one score per utterance. That score is useful for ranking systems, yet it says little about why a borderline item should be trusted, deferred, or reviewed. Two utterances can fall in the same score band for different reasons, for example because passive and retrieval evidence disagree or because the keyed probe is unavailable. We ask whether the final decision can remain scalar without discarding that provenance. We answer this question with an auditable decision record that carries four aligned cues into a late calibration step: a passive detector score, a conditional keyed-probe score on a marked derivative, retrieval support, and a speaker-profile margin, together with explicit disagreement coordinates. On the 4,080-example ASVspoof 5 Track 1 matched subset, the fixed retrieval-augmented rule improves on retrieval-only evidence, from 15.84 percent to 11.91 percent EER, and late calibration over the full record reaches 8.43 percent EER. At a 33.75 percent review budget, the exposed cue union covers 82.85 percent of the calibrated model's errors. The best passive WavLM run still reaches 6.71 percent EER, so we do not present the decision record as a stronger standalone detector. Its contribution is to preserve the evidence behind each surfaced utterance while still producing one operating score for thresholding and review.

</details>


### [220] [Iterative Audio Separation with Mixture Consistency via MIMO Model Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07226)
*Yukara Ikemiya,WeiHsiang Liao,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，通过将音频源分离模型扩展到多输入多输出（MIMO）配置，实现具有混合一致性（mixture consistency）的稳定且有效的迭代式音频分离。该框架在保持架构优势和混合一致性特征的同时引入迭代预测，并结合判别器和生成模型进行扩展。实验表明，将其应用于最先进的分离模型可取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在音频分离领域，混合一致性对于需要精确相位和音色信息的应用至关重要。尽管扩散模型等迭代方法在语音增强或用户引导的目标源分离任务中取得了感知上优越的效果，但现有方法大多集中在通过架构改进实现单步分离（SISO或SIMO配置），因为具有混合一致性的音频分离通常被视为具有唯一解的回归问题。作者希望通过扩展至MIMO配置，在保留架构优势和混合一致性特性的同时引入迭代预测机制。

Method: 本文提出将现有音频源分离模型的架构从SISO/SIMO配置扩展为多输入多输出（MIMO）配置，从而在不牺牲架构优势的前提下实现迭代式预测。同时进行了全面的消融研究，将框架与判别器（discriminators）结合，并进一步扩展为生成模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，将该框架应用于当前最先进的源分离模型时，性能获得了显著的提升。消融研究验证了MIMO配置与判别器结合以及扩展为生成模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过MIMO架构扩展，可以在保持混合一致性特性的同时实现迭代式音频分离，且该框架具有通用性，能够为现有最先进的分离模型带来稳定的性能增益。

Abstract: This paper proposes a general framework for stable and effective iterative audio separation with mixture consistency by extending source separation models to a multi-input multi-output (MIMO) configuration. In the field of audio separation, mixture consistency is an essential property for many applications that require accurate phase and timbral information of target sources. While iterative approaches such as diffusion models achieve perceptually superior results in speech enhancement or user-guided target source separation tasks, most existing methods focus on single-step separation with a single-input single-output (SISO) or single-input multi-output (SIMO) configuration through architectural improvements, since mixture-consistent audio separation is generally regarded as a regression problem that admits a unique solution. By extending these architectures to a MIMO configuration, we introduce iterative prediction without compromising the architectural advantages or the characteristics of mixture consistency. We conduct a comprehensive ablation study of combining the framework with discriminators and extending it to a generative model. Experimental results demonstrate significant performance improvements when applying the proposed framework to state-of-the-art separation models.

</details>


### [221] [TAD: Token-Adaptive Contrastive Decoding with Confidence-Guided Gating for Hallucination Mitigation in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07286)
*Heyu Chang,Nianwen Si,Hao Zhang,Wenlin Zhang,Dan Qu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Adaptive Decoding (TAD)，一种无需训练的推理策略，通过在首个解码步骤中将真实音频与静音参考的logits进行对比来抑制大型音频语言模型(LALMs)的音频幻觉（即对不存在的音源回答"yes"）。TAD采用token自适应、置信度引导的门控机制，避免了固定对比强度的过度修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型(LALMs)在音频问答中会幻觉性地输出不存在的音频事件（将"no"错误回答为"yes"），严重削弱了模型在音频问答任务中的可靠性与可信度。

Method: 提出Token-Adaptive Decoding (TAD)，这是一种无需训练的幻觉缓解策略。其核心包括：(1) 将真实音频条件下的logits与匹配的静音参考进行对比，用于锚定初始的是/否决策；(2) 引入token自适应、置信度引导的门控机制，该机制在首个解码步骤中对决策至关重要，在肯定性token上采用类别条件约束；(3) 利用音频-静音差值（audio-silent margin）来避免在证据不足或充分时产生过度修正。

Result: 在AudioCaps-Hallucination数据集上，相对于固定对比强度的对照方法Audio-Aware Decoding (AAD)，TAD在Qwen2上的F1提升了0.059至0.117（覆盖Popular、Adversarial和Random三个划分），在Gemma上提升0.025至0.064。在Clotho-AQA上，TAD将Qwen2的F1从0.810提升到0.816，在Gemma上表现与AAD相当。

Conclusion: TAD通过token自适应的对比解码策略，在不进行额外训练的情况下有效缓解了LALMs的音频幻觉问题，在不同模型和数据划分上均优于或持平于固定强度的对照方法，验证了自适应门控和音频-静音余量机制的有效性。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) can hallucinate audio objects, answering "yes" to absent sound events, thus undermining reliability in audio question answering. We propose Token-Adaptive Decoding (TAD), a training-free strategy for hallucination mitigation that grounds the initial yes/no decision by contrasting logits under real audio with a matched silent reference. TAD introduces a token-adaptive, confidence-guided gate that is decision-critical at the first decoding step and class-conditional on affirmative tokens, using the audio-silent margin to avoid overcorrection when evidence is weak or already sufficient. Experiments on AudioCaps-Hallucination show that, relative to Audio-Aware Decoding (AAD), a contrastive baseline with fixed contrast strength, TAD improves F1 for Qwen2 by 0.059 to 0.117 across Popular, Adversarial, and Random splits, and for Gemma by 0.025 to 0.064, while on Clotho-AQA it raises F1 from 0.810 to 0.816 on Qwen2 and remains comparable to AAD on Gemma.

</details>


### [222] [Silent Metronome: Rhythmic Grounding for Live Music Accompaniment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07688)
*Kevin Bretz,Derya Soydaner,Aske Plaat*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出Silent Metronome (SiMe)方法，为实时流式伴奏生成模型提供独立的节奏参考通道（编码节拍内与小节内的相位、速度及拍号），从而避免模型仅依赖自身不完美的过去输出推断节奏而导致的节奏漂移问题。实验表明，节拍对齐性能较严格因果基线提升3.2倍，甚至超越拥有1秒前瞻的非因果参考模型，同时输入与伴奏间的连贯性仅相差1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时伴奏模型受到严格因果性约束，必须在收听未来输入之前逐帧输出。由于缺乏独立的时钟或时间参考，模型只能从自身历史输出中反推节奏、速度与节拍相位，而模型自身输出本身可能存在误差，导致误差累积并表现为明显的节奏漂移（rhythmic drift）。作者类比人类合奏时共享节拍器的做法，认为应显式为模型提供不可漂移的节奏参考信号。

Method: 1) 提出Silent Metronome (SiMe)：将节拍内相位与节拍内的小节相位编码为正弦/余弦周期函数，结合速度(tempo)与拍号(time signature)构成独立的条件通道，与音频输入共同馈入模型。该条件信号不依赖生成音频，因此不会随模型误差漂移。2) 引入互补的辅助头(auxiliary heads)约束潜空间表征，包括一个新颖的预测模型自身未来token的头。3) 在严格因果设置下训练，评测时节奏信号取自真实标注(ground-truth annotations)。

Result: 在严格因果设置下，节拍对齐较基线提升3.2倍，并超越拥有完整1秒前瞻的非因果参考系统；输入音频与伴奏之间的连贯性（coherence）仅比该非因果参考低1个百分点以内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，流式伴奏系统不应让模型暗中推断节奏，而应将节奏作为显式共享的信号加以传递，正如人类乐队合奏所做的那样。这一节奏参考通道提供了不可漂移的时间锚点，是消除因果生成中节奏累积误差的关键。

Abstract: Live accompaniment models generate music for an incoming audio stream, committing to each output frame before hearing what comes next. In this strictly causal setting the model must infer tempo, meter, and metrical phase from its own imperfect past, whereby compounding errors quickly become audible as rhythmic drift. Put simply, the model has ears but no temporal reference, so when the ears hear imperfect, ambiguous music, the model will produce a flawed output. We propose Silent Metronome (SiMe), which gives it the temporal reference, encoding the phase within the beat and within the bar as periodic functions, pairing them with tempo and time signature, and supplying the result as a separate conditioning channel. Because this reference is independent of the generated audio, it cannot drift. Complementary auxiliary heads shape the latent representation, including a novel head that predicts the model's own future tokens. With the metrical signal taken from ground-truth annotations, beat alignment improves by a factor of 3.2 over the strictly causal baseline and surpasses a non-causal reference granted a full second of look-ahead. Coherence between input and accompaniment stays within a single point of that reference. These results suggest that streaming accompaniment systems should treat rhythm as a signal to be shared, as human ensembles do, rather than inferred.

</details>


### [223] [Clean Accuracy Does Not Guarantee Provenance Robustness: A Prospective Codec-Stress Evaluation of Audio Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07981)
*Gang Shi*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文通过前瞻性注册实验证明，音频来源归属（provenance attribution）在经编解码器（codec）传输后性能显著下降。在单阶段编解码传输下，WavLM-Base+在两个语料库上的Macro-F1分别损失53.5和70.3个百分点，W2V2-BERT 2.0损失61.0和49.8个百分点。性能退化高度依赖具体条件和表征类型，两个编码器在11/12条件中表现不同，且退化在多种表征族和线性头中普遍存在。MP3在不同感知指标下排序不一致，表明干净基准的高准确率不能表征部署鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往音频来源归属研究在干净基准上接近天花板准确率，但实际分析中音频通常经过转码（transcoding）。现有工作未系统测量编解码传输对归属准确率的实际影响，因此需要在前瞻性注册框架下量化真实部署条件下的性能退化程度及其条件依赖性。

Method: 采用前瞻性注册（prospectively registered）实验设计：在训练任何归属模型之前，先从保真度元数据固定分析区域，进行单阶段codec传输下的闭集归属测量。使用两个语料库、同时在组件级频带上评估WavLM-Base+和W2V2-BERT 2.0两种编码器，并对比ECAPA-TDNN（干净条件合格）与Proxy-Anchor头以排除表征族或弱线性头因素。超参差（prespecified）设定±5个百分点作为编码器差异阈值，并使用SI-SDR和PESQ-WB两个感知指标排序条件。

Result: 在支持集（in-support）内，WavLM-Base+在两个语料库上损失53.5 [43.5, 63.6]和70.3 [63.0, 77.5] Macro-F1点，W2V2-BERT 2.0损失61.0 [56.8, 65.1]和49.8 [41.6, 57.9]点。退化在条件网格内差异巨大（同一网格内WavLM损失范围-0.4至+53.5点），两种编码器有六/十二个条件超出±5点预设界线。干净合格的ECAPA-TDNN与Proxy-Anchor头同样退化为可比程度。注册的匹配保真度对比在该网格上不可估计。MP3@8kbit/s在SI-SDR中排中游但在PESQ-WB中垫底，且引起最大的准确率损失。

Conclusion: 编解码传输对音频来源归属准确率的破坏是严重且条件高度依赖的，跨表征族和分类头均普遍存在。单一的干净基准准确率不能表征部署鲁棒性，需要结合多条件编解码评估与任务相关的感知度量才能得出可靠结论。

Abstract: Audio provenance attribution - which system produced a synthetic utterance - is reported at near-ceiling accuracy on clean benchmarks, yet audio reaching an analyst has usually been transcoded. We report a prospectively registered measurement of closed-set attribution after single-stage codec transport, with the analysis region fixed from fidelity metadata before any attribution model was trained. On two corpora, in-support losses reach 53.5 [43.5, 63.6] and 70.3 [63.0, 77.5] Macro-F1 points for WavLM-Base+, and 61.0 [56.8, 65.1] and 49.8 [41.6, 57.9] for W2V2-BERT 2.0, under simultaneous component-level bands. Degradation is strongly condition- and representation-dependent: within one in-support grid WavLM losses run from -0.4 to +53.5 points, and the two encoders differ beyond a prespecified +/-5-point margin at six of twelve conditions. A clean-qualified ECAPA-TDNN and a Proxy-Anchor head degrade comparably, so the effect is not confined to one representation family or a weak linear head. The registered matched-fidelity comparison was not estimable on this grid, and waveform and perceptual measures order the conditions differently: MP3 at 8 kbit/s ranks mid-grid on SI-SDR but last on PESQ-WB while causing the largest loss. For the tested tasks, corpora, representations and codec grid, a clean accuracy figure does not by itself characterise deployment robustness.

</details>


### [224] [Geometry-Informed Distributed Acoustic Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08026)
*Yiyuan Yang,Shitong Xu,Niki Trigoni,Andrew Markham*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出一种基于几何信息的分布式声学场景理解框架，利用分布式麦克风、音频频谱变换器和拓扑感知图神经网络融合时空声学特征，并将特征解码为离散语义三元组，最后通过冻结的大语言模型结合环境几何进行空间理解、推断缺失过渡并生成物理一致的场景叙事。在多房间模拟器上优于集中式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多房间环境中的声学场景理解具有挑战性，现有系统多依赖单一集中式麦克风阵列，由于墙壁和门会阻挡声音信号，导致性能不佳。

Method: 提出了几何信息引导的分布式声学场景理解框架：采用分布式麦克风采集信号；使用音频频谱变换器（Audio Spectrogram Transformer）与拓扑感知图神经网络（topology-aware GNN）融合时空声学特征；将融合特征解码为离散语义三元组；最后利用冻结的大语言模型结合环境几何信息进行空间理解、推断缺失场景过渡并生成物理一致的场景叙事。

Result: 在自定义多房间模拟器上的实验表明，该框架优于集中式基线方法，并在模拟遮挡条件下提升了空间一致性。

Conclusion: 分布式声学感知结合拓扑感知特征融合与大语言模型的符号推理，是解决多房间声学场景理解中遮挡与空间一致性问题的一种有效途径。

Abstract: Acoustic scene understanding in multi-room environments is a difficult task. Most existing systems use a single centralized microphone array, and they often fail because walls and doors block sound signals. To address this challenge, we propose a geometry-informed distributed acoustic scene understanding framework. Our system leverages distributed microphones and uses an audio spectrogram transformer and a topology-aware graph neural network to fuse spatio-temporal acoustic features. Then, these features are decoded into discrete semantic triplets. Finally, a frozen large language model combines these symbolic observations with the environmental geometry. This allows the system to perform spatial understanding, infer plausible missing transitions, and generate a physically consistent narrative of the scene. Experiments on a custom multi-room simulator demonstrate that our framework outperforms centralized baselines and improves spatial consistency under simulated occlusion.

</details>


### [225] [Stabilizing Instruction Supervision for Instruct-TTS via Controllable Diversification and Drift Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08204)
*Yizhong Geng,Kecan Mao,Qifei Li,Cong Wang,Yingming Gao,Ruimin Wang,Chunfeng Wang,Hao Li,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文发现Instruct-TTS系统通过LLM重写将结构化风格标签扩展为自然语言训练指令时，超过40%的无约束重写存在语义漂移，破坏监督信号并削弱泛化能力。作者将问题形式化为"指令监督不稳定性"，并提出数据中心的稳定化方案，包含三个互补机制：可控指令多样化（系统性扩展）、基于LLM的漂移过滤（质量控制）和属性对齐监督（将韵律控制锚定在声学扰动上）。在InstructTTSEval中文子集上，指令遵循率从无微调的34.5%和朴素微调的51.0%提升至56.4%；约束重写将漂移从40.4%降至15.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Instruct-TTS系统依赖LLM将结构化风格标签重写为自然语言训练指令，但作者发现大量（超过40%）无约束重写会产生语义漂移，这些漂移指令作为训练监督会污染模型学习并削弱泛化性能。这一问题此前未被系统识别和形式化。

Method: 提出数据中心的"稳定化配方"，包含三个互补机制：(1) 可控指令多样化——通过约束性重写实现系统性的指令扩展以提升覆盖度；(2) 基于LLM的漂移过滤——对生成的指令进行语义漂移的质量控制；(3) 属性对齐监督——利用声学扰动将韵律控制与属性表征进行对齐以实现grounding。

Result: 在InstructTTSEval中文子集上，该方法将指令遵循率从无微调的34.5%和朴素微调的51.0%提升至56.4%；采用的约束性重写策略将指令漂移率从40.4%显著降低至15.4%。消融实验证实三个机制之间具有互补性。

Conclusion: 三个机制组合有效解决了Instruct-TTS的指令监督不稳定问题，验证了数据中心方法在指令驱动生成任务中的价值。作者进一步指出其提出的漂移分类体系可能超越TTS领域，泛化应用于更广泛的指令驱动生成场景。

Abstract: Instruct-TTS systems expand structured style labels into natural-language training instructions through LLM rewriting, yet we find that over 40% of unconstrained rewrites contain semantic drift that corrupts supervision and weakens generalization. We formalize this problem as instruction supervision instability and propose a data-centric stabilization recipe that jointly improves coverage and fidelity through three mechanisms: controllable instruction diversification for systematic expansion, LLM-based drift filtering for quality control, and attribute-aligned supervision that grounds prosody control in acoustic perturbations. On the Chinese split of InstructTTSEval, our recipe raises instruction-following from 34.5% without fine-tuning and 51.0% with naive fine-tuning to 56.4%, while constrained rewriting reduces drift from 40.4% to 15.4%. Ablations confirm the three mechanisms are complementary, and the drift taxonomy may generalize to instruction-driven generation beyond TTS.

</details>


### [226] [Noise Adaptive Streaming Audio-Visual Speech Token Enhancement for Robust Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08390)
*Bella Godiva,Yeonju Kim,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出AV-STE，一种模块化流式视听前端，利用音频和唇部视频恢复被噪声破坏的语义语音token，再送入冻结的语音对话大模型（如Moshi），无需对对话模型进行任何多模态训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工口语对话系统的纯音频感知在背景噪声和重叠语音下表现不佳，导致响应不连贯。现有视听对话方法通常需要适配或微调大型语音对话模型本身来处理视觉输入，多模态训练成本高昂，限制了实用性。

Method: AV-STE作为模块化流式视听前端，在噪声音频和唇部视频到达语音LLM之前，先对损坏的语义语音token进行恢复。该模块解耦于下游对话模型，使其完全冻结无需训练，保留预训练的对话能力。可与冻结的Moshi等模型集成。

Result: 与冻结的Moshi集成后，在数据集内说话人干扰场景下，GPT-4o评判的平均响应连贯性从1.42提升至1.91，同时基本保持轮次切换行为。增益可迁移到域外Seamless Interaction场景。

Conclusion: AV-STE证明无需训练大型语音对话模型即可通过模块化视听前端有效增强其在噪声条件下的鲁棒性，兼顾性能提升与计算成本控制，并具备跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems enable simultaneous listening and speaking, but their audio-only perception often fails under background noise and overlapping speech, leading to incoherent responses. Recent audio-visual dialogue approaches show that incorporating visual cues such as lip movements improve robustness under audio corruption. However, existing approaches often adapt the large speech dialogue model itself to process visual input, requiring costly multimodal training. We propose AV-STE, a modular streaming audio-visual front-end that restores corrupted semantic speech tokens from noisy audio and lip video before they reach the speech LLM. The downstream dialogue model remains entirely frozen, preserving its pretrained conversational capabilities. When integrated with frozen Moshi, AV-STE improves average GPT-4o-judged response coherence from 1.42 to 1.91 under same-dataset speaker interference while largely preserving turn-taking behavior. Gains also transfer to out-of-domain Seamless Interaction.

</details>


### [227] [X2Streaming-ASR: wait when uncertain, emit when ready for streaming ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08672)
*Zhiwei Lin,Kaiqi Fu,Rime Wen,Zehan Liu,Shawn Qin,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出X2Streaming-ASR流式语音识别系统，将流式识别拆分为"何时提交"与"提交什么"两个子问题，通过三阶段训练（先建立流式识别能力、再用自动探测轨迹预热提交策略、最后用字符级分段组相对奖励优化策略），在AISHELL-1/2/3和WenetSpeech上实现27-84ms的平均字符级提交延迟，显著优于409-585ms的基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式ASR系统通常采用固定块大小、前视或目标延迟等启发式方法，或鼓励在声学边界附近发射输出，这些方法没有在单次通过、硬提交约束下直接优化每个输出位置应使用的额外上下文量。需要一种能够显式建模提交决策、同时对识别精度和延迟进行联合优化的方法。

Method: X2Streaming-ASR将流式识别分解为"何时提交"(when to commit)和"提交什么"(what to commit)两个部分。其核心创新是三阶段训练流程：(1) 第一阶段建立流式识别能力；(2) 第二阶段用自动探测的轨迹来预热提交策略（warm-start）；(3) 第三阶段使用字符级、分段分配的组相对奖励（segment-assigned group-relative rewards）同时针对识别准确率和延迟对策略进行细化优化。

Result: 在AISHELL-1/2/3和WenetSpeech四个中文数据集上，X2Streaming-ASR实现27-84ms的平均字符级提交延迟（相对强制对齐的字符端点），而评估的流式基线系统为409-585ms。在AISHELL-1和AISHELL-3上，该系统在所有评估系统中取得最优的流式CER（字符错误率），同时延迟大幅降低。

Conclusion: 通过将"提交时机决策"作为可学习的策略而非固定启发式规则，并采用三阶段训练逐步引导策略优化，X2Streaming-ASR能够在保持甚至超越识别精度的前提下，大幅降低提交延迟，证明了在硬提交约束下直接优化上下文利用量是可行且有效的方向。

Abstract: Streaming automatic speech recognition (ASR) for real-time voice agents and full-duplex dialogue must provide accurate partial transcripts with low commit latency. Existing systems commonly use a fixed chunk size, look-ahead, or target delay, or encourage emissions near estimated acoustic boundaries. These approaches do not directly optimize how much additional context to use at each output position under a single-pass, hard-commit constraint. We propose X2Streaming-ASR, which decomposes streaming recognition into when to commit and what to commit. Its three-stage training procedure first establishes streaming recognition ability, then warm-starts the commit policy with automatically probed trajectories, and finally refines the policy using character-level, segment-assigned group-relative rewards for recognition accuracy and latency. Across AISHELL-1/2/3 and WenetSpeech, X2Streaming-ASR achieves a mean character-level commit latency of 27-84 ms relative to forced-aligned character endpoints, compared with 409-585 ms for the evaluated streaming baselines. It achieves the best streaming CER among the evaluated systems on AISHELL-1 and AISHELL-3 with substantially lower latency.

</details>


### [228] [TontaubeV1: Streaming Text-to-Speech with Hierarchical Codec Modeling and Bounded Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08703)
*Fritz Cremer,Jonathan Cremer*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: TontaubeV1是一个在单张消费级GPU上实现流式语音合成的同时保持自然韵律的模型，采用12.5 Hz层次化DualCodec表示，将语义流与声学精化分离。模型由Qwen3-1.7B派生变换器预测语义流和时长，三个Qwen3-0.6B派生变换器逐步添加声学精化，总参数2.9B。在RTX 5090上首音频延迟约200ms，端到端RTF为0.08（单输入）/0.02（八并发），韵律质量匹配ElevenLabs Flash v2.5并优于多个竞品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音合成系统常常面临自然韵律与推理效率之间的权衡：更高的感知质量通常意味着更高的计算成本和延迟。该论文旨在解决这一核心矛盾，寻求在不牺牲韵律自然度的情况下实现高效流式推理。

Method: 采用12.5 Hz层次化DualCodec编码，将语音分为语义流和逐级声学精化。核心假设是韵律结构在语义流生成时已基本确立，因此由一个Qwen3-1.7B派生的变换器预测语义流并确定话语时长，三个更小的Qwen3-0.6B派生变换器各负责一层声学精化。文本按字符标记而非子词，文本与音频标记在共享位置配对支持有界上下文的长文本生成。通过将重叠的DualCodec重建映射到VibeVoice声学潜空间并因果解码，实现了流式输出。模型支持最多一分钟参考音频作为声音条件。

Result: 模型总计约2.9B参数，在单张RTX 5090上流式路径首音频约200ms。非流式测量中单输入端到端RTF为0.08，八并发总计RTF为0.02。在LLM-as-a-judge有声书朗读基准上，韵律质量与ElevenLabs Flash v2.5持平，优于Fish Audio S2 Pro、April 2026 Gradium API和Cartesia Sonic 3。

Conclusion: TontaubeV1成功展示了层次化语义-声学分离设计在平衡自然韵律与高效流式推理方面的有效性，在消费级GPU上实现了接近SOTA商用系统的韵律质量。模型权重已在Hugging Face上以Tontaube社区模型许可证1.0发布。

Abstract: Text-to-speech systems often face a trade-off between natural prosody and efficient inference: higher perceptual quality typically comes at increased computational cost and latency. We present TontaubeV1, a model that preserves natural prosody while enabling streaming from a single consumer GPU. Speech is encoded by the hierarchical DualCodec representation at 12.5 Hz, which separates a semantic stream from successive acoustic refinements. Our design assumes that prosodic structure is largely established when the semantic stream is generated, and allocates capacity accordingly: a Qwen3-1.7B-derived transformer predicts that stream and thereby the utterance duration, while three progressively smaller Qwen3-0.6B-derived transformers each add one acoustic refinement. Text is tokenized per character rather than by subword. Paired text and audio markers at shared positions support long-form generation with bounded context, and overlapping DualCodec reconstructions are mapped into the VibeVoice acoustic latent space and decoded causally, enabling streaming despite DualCodec's noncausal decoder. The model accepts up to one minute of reference audio for voice conditioning and is designed primarily for English and German, with additional multilingual support. The four predictors total 2.9B parameters; on a single RTX 5090 the streaming path reaches approximately 200 ms to first audio. In separate non-streaming measurements, the end-to-end real-time factor (RTF) is 0.08 for one input and the aggregate RTF is 0.02 across eight concurrent inputs. On our LLM-as-a-judge audiobook-reading benchmark, TontaubeV1 matches ElevenLabs Flash v2.5 and outperforms Fish Audio S2 Pro, the April 2026 Gradium API, and Cartesia Sonic 3 on prosody. The model weights are released on Hugging Face under the Tontaube Community Model License 1.0.

</details>


### [229] [AuK Technical Report: An Open-Source Foundational Model for Speech Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08936)
*Ziyang Ma,Zhikang Niu,Wenming Tu,Tianrui Wang,Ruiqi Yan,Junxi Liu,Yanru Huo,Nickk Huang,Yang Liu,Qicong Xie,Zeyu Xie,Hui Wang,Haitao Li,Zixuan Jiang,Yalin Li,Jie Fang,Yifan Duan,Zeyue Tian,Guangzheng Li,Haina Zhu,Shuyi Wang,Jinwen Wang,Mingyu Cui,Tian Tan,Auden,Sen Liang,Steve Yves,Shan Yang,Liefeng Bo,Zilong Zheng,Kai Yu,Eng-Siong Chng,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: AuK是一个开源基础模型，通过自然语言指令和音频上下文的统一接口实现语音生成与编辑。该模型基于约30.3亿指令-音频实例和195万小时有效监督训练，涵盖五大任务族（语音生成、内容编辑、增强与分离、副语言编辑、声学编辑）。架构上结合了多模态大语言模型（语义条件）、联合训练的VAE（声学条件）以及混合整流流Transformer（双流MMDiT块+单流DiT块）。训练过程包含生成预热、联合预训练，以及人类反馈偏好优化和奖励强化学习的后训练。通过一致性初始化和任务路由的解耦DMD蒸馏，AuK-Flash可在4步推理且无需CFG的情况下实现较完整模型4.5倍的墙钟加速。实验表明其在零样本和指令控制的语音生成及通用指令编辑任务上具有领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音生成和编辑模型往往针对单一任务或任务族单独设计，缺乏统一框架来同时支持生成与多种编辑能力（如内容编辑、增强、分离、副语言及声学编辑）。作者旨在构建一个开源基础模型，通过统一的自然语言指令和音频上下文接口，实现语音生成与编辑的联合建模，以降低多任务门槛并推动领域 reproducibility。

Method: AuK的架构由三部分组成：多模态大语言模型用于语义条件编码；在语音、通用音频和音乐上联合训练的VAE提供声学条件；混合整流流Transformer采用双流MMDiT块与统一单流DiT块的组合进行生成。训练流程为：先用纯生成数据预热，再进行生成与编辑的联合预训练；随后通过人类反馈偏好优化（针对开放式编辑）和奖励强化学习（针对语音生成）进行后训练。推理侧采用一致性初始化与任务路由的解耦DMD进行模型蒸馏，得到AuK-Flash，支持4步无CFG推理。

Result: AuK-Flash在4步推理且不使用classifier-free guidance的条件下，相较完整模型实现4.5倍墙钟加速。实验显示AuK在零样本及指令控制下的语音生成和通用指令编辑任务上达到领先水平，在信号级恢复（增强/分离等）任务上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: AuK证明了一个统一的指令+音频上下文接口可以同时支撑语音生成和多种编辑任务，并通过完善的数据规模（30.3亿指令-音频对）、多阶段训练策略和高效蒸馏手段实现SOTA性能与实用推理速度。作者开源了源码与模型权重以促进可复现性与后续研究。

Abstract: We introduce AuK, an open-source foundational model that unifies speech generation and editing through a common interface of natural-language instructions and audio context. To support this broad capability set, we construct approximately 3.03 billion instruction--audio instances and 1.95 million hours of effective supervision across five task families: speech generation, content editing, enhancement and separation, paralinguistic editing, and acoustic editing. AuK combines a multimodal large language model for semantic conditioning, an VAE jointly trained on speech, general audio, and music for acoustic conditioning, and a hybrid rectified-flow Transformer that performs dual-stream MMDiT blocks followed by unified single-stream DiT blocks for generation. Training begins with generation-only warm-up and proceeds to joint generation--editing pre-training. We then apply complementary post-training strategies: human-feedback preference optimization for open-ended editing and reward-based reinforcement learning for speech generation. To reduce inference cost, we further distill the model with consistency initialization and task-routed Decoupled DMD. The resulting AuK-Flash performs 4-step inference without classifier-free guidance and achieves a 4.5 wall-clock speedup over the full model under matched conditions. Experiments demonstrate leading performance on zero-shot and instruction-controlled speech generation and general instruction-guided editing, while remaining competitive on signal-level restoration tasks. We release both the source code and model weights to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [230] [Disentangled Global-Local Feature Learning with E-Branchformer for Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08948)
*Phuong Tuan Dat,Ho Bao Thu,Nguyen Tran Trung,Pham Viet Hoang,Nguyen Thi Thu Trang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于E-Branchformer架构的音频深度伪造检测方法，通过并行双分支（多头自注意力捕获全局上下文，卷积捕获局部时序模式）结合自监督语音表征，在ASVspoof 2021 LA、DF和In-the-Wild数据集上分别达到0.88%、1.85%和6.30%的等错误率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音合成技术（如TTS和语音转换）的快速发展对基于语音的身份认证系统构成严重威胁，亟需鲁棒的深度伪造检测方法来应对日益复杂和多样化的伪造攻击。

Method: 提出E-Branchformer架构，采用并行分支结构：多头自注意力分支捕获全局上下文依赖，卷积分支捕获局部时序模式；集成深度可分离卷积（DWConv）和Squeeze-and-Excitation（SE）模块，在特征融合后将精炼的patch token信息注入分类token以增强判别能力。

Result: 在ASVspoof 2021 LA、DF和In-the-Wild三个数据集上分别取得0.88%、1.85%和6.30%的等错误率，性能达到最先进水平并大幅超越现有方法；消融实验证实双分支架构提供互补性判别信息，SE聚合显著改善SSL特征集成，DWConv与SE模块联合对分类token增强至关重要。

Conclusion: 该架构在真实场景中展现出优异的泛化能力，能有效应对多样化声学条件和未见过的伪造攻击，为语音深度伪造检测提供了鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of voice synthesis technologies such as text-to-speech and voice conversion poses significant threats to speech-based authentication systems, necessitating robust deepfake detection methods. In this work, we propose a novel E-Branchformer-based architecture that effectively leverages self-supervised speech representations for audio deepfake detection. Our model employs parallel branches to simultaneously capture global contextual dependencies through multi-head self-attention and local temporal patterns through convolutional processing. To enhance discriminative capability, we integrate depthwise convolution and Squeeze-and-Excitation modules that enrich the classification token with refined patch token information after feature merging. Extensive experiments on ASVspoof 2021 LA, DF, and In-the-Wild datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance with equal error rates of 0.88%, 1.85%, and 6.30% respectively, substantially outperforming existing methods. Comprehensive ablation studies validate that the dual-branch architecture provides complementary discriminative information, Squeeze-and-Excitation Aggregation significantly improves SSL feature integration, and the combination of DWConv and SE modules is critical for effective class token enhancement. The superior performance on real-world scenarios demonstrates strong generalization capability to diverse acoustic conditions and unseen spoofing attacks.

</details>


### [231] [TASTE2: Text-Aligned Speech Modeling and Deployment toward Full-Duplex Voice Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08956)
*Yi-Chang Chen,Chun Wei Chen,Dien-Ruei Wu,Jie Lin,Yu-Kuan Fu,Yang-Hsien Lin,Eddie TC Huang,Simon See,Hung-yi Lee,Da-Shan Shiu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出TASTE2，将TASTE从话语级转换扩展为增量式全双工语音对话系统。通过共享文本-词元词汇表、模态对齐对话训练、以及增量式语音逆分词器，TASTE2实现了流式语音处理、对话中的打断处理和边生成边语音合成。在LLaMA-Questions上，TASTE2 (Merge)达到56.3%（相对文本模型保留98.2%准确率），构建的TASTE2 VoiceBot能在Full-Duplex-Bench v1.0上良好处理打断。此外，本文首次系统表征了基于TASTE模型的显式副语言控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音交互需要超越话语级转换的能力，即处理流式语音、管理话轮转换和打断，同时保留预训练的语言能力和声学副语言线索。作者质疑现有TASTE框架是否能为这一目标提供可行路径，并试图将其从话语级转换扩展为增量式对话系统。

Method: TASTE2将话语级TASTE改造为增量式对话栈：(1) 使用共享文本-词元词汇表，消除词级平均；(2) 模态对齐对话训练，使每个文本词元预测一个连续音频潜变量，无需交织异构词元流；(3) 引入增量式语音逆分词器，通过CosyVoice2实现流式合成。系统还经过语音和对话两阶段训练，并构建了TASTE2 VoiceBot实现增量处理和打断（barge-in）时停止生成。

Result: TASTE2 (Merge)在LLaMA-Questions上达到56.3%，相对Qwen2.5-7B Instruct纯文本参考（57.3%）保留98.2%准确率；TASTE2 (Direct)达到53.0%（保留92.4%）。TASTE2 VoiceBot在Full-Duplex-Bench v1.0上能良好处理打断并维持对话连贯性。在双NVIDIA RTX A6000上经TensorRT加速后平均首音频延迟为2.701秒。快速语速经对话SFT后可作为跨策略概念验证，而情绪控制依赖具体策略，其余副语言属性控制较弱。

Conclusion: TASTE2证明基于TASTE的建模是实现全双工系统的实用路径，同时指出自然对话鲁棒性、语音生成延迟和通用的副语言特征控制仍是待解决的开放挑战。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice interaction requires more than utterance-level conversion. It must process streaming speech, manage turn-taking and interruptions, while preserving pretrained linguistic competence and acoustic paralinguistic cues. We ask whether TASTE (Text-Aligned Speech Tokenization and Embedding) provides a viable path toward this goal. We present TASTE2, which transforms utterance-level TASTE into an incremental dialogue stack. A shared text-token vocabulary removes word-level averaging, while modality-aligned dialogue training predicts one continuous audio latent per text token without interleaving heterogeneous token streams. An incremental Speech Detokenizer enables streaming synthesis through CosyVoice2. After speech and dialogue training, TASTE2 (Merge) reaches 56.3% on LLaMA-Questions against a 57.3% Qwen2.5-7B Instruct text-only reference (98.2% accuracy retention), and TASTE2 (Direct) reaches 53.0% (92.4% retention). We build TASTE2 VoiceBot, which processes user speech incrementally, streams synthesized audio, and stops generation on barge-in. On Full-Duplex-Bench v1.0, TASTE2 and TASTE2 VoiceBot handle interruptions well while maintaining high conversational coherence. Natural conversation remains challenging, and deployed mean time to first audio is 2.701 s on two NVIDIA RTX A6000 after TensorRT acceleration. Finally, to our knowledge, we provide the first systematic characterization of explicit paralinguistic control in a TASTE based model. Fast speaking rate serves as a cross-strategy proof of concept after dialogue SFT, while emotion control is strategy dependent and the remaining attributes stay weak. Together, these results establish TASTE based modeling as a practical route toward full-duplex systems while identifying natural conversation robustness, speech generation latency, and feature general paralinguistic control as open challenges. Explore TASTE2 online.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [SETEAB: Multiscale approach with Squeeze-and-Excitation Temporal Enhanced Aware Block for Speech Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06101)
*Duy Vo,Kiet Anh Hoang,Hao Do*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向语音情感识别（SER）的新型轻量级多尺度架构，包含三个关键创新：基于深度可分离卷积的子采样模块以减小模型规模与计算量、Squeeze-and-Excitation块以增强通道级重校准、以及新提出的时间增强感知块以强化时间依赖建模。在基准数据集上，该方法在降低计算复杂度的同时取得了更高准确率，并展现出优于多数最新SER网络的跨语料库泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在同时提升模型的紧凑性、识别性能和泛化能力，解决现有SER模型在计算复杂度与准确率之间难以兼顾的问题。

Method: 提出了一个轻量级多尺度SER架构，由三个核心模块组成：(1) 基于深度卷积的子采样模块，用于减小模型尺寸并保留情感关键线索；(2) Squeeze-and-Excitation块，实现通道级特征重校准，增强表征鲁棒性；(3) 新设计的时间增强感知块（Temporal Enhanced Aware Block），用于强化时间依赖建模，产生更具判别力的情感感知特征。

Result: 在基准SER数据集上的实验表明，该方法在降低计算复杂度的同时取得了更高的识别准确率，并且在跨语料库（cross-corpus）场景下展现出比多数近期先进SER网络更强的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级多尺度架构能够同时兼顾紧凑性、识别准确率与跨语料库泛化能力，为SER任务提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel lightweight multiscale architecture for speech emotion recognition (SER) with three key innovations. First, a depthwise convolution-based subsampling module is introduced to reduce model size and computation while preserving salient emotional cues. Second, a Squeeze-and-Excitation block is integrated to enhance channel-wise recalibration and improve representation robustness. Third, a new Temporal Enhanced Aware Block is designed to strengthen temporal dependency modeling and produce more discriminative emotion-aware features. The proposed model is explicitly designed to jointly improve compactness, recognition performance, and generalizability. Experiments on benchmark SER datasets show that our method achieves higher accuracy with reduced computational complexity, while also delivering stronger cross-corpus performance than most recent advanced networks for SER.

</details>


### [233] [Direction-Preserving Active Noise Control with a Conditional Control-Filter Estimation Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07173)
*Ziyi Yang,Zhengding Luo,Boxiang Wang,Libin Zhang,Woon-Seng Gan*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方向保持主动噪声控制（DP-ANC）方法，将其建模为方向条件化的"对消-保持"优化问题，利用基于特征级线性调制（FiLM）的卷积网络，在单次前向传播中直接从混合参考观测和指定期望方向估计多通道FIR控制滤波器组，在保留传统前馈ANC信号路径的同时，实现了22.8 dB平均噪声降低和-11.4 dB期望信号失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动噪声控制仅最小化误差麦克风处的总扰动，无法区分期望声音与噪声。现有DP-ANC方法要么需要针对每个新观测重复进行解析优化，要么需要通过通透次生源路径估计和再现期望分量，存在计算开销大或路径依赖的问题。

Method: 将DP-ANC表述为方向条件化的对消-保持优化问题，构建分量分离目标函数，联合惩罚残余噪声能量和期望分量引起的控制响应，通过标量加权参数控制对消与保持之间的权衡。利用特征级线性调制（FiLM）对卷积网络注入期望方向条件，并采用可微分的次生路径感知前向模型进行训练。部署时，网络在单次前向传播中直接估计完整的多通道FIR控制滤波器组，同时保留传统前馈ANC信号路径。

Result: 在超过3300个评估案例中，选定工作点实现了22.8 dB的平均噪声降低，期望信号失真为-11.4 dB。使用实测入耳式设备传递函数进行的验证表明，在实测声学配置下性能保持一致。

Conclusion: 该方法通过端到端学习的卷积网络结合FiLM条件化，克服了现有DP-ANC方法需要逐一优化或依赖通透路径的局限性，在噪声抑制与期望信号保持之间实现了可控的权衡，并在实测声学环境下展现出良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Conventional active noise control (ANC) minimizes the total disturbance at the error microphone without distinguishing desired sound from noise. Direction-preserving ANC (DP-ANC) instead aims to attenuate a noise component arriving from a direction other than the specified desired direction while preserving sound naturally arriving from that direction. Existing approaches typically either require analytical optimization to be repeated for each new observation or estimate and reproduce the desired component through a hear-through secondary-source path. To address these limitations, this paper formulates DP-ANC as a direction-conditioned cancellation-preservation optimization problem. A component-separated objective jointly penalizes residual noise energy and the control response induced by the desired component, with a scalar weighting parameter controlling the cancellation-preservation trade-off. A convolutional network conditioned on the specified desired direction through feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM) is trained using a differentiable secondary-path-aware forward model. At deployment, the network estimates the complete multichannel finite impulse response (FIR) control-filter bank directly from a mixed-reference observation and the specified desired direction in a single forward pass, while retaining the conventional feedforward ANC signal path. Over 3300 evaluation cases, the selected operating point achieves 22.8 dB mean noise reduction with a desired-signal distortion of -11.4 dB. Validation using measured in-ear-device transfer functions further demonstrates consistent performance under measured acoustic configurations.

</details>


### [234] [Open-Set Vessel Re-Identification from Underwater Ship-Radiated Noise with a Raw-Waveform Selective-Kernel Acoustic Neural Network (SKANN) and a Cross-Passage Evaluation Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07399)
*Sunil Tyagi*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文首次将水下声学目标识别从闭集分类转向开放式、跨航次（cross-passage）的船舶重识别任务，提出了严格的评估协议以剔除多条高分捷径，并设计了基于原始波形的SKANN编码器。实验表明在40艘船的IARA数据集上，嵌入方法跨航次rank-1仅0.25，与自动化窄带音调比较器（0.26）在排名顶部无显著差异，融合后可达0.35；同时发现过境去重可去除16-21个百分点的表观优势，ShipsEar数据无法将船体身份与录音信道分离，跨网络微调对未见船舶无效。结果支持将方法用于分析人员的排序短名单辅助筛选，而非身份识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下声学目标识别研究收敛于按船舶类型进行分类的闭集任务，这并不能回答监控系统是否此前听过该船体（即个体身份识别）这一关键操作性问题。作者旨在填补这一空白，形式化定义开放式、跨航次的船舶重识别任务，并设计一个排除数据泄漏和捷径的严格评估协议。

Method: (1) 提出评估协议：基于MMSI/IMO的船体不相交划分、跨航次查询/图库划分、纯源头图库、音频裁定过境去重门控，以消除两条最易获得高分的路径。(2) 设计SKANN编码器：前端为四尺度学习滤波器组，通过选择性核注意力融合，以角间隔目标函数训练；数据增强策略扰动录音链路、环境噪声和多径效应，同时保留携带身份信息的窄带谱线。(3) 对比基线：自动化窄带音调比较器及两者分数融合。(4) 附加分析：ShipsEar数据集的身份-信道混淆检测、跨网络微调泛化实验。

Result: 在40艘船的IARA图库（96查询、98航次候选）上，SKANN嵌入跨航次rank-1为0.25，窄带音调比较器为0.26，二者在排名顶部统计上不可区分；嵌入在整体排序上更可靠（AUC 0.82 vs 0.76）；分数融合达到rank-1 0.35，是唯一达到名义显著性的对比，仅作为部分互补性的证据。过境去重单独移除16-21个百分点的表观rank-1优势。ShipsEar上船体身份与录音信道无法分离；跨网络微调只对微调中见过的船舶有效，对未见船舶为零结果。

Conclusion: 在公开水声数据上，跨航次船舶重识别的绝对性能仍然有限，任何方法都不足以支撑直接身份识别。结果建议将此类系统的用途定位为分析人员的排序短名单辅助（triage）工具，而非自动识别系统。同时说明去重等协议细节对评估公平性的影响甚至大于方法间的差异，公开数据所能支撑的结论存在明确边界。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Underwater acoustic target recognition has converged on closed-set classification by vessel type, a task that does not answer whether a monitoring system has heard this hull before. We formalise open-set, cross-passage vessel re-identification on public hydrophone data and specify a protocol that removes the two easiest routes to a high score: hull-disjoint splits keyed to MMSI/IMO, galleries and queries from disjoint passages of each hull, source-pure galleries, and an audio-adjudicated transit-deduplication gate. We describe SKANN, a raw-waveform encoder whose front end is a four-scale bank of learned filters fused by selective-kernel attention, trained with an angular-margin objective and an augmentation regime that perturbs recording chain, ambient noise and multipath while preserving the narrowband lines that carry identity. On a 40-hull IARA gallery (96 queries, 98 passage candidates), cross-passage rank-1 is 0.25 for the embedding and 0.26 for an automated narrowband-tonal comparator; the two are statistically indistinguishable at the top of the ranking, the embedding orders the rest of the list more reliably (AUC 0.82 vs 0.76), and their score fusion reaches rank-1 0.35 -- the only contrast that attains nominal significance, presented as evidence of partial complementarity, not as a recommendation. Transit deduplication alone removes a 16-21 point apparent rank-1 advantage, larger than any between-method difference. Two further findings delimit what public data can support: ShipsEar cannot separate hull identity from recording channel under an identity protocol, and cross-network fine-tuning helps vessels seen during fine-tuning but is a null result on unseen ones. The results support analyst triage over a ranked shortlist, not identification. Checkpoint, validation embeddings, transit map and per-query outputs are released under CC-BY-4.0 (doi:10.5281/zenodo.22160138).

</details>


### [235] [Beyond Localisation Accuracy: Sensorimotor Effects of HRTF Individualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08422)
*Fulvio Missoni,Katarina C. Poole,Tim Murray-Browne,Andrea Canessa,Lorenzo Picinali*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本研究通过更具生态效度的听觉引导视觉搜索范式，发现个体化HRTF在无回声条件下能使听者响应速度平均加快约200ms（尤其对前后方向声源），且该优势主要体现在运动启动阶段，而混响条件下HRTF依赖的差异消失。这表明HRTF个体化能影响听者的感觉运动规划与定向启动行为，即使传统定位精度差异有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上HRTF个体化效果主要依据定位精度来评估，但日常听觉涉及多感觉整合、身体与环境线索以及运动转化，定位精度可能无法充分捕捉HRTF个体化对感觉运动加工过程的真实影响。

Method: 采用听觉引导的视觉搜索范式，听者在可自由移动的条件下通过共位虚拟声音定位视觉目标，比较个体化与非个体化HRTF在无回声与混响两种声学条件下的表现；以响应时间和运动组织指标评估行为表现。

Result: 无回声条件下个体化HRTF比非个体化HRTF响应更快（平均约200ms），对前后声源位置的优势最为明显；该优势主要体现于运动启动阶段，运动总范围仅受微弱影响。混响条件下HRTF依赖差异消失。

Conclusion: HRTF个体化能够影响听者规划和启动定向行为的方式，即使传统定位结果的差异有限。将感觉运动行为的评估与定位绩效相结合，可为HRTF个体化的感知收益提供更敏感且更具生态效度的刻画。

Abstract: Everyday listening requires the brain to integrate cues from the body, environment, other senses, and movement, continuously translating auditory information into action. Yet HRTF individualisation is still commonly assessed through localisation accuracy, which may not fully capture its effects on this sensorimotor process. Here, we investigate whether these effects can instead be revealed through behaviour in a more ecologically valid listening task. We used an aurally guided visual search paradigm in which listeners located a visual target using a co-located virtual sound while moving freely, comparing individualised and non-individualised HRTFs under anechoic and reverberant conditions. Performance was assessed through response times and measures of movement organisation. In anechoic conditions, individualised HRTFs produced faster responses than non-individualised HRTFs, with an average reduction of approximately 200ms and the clearest benefit for front-back source locations. This advantage was expressed primarily in movement initiation, whereas overall movement extent was only weakly affected. Under reverberant conditions, HRTF-dependent differences disappeared. These results suggest that HRTF individualisation can influence how listeners plan and initiate orienting actions even when differences in conventional localisation outcomes are limited. Assessing sensorimotor behaviour alongside localisation performance may therefore provide a more sensitive and ecologically relevant account of the perceptual benefits of HRTF individualisation.

</details>


### [236] [Semantic Refinement of Universal Audio Representations through Audio-Description Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08429)
*Lejun Min,Junyu Dai,Ruichen Zheng,Xinyue Fan,Yang Xiang,Huaichen Zhang,Xingchen Song,Yufei Shi,Han Zhao,Xiangang Li*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文研究在声学预训练编码器(BEST-RQ、重建和CTC的基础上)添加音频-描述对齐的语义精炼方法。通过对照实验区分正确配对与额外对比目标的作用，发现正确对齐在三个配对的种子上使领域平衡分类在线性探针上提升4.66分、序列感知LLM读出上提升2.59分，且每个领域均有正向改善。正确配对贡献了87%的线性探针增益。此外，一个24层的扩展模型在与领先公共编码器的共享评估器对比中仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用音频表示需在保留声学细节的同时，使高层概念可被不同容量的下游模型(如语音、音乐、环境音)访问。作者旨在探究在声学预训练基础上进行语义精炼的有效性，并严格区分正确描述对齐带来的增益与额外对比学习目标本身的贡献，以确保改进源于语义对应关系而非训练机制。

Method: 以BEST-RQ、重建和CTC为基础构建声学预训练编码器，在其上添加音频-描述对齐进行语义精炼。实验设计包含对照轨迹(控制、打乱描述、正确配对)，以分离正确对应关系与额外对比目标的独立影响。每个端点被冻结后，使用时间均值线性探针(评估信息是否可直接访问)和序列感知LLM读出(评估信息是否对更强模型仍然有用)进行评测，覆盖三个配对的随机种子。

Result: 正确对齐在领域平衡分类上，线性探针提升4.66分、序列感知LLM提升2.59分，且所有领域均为正向变化。正确配对解释了87%的线性探针增益；LLM读出中，正确对应关系在字幕生成上体现出最明确的特异性收益。密集声学目标在两种读出方式下均提供互补增益。24层扩展模型在共享评估器下与领先公共编码器竞争时保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 音频-描述的正确语义对齐能有效提升通用音频表示的分类与高层语义能力，其增益主要来源于正确的配对对应关系而非单纯的对比训练目标。即使在更强的序列感知模型读出让，正确对齐仍能带来增益，表明语义精炼信息具有可访问性与泛化价值。该配方可扩展到更大规模模型并与现有领先编码器相竞争。

Abstract: Universal audio representations must preserve acoustic detail while making high-level concepts accessible across speech, music, environmental sound, and downstream models of different capacities. We study semantic refinement of an acoustically pretrained encoder by adding audio-description alignment to a foundation of BEST-RQ, reconstruction, and CTC. We compare matched control, shuffled-description, and correctly paired trajectories to distinguish correct correspondence from an extra contrastive objective. Each endpoint is frozen and evaluated with a temporal-mean linear probe and a sequence-aware LLM readout, testing whether the refined information is directly accessible and remains useful to a stronger model. Across three paired seeds, correct alignment improves domain-balanced classification by 4.66 points with the linear probe and 2.59 points with the sequence-aware LLM, with positive changes in every domain. Correct pairing accounts for 87% of the linear-probe gain, while the LLM shows its clearest correspondence-specific benefit in captioning. Dense acoustic objectives provide complementary gains under both readouts. A separate 24-layer continuation remains competitive with leading public encoders under the shared evaluator, supporting the recipe beyond the controlled study.

</details>


### [237] [Spatial Audio Coding Through Relative Room Impulse Response Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08542)
*Nour Bouayed,Adrien Llave,Jérôme Daniel,Pascal Scalart*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于相对空间房间冲激响应（ReSRIR）显式与盲估计的高阶Ambisonics (HOA)编码方案，利用HOA信号的波束成形版本作为参考信号，通过开发ReSRIR的结构与稀疏性实现高效参数化压缩，在单传输通道场景下达到比IVAS更高的压缩率且质量相当或略优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式虚拟听音依赖HOA等多通道空间音频技术，随着空间分辨率提高通道数增加，需高效压缩以适应带宽受限网络（目标码率接近VoLTE的25 kbps）。现有最先进的参数化编解码器IVAS在混响内容上性能下降，尤其在低码率下，表明其无法准确建模室内声学，因此需要新的编码方案。

Method: 提出一种新型HOA编码方案，核心是对ReSRIR进行显式且盲估计，利用HOA信号的波束成形版本作为参考信号；通过利用ReSRIR的结构与稀疏性，导出一个高效的参数化表示用于沉浸式音频编码。

Result: 实验评估表明，在单传输通道场景下，所提方法相比IVAS实现了更高的压缩率，同时保持与IVAS相当或略优的音质。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于ReSRIR盲估计的HOA编码方案能够在低码率下有效压缩混响内容，克服了IVAS在房间声学建模上的局限，为沉浸式音频在带宽受限网络上的传输提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Immersive virtual listening relies on spatial audio technologies such as Higher-Order Ambisonics (HOA), which represent sound scenes as multichannel signals. As the desired spatial resolution increases, so does the number of channels, making efficient compression essential for transmission over bandwidth-limited networks. Moreover, to facilitate deployment by network operators, the target bitrate for immersive audio coding should ideally remain close to the 25 kbps currently allocated to VoLTE audio services. State-of-the-art parametric codecs, such as the recently standardized Immersive Voice and Audio Services (IVAS) codec, achieve compression by transmitting spatial metadata together with a reduced number of transport channels. However, recent studies have shown that IVAS performance degrades on reverberant content, particularly at low bitrates, a limitation that suggests its inability to accurately model room acoustics. In this paper, we propose a novel HOA coding scheme based on the explicit and blind estimation of the Relative Spatial Room Impulse Response (ReSRIR), using a beamformed version of the HOA signal as a reference signal. By exploiting the structure and sparsity of the estimated ReSRIR, we derive an efficient parametric representation for immersive audio coding. Experimental evaluations show that the proposed method achieves higher compression than IVAS in the single-transport-channel regime, while maintaining comparable to slightly better quality.

</details>


### [238] [Interpreting Dolphin Vocal Sequences via Multiple Sequence Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08795)
*Daniel Kohlsdorf,Denise Herzing,Thad Starner*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出将生物信息学中的ClustalW算法改编为连续高斯核相似度度量，用于分析海豚发声的声学数据，通过生成多序列比对可视化来揭示野生动物的群体交流结构模式，如攻击情境下的同步爆发脉冲等时间性主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解海豚交流对于揭示野生群体的语言复杂性和社会结构至关重要，但标准频谱图检查难以检测到深层结构模式，需要更有效的分析工具。

Method: 将ClustalW生物信息学算法应用于连续声学数据，将海豚发声视为高维频谱特征向量；用连续高斯核相似度度量替代传统的离散评分方式，生成多序列比对（MSA）可视化。

Result: 生成的比对可视化揭示了共享的结构模式，能够在攻击性情境中发现诸如同步爆发脉冲等难以通过标准频谱图方法检测的时间性动机。

Conclusion: 该跨学科方法（生物信息学算法+声学分析）为海豚交流研究提供了新的分析视角，有望深化对海洋哺乳动物社会交流模式的理解。

Abstract: Dolphin communication understanding is essential for uncovering the linguistic complexity and social structures of wild pods. We adapt the ClustalW bioinformatics algorithm to analyze continuous acoustic data, treating vocalizations as high-dimensional spectral feature vectors. By replacing discrete scoring with a continuous Gaussian kernel similarity measure, our framework generates Multiple Sequence Alignment (MSA) visualizations that reveal shared structural patterns. These alignments highlight temporal motifs such as synchronized burst pulses in aggressive contexts that are difficult to detect through standard spectrogram inspection.

</details>


### [239] [Omni Interaction Agent Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08977)
*Orantqing,Shengpeng Ji,Junlong Tong,Jialong Zuo,Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Yangzhuo Li,Shangda Wu,Franz,Evan,Theron Veyra,Changhao Pan,Jingyu Lu,Dongchao Yang,Zhifei Xie,Yang Tan,Xiaoyu Shen,Xiaoda Yang,Wenfu Wang,Teddysun,Steveyves,Zhou Zhao,Bryanytian*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出Gander，一个端到端模型，在单一框架内统一了全模态感知、实时交互和智能体能力。与传统回合制范式不同，Gander持续接收视频、语音和文本等多模态流式输入，支持自然全双工交互。其采用Cerebellum-Brain协作架构，Cerebellum负责实时交互与全模态对话，Brain负责复杂推理与高层智能体任务；Cerebellum基于流式Thinker-Talker架构构建，在块级别将输入输出展平为有序token流以实现低延迟连续交互。实验表明Gander在对话、全模态理解、交互能力与智能体智能四个维度表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型多采用回合制交互范式，难以支持自然、连续的全双工交互，也无法同时兼顾实时感知响应与复杂推理的智能体任务，亟需一个统一的端到端框架。

Method: 提出Cerebellum-Brain双组件协作架构：Cerebellum负责实时交互与全模态对话能力（基于流式Thinker-Talker架构，在块级别将输入输出展平为有序token流）；Brain负责复杂推理与高层智能体任务。两组件通过工具调用与智能体编排运行时持续交互。

Result: 内部人类评估显示，Gander在保持SOTA开源模型自然且富有表现力的口语对话能力的同时，在全模态交互中实现有竞争力的性能；在背景噪声干扰、多方交互和回通道通信等真实场景中展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Gander验证了单一框架内统一全模态感知、实时交互与智能体能力的可行性，允许用户随时打断模型，模型也能主动提供中间反馈或追问；作者将模型、代码与数据开源以促进社区进一步研究与发展。

Abstract: In this work, we present Gander, an end-to-end model that unifies omni perception, realtime interaction, and agentic capabilities within a single framework. In contrast to turn-based conventional paradigms, Gander continuously receives streaming inputs across multiple modalities, including video, speech, and text, enabling natural full-duplex interaction in both everyday conversations and complex workflow-oriented agent scenarios. Users can interrupt the model at any time, while the model can also proactively provide intermediate feedback or ask follow up questions. To natively support these capabilities, Gander adopts two key architectural designs: 1) It employs a Cerebellum-Brain collaborative framework, in which the Cerebellum is responsible for realtime interaction and omni conversational capabilities, while the Brain handles complex reasoning and higher-level agentic tasks. The two components interact continuously through tool calling and the agent orchestration runtime. 2) The Cerebellum is built upon a streaming Thinker-Talker architecture, user inputs and model outputs are further flattened into an ordered token stream at the chunk level, providing a unified representation for low latency, continuous interaction. We conduct comprehensive evaluations of Gander across four dimensions: conversational ability, omni understanding, interactive capability, and agentic intelligence. Internal human evaluations demonstrate that Gander maintains the natural and expressive spoken dialogue capabilities of SOTA open source models while achieving competitive performance in omni interaction. Gander also demonstrates robustness in challenging real-world scenarios, including background noise interference, multi-party interactions, and backchannel communication. We release Gander together with its models, code, and data to facilitate further research and development in the community.

</details>
